GUIA LEVEO (mía ctm)
Human
1-13 Elwynn Forest:
1-6 Northshire:

01) In front of you at Northshire Abbey's entrance, from Deputy Willem, accept "A Threat Within" 48.44.
02) Next to you, go in Northshire Abbey and turn in "A Threat Within" 49.42, accept "Kobold Camp Cleanup".
03) Just outside of Northshire Abbey's entrance, from Deputy Willem, accept "Eagan Peltskinner" 48.44 (#1 on map).
04) Outside on the NW side yard of Northshire Abbey, turn in "Eagan Peltskinner" 49.40, accept "Wolves Across the Border".
05) Do "Kobold Camp Cleanup" along with "Wolves Across the Border": kill Kobold Vermins /target and kill/loot wolves around 48.38.  The Kobolds are just north of Northshire Abbey (#5 on map) and the Diseased wolves are scattered all around the starting zone (but west of the river).
06) Outside on the NW side yard of Northshire Abbey, turn in "Wolves Across the Border" 49.40 (#4 on map).
07) In front of the wagon & red tent, make sure you are resupplied 47.42.
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
08) Just inside of Northshire Abbey, turn in "Kobold Camp Cleanup" 49.42 (#2 on map).  Accept "Investigate Echo Ridge" and your class's quest .
09) Go north of Northshire Abbey and do "Investigate Echo Ridge": kill Kobold Workers /target.  There are multiple camps up north and there is a larger concentration before the cave entrance around 48.32.
10) Make sure you are 1,150 XP into level 3 or greater, grind until you are.  Work your way to get resupplied while doing so 48.41.
11) Go inside of Northshire Abbey and turn in "Investigate Echo Ridge" 49.42 (#2 on map), accept "Skirmish at Echo Ridge".
12) Just outside of Northshire Abbey's entrance, from Deputy Willem, accept "Brotherhood of Thieves" 48.44(#1 on map).

13) Go east past the river and do "Brotherhood of Thieves": kill/loot the Defias Thugs around 54.49.
#1


 
01) Just outside of Northshire Abbey's entrance, from Deputy Willem, turn in "Brotherhood of Thieves" 48.44.  Accept "Milly Osworth" and "Bounty on Garrick Padfoot".
02) Just outside of Northshire Abbey make sure you are repaired/resupplied 47.42.
03) Go in the Echo Ridge Mine up north (entrance at 48.32) and do "Skirmish at Echo Ridge": kill Kobold Laborers.
04) Go to the NE outside of Northshire Abbey (next to the stables/wagon) and turn in "Milly Osworth" 51.39, accept "Milly's Harvest".
05) Go east across the river to Northshire Vineyards around 54.49 and do "Milly's Harvest": loot Milly's Harvest (crates) on the ground around the farm.  Keep the next step in mind:
06) Just east of the Northshire Vineyards do "Bounty on Garrick Padfoot": kill/loot Garrick Padfoot /target (in front of the shack, 57.48).
07) Make sure you are level 5.  Die on purpose, res at spirit, then go to the NE outside of Northshire Abbey (next to the stables/wagon) and turn in "Milly's Harvest" 51.39 (#4 on map), accept "Grape Manifest".
08) In front of Northshire Abbey's entrance, from Deputy Willem, turn in "Bounty on Garrick Padfoot" 48.44 (#1 on map).
09) Just inside of Northshire Abbey, turn in "Skirmish at Echo Ridge" 49.42.  Accept "Report to Goldshire".
10) Make a right inside of Northshire Abbey, then go up through the spiral staircase entrance 50.41 to the top of the Abbey and turn in "Grape Manifest" 49.42.
11) Go outside of Northshire Abbey and make sure you are repaired/resupplied 47.42 (#2 on map).
12) Leave Northshire Valley by heading SW exit 47.48.
13) In front of the lion fountain, accept "Rest and Relaxation" 46.48.
#02

 
6-11 Goldshire (log)
01) Follow the main path south to Goldshire and make sure you are at least 325 XP away from level 6 (2,475 XP into lvl 5) for new spells/abilities (you can grind mobs west of the main path), then go to Goldshire and in front of the refinery, turn in "Report to Goldshire" 42.66, accept "The Fargodeep Mine".
02) Enter Goldshire's Inn (entrance at 43.66).  Make a sharp left and accept "Kobold Candles" 43.66.
03) Next to you at Innkeeper Farley 44.66, turn in "Rest and Relaxation".  Also make Goldshire your new home for your Hearthstone from the Innkeeper.  Make sure you are resupplied.
04) Get your level 6 spells/abilities at Goldshire. 
05) If you have an extra silver to spare:  Upstairs in the Inn and in the middle room from the Physician 43.66, learn Apprentice First Aid.
06) On the south side of Goldshire by the wagon, accept "Gold Dust Exchange" 42.67.
07) Go SW to The Stonefield Farm and next to the house, accept "Lost Necklace" 34.84 and "Princess Must Die!" 35.84.  ALSO:  While on your way, start kill/looting boars to collect  Chunk of Boar Meats (for a future quest), and don't sell them to a vendor!
08) Go east to The Maclure Vineyards and in front of the barn turn in "Lost Necklace" 43.86, accept "Pie for Billy". 
09) Go south in The Maclure Vineyards and in the small house, accept "Young Lovers" 43.90.
10) Stop at the vendor /target 42.89 to get resupplied.  NOTE:  remember not to sell your Chunk of Boar Meats!
11) Go west to The Stonefield Farm (watch out for lvl 9 bears) and next to the river turn in "Young Lovers" 30.86, accept "Speak with Gramma".  ALSO:  While on your way, grind on Boars to collect 4 Chunk of Boar Meats (for a future quest), and don't sell them to a vendor!
12) All around southern Elwynn Forest around 33.85 do "Pie for Billy": kill/loot Boars.  NOTE:  You may already have the 4 Chunk of Boar Meats by grinding previously!
13) At the small house in The Stonefield Farm, next to the well (35.84, #7 on map), turn in "Pie for Billy", accept "Back to Billy".
14) In the small house east in The Stonefield Farm, turn in "Speak with Gramma" (35.84, #7 on map), accept "Note to William".
15) Go east to The Maclure Vineyards and in front of the barn turn in "Back to Billy" (43.86, #8 on map), accept "Goldtooth".
#03

 

01) Go NW to Fargodeep Mine and take the upper mine entrance 39.82.  Keep hugging the left side of the mine until you get to Goldtooth /target 42.78 and kill/loot him for "Goldtooth".  Then make sure "Gold Dust Exchange" along with "Kobold Candles" is finished by kill/looting the Kobolds inside or outside the mine.
02) Exit the mine exit 39.82, then go turn in "Goldtooth" 35.84.
Vendor, 2a) Stop at the Fruit Seller /target 34.83 to sell your junk.
03) Grind your way back to Goldshire, make sure you are at least 2,680 XP into level 7 before getting there.  Then by the wagon, turn in "Gold Dust Exchange" 42.67, accept "A Fishy Peril".
04) In front of the refinery, turn in "The Fargodeep Mine" 42.66, accept "The Jasperlode Mine".  And turn in "A Fishy Peril", accept "Further Concerns".
05) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied (in the refinery) 42.66.
06) In the Inn, near the entrance, turn in "Kobold Candles" 43.66, accept "Shipment to Stormwind".  Also turn in "Note to William", accept "Collecting Kelp".
First Aid? 6a) It's wise now to make sure you get Apprentice First Aid if you haven't yet:  Go upstairs in the Inn and in the middle room from the Physician 43.66, learn Apprentice First Aid.
07) Get your level 8 spells/abilities at Goldshire. 
08) Go east a bit to Crystal Lake around 56.69 and do "Collecting Kelp": kill/loot the murlocs around the lake.  TIP: Avoid going to the islands in the lake, stay around the perimeter of the lake for easy spaced out targets.
09) Go NE and enter Jasperlode Mine (entrance at 62.53) and do "The Jasperlode Mine": work your way into the mine until you get it scouted.
10) Go SE to the bridge where the main path and the river meet and then turn in "Further Concerns" 74.72, accept "Find the Lost Guards" and "Protect the Frontier".
11) Go NE a bit to Eastvale Logging Camp at the small house and accept "Red Linen Goods" 80.69.
12) NE a bit more, next to the wagon, accept "A Bundle of Trouble" 81.66.
13) Just east, you can go to the Bowyer 83.66 to get repaired/resupplied.
#04

 

01) Go just north of Eastvale Logging Camp around 80.61 and start working on "Protect the Frontier" and "A Bundle of Trouble": kill Prowlers (wolves) and Young Forest Bears.  Also loot the Bundle of Wood around the trees.  Work your way to the next step:
02) Grind NW to the shore and click on A half-eaten body 73.60 to turn in "Find the Lost Guards", accept "Discover Rolf's Fate".
03) Go NE next to the murloc camp (watch out for the multiple murlocs) and click on Rolf's corpse 80.55 to turn in "Discover Rolf's Fate", accept "Report to Thomas".
04) Make sure "A Bundle of Trouble" is finished by looting the Bundle of Wood around the trees north of Eastvale Logging Camp.  Also finish "Protect the Frontier" by killing Prowlers (wolves, #1 on map) and Young Forest Bears.
05) Go to the middle of Eastvale Logging Camp, next to the wagon and turn in "A Bundle of Trouble" 81.66.
06) You can go east a bit to the Bowyer 83.66 to get repaired/resupplied.
07) Go to where the main path and the river meets 74.72 and turn in "Protect the Frontier" and "Report to Thomas", accept "Deliver Thomas' Report".  Also accept "Report to Gryan Stoutmantle".
08) Go SW to Brackwell Pumpkin Patch around 70.79 and do "Red Linen Goods": kill/loot the Defias mobs for the bandanas around the farm.  NOTE:  Any of the Defias mobs has a very low chance to drop an item called Westfall Deed, which starts "Furlbrow's Deed" (accept it if you are able to find it).  Keep the next step in mind:
09) At Brackwell Pumpkin Patch, attempt "Princess Must Die!": try to kill/loot Princess /target.  This lvl 9 boar patrols around the farm and has two lvl 7 bodyguards.  TIPS: If you can get help near by, do so.  You can use the fences to jump around them as the boars will keep running around them.  If you did not succeed, you will be able to solo this later.  Don't worry about dying because that is what we will want to do next anyways.
#05

 

02) At the small house at Eastvale Logging Camp, turn in "Red Linen Goods" 79.69.
03) Hearth to Goldshire.
04) In the Inn, near the entrance, turn in "Collecting Kelp" 43.66.  Wait a few seconds, then accept "The Escape".
05) Just outside of the Inn, in front of the refinery 42.66, turn in "The Jasperlode Mine" and "Deliver Thomas' Report", accept "Cloth and Leather Armor".  Also accept "Westbrook Garrison Needs Help!".
06) Go into the refinery next to you and accept "Elmore's Task" 42.66.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
07) Go south to The Maclure Vineyards and in the small house, turn in "The Escape" 43.90.
08) Go west to The Stonefield Farm and in front of the small house, turn in (if you did it) "Princess Must Die!" 35.84.
09) Go NW to Westbrook Garrison and in front of the garrison's entrance, from the Wanted Poster 25.75, accept "Wanted:  "Hogger".
10) Next to you, turn in "Westbrook Garrison Needs Help!" 24.74, accept "Riverpaw Gnoll Bounty".
#06

 
11-11 Westfall + Stormwind (log)
01) Follow the main path west to enter Westfall, and just off to your right next to the wagon, turn in (if you have it) "Furlbrow's Deed" (60.19 in Westfall).  Next to you, accept "Westfall Stew".
02) Follow the main path south and off to your right go in the house at Saldean's Farm and turn in "Westfall Stew" 56.30, SKIP the next part.
03) Go south to Sentinel Hill at the tower and turn in "Report to Gryan Stoutmantle" 56.47, SKIP "The People's Militia".
04) Back inside the tower, accept "A Swift Message" 57.47.
05) Just south of the tower, at the Gryphon Master 57.53, turn in "A Swift Message", accept "Continue to Stormwind".  Then take a flight to Stormwind, Elwynn.
06) In Stormwind, go to Old Town and in the Limited Immunity, turn in "Continue to Stormwind" 77.61, accept "Dungar Longdrink".
07) Near the entrance in Stormwind, enter Pestle's Apothecary and turn in "Shipment to Stormwind" 63.74.
08) Slightly north, you can go in Weller's Arsenal and from the Weapon Master 64.69, you can learn new weapon possibilities if you can afford to.
09) Go east to the Gryphon Master 71.73 in Stormwind and turn in "Dungar Longdrink", accept "Return to Lewis".  Then fly to Sentinel Hill, Westfall.

10) In Westfall, go north a bit inside the tower and turn in "Return to Lewis" (57.47, #3 on map).
#07

 
11-12 Elwynn Forest
01) Go to Forest's Edge around 27.86 and do "Riverpaw Gnoll Bounty": kill/loot Riverpaw gnolls.  NOTES:  While doing this quest any of the gnolls has a very small chance of dropping an item called Gold Pickup Schedule, which starts "The Collector", accept it if you are able to find it.  A rare mob named Gruff Swiftbite /target has a 100% drop rate for that item, so keep an eye out for him.  Also, while doing this quest you can gather a group and then work your way to do the next quest:
02) If you have a group you can try to do "Wanted:  "Hogger": kill/loot Hogger /target (lvl 11 Elite gnoll).  If you don't have a group, you can attempt to solo him if you dare, if not it can be skipped.
03) Go north to Westbrook Garrison and in front of the entrance, turn in "Riverpaw Gnoll Bounty" 24.74.
04) Die on purpose (res at spirit) to get back to Goldshire and in front of the refinery 42.66, turn in (if you have it) "The Collector" and (if you did it) "Wanted:  "Hogger".  If it's available, accept "Manhunt".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied in the refinery.
05) Go east on the main path and at the bridge/river accept "Bounty on Murlocs" 74.72.
06) Do (if you have it) "Manhunt": go SW to Brackwell Pumpkin Patch and in the small house kill/loot Morgan the Collector 71.81.  ALSO:  If you haven't yet, at Brackwell Pumpkin Patch you can try to do "Princess Must Die!": kill/loot Princess /target.  This lvl 9 boar patrols around the farm and has two lvl 7 bodyguards.  Try to team up with someone in the area if you can or you can try to solo it.  If you can't do it, then skip it.
07) Do "Bounty on Murlocs": kill/loot Murlocs along the coast of the river south running through Elwynn Forest around 70.84.
08) Go back to the bridge where the main path and the river meet and turn in "Bounty on Murlocs" (74.72, #5 on map).
09) Go NE to Eastvale Logging Camp and at the small house turn in "Cloth and Leather Armor" 80.69.
10) Follow the main path east into Redridge Mountains (94.72 in Elwynn Forest).
11) Follow the main path east and at the first intersection, accept "Encroaching Gnolls" (15.71 in Redridge).
12) Go NE and on your left before the bridge turn in "Encroaching Gnolls" 31.60, accept "Assessing the Threat".
13) Next to you, get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master 31.59.
14) If you need to turn in either "Manhunt" or "Princess Must Die!" then Hearth to Goldshire.  If you cannot hearth then fly to Stormwind, drop in the water to die on purpose, and res at spirit to get back to Goldshire.  If you DONT need to turn in either of those quests then just fly to Stormwind, Elwynn and skip to the next section below.
15) Upgrade First Aid in the Inn (if you need to), then exit the Inn, and in front of the refinery turn in "Manhunt" (42.66, #4 on map).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
16) If you did "Princess Must Die!", go SW to The Stonefield Farm and in front of the small house 35.84, turn it in.  Afterwards go east a bit and die on purpose to the kobolds around 37.83, then res at spirit.
#09

 
11-12 Ironforge + Dun Morogh
01) If you still have any of the following quests in your questbook, they can be Abandoned:  "Wanted: "Hogger" and "Princess Must Die!".
02) Go north to enter Stormwind, then go to the Dwarven District and turn in "Elmore's Task" 60.34.  Accept "Stormpike's Delivery".
03) Go NE in the Dwarven District to enter the Deeprun Tram 69.31.  Then wait for the tram ride to show up and it will take you to Ironforge's side of the Deeprun Tram.
04) Once at the other side of the Deeprun Tram, accept "Deeprun Rat Roundup" (this is accepted between the two Tram tracks).
05) Just around the immediate area do "Deeprun Rat Roundup": use the Rat Catcher's Flute quest item on the Deeprun Rats.
06) Go back between the two tracks at the Deeprun Tram, and turn in "Deeprun Rat Roundup".  NOTE:  "Me Brother, Nipsy" will be skipped for now.
07) Exit the Deeprun Tram by heading north through the tunnel..
08) In the middle of Ironforge at The Great Forge, get the Flight Path there from the Gryphon Master 56.48.
First Aid? 08a) You can upgrade First Aid up in Ironforge Physician, entrance at 55.57.
09) Go SW to leave Ironforge (exit at 15.86).
10) In Dun Morogh, follow the main path east to Gol'Bolar Quarry and accept "The Public Servant" 69.56.
11) Next to you, behind the tent, accept "Those Blasted Troggs!" 69.56.
12) Drop down in the Quarry and enter the mine (entrance at 71.56) and do "The Public Servant" along with "Those Blasted Troggs!": kill Rockjaw Skullthumpers and Rockjaw Bonesnappers (only in mine).  Focus inside the mine more, as both of the these mobs are found in there.
13) Go back up and turn in "The Public Servant" (69.56, #10 on map).
14) Next to you, behind the tent, turn in "Those Blasted Troggs!" 69.56.
15) Follow the main path east to enter Loch Modan (entrance at 86.51)..
#10

 
12-13 Loch Modan
01) Once you enter Loch Modan you will arrive at Valley of Kings, and in front of the watchtower, accept "In Defense of the King's Lands" (22.73 in Loch Modan).
02) Go up in the watchtower in front of you and accept "The Trogg Threat" 23.74.
03) Follow the main path NE, then make a right to go to Stonesplinter Valley around 32.73 and do "In Defense of the King's Lands" along with "The Trogg Threat": kill 10 Stonesplinter Troggs and 10 Stonesplinter Scouts, and loot them for the Stone Tooth.
04) Go back west to Valley of Kings and turn in "In Defense of the King's Lands" (22.73, #1 on map).
05) Go up in the watchtower and turn in "The Trogg Threat" 23.74.
06) Go north to Thelsamar around 34.48 and find Mountaineer Kadrell /target (he patrols around town) and accept "Rat Catching" and "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task".
07) Go in the Inn (the building with the big barrels around it) and the first NPC you see (Innkeeper Hearthstove, 35.48), talk to him to make this Inn your new home.
08) At the bar, from Vidra Hearthstove, accept "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" 35.49.  Make sure you are resupplied.  If you need 6 slot bags, you can purchase them from the General Supplies vendor in the Inn (if you can afford them).
#11

 

01) While working your way north to the next steps, also start working on "Rat Catching" by kill/looting Tunnel Rat mobs (kobolds) along the west side of the main path (#3 on map).  Also start working on "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" by killing boars, bears and spiders (in the open fields of Loch Modan, #1 on map):
02) Grind north to Algaz Station, go up in the watchtower and turn in "Stormpike's Delivery".  And turn in "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task" 25.18, accept "Stormpike's Order".  Accept "Filthy Paws".  NOTE:  You can get repaired/resupplied at the bottom of the watchtower but do not sell your "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" quest items to a vendor!
03) Go east to Silver Stream Mine around 35.19 and finish "Rat Catching": kill/loot the Tunnel Rat mobs (kobolds) outside of the mine.  And if you have a group, do "Filthy Paws": loot the Miner's League Crates inside the mine.  You can skip the quest if you cannot get a group (but keep it accepted, as you will be able to solo it later).
04) If you did "Filthy Paws " go west and turn in up in the watchtower at Algaz Station, (25.18, #2 on map).
05) Finish "Thelsamar Blood Sausages": kill boars, bears and spiders (in the open fields of Loch Modan, #1 on map).
06) Hearth (if you can) to Stoutlager Inn (Thelsamar) and in the Inn at the bar, turn in "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" 35.49.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
07) In Thelsamar, find Mountaineer Kadrell /target (he patrols back and forth in the town around 34.48) and turn in "Rat Catching".
#12

 

01) You should be level 12 now, if not grind to it.

02) South in the town, go to the Gryphon Master 34.51 and take a flight to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
03) Get your level 12/14 spells/abilities. 
04) You can upgrade First Aid upstairs in the Ironforge Physician 55.58..
05) If you can afford to, you can learn new weapon possibilities south at the two Weapon Masters inside Timberline Arms 62.89.

WOTLK, 10a) Go through the Deeprun Tram 77.51.

WOTLK, 10b) Then take one of the two Deeprun Trams to take you to Stormwind City...

WOTLK, 10c) Once you arrive at the other side of the Deeprun Tram, go north through the tunnel to get into Stormwind City.

WOTLK, 10d) At Stormwind City, go to Stormwind Harbor and get on the boat 23.56 to take you to Darkshore.

11) Once at Auberdine, follow the docks east, and on your right accept "For Love Eternal" (on the dock, 36.44).
12) Continue east on the dock toward the Inn, but go upstairs and accept "Buzzbox 827" 37.44.
13) Just south of the Inn, go up on the platform and from the Hippogryph Master (36.46 in Darkshore), get the Flight Path.

Night Elf
1-6 Shadowglen (Teldrassil)
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, WotLK, or SoM)
01) In front of you from Conservator Ilthalaine, accept "The Balance of Nature" 59.45.
02) Start working on "The Balance of Nature": kill Young Nightsabers and Young Thistle Boars around the immediate area.  Work your way to the next steps while doing this:
03) Go east to the entrance of Aldrassil and accept "The Woodland Protector" 60.42.
04) Near where you started from, at the Dryad Tarindrella, turn in "The Woodland Protector" 58.45, accept the next part.
05) Go just west to the Grell camps around 56.46 and do "The Woodland Protector": kill/loot Grell and Grellkin.
06) Back at the Dryad Tarindrella, turn in "The Woodland Protector" (58.45 #4 on map).
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
07) Make sure "The Balance of Nature" is finished, then turn it in (at the starting spot 59.45, #1 on map).  Accept "The Balance of Nature" (Part2).
08) Go near the entrance of Aldrassil, next to the Cauldron and accept "A Good Friend" 61.42.
09) Go in Aldrassil to get repaired/resupplied 59.41.
10) Go to the west side of Aldrassil at the bottom of the ramp entrance and accept "Webwood Venom" 58.42.
#01

 
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Grind NW to Iverron (sitting between some mountains) and turn in "A Good Friend" 55.33, accept "A Friend in Need".
02) Just east of you, do "Webwood Venom": kill/loot Webwood Spiders.  A lot of spiders can be found outside of the cave and more inside 57.32.
03) All around the north side of Shadowglen, do "The Balance of Nature": kill Mangy Nightsabers and Thistle Boars.
04) Die on purpose, then res at Spirit Healer.

05) Go south to the starting spot and from Conservator Ilthalaine, turn in "The Balance of Nature" 59.45. 
06) Go east near the entrance of Aldrassil, next to the Cauldron and turn in "A Friend in Need" 61.42, accept "Iverron's Antidote".
07) Go in Aldrassil to get repaired/resupplied 59.41.
08) Go to the west side of Aldrassil at the bottom of the ramp entrance and turn in "Webwood Venom" 58.42, accept "Webwood Egg".
09) Go west to the Grellkin camp around 55.39 and do part of "Iverron's Antidote": kill/loot Grellkin or Grell for the Hyacinth Mushrooms.  NOTES:   You can also find these from Hyacinth Mushroom objects around the trees & mountains all around Shadowglen, but focus on kill/looting the Grellkin for faster leveling.
10) Go east to the two small ponds around 58.38 and do part of "Iverron's Antidote": loot the Moonpetal Lily (yellow flowers) around the ponds.
#02

 

01) Go NW to Shadowthread Cave entrance 57.32 and do "Webwood Egg" along with finishing "Iverron's Antidote": kill/loot the spiders while working your way NW in the cave to eventually loot a Webwood Egg.
02) Use your Hearthstone to hearth to the starting spot.
03) Go east near the entrance of Aldrassil, next to the Cauldron and turn in "Iverron's Antidote" 61.42, accept the next part.  NOTE:  Work somewhat quickly to do the next steps as this quest is on a 5 min timer.
04) Go in Aldrassil to get repaired/resupplied 59.41.
05) Go to the west side of Aldrassil at the bottom of the ramp entrance and turn in "Webwood Egg" 58.42.  Accept "Tenaron's Summons".
06) Go up the ramp next to you, to the very top and then turn in "Tenaron's Summons" 59.39, accept "Crown of the Earth".
07) Go NW to Iverron (sitting between some mountains) and turn in "Iverron's Antidote" 55.33.
08) Go east to the Moonwell 60.33 and do "Crown of the Earth": use the Crystal Phial quest item in the Moonwell.
09) Die on purpose, then res at Spirt Healer.

10) Go to the top of Aldrassil (ramp entrance at 58.41, #5 on map) and turn in "Crown of the Earth", accept the next part.
11) Follow the main path south to leave Shadowglen and on your way out, accept "Dolanaar Delivery" 61.48.
#03

 
6-10 Dolanaar
01) Follow the main path to Dolanaar and on your way there, off to your right, from Zenn Foulhoof, accept "Zenn's Bidding" 60.56.
02) Keep going west to Dolanaar and make sure you are at least 340 XP away from level 6 before getting there for new spells/abilities.  Once at Dolanaar, at the bottom of the tower entrance, accept "Denalan's Earth" 56.58.
03) Just up the ramp, accept "A Troubling Breeze" 56.57.
04) If you have 6 or more silver:  Just inside the tower, from the First Aid Trainer 55.57, learn Apprentice First Aid.  NOTE:  If not, you will want to make sure you save your money for spells/abilities which is more important.
05) Next to you, go up the ramp to the very top of the tower and accept "Twisted Hatred" and "The Emerald Dreamcatcher" 55.57.
06) Drop down and go in the Inn to get repaired/resupplied 56.59.
07) Go up the ramp to the Innkeeper and turn in "Dolanaar Delivery" 56.60.  Also make Dolanaar your new home for your hearthstone.
08) Go south in Dolanaar to the Moonwell and turn in "Crown of the Earth" 56.62, accept the next part.   NOTE:  If you are already lvl 6 right now, then do the next step first before doing this!
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
#04

 

01) Go SE to the small camp at Lake Al'Ameth and turn in "Denalan's Earth" 61.68.  Accept "Timberling Seeds" and "Timberling Sprouts".

03) Go back to the small camp at Lake Al'Ameth and turn in "Timberling Seeds" (61.68, #1 on map), accept "Rellian Greenspyre".  Also turn in "Timberling Sprouts".
04) Start working on "Zenn's Bidding": kill/loot nightsabers (cats), spiders and owls.  While working on this, work your way to the next steps:
05) Go NE to the Moonwell at Starbreeze Village and do "Crown of the Earth": use the Jade Phial quest item in the Moonwell 63.58.
06) Follow the path east and go up in the first building on your right and turn in "A Troubling Breeze" 66.59, accept "Gnarlpine Corruption".
07) Go in the east most building at Starbreeze Village and do "The Emerald Dreamcatcher": loot Tallonkai's Dresser 68.60.
08) Finish "Zenn's Bidding": kill/loot nightsabers (cats), spiders and owls around the open fields of Teldrassil (#4 on map).
09) Go to Zenn Foulhoof north of the main path between Dolanaar and Starbreeze Village and turn in "Zenn's Bidding" 60.56.  NOTE:  Make sure you save the quest reward item for later, do not sell it to a vendor!
10) Go west to Dolanaar and at the bottom of the tower entrance, accept "Seek Redemption!" 56.58.  NOTE:  Save any Small Spider Legs you have as you will use them for a later quest.
11) Just up the ramp, turn in "Gnarlpine Corruption" 56.57,
First Aid? 11b) If you have 6 or more silver (and haven't yet):  Just inside the tower, from the First Aid Trainer 55.57, learn Apprentice First Aid.  NOTE:  You can also get resupplied in the tower.  You will want to make sure you save your money for spells/abilities which is more important.
12) Go south in the Inn to get repaired/resupplied 56.59.
13) Go south in Dolanaar to the Moonwell and turn in "Crown of the Earth" 56.62, accept the next part.
14) Just east of you at the Cauldron, Learn Apprentice Cook from the Cook 57.61.  Then accept "Recipe of the Kaldorei" (cooking quest).  Then as you are doing the next sections start working on this by kill/looting any spiders along the way and do not sell the Small Spider Legs to a vendor.  Also turn this in once you get all the legs.
#05

 

01) Do "Seek Redemption!": loot 3 Fel Cones.  These are found around the trees of Teldrassil.  See map for locations.
02) Go north of the main path between Dolanaar and Starbreeze Village at Zenn Foulhoof 60.56 and turn in "Seek Redemption!".
03) If you happen to have a group, continue on with the next steps (even if you are lvl 7).  If you are going solo, make sure you are level 8 before proceeding, grind on any mobs in the immediate area to level 8.
04) Go to Dolanaar and in the Inn, get repaired/resupplied 56.59.
05) If you are level 8, get new spells/abilities at your class trainer (if you haven't yet). 
06) Go just north to Fel Rock (cave entrance at 55.52), enter the cave and do "Twisted Hatred": keep hugging the right side of the cave and then make a left after the river, then see if Lord Melenas /target 52.50 (an orange satyr) is standing at the edge and kill/loot him.  If he is not there, continue hugging the right side of the cave and you will eventually get to him.  This mob can be hard to solo so be prepared.  Use the Severed Voodoo Claw to help you kill him.  If you cannot do it, then skip it.  Then exit the cave by heading SE.
07) Go to the bottom of the tree tower at Dolanaar 55.57 to get resupplied at Danlyia, then go up to the top of the tower and turn in "Twisted Hatred" and "The Emerald Dreamcatcher", accept "Ferocitas the Dream Eater".
08) Go NE of Starbreeze Village and do "Ferocitas the Dream Eater": kill 7 Gnarlpine Mystics and kill/loot Ferocitas the Dream Eater (grey color furblog at 69.53), then loot the Gnarlpine Necklace that drops (in your inventory).
09) Hearth to Dolanaar.
10) In the Inn, get repaired/resupplied and get lvl 8 spells/abilities if you haven't yet. 
11) Go to the top of the tree tower at Dolanaar 55.57 (#7 on map) and turn in "Ferocitas the Dream Eater".

14) If you need to, check again to see if Blackmoss the Fetid /target is there (a lvl 13 rare at around 44.30 or around 43.27 #7 on map).  Try to kill/loot this mob, he drops an item, which starts "The Moss-twined Heart", accept it.  NOTES:  This mob has a 2 hour respawn, if you cannot kill him, then it can be skipped.

#01

03) Go to the dwarf camp south on the main path and accept "Scalding Mornbrew Delivery" 25.76.  NOTE:  This quest has a 5 min time limit, as long as you do not go AFK or something you should have plenty of time to get this in.

11) All around western Dun Morogh (past Iceflow Lake) continue working on "A Favor for Evershine" by killing the Ice Claw Bears, Elder Crag Boars and Snow Leopards (#7 on map).  Once you hit level 8, Hearth, or just go back to Kharanos.
#06

 
8-10 Dun Morogh (log)
01) In Thunderbrew Distillery (the Inn), at Innkeeper Belm 47.52, purchase a Thunder Ale.  Make sure you are resupplied.

#07

 

01) All around western Dun Morogh (past Iceflow Lake) finish "A Favor for Evershine": kill Ice Claw Bears, Elder Crag Boars and Snow Leopards around the open fields of Dun Morogh.

#08

 
10-11 Dun Morogh
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Follow the main path east to Gol'Bolar Quarry and finish (if you still need to) "Those Blasted Troggs!" by killing the Rockjaw Skullthumpers at Gol'Bolar Quarry around 68.59 and "The Public Servant" by killing Rockjaw Bonesnappers in the cave (entrance at 71.56).  These quests are accepted at the Quarry there!
02) Go back up and turn in "The Public Servant" 69.56.
03) Next to you, behind the tent, turn in "Those Blasted Troggs!" 69.56.
04) Go north to Amberstill Ranch and accept "Protecting the Herd" 63.50.

05) Just to the west of you is a hidden path that leads up the mountain, go up it then head north and do "Protecting the Herd": kill/loot Vagash /target around 62.34.  This lvl 11 Yeti patrols up around the mountain ledges.  This can be skipped if it gives you troubles.

06) Back at Amberstill Ranch, turn in "Protecting the Herd" (63.50, #4 on map).

07) Follow the path NE to North Gate Outpost and accept "The Lost Pilot" 84.39.
08) Go NW a bit and click on A Dwarven Corpse to turn in "The Lost Pilot" 80.36, accept "A Pilot's Revenge".
09) Go just SW up the path a bit and do "A Pilot's Revenge": kill/loot Mangeclaw /target 78.38.
10) Back east at the Outpost turn in "A Pilot's Revenge" (84.39, #7 on map).
11) Go to the South Gate Outpost and turn in "Shimmer Stout" 86.49, accept "Stout to Kadrell".
12) Enter South Gate Pass (entrance at 17.59) and go through it to get to Loch Modan..
#16

 
11-12 Loch Modan (log)
01) Once you enter Loch Modan you will arrive at Valley of Kings, and in front of the watchtower, accept "In Defense of the King's Lands" (22.73 in Loch Modan).
02) Go up in the watchtower in front of you and accept "The Trogg Threat" 23.74.
03) Follow the main path NE, then make a right to go to Stonesplinter Valley around 32.73 and do "In Defense of the King's Lands" along with "The Trogg Threat": kill 10 Stonesplinter Troggs and 10 Stonesplinter Scouts, and loot them for the Stone Tooth.
04) Go back west to Valley of Kings and turn in "In Defense of the King's Lands" (22.73, #1 on map).
05) Go up in the watchtower and turn in "The Trogg Threat" 23.74.
06) Go north to Thelsamar around 34.48 and find Mountaineer Kadrell (he patrols back and forth in the town) and turn in "Stout to Kadrell".  From him accept "Rat Catching" and "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task".
07) Go in the Inn (the building with the big barrels around it), (entrance at 35.48).. and the first NPC you see (Innkeeper Hearthstove, 35.48), talk to him to make this Inn your new home.
08) At the bar, from Vidra Hearthstove, accept "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" 35.49.  Make sure you are resupplied.  If you need 6 slot bags, you can purchase them from the General Supplies vendor in the Inn (if you can afford them).
#17

 

01) While working your way north to the next steps, also start working on "Rat Catching" by kill/looting Tunnel Rat mobs (kobolds) along the west side of the main path (#3 on map).  Also start working on "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" by killing boars, bears and spiders (in the open fields of Loch Modan, #1 on map):
02) Grind north to Algaz Station, go up in the watchtower and turn in "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task" 25.18, Accept "Stormpike's Order".  Also accept "Filthy Paws".  NOTE:  You can get repaired/resupplied at the bottom of the watchtower but do not sell your "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" quest items to a vendor!
03) Go east to Silver Stream Mine around 35.19 and finish "Rat Catching": kill/loot the Tunnel Rat mobs (kobolds) outside of the mine.  And if you have a group, do "Filthy Paws": loot the Miner's League Crates inside the mine.  You can skip the quest if you cannot get a group (but keep it accepted, as you will be able to solo it later).
04) If you did "Filthy Paws " go west and turn in up in the watchtower at Algaz Station, (25.18, #2 on map).
05) Finish "Thelsamar Blood Sausages": kill boars, bears and spiders (in the open fields of Loch Modan, #1 on map).
06) Hearth (if you can) to Stoutlager Inn (Thelsamar) and in the Inn at the bar, turn in "Thelsamar Blood Sausages" 35.49.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
07) In Thelsamar, find Mountaineer Kadrell (he patrols back and forth in the town) and turn in "Rat Catching".
08) East in Thelsamar, down in the hut, accept "Honor Students" 37.48 .
#18


01) You should be level 12 now, if not grind to it.
02) South in the town, go to the Gryphon Master 34.51 and turn in "Honor Students", accept "Ride to Ironforge".  Then take a flight to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
03) In Ironforge, go north to Deep Mountain Mining Guild and turn in "Ride to Ironforge" 51.26, accept "Gryth Thurden".
04) Go to the Gryphon Master 55.48 and turn in "Gryth Thurden", accept "Return to Brock".  Then take a flight to Thelsamar, Loch Modan.
05) East in Thelsamar, down in the hut, turn in "Return to Brock" 37.48.
06) South in the town, go to the Gryphon Master (34.51, #2 on map) and take a flight to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
07) You can upgrade First Aid upstairs in the Ironforge Physician 55.58..
08) If you can afford to, you can learn new weapon possibilities south at the two Weapon Masters inside Timberline Arms 62.89.

WOTLK, 13a) Go through the Deeprun Tram 77.51, and then between the two Tram track accept "Deeprun Rat Roundup".

WOTLK, 13b) Just around the immediate area do "Deeprun Rat Roundup": use the Rat Catcher's Flute quest item on the Deeprun Rats.

WOTLK, 13c) Go back between the two tracks at the Deeprun Tram, and turn in "Deeprun Rat Roundup", accept "Me Brother, Nipsy".

WOTLK, 13d) Then take one of the two Deeprun Trams to take you to Stormwind City.

WOTLK, 13e) Once you arrive at the other side of the Deeprun Tram, turn in "Me Brother, Nipsy" between the two tram tracks.  Then go north through the tunnel to get into Stormwind City.

WOTLK, 13f) At Stormwind City, go to Stormwind Harbor and get on the boat 23.56 to take you to Darkshore.

14) Once at Auberdine, follow the docks east, and on your right accept "For Love Eternal" (on the dock, 36.44).
15) Continue east on the dock toward the Inn, but go upstairs and accept "Buzzbox 827" 37.44.
16) Just south of the Inn, go up on the platform and from the Hoppogryph Master (36.46 in Darkshore), get the Flight Path.















12+


12-17 Darkshore (log)
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, SoM, TBC or WotLK)
01) At Auberdine on the Hippogryph dock, accept "Washed Ashore" 37.46.
02) Go in the Inn and from Innkeeper Shaussiy 37.44, make Auberdine your new home.  NOTE:  From the Fish Vendor /target in the Inn you can purchase Longjaw Mud Snapper, which is a cheap tier 2 food.
03) NIGHT ELF ONLY (other races should have already did this):  Go west of the Inn on the long docks and accept "For Love Eternal" 36.44.
04) NIGHT ELF ONLY (other races should have already did this):  Go back toward the Inn, but go upstairs and accept "Buzzbox 827" 37.44.
05) Just outside of the Inn, next to the mailbox, accept "Cave Mushrooms" 37.44.
06) Just across the path from you near the moonwell, accept "The Red Crystal" 38.43.
07) Go north across the bridge and into the merchant building then accept "Bashal'Aran" and "Tools of the Highborne" 37.40.

GET BAGS:  At this point if you do not have all 6 slot bags it is highly recommended to buy them from the General Goods (37.40, /target) vendor in the merchant building, as long as you can afford them.
08) Take the SE bridge to the other building and at the entrance, accept "Plagued Lands" 39.43.
09) Go inside the building and accept "How Big a Threat?" 39.43.
10) Work your way down south along the shore while doing "Buzzbox 827": kill/loot crabs.  Work your way to the Beached Sea Creature 36.51 and click on it to loot the Sea Creature Bones for "Washed Ashore".
11) Go back north to Auberdine and next to the Hippogryph dock, off in the grass, from Buzzbox 827 (37.46, #1 on map), turn in "Buzzbox 827", accept "Buzzbox 411".
12) On the Hippogryph dock, turn in "Washed Ashore" 37.46, accept the next part.
13) Go west out in the sea and do "Washed Ashore": loot the Skeletal Sea Turtle's head 32.46.
14) Around in the sea do "Buzzbox 411": kill/loot Darkshore Threshers /target.
#01

 

01) Go back to Auberdine on the Hippogryph dock and turn in "Washed Ashore" 37.46.
02) In the town, get repaired/resupplied 37.44.
03) Go north to the shore, at the Beached Sea Creature 42.31 and click on it to accept "Beached Sea Creature".
04) Go north along the shore and at Buzzbox 411 42.29, turn in "Buzzbox 411", accept "Buzzbox 323".
05) As you work your way to do the next steps, start working on "Buzzbox 323" by kill/looting the Moonstalkers (cats):
06) Go south to Bashal'Aran, up on the hill, in the blue mist, turn in "Bashal'Aran" 44.36, accept the next part.
07) Just south of where you are at around 45.37, do "Bashal'Aran": kill/loot Vile Sprites and Wild Grell.
08) Go back up on the hill at Bashal'Aran, in the blue mist, turn in "Bashal'Aran" (44.36, #6 on map), accept the next part.
09) Just south of where you are at around 45.37, do "Bashal'Aran": kill/loot Deth'ryll Satyr /target (orange colored patrolling satyr).
10) Go back up on the hill at Bashal'Aran, in the blue mist, turn in "Bashal'Aran" (44.36, #6 on map), accept the next part.
11) Grind down south and on top of the hill, simply run up close to the Mysterious Red Crystal 47.49 for "The Red Crystal".
12) Go west across the main path into the furbolg camp 40.54 to simply get it discovered for "How Big a Threat?".
13) Go down south on the main path and accept "The Fall of Ameth'Aran" 40.60.
#02

 

01) Go east at Ameth'Aran and start working on "Tools of the Highborne": kill/loot the Highborne mobs (apparitions).  While doing this, also work your way to do the next steps:
02) Do part of "The Fall of Ameth'Aran": next to the old dark gazebo, click The Lay of Ameth'Aran 43.59 white tablet laying on the ground.
03) Go south to the other gazebo and do "Bashal'Aran": click on the Ancient Flame 42.62.  Also do "For Love Eternal": find and kill/loot Anaya Dawnrunner /target, she patrols clockwise around this gazebo.
04) Go south a bit more and do the last part of "The Fall of Ameth'Aran": click on the The Fall of Ameth'Aran tablet standing upright 43.63.
05) Make sure "Tools of the Highborne" is finished:  kill/loot the Highborne mobs (apparitions, #1 on map) around Ameth'Aran.
06) Make sure you are at least 6,000 XP into level 13 before continuing.  Grind on Highborne mobs (apparitions) around Ameth'Aran until you are.
07) Go west to the main path and turn in "The Fall of Ameth'Aran" 40.60.
08) To the west of the main path do "Plagued Lands": use Tharnariun's Hope quest item on a Rabid Thistle Bear /target around 39.60.  NOTE:  Make sure you are in range and in clear view of the mob when using the quest item, otherwise you might miss and will have to go back to Auberdine to get a new one.
#03

 

01) Hearth to Auberdine.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) Go west of the Inn on the long docks and turn in "For Love Eternal" 36.44.
03) Just outside of the Inn and across the path by the moonwell, turn in "The Red Crystal" 38.43, accept "As Water Cascades".
04) Use the Empty Water Tube quest item at the moonwell next to you 38.44.
05) Go east across the bridge and at the entrance to the building, turn in "Plagued Lands" 39.43, accept "Cleansing of the Infected".
06) Go in the building and turn in "How Big a Threat?" 39.43, accept the next part.  Also accept "Thundris Windweaver".
07) Go up in the building and accept "The Tower of Althalaxx" 39.44.
08) Take the bridge going north and at the dwarf area by the wagon, accept "Deep Ocean, Vast Sea" 38.41.
09) Go in the merchant building next to you 37.40 and turn in "Tools of the Highborne" and "Thundris Windweaver", accept "The Cliffspring River".
10) Go to the Hippogryph dock (south of the Inn) and turn in "Beached Sea Creature" 37.46.

11) You should be level 14 now.  If you are a Mage, Paladin, Hunter, Priest, Rogue or Warrior and could benefit from getting your level 14 spells/abilities, then you can do that now in Darnassus.    The other classes can take the boat 32.44 to Stormwind to get their spells/abilities.  So if you can benefit from getting your lvl 14 abilities, Night Elves can take a Hippogryph ride from the Hippogryph Master next to you.  If you are dwarf, gnome or human you will have to take the Boat to Teldrassil 33.40, then to get back to Auberdine take the flight path (at 58.94 in Teldrassil).
#04

 

01) As you work on the following steps start working on "Cleansing of the Infected" by killing the Rabid Thistle Bears.  Also continue working on "Buzzbox 323" by kill/looting the Moonstalkers (cats):
02) Go north to Bashal'Aran, up on the hill, in the blue mist, turn in "Bashal'Aran" 44.36.
03) Go NW out in the sea and do "Deep Ocean, Vast Sea": loot the two chests in the two sunken boats.  First one, go under the boat and enter the crack on the bottom of the boat (entrance at 39.29), then head NW in the boat and loot the Silver Dawning's Lockbox 38.29:
04) Head NE and enter the bottom of the other boat 40.27, then head west in the boat and loot the Mist Veil's Lockbox 40.27.
05) Go NE on the shore and click on the Beached Sea Turtle's arm 44.21 to accept "Beached Sea Turtle".
06) Just south of you in the open fields of Darkshore, finish "Buzzbox 323" by kill/looting the Moonstalkers /target (cats, #1 on map).
07) Follow the main path east and just past the bridge on your left is Buzzbox 323 51.25, from it turn in "Buzzbox 323", accept "Buzzbox 525".
08) Next to you, drop down at the bottom of the waterfall and do "The Cliffspring River": use the Empty Sampling Tube quest item at the bottom of the waterfall 51.26.
#05

 

01) Go south and up on the hill, click on the Mysterious Red Crystal 47.49 to turn in "As Water Cascades", accept "The Fragments Within".
02) Go SW past the main path to the furbolg camp around 40.54 and do "How Big a Threat?": kill the required amount of Blackwood furbolgs.
03) Work your way down south while killing Rabid Thistle Bears (#7 on map) for "Cleansing of the Infected".  Eventually make your way to the shoreline at the Beached Sea Turtle 37.62 and click on its head to accept "Beached Sea Turtle".
04) Continue down south while killing Rabid Thistle Bears (#7 on map) for "Cleansing of the Infected".  Eventually make your way to the shoreline at the Beached Sea Creature 36.71 and click the body to accept "Beached Sea Creature".
05) Go south to the Beached Sea Creature 33.81 and click where the neck meets the body to accept "Beached Sea Creature".
06) If you have "Grove of the Ancients":  Turn this in at Onu big tree NPC, 44.76.
07) East of you around the open fields of Darkshore around 38.71 finish "Cleansing of the Infected": kill Rabid Thistle Bears.  Also start working on "Buzzbox 525" by kill/looting Grizzled Thistle Bears.
08) Go down south around 40.81 and finish "Buzzbox 525": kill/loot Grizzled Thistle Bears (#7 on map).
09) Just right off the main path go to Buzzbox 525 41.81 and turn in "Buzzbox 525".
#06

 

01) Keep grinding until you are 6,370 XP3,550 XP into level 15, then Hearth to Auberdine.
02) Go to the Hippogryph dock (south of the Inn, 37.46) and turn in ALL "Beached Sea" quests you may have.
03) Go below the Hippogryph dock, next to the shore and accept "Fruit of the Sea" 36.45.  NOTE:  "The Family and the Fishing Pole" is a fishing quest that takes too much time for XP and I recommend skipping it.  If you are bored and want to learn fishing or do something different you can give it a try.
05) Just across the path from you, near the moonwell, turn in "The Fragments Within" 38.43.
06) Take the bridge north and just outside of the merchant building (to your left), accept "The Absent Minded Prospector" 37.42.
07) Go in the merchant building next to you and turn in "The Cliffspring River" 37.40, accept "The Blackwood Corrupted" (requires lvl 15).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
08) Just outside of the building at the dwarf camp next to the wagon, turn in "Deep Ocean, Vast Sea" 38.41.
09) Take the SE bridge to the other building and at the entrance, turn in "Cleansing of the Infected" 39.43, accept "Tharnariun's Hope".
10) Go in the building and turn in "How Big a Threat?" 39.43.  Accept "A Lost Master".
11) Go to the moonwell in Auberdine and use your Empty Cleansing Bowl quest item in it 38.44.

12) You should be level 16 by now:  If you are a Mage, Paladin, Druid, Priest, Rogue or Warrior and could benefit from getting your level 16 spells/abilities, you can do that now in Darnassus.  The other classes can take the boat 32.44 to Stormwind to get their spells/abilities.  So if you can benefit from getting your lvl 16 abilities, you can take a Hippogryph ride from the Hippogryph Master 36.46 or you might need to take the boat on the docks.  Then hearth (or fly back) to Auberdine.
#07

 

01) Go NE and do part of "The Blackwood Corrupted": loot the Blackwood Grain Stores 51.35.
02) Go up to the small cave (path entrance at 53.37).  Once up there do "Tharnariun's Hope": kill Den Mother.
03) Go NE a bit to the other furbolg camp and finish "The Blackwood Corrupted": loot the Blackwood Nut Stores 52.33.  See next steps for what else to do:
04) On the other side of this camp, loot the Blackwood Fruit Stores 53.33.
05) Use the Filled Cleansing Bowl quest item at the Bonfire in the middle of the camp 52.33.  Kill Xabraxxis (lvl 19) that spawns, then loot Xabraxxis' Demon Bag that spawns on the ground.  NOTES: You can use the Filled Cleansing Bowl 20 yards away from the Bonfire and when you do it will make you friendly with all the furblogs at the camp for a minute giving you an easier chance to kill the lvl 19 satyr.
06) Go south, then up and through the waterfall in front of the cave (path entrance at 55.32) and do "Cave Mushrooms": loot the mushrooms on the ground in the cave.  NOTE:  This can be hard to solo for some classes (rogues, warriors, etc), in that case try to get help or the guide will remind you once more to come back here once you are stronger later on (but it will be a tad off route).
07) Go NE and just off the main path next to a rock, turn in "The Tower of Althalaxx" 55.25, accept the next part.
08) Go SE a bit (around the tower, 55.27) and do "The Tower of Althalaxx": kill/loot Dark Strand Fanatics (female humans).  Warning:  Do NOT go inside the tower, as there is higher level mobs in there!
09) Back just off the main path next to a rock, turn in "The Tower of Althalaxx" (55.25, #7 on map), accept the next part.
10) Go north to the shore and click on the Beached Sea Turtle's head 53.18 to accept "Beached Sea Turtle".
11) Work your way west along the shore and do "Fruit of the Sea": kill/loot Encrusted Tide Crawlers (higher lvl crabs) and Reef Crawlers (lower lvl crabs more west).
#09

 

01) Hearth to Auberdine.  If hearthstone is still on a cooldown, then die on purpose, res at spirit, then go to Auberdine.  If you died on purpose, then turn in the following 5 quests in reverse:
02) Go below the Hippogryph dock, next to the shore and turn in "Fruit of the Sea" 36.45.
03) Go to the Hippogryph dock (south of the Inn) and turn in "Beached Sea Turtle" 37.46.
04) Just outside of the Inn, next to the mailbox, turn in "Cave Mushrooms" 37.44, accept "Onu".
05) Take the north bridge and in the merchant building, turn in "The Blackwood Corrupted" 37.40.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
06) Take the SE bridge to the other building and at the entrance, turn in "Tharnariun's Hope" 39.43.
10) Out on the long docks, take the boat to Stormwind City 32.44.

11) The goal now is to get to Loch Modan to continue questing.  If you are a Dwarf, Gnome or Human then go to the Gryphon Master 71.73 and fly to Thelsamar, Loch Modan, then skip to the Loch Modan section below!  Night Elves will have a much longer journey, so if you are a Night Elf, follow the steps below:

12) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Go NE in the Dwarven District to enter the Deeprun Tram 69.31.  Then wait for the tram ride to show up and it will take you to Ironforge's side of the Deeprun Tram.

13) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Once at the other side of the Deeprun Tram, accept "Deeprun Rat Roundup" (this is accepted between the two Tram tracks).

14) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Just around the immediate area do "Deeprun Rat Roundup": use the Rat Catcher's Flute quest item on the Deeprun Rats.

15) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Go back between the two tracks at the Deeprun Tram, and turn in "Deeprun Rat Roundup".  NOTE:  "Me Brother, Nipsy" will be skipped for now.

16) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Exit the Deeprun Tram by heading north through the tunnel..

17) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  At Ironforge get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master 55.48.

18) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Exit Ironforge south 16.85.

19) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Run all the way east through Dun Morogh to the South Gate Pass 82.53 and go through it. Then go through the other tunnel of the South Gate Pass 17.59.
#10

 
17-18 Loch Modan (log)
02) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  Go NE to Thelsamar, find Mountaineer Kadrell (he patrols back and forth in the town around 35.47) and from him accept "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task".
03) NIGHT ELF ONLY:  South in the town, go to the Gryphon Master 34.51 and get the FP.
04) At Thelsamar, go down in the eastern most hut and accept "Ironband's Excavation" (37.47 in Loch Modan).
05) Go east to Ironband's Excavation Site and turn in "Ironband's Excavation" 65.67, accept "Gathering Idols".
06) Across the camp, accept "Excavation Progress Report" 66.66.
07) Just to the east of you around the Excavation Site around 67.63, do "Gathering Idols": kill/loot the troggs.
08) Go back to the small camp at the Excavation Site and turn in "Gathering Idols" (65.67, #5 on map).
09) Go east to enter The Farstrider Lodge (entrance on the east side of the lodge), make a right, then accept "Crocolisk Hunting" 82.62. 
10) At the other side of the lodge, accept "A Hunter's Boast" 83.65.  NOTE:  This quest is on a 15 min timer, so work somewhat quickly before it expires.
#11

 

01) Just west of The Farstrider Lodge around 77.73, do "A Hunter's Boast": kill 6 Mountain Buzzards.
02) Go back to The Farstrider Lodge, make a left and turn in "A Hunter's Boast" 83.65, accept "A Hunter's Challenge".  NOTE: This quest is on a timer, so work somewhat quickly before it expires.
03) Go NW (starting at starting at 76.46) and do "A Hunter's Challenge": kill 5 Elder Mountain Boars.
04) Go back to The Farstrider Lodge, make a left and turn in "A Hunter's Challenge" (83.65, #2 on map).
05) In the middle of the lodge, accept "Vyrin's Revenge" 82.64.
06) Go NW next to the lake and accept "Bingles' Missing Supplies" 64.48.
07) Along the eastern shore of The Loch lake and on the two smaller islands in the lake do "Crocolisk Hunting": kill/loot Loch Crocolisks.
08) Go up north to the bigger island, starting at the bottom eastern part and do "Bingles' Missing Supplies": loot the 4 quest objects on the ground.  First one, loot Bingles's Blastencapper next to the hut 54.27.  See next steps for the other 3 to loot:
09) Go west and loot Bingles's Toolbucket (wrench) next to the shore 49.30.
10) Go NE a bit and loot Bingles's Toolbucket (hammer) next to the light colored wagon 52.24.
11) Go NW a bit and loot Bingles's Toolbucket (screwdriver) at the trogg camp 48.20.
#12

 

01) Go NW to the entrance of Stonewrought Dam, go inside and you can get repaired/resupplied 41.13.  Be careful not to sell your Crocolisk Meat to a vendor!
02) Go SE in the Dam and then under the tent, accept "A Dark Threat Looms" 46.14.
03) Go east to exit the Dam, then on the outside entrance of the Dam, you will see two Dark Iron Sappers.  They are guarding a Suspicious Barrel 56.13, click it to turn in "A Dark Threat Looms", accept the next part.
04) Go west back onto the Dam and under the tent, turn in "A Dark Threat Looms" (46.14, #2 on map), SKIP the next part.
05) Go west to Silver Stream Mine entrance 35.19 and do (if you haven't yet) "Filthy Paws": loot the Miner's League Crates inside the mine.  NIGHT ELVES:  You will not have this, so skip this step!
06) Turn in (if you need to) "Filthy Paws" up in the tower (west at 25.18).  And for Night Elves: You won't have "Filthy Paws" quest, but do turn in "Mountaineer Stormpike's Task", accept "Stormpike's Order".
07) NOTE: You can get repaired/resupplied down below in the tower from Gothor Brumn 24.18.
08) Follow the main path south to Thelsamar and go down in the eastern most hut and turn in "Excavation Progress Report" 37.47, accept "Report to Ironforge".
09) Go SE around 43.65 and try to do "Vyrin's Revenge": kill/loot Ol' Sooty /target (lvl 20 at 3 different spawns, see map).

10) Go NE next to the lake and turn in "Bingles' Missing Supplies" 64.48.
11) Go east to The Farstrider Lodge, make a right in the lodge, then turn in "Crocolisk Hunting" 82.62.
12) At the other side of the lodge, turn in "Vyrin's Revenge" 83.65, accept the next part.

13) Turn in "Vyrin's Revenge" in the middle of the lodge 82.64.

15) First make sure you are level 20 (grind on any mobs until you are), then Go back to Thelsamar.
#13

 

01) Go to the Gryphon Master 34.51 south in Thelsamar and take a flight to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
02) You can upgrade First Aid up in the building from Nissa Firestone 55.58.
04) Go NE in Ironforge and turn in "Report to Ironforge" 75.12, SKIP "Powder to Ironband".
05) Find your class trainer and get new spells/abilities (if you need to).  Also, If you can afford it, you can visit the Weapon Masters at Timberline Arms 62.89 to learn new weapon possibilities.
06) Go east to Tinker Town and accept "Speak with Shoni" 69.51.
07) Go east to enter The Deeprun Tram 75.51.  Take the Tram ride and once you get to the other side exit north through the tunnel.
08) Once at Stormwind, just SW, turn in "Speak with Shoni" 63.34, SKIP "Underground Assault" (Dungeon).
09) Go just SE a bit and turn in "Stormpike's Order" 65.37.
10) You can stop at the Weapon Master (at The Trade District, 64.69) and learn new weapon possibilities if you can afford to.
11) N/A
11a) Go NW to The Park and accept "The Corruption Abroad" 21.5636.68.

Level 20 Mounts, 11b)  At level 20 mounts become available.  If you happen to be level 20 and if you have the gold then get your mount ASAP.  It differs depending on your class & race and it costs roughly 5 gold.  Check out my Mount Guide on where to find your mount trainer & vendor.

12) N/A
13) NIGHT ELF, GNOME & DWARF ONLY: SE in The Trade District, get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master (71.73, #12 on map).

20-21 Darkshore (log)
01) Hearth to Auberdine.

03) East in Auberdine, under the gazebo, turn in "The Corruption Abroad" 38.43 , SKIP "Researching the Corruption" (Dungeon).
Cave Mushrooms, 03b) IF YOU STILL NEED TO DO "Cave Mushrooms": Go NE, then up and through the waterfall in front of the cave (path entrance at 55.32 ) and loot the mushrooms on the ground in the cave.
Cave Mushrooms, 03c) IF YOU STILL NEED TO DO "Cave Mushrooms": Hearth (or go back) to Auberdine, and just outside of the Inn, next to the mailbox, turn in "Cave Mushrooms" 37.44, accept "Onu".
04) Follow the main path south to the Grove of the Ancients and from Onu big tree NPC, 44.76, turn in "Onu", accept "The Master's Glaive".
05) As you work on the following steps also start working on "A Lost Master" by kill/looting Moonstalker Sires and Moonstalker Matriarch (cats) south in Darkshore:
06) Go SW to The Master's Glaive (the big glowing sword 39.86) and get it discovered for "The Master's Glaive".  Then use the Phial of Scrying quest item to spawn a Scrying Bowl next to you, then turn in "The Master's Glaive", accept "The Twilight Camp".
07) At The Master's Glaive there are two books around the altar (table), click on the Twilight Tome 39.86 on the north side of the altar to turn in "The Twilight Camp", accept "Return to Onu".
08) Go south a bit behind the big glowing sword and accept and do the escort "Therylune's Escape" 39.87.  This escort only takes a couple minutes and leads to the main path east.  NOTE:  While kill/looting the Twilight mobs in this area you have a chance to get an item, that starts "The Powers Below", accept it if you were able to find it.
09) Go west to Remtravel's Excavation and turn in "The Absent Minded Prospector" 36.84.  Accept and do the next part (escort).  Simply escort him around the Excavation site.  Try to keep aggro off of Prospector Remtravel /target, as he has low HP and can die easily.
#02

 

01) Go west to the shore at the small Greymist murloc camp 37.77 and do "WANTED: Murkdeep!": clear all the murlocs at the small camp, then kill the two Greymist Warriors that spawn, then kill Murkdeep that spawns after that.  NOTE:  These mobs spawn at the sea and run onto the coast.
03) Go south along the shore a bit more and click on the Beached Sea Turtle's arm 32.84 to accept "Beached Sea Turtle"
04) Go south along the shore a bit more and click on the Beached Sea Turtle's head 31.86 to accept "Beached Sea Turtle"
05) South a bit more, click on the Beached Sea Creature where the neck meets the body 31.87 to accept "Beached Sea Creature".
06) Go NE to the Grove of the Ancients and from Onu (big tree NPC, 44.76), turn in "Return to Onu".  Wait a few seconds, then accept "Mathystra Relics".
07) Next to you, get resupplied 44.76.  NOTE:  At this point you no longer need the Phial of Scrying quest item.  This can be destroyed to save bag space.
08) At the Grove of the Ancients, accept and do the escort "The Sleeper Has Awakened" 44.76.  First thing to do is loot Kerlonian's Chest next to you to get the Horn of Awakening.  Kerlonian Evershade /target falls asleep periodically and you will need to use this horn to wake him up, so keep an eye out for him.  Lead him to the next step:
09) Go south a along the main path and right before entering Ashenvale make a left on the path into some mountains and from Sentinel Aynasha 46.90 /target, accept and do the escort "One Shot. One Kill." (no exclamation mark): simply wait right there and kill several waves of furblogs.
10) Keep escorting Kerlonian Evershade south into Ashenvale... 27.37
#03

 
21-22 Ashenvale
01) Lead Kerlonian Evershade /target south into Ashenvale to Maestra's Post.  Keep aggro off of Kerlonian Evershade as he can die easily.  As you work your way south, there will be several tough spawns along the main path that can be avoided by staying away from the main path by at least 30 yards.  I recommend traveling just east of the main path starting at 27.25, then work your way south through the wilderness to avoid the tough spawns.  Right at the entrance to the hut, turn in "One Shot. One Kill." 27.37.  Then go in the hut and turn in (if you succeeded with it) "The Sleeper Has Awakened" 27.36.  If you failed the escort you will have another chance later in the guide to try again.

06) Go north to Bathran's Haunt around 31.26 and do "Bathran's Hair": loot the Plant Bundles around the area.  They are kind of hard to see.

Operators until the Blueprints drop (its a low drop rate).  Also kill 10 Loggers /target and Deforesters /target (scattered all around Windshear Crag).  NOTE:  There are a lot of operators on the big tank shredder 63.54, and more around other structures in the area.  Keep the next step in mind:

23-24 Darkshore (log)

12) Go back north to the small hill (57.13, #6 on map) and then kill The Threshwackonator 4100.  Then turn in "Gyromast's Revenge".  NOTE:  Do NOT sell the reward received: Elixir of Water Breathing (you will be using this later).

01) Hearth to Auberdine.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  NOTE:  The quest item Gyromast's Key is no longer used for anything and can be destroyed to free bag space.

#10

#11

 
24-24 Ashenvale (log)
01) In Astranaar, on the main path, next to the direction sign, turn in "Pridewings of Stonetalon" 35.49, SKIP "Kayneth Stillwind" (for now).
02) Go in the Inn and turn in "Trek to Ashenvale" 37.50.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) At the SE most hut at Astranaar, turn in "The Ruins of Stardust" 37.52.  Wait a few seconds, then accept "Fallen Sky Lake".
04) Go SW to Fire Scar Shrine and go to the path at 26.63..
05) Go up the path and do "The Tower of Althalaxx": kill/loot Ilkrud Magthrull /target 25.61.
06) Grind your way north through Thistlefur Village and then do "Culling the Threat": kill/loot Dal Bloodclaw /target (grey furblog patrols around 38.35).
07) Keep grinding on furblogs in Thistlefur Village until you are at least a 1/4 way into level 24.
08) Go west to Maestra's Post and turn in "The Tower of Althalaxx" 26.39, accept the next part.
09) Next to you, I recommend skipping the escort "Supplies to Auberdine" (overall) unless you have help.  This difficult escort leads north to the border of Darkshore and Ashenvale and does not yield any crazy XP/time, but it is worth it if it can be done on the first try.  If you do go for it, keep all aggro off of Delgren the Purifier /target as he is very fragile and will die quickly.  There are several waves of tough mobs and you have very little opportunity to eat/drink as he runs quick.  If you did succeed with it, then turn it in at Maestra's Post (26.39 in Ashenvale, #8 on map) when done.
10) N/A
11) Go to Astranaar and in the Inn, turn in "Culling the Threat" (37.50, #2 on map).   Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
11b) Go SE to the furbolg camp 51.60 and do "An Aggressive Defense": kill the required amount of Foulweald furblogs.
12) Go to Silverwind Refuge and accept "Elemental Bracers" 50.67.
13) NOTE: You can purchase Healing Potions and Lesser Mana Potions (in limited supply) from the Alchemy Supplies 51.67 vendor at the Refuge.
14) Just south of you at Mystral Lake do "Elemental Bracers": kill/loot the Befouled Water Elementals for 5 Intact Elemental Bracers, then use the Divining Scroll quest item to complete the quest.
15) Go back to Silverwind Refuge and turn in "Elemental Bracers" (50.67, #12 on map), SKIP "Mage Summoner" accept "Mage Summoner".
Hardcore, 15a) Do "Mage Summoner": go all the way into The Barrens entrance 48.5 (follow main path), then up a long mountain slope starting at 49.15, then kill/loot Sarilus Foulborne 48.19.

Hardcore, 15b) Go back to Ashenvale Entrance 48.5 and turn in "Mage Summoner" 50.67.  IMPORTANT: The quest reward Light of Elune is very special for Hardcore because it can keep you alive easily with one use!  Please read the comments on WowHead for a very useful macro this item can be used with.  This can save your life under most circumstances (but with only a single use).

15c) Go back NW to Astranaar and in the Inn, turn in "An Aggressive Defense" 37.50 (#1 on map).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
16) Hearth to Auberdine.
17) Go out on the long docks at Auberdine and get on the boat to Stormwind City 32.44.

WOTLK, 17a) Go to the Gryphon Master 71.73and fly to Ironforge.   DRAENEI ONLY:  Draenei who did Bloodmyst Isle will only be able to get the Flight Path right now.  You will then need to take The Deeprun Tram ride 69.31 in order to get to Ironforge.  Then once you arrive at Ironforge, make a left and turn in "An Old Colleague" 72.52.  Then get the Flight Path at Ironforge from the Gryphon Master56.48.

WOTLK, 17b) It is now time to travel to Menethil Harbor, Wetlands.  We will take the big shortcut west in Dun Morogh.  First leave Ironforge SW (exit at 15.86).  NOTE: The normal route to get there would be to take the flight path to Thelsamar then go north through Dun Algaz to get to Menethil, but we wont be doing this!

WOTLK, 17c) Go west in Dun Morogh and above Brewnall Village take the hidden ramp up the mountains (starting at 31.33).  This is kind of hard to find, See Video.

WOTLK, 17d) Continue north up the mountains, you will need to run for about 45 seconds then jump off the big mountain to your left about here 44.59to die on purpose.  This should take you to the graveyard at Baradin Bay, res at spirit.

WOTLK, 17e) Go SW to enter Menethil Harbor and from the Gryphon Master (9.60 in Wetlands), get the Flight Path.
#12

 
24-27 Wetlands (log)
01) On the docks at Menethil Harbor, accept "Claws from the Deep" 8.59.
02) Go north near the stables and accept "Young Crocolisk Skins" 8.56.
03) On the southern part of the town, next to the mailbox/Inn, accept "The Third Fleet" and "The Greenwarden" 11.60.
04) Go in the Inn next to you and talk to the Innkeeper (by the bar 11.61) to make Deepwater Tavern (Menethil Harbor) your new home.  Also from the Innkeeper, purchase a Flagon of Dwarven Honeymead for "The Third Fleet".  If you are lvl 25 remember to upgrade to the lvl 25 food/water!
05) Go upstairs in the Inn (at the middle room) and turn in "The Absent Minded Prospector" 11.60, accept the next part.
06) Please note that there is a First Aid Trainer 11.61 upstairs in the Inn that you can use to upgrade First Aid.
07) Just outside of the Inn, turn in "The Third Fleet" (11.60, #3 on map), accept "The Cursed Crew".
08) On the east side of town in front of the small house, accept "Digging Through the Ooze" 12.58.
09) Enter the Fortress, make two lefts and at Neal Allen 11.57, see if you can purchase a Bronze Tube and a Gyrochronatom from him (if you haven't yet elsewhere), these are used for later quests.  If you did, you can store them in the mailbox by sending them to your ALT and return them back to you.

09) Next to you do the last part of "The Tower of Althalaxx": click on the Circle of Imprisonment (on the ground, 82.49).
10) At Satyrnaar finish "Satyr Slaying!" and "A shameful Waste": kill/loot any satyr demons for the 16 horns.  And pick up 5 Satyrnaar Fel Wood.
11) Go south of Splintertree Post (horde town) and do "Forsaken Diseases": loot a Bottle of Disease on either the table or barrel 75.72.  Keep the next step in mind:
12) All around this area 73.74 do "Raene's Cleansing": kill/loot Rotting Slimes until you get the Iron Pommel.  You can also find this in the Rusty Chests that drop from the slimes.

01) Go SW to the small island at Fallen Sky Lake and do "Fallen Sky Lake": kill/loot Shadethicket Oracle /target (swamp elemental) 67.82.

TBC, 2a) East in Forest Song turn in "Satyr Slaying!" (this dryad could be patrolling around the area) 87.43.  Also from the patrolling Ancient of War turn in "A shameful Waste".

32-33 Stranglethorn Vale (log)
00) In STV, follow the path west then north to get to the Rebel Camp  and get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master 38.4.

01) Accept "The Second Rebellion" and "Bad Medicine" 38.3.

03) Next to you, make sure you are resupplied 38.3.

05) Go SE to Kurzen's Compound and do "The Second Rebellion" and "Bad Medicine": kill 15 Kurzen Jungle Fighters /target, kill/loot the mobs for the Jungle Remedy and loot a Kurzen Supplies (crates).  Also IF you have the "Bookie Herod" quest:  Upstairs in the house, click on the Bookie Herod's Records to turn in "Bookie Herod" 44.9, accept "The Hidden Key".

07) Go SW past the river to Nesingwary's Expedition and accept "Welcome to the Jungle" 36.11.
08) Next to you, turn in "Welcome to the Jungle" 36.11.  NOTE: For SoM/WotLK we will skip all Nesingwary hunting quest due to the faster leveling rate.
09)   NOTE:  The "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" quest requires that you save any Green Hills of Stranglethorn pages you find.  These pages can drop from any humanoid mob in STV.  You will need one of each of the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  I recommend mailing them to your alt for storage.  Once your alt collects all the pages, mail them back to your main.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  You don't need to accept the quest to get the page drops, so accept and turn this in if you get all the pages.  You will return to STV numerous times in the future for more chances to find the pages.
10) At the camp get repaired/resupplied from Jaquilina Dramet 36.11.

33-34 Thousand Needles (Shimmering Flats) (log)
01) At Mirage Raceway, turn in "The Brassbolts Brothers" 78.77.  Accept "Hardened Shells".  And next to you accept "Salt Flat Venom".  Next to you from the dwarf, accept "Rocket Car Parts" 78.77.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) East at the Goblin's side of Mirage Raceway accept "Load Lightening" 80.76.
03) SE in Mirage Raceway by the starting line accept "A Bump in the Road" 82.78.
04) Do The Shimmering Flats grind.  Do the following 5 steps below (in any order) all together around The Shimmering Flats:
05) "Rocket Car Parts": pick up Rocket Car Rubble from the ground.  These are scattered all around The Shimmering Flats.
06) "Hardened Shells": kill/loot turtles all over The Shimmering Flats.  ALSO:  Make sure you collect 10 Turtle Meat for a later quest at Southshore in Hillsbrad, and do not sell them to a vender!  You can get the Turtle Meat from any turtle at The Shimmering Flats and there is a large amount up north around 83.55.
07) "Load Lightening": kill/loot vultures (Salt Flats Scavengers & Salt Flats Vultures).  There is a lot around 88.65.
08) "Salt Flat Venom": kill/loot scorpids (Scorpid Reavers & Scorpid Terrors).  Large amount around 72.73.
09) "A Bump in the Road": kill basilisks.  Saltstone Basilisks are NW, Saltstone Crystalhides are everywhere but NW, and the Saltstone Gazers /target are south at Tahonda Ruins around 77.86.
10) Once all done, go back to Mirage Raceway (at the gnomes section) and from the dwarf (at 78.77, #1 on map) turn in "Rocket Car Parts".  Accept "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" and "Hemet Nesingwary".  Then next to you 78.77, turn in "Salt Flat Venom" and "Hardened Shells".  SKIP "Martek the Exiled" (for now).
11) East at the goblin section of the Mirage Raceway turn in "Load Lightening" (80.76, #2 on map), accept "Goblin Sponsorship".
12) SE in Mirage Raceway by the starting line turn in "A Bump in the Road" (82.78, #3 on map).
13) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  Also make sure you are level 34, grind on any mobs until you are.
14) Go south to the path that leads into Tanaris (starting at 74.94)...
15) Follow the path south to Gadgetzan and at the SE corner of the town, go in the bank (hut at 52.29 in Tanaris) and put the following items in there:  stack of 10 Turtle Meat.  Get out the follow item:   (if you have it) 4 Lesser Bloodstone Ore (or could be in mailbox).
17) At the Gryphon Master 51.29 fly to Ratchet, The Barrens.

18) At Ratchet, go in the engineering building and turn in "Goblin Sponsorship" 63.36, accept the next part.

19) Go to the goblin on the docks and turn in "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" 63.38, accept "Parts for Kravel".  Also accept "Passage to Booty Bay".

20) At the end of the docks 64.39, get on the boat to take you to Booty Bay.  NOTE: If you could benefit from getting new spells/abilities then hearth to Stormwind, get new abilities, then fly to Booty Bay, STV.

08) REMINDER:  The "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" quest requires that you save any Green Hills of Stranglethorn pages you find.  These pages can drop from any humanoid mob in STV.  You will need one of each of the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  I recommend mailing them to your alt for storage.  Once your alt collects all the pages, mail them back to your main.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  You don't need to accept the quest to get the page drops, so accept and turn this in at Nesingwary's Expedition 36.11 if you get all the pages.  You will return to STV numerous times in the future for more chances to find the pages.
09) NOTE:  You can destroy the Library Scrip as it's not used for gaining XP (or bank it if you are not in a hurry).
