03) Grind east to Beezil's Wreck 54.57 and turn in "The Zeppelin Crash".  Accept "Corrosion Prevention" and "Secure the Cargo!".
04) Around the immediate area do "Corrosion Prevention" along with "Secure the Cargo!": stand near a Power Core Fragment to obtain a buff, then use the Ooze Buster quest item on oozes.  NOTE:  You don't actually need to attack the oozes.  And loot 8 Zeppelin Cargo (boxes laying around).  Also do the next step in the same area:
05) Go SE to Beezil's Wreck and do "Razzeric's Tweaking": loot the Gizmorium Shipping Crate (next to the crashed zeppelin, 54.56).
06) Back at Beezil's Wreck 54.57 (#3 on map) turn in "Corrosion Prevention" along with "Secure the Cargo!", accept "Delivery for Drazzit".
07) Go to the small island east and at the small dock turn in "Is it Real?" 59.60 , accept "Nat's Bargain".
08) Do "Nat's Bargain": first use the Pagle's Fish Paste, Extra Strength quest item at the quest giver then swim SW at the boat under the water around 56.63  and then kill the Lurking Shark /target that comes.
09) Back NE at the small island on the dock turn in "Nat's Bargain" 59.60 (#7 on map), accept "Oh, It's Real".
10) Go NW to Direhorn Post and do "Raze Direhorn Post!": use Tabetha's Torch quest item on the following 3 tents:
11) Burn the East Tent 48.48.
12) Burn the Northeast Tent 47.47.  Also at this tent kill/loot Apothecary Cylla , she drops an item which starts "The Apothecary's Letter", accept it.
13) Burn the North Tent 47.46.
14) Around Direhorn Post finish "Direhorn Raiders": kill 12 Grimtotems (taurens of any type).
15) Around The Quagmire around 45.49 do the next 2 quests together:
16) "The Reagent Thief": kill/loot Noxious Shredders (green serpents) or Darkfang Creepers (orange spiders).
17) "The Grimtotem Weapon": use the Captured Totem quest item at the Mottled Drywallow Crocolisk or a Drywallow Daggermaw, then kill them.  NOTE: The crocs must be pulsed by the totem on death to get credit.
18) Back at Tabetha's Farm on the small dock turn in "The Apothecary's Letter" 45.58 (next to #1 on map).
19) Inside the cabin 46.57 (#1 on map) turn in "Raze Direhorn Post!", accept "Justice for the Hyals".  And next to you turn in "The Grimtotem Weapon" and "The Reagent Thief".
20) Just outside at the side of the cabin 46.57 turn in "Direhorn Raiders".
#44

 
Mudsprocket
01) Go SW to Mudsprocket (neutral goblin town starting at TBC) and in the hut 42.73 turn in "Delivery for Drazzit" and "Mission to Mudsprocket".
02) From the Wanted Poster at the bottom of the steps accept "WANTED: Goreclaw the Ravenous" 42.73.
03) Next to you by the green containers accept "Catch a Dragon by the Tail" 42.73.
04) In the Inn accept "Bloodfen Feathers" 42.74.
05) Go NW to Bloodfen Burrow around 34.65 and do "Bloodfen Feathers" along with "WANTED: Goreclaw the Ravenous": kill/loot the Bloodfen raptors.  And kill Goreclaw the Ravenous (big gray raptor /target).
06) Go back SE to Mudsprocket and in the small hut turn in "WANTED: Goreclaw the Ravenous" 42.73 (#1 on map).
07) In the Inn turn in "Bloodfen Feathers" 42.74 (#4 on map).  Accept "Banner of the Stonemaul" and "The Essence of Enmity".
#45

 

01) Go NW to The Den of Flame entrance at 40.67 and start working on "The Essence of Enmity" use the Brogg's Totem quest item on Fireman dragon corpses.  While doing this continue on:
02) Enter the cave at The Den of Flame (entrance at 38.66).
03) Go to the end of the cave and do "Banner of the Stonemaul": loot the Stonemaul Clan Banner 38.69.
04) Finish "The Essence of Enmity": use the Brogg's Totem quest item on Firemane dragon corpses.  These can also be found around #6 on map.
05) Back at Mudsprocket in the Inn turn in "Banner of the Stonemaul" and "The Essence of Enmity" 42.74 .  Accept "Spirits of Stonemaul Hold".
06) NE at Stonemaul Ruins around 45.66 do "Spirits of Stonemaul Hold": click on the Ogre Remains (skeletons on the ground), then kill the ogre spirits that spawn.
07) Back in the Inn at Mudsprocket turn in "Spirits of Stonemaul Hold" 42.74  (#5 on map), accept "Challenge to the Black Flight".
08) Start working on "Catch a Dragon by the Tail" by looting any Wyrmtail (plants) you see along the way:
09) Go SE to the entrance of Onyxia's Lair and do "Challenge to the Black Flight": use the Stonemaul Banner quest item at the entrance 52.76.  Then kill the dragon that spawns.
10) Work your way back NW to Mudsprocket while finishing "Catch a Dragon by the Tail": loot 8 Wyrmtail (plants).  These are found all around Wyrmbog (#8 on map).
11) Back at Mudsprocket by the green containers turn in "Catch a Dragon by the Tail" 42.73 .
12) In the Inn turn in "Challenge to the Black Flight" 42.74 (#5 on map).
13) At the NE outskirts of Mudsprocket, go to the Flight Master 43.72  and fly to Theramore, Dustwallow Marsh.
#46

 

01) When you land do "Survey Alcaz Island": talk to Cassa Crimsonwing /target 67.51 to send you on a gryphon ride.
02) On the docks at Theramore turn in "Oh, It's Real" 70.52, accept "Take Down Tethyr!".
03) There on the docks do "Take Down Tethyr!": use the 3 Cove Cannons around the dock to fire and kill Tethry in the water.  TIP: Tethry does a splash attack at every 2nd cannon shot you do at him that could knock you back.  For every 2nd shot use the cannon closest to the quest giver at 70.52 , so you wont be knocked back into the water, giving you a faster time to recover.
04) On the docks turn in "Take Down Tethyr!" 70.52 (#2 on map).
05) Go up in the Keep and turn in "Justice for the Hyals" 68.49, accept "Peace at Last".
06) Go to the top of the wizard building and turn in "Survey Alcaz Island" 66.49 .
07) If you still need to:  Go in the Inn and turn in "Stinky's Escape" 66.45, and "Mudrock Soup and Bugs" 66.45, accept "... and Bugs".
08) Go to the graveyard just NW of Theramore and do "Peace at Last": use the Wreath quest item on the Hyal Family Monument (grave 64.43).
09) Back up in the Keep turn in "Peace at Last" 68.49 (#5 on map).  Dustwallow Marsh is finally finished, phew!!!!
10) South in the town, from the Gryphon Master 67.51, fly to Ratchet, The Barrens.
11) At the Ratchet Bank 63.37, get out the following item:  Sample Elven Gem.  And put the following items in the bank:  Seaforium Booster.
12) Go on the dock to get on the boat to go to Booty Bay 64.39.

39-40 Stranglethorn Vale (log)

08) N/A

#51

 

01) Hearth to Booty Bay.
02) Go to the top of the Inn and up at the boat-deck, from Baron Revilgaz, turn in "Water Elementals" (up at 27.77), SKIP "Magical Analysis" (for now).

Geologist around 44.20, or Murkgill murlocs around 34.22  NOTE:  Instead of the solo grinding, you could get a 5 man group to do the Scarlet Monastery dungeon.  If things get to messy in STV, you can also go grind Boulderfist ogres in Arathi Highlands at around 52.76 or around 32.44. (but do the first four steps in the next section first).
#52

 

01) At Booty Bay, on the lower docks towards the boat drop off, turn in "Scaring Shaky" 27.74, accept "Return to MacKinley".

 EPIC MOUNT? 10b) EPIC MOUNT:  At level 40 epic ground mounts become available, and only cost about 12 gold.  For more info go here.

11) Go to the middle of Ironforge to the Gryphon Master 55.48 and fly to Thelsamar, Loch Modan.  First go to the Gryphon Master56.48 and fly to Thelsamar, Lock Modan.    DRAENEI ONLY:  Draenei who did Bloodmyst Isle will have to manually run all the way to Thelsamar to get the Flight Path there by first exiting Ironforge (exit at 15.86).  Then enter and go through South Gate Pass entrance 82.53, then the other one entrance 17.59.  Then head north in Lock Modan to get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master 34.51.
40-41 Badlands
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, SoM, TBC or WotLK)
01) At Loch Modan, go in the Inn (building with all the big barrels around it) and from the first NPC you see make Stoutlager Inn (Thelsamar) your new home 36.48.
02) Go in the small house just east of the Inn and accept "Badlands Reagent Run" 37.49.
03) Go east in Loch Modan to Ironband's Excavation Site and turn in "Ironband Wants You!" 66.66, accept "Find Agmond".
04) Head south into Badlands (47.82 in Loch Modan)..
05) Go south to the small dwarf camp 54.43 and accept "Fiery Blaze Enchantment", "Mirages" and (next to you) "A Dwarf and His Tools".
06) Grind your way SW to Valley of Fangs 42.53 and turn in "Martek the Exiled", accept "Indurium".  Next to you, accept "Barbecued Buzzard Wings".  And if you have 9 Blue Pearls with you, accept and turn in "Pearl Diving".
07) Go SE a bit to Agmond's End and from the Battered Dwarven Skeleton 51.62, turn in "Find Agmond", accept "Murdaloc".
08) Go south a bit around 52.67 and do "Indurium" along with "Murdaloc": kill/loot the Stonevault mobs for the Indurium Flakes, kill 12 Stonevault Bonesnappers and Murdaloc /target (at 50.66 back in the camp next to an outhouse).
09) Go back north to Valley of Fangs and turn in "Indurium" (42.53, #6 on map), wait a few seconds then accept "News for Fizzle".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
#01

 

01) Go NW to The Dustbowl, and at the gnome 26.45, accept "Study of the Elements: Rock".  If you have a Frost Oil item, accept and complete the quest "Coolant Heads Prevail".  Then if you have a Gyrochronatom item, accept and complete the quest "Gyro... What?".
WOLK, 1b) Also, if you completed "Gyro... What?" and you have a Healing Potion and a Lesser Invisibility Potion with you, accept and complete "Liquid Stone" 26.44.

02) Do "Study of the Elements: Rock" kill/loot Lesser Rock Elementals up north on the cliffs around 20.44.
03) Go to The Dustbowl at the gnome (26.45, #1 on map) and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock", accept the next part.
04) Grind your way down south and do "Study of the Elements: Rock": kill/loot Rock Elementals around 37.75.
05) Grind your way north to The Dustbowl at the gnome (26.45, #1 on map) and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock", accept the next part.
06) Do "Barbecued Buzzard Wings": kill/loot Buzzards.  There is a large amount of Giant Buzzards at 17.60.  And remember to not sell the Buzzard Wings to a vendor.
07) Go south and do "Study of the Elements: Rock": kill/loot Greater Rock Elementals around 14.86, and more around 4.79.
08) Go north to The Dustbowl at the gnome (26.45, #1 on map) and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock".  If it's available, accept "This Is Going To Be Hard", and turn it in right next to you and accept the next part.  Turn that in right next to you and accept the next part.  Kill/loot the Fam'retor Guardian that spawns in.  Then turn it in.
10) And also if you have a Patterned Bronze Bracers with you, accept (if it is available) "Stone Is Better than Cloth", then turn it in.
11) Go east (in the middle of Badlands) and turn in "Barbecued Buzzard Wings" 42.53.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
12) Go NE to Hammertoe's Digsite and under a canopy, next to a table, from a Crumpled Map on the ground 53.34, accept "A Sign of Hope".
13) In the immediate area around the Digsite do "A Dwarf and His Tools": kill/loot Shadowforge mobs (dwarves) until the Ryedol's Lucky Pick drops.
#02

 

01) Go NE to Camp Kosh and do "Mirages": loot the Excavation Supply Crate 67.23.  This could be at random spots around the camp.
02) Go SE to Lethlor Ravine (path entrance at 67.30) and do "Fiery Blaze Enchantment" (Elite): kill/loot the dragon whelps until the Black Drake's Heart drops (very low drop rate).  NOTES:  Watch out as you get deeper into the ravine there are high level elite dragons, but the whelps are easy to take down.  There are more whelps down south in the ravine around 75.67.  The elite dragons have a higher % droprate for the item though (I was able to solo the 43 elites as a lvl 40 hunter).  The droprate is so bad (for at least the whelps) that if after you have grinded to lvl 41 you can skip the quest if you have not found the item by then.
03) Go west to the small dwarf camp 54.43 and turn in all quests: (if its done) "Fiery Blaze Enchantment", "Mirages" (accept "Scrounging"), "A Dwarf and His Tools" and "A Sign of Hope", SKIP the next part, (Dungeon).
04) Go south a bit to Garek (blood elf, male) and accept "Tremors of the Earth" 62.54.
05) As you do the following quests, keep an eye out for Boss Tho'grun /target (for the quest "Tremors of the Earth").  This ogre patrols in a large circle counter-clockwise around the SW part of Badlands.  He has 5 bodyguards. (See Video).  NOTE:  You can focus on the next steps until he shows up...
#03

 

01) Go south a bit more to Camp Boff around 62.69 and start working on "Scrounging": kill/loot the ogres for the Scrap Metal.  The scrap can drop from any ogre in Badlands.  When you run out of ogres to kill, work west to the next step:
02) As you work west to Camp Cagg around 10.78 watch out for Boss Tho'grun (#3 on map) along the way, then finish "Scrounging": kill/loot ogres for the Scrap Metal.
03) Go east along Boss Tho'grun /target patrol path and do "Tremors of the Earth": kill/loot Boss Tho'grun.  This ogre patrols in a large circle counter-clockwise around the SW part of Badlands.  He has 5 bodyguards. (See Video).
04) Go east to Garek (night elf) and turn in "Tremors of the Earth" 62.54, SKIP the next part (high lvl Elite).
05) Go NW to the small dwarf camp and turn in "Scrounging" 54.43.
06) Make sure "Badlands Reagent Run" is finished.  It should have gotten completed by doing all the other quests in Badlands.
07) Hearth to Stoutlager Inn (Thelsamar).  Make sure you are resupplied.
08) Go in the small house just east of the Inn and turn in "Badlands Reagent Run" 37.49.
09) Go east in Loch Modan to Ironband's Excavation Site and turn in "Murdaloc" 66.66, SKIP "Agmond's Fate" (Dungeon).
10) Go to the Gryphon Master (SW in Thelsamar 34.51) and fly to Stormwind, Elwynn.
#04

 

01) At Stormwind, go to the bank 63.78 and get out the following items:  any extra cloth for cloth donations.

03) N/A
04) N/A

#05

 
41-42 Swamp of Sorrows
01) At Darkshire, go in the Inn and from the Innkeeper 74.44 make Darkshire your new home.
02) In front of the big house (north of the path), turn in "Vital Supplies" 76.46, SKIP "Supplies for Nethergarde" (for now).
03) Run east through Deadwind Pass, follow the main path and then make a left into Ariden's Camp (52.38 in Deadwind Pass) just to discover it for XP.
04) Continue east into Swamp of Sorrows (SoS)... 14.60
05) While questing through out SoS keep an eye out for swamp elementals and kill/loot them for "Mazen's Behest".  You only need one item, but it is a very low drop rate:
06) As you enter SoS start working on "Dream Dust in the Swamp": kill/loot the dragon whelps around the lake (around 14.60 in SoS) for the dream dust.  Just clear the whelps in this area once.  When you run out of whelps to kill, move on to the next steps (You can revisit this area later):
07) Go east a bit to Watcher Biggs 27.60 and accept "Encroaching Wildlife".
08) Right around the immediate start working on "Encroaching Wildlife": kill Young Sawtooth Crocolisks, Sorrow Spinners (spiders) and Swamp Jaguars (cats).  While doing this, work your way to the next 2 steps:
09) Go north and then find and kill/loot Noboru the Cudgel /target.  See patrol path on map (spawns at 47.39).  He drops an item which starts "Noboru the Cudgel", accept it.
10) Go to 46.36 and see if there is a Scattered Crate there (its next to the river) and loot the item inside to accept "The Lost Supplies".  If its not there, you will have another opportunity later, also keep an eye out for it as you continue on if you have not got it yet.
11) Go NW to The Harborage and turn in "Noboru the Cudgel" 26.31.  Accept "Draenethyst Crystals" (no exclamation mark).  NOTE:  You can get resupplied here from Masat T'andr /target.
TBC, 11a) Next to you accept "Mercy for the Cursed" 26.32.

12) Go NW to Misty Valley around 15.39 and clear the swamp elementals there to see if you can get the item for "Mazen's Behest".  If you can't find it, just move on:
14) Finish "Encroaching Wildlife" (#8 on map), then turn it in back at Watcher Biggs (27.60, #7 on map) and accept "The Lost Caravan".
#06

 

01) Go NE to Fallow Sanctuary and start working on "Draenethyst Crystals": loot the Draenethyst Crystals around the village while working your way to the broken down caravan NE in the sanctuary and loot the Caravan Chest 64.18 for "The Lost Caravan".  Also if you haven't yet, keep an eye out for a Scattered Crate and accept "The Lost Supplies" from the item inside.
02) Next to you at the human /target in the cage 65.18, accept and do the escort "Galen's Escape".  Also finish "Draenethyst Crystals" along with "Mercy for the Cursed": loot the Draenethyst Crystals around the village and also kill 3 Cursed Lost Ones.
03) Above Stonard (horde town), at Galen's Strongbox (next to a tent 48.40), turn in (if you were able to do it) "Galen's Escape".
04) Go west to The Harborage and turn in "Draenethyst Crystals" 26.31.  Next to you turn in "Mercy for the Cursed".  NOTE:  You can get resupplied here from Masat T'andr /target.
05) If you still need to, go NW to Misty Valley around 15.39 and clear the swamp elementals there to see if you can get the item for "Mazen's Behest".  If you can't find it, just move on:
06) Go south to Watcher Biggs 27.60 and turn in "The Lost Caravan", accept "Driftwood".
07) Go south on the main path to go into Blasted Lands (entrance at 33.68 in SoS)..
08) As soon as you enter Blasted Lands, immediately go SE (off the path) to get to Nethergarde Keep.  At the blacksmith 66.17, make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
09) If its finished, turn in "Mazen's Behest" up in the tower 68.19.
10) At the stables, turn in "The Lost Supplies" 67.21.
11) At the south end of town, go up the spiral wood tower and from the Gryphon Master 66.24, fly to Darkshire, Duskwood.
12) At Darkshire, in front of the big house, accept "Supplies for Nethergarde" 76.46.
13) Go east a bit to the Gryphon Master (up at 77.44) and fly to Nethergarde Keep, Blasted Lands.
14) At Nethergarde Keep, at the stables, turn in "Supplies for Nethergarde" (67.21, #10 on map).
#07

 

01) Run back north into SoS (52.5 in Blasted Lands) then start heading east, you can go south of the Horde town Stonard, you will have to run past a few high level guards...
02) Go just north and jump in the water towards Temple of Atal Hakkar 65.56 to get it discovered for "In Search of The Temple".  And if you need to, finish "Dream Dust in the Swamp" by kill/looting the dragons around the Temple of Atal'Hakkar lake (Pool of Tears).
03) Continue east to the shore around 84.85 and do "Driftwood" along with "... and Bugs": work your way north along the shore and kill/loot crabs for the 12 Pristine Crawler Legs and loot the Sundried Driftwood.
04) Hearth to Darkshire.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) If you have a mount, then Mount east through Deadwind Pass then into SoS and at Watcher Biggs (27.60 in SoS) turn in "Driftwood", accept "Deliver the Shipment".   OR:  If you do not have a mount, then go east a bit to the Gryphon Master (up at 77.44) and fly to Nethergarde Keep, Blasted Lands, then head north to SoS and at Watcher Biggs (27.60 in SoS) turn in "Driftwood", accept "Deliver the Shipment".
06) If you still need the item for "Mazen's Behest", then go NW to Misty Valley around 15.39 and clear the swamp elementals there to see if you can get it.  If you found the item and still need to turn in "Mazen's Behest", then keep grinding on any mobs until you are 1.5 bars away from lvl 42.  Or if you have already turned in "Mazen's Behest" or have not found the item for it, then keep grinding until you are 1 bar away from lvl 42 before continuing..
07) Go back south into Blasted Lands, then go to Nethergarde Keep and (if you need to), go up in the tower and turn in "Mazen's Behest" 68.19.
08) At the stables, turn in "Deliver the Shipment" 67.21.
09) At the south end of town, go up the spiral wood tower and from the Gryphon Master 66.24, fly to Stormwind, Elwynn.
10) At Stormwind, go to the Bank 63.78 and put the following items in the bank:  12 Pristine Crawler Legs.  And get out of the bank:  (if you still need to and have it) 4 Lesser Bloodstone Ore (used for a quest in STV).
11) Go north to the Dwarven District and turn in "In Search of The Temple" 69.40, accept "To The Hinterlands".
GET NEW SPELLS/ABILITIES, 11b) You should be level 42 now.  Get new spells/abilities. 

12) Hearth (if you can), then at the Gryphon Master 77.44 fly to Booty Bay, STV.  If you cannot hearth, then go to the Gryphon Master 71.73 in Stormwind and fly to Booty Bay, Stranglethorn.
#08

 
42-43 Stranglethorn Vale
01) At Booty Bay, upstairs in the Inn, turn in "Dream Dust in the Swamp" 27.77, wait a few seconds then accept "Rumors for Kravel":
02) Next to you from the dwarf, accept "Up to Snuff".
03) NOTE:  If you still need to, and if you have 4 Lesser Bloodstone Ore with you, accept and turn in "Favor For Krazek", then accept "Return to Corporal Kaleb".
04) Next to the steps, accept "Skullsplitter Tusks".
05) Up on the boat-deck, from Fleet Master Seahorn 27.77, accept "The Bloodsail Buccaneers".
06) Drop down in the Inn and make BB your new home 27.77.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
07) Next to you 27.77, accept "Venture Company Mining" and "Zanzil's Secret".
08) Go next to the bank/mailbox and accept "Akiris by the Bundle" 27.76.
Green Hills, 8a) If you have all the pages for "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn", then get them out now.  This quest requires that you have the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.
09) In the cabin (next to the hanging shark), accept "Voodoo Dues" 28.77.
10) Go up the docks and from Dizzy One-Eye, accept "Keep An Eye Out" 29.76.
11) Just east of BB along the shore, do "The Bloodsail Buccaneers" along with "Up to Snuff" and "Keep An Eye Out": kill/loot the Bloodsail mobs for the Snuff along with Dizzy's Eye.  They can drop from any Bloodsail mob (low drop rates).  Also, kill 10 Bloodsail Swashbucklers.  And loot the Bloodsail Orders on the small boat 28.83 or it could be on the table 27.83 at the camp at the end of the beach (or at other spots at the camps).  And next to you, loot the Bloodsail Charts 27.83 on top of the box next to the eastern most tent (See Image for Possible Locations).  It could also be at other spots at the various camps along the beach.
12) Go west (follow path on map) and do "Akiris by the Bundle": kill/loot the Naga Explorers (starting at 25.64).  Medium drop rate.
13) N/A
#09

 

01) Go east across the path to the Ruins of Jubuwal and do "Zanzil's Secret" along with "Voodoo Dues": kill/loot the Zanzil mobs for Zanzil's Mixture and kill/loot the 3 named mobs.  Maury "Club Foot" Wilkins /target 35.51.  See next steps for the other 2 mobs to kill/loot...
02) Kill/loot Jon-Jon the Crow /target 35.52 by the Bonfire.

Green Hills, 4b) If you have all the pages for "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn", then go to Nesingwary's Expedition, and accept and turn this in 36.11.  NOTE:  You will have another chance later to turn this in if you do not have all the pages now.  Then you can get repaired/resupplied at the camp.

09) Exit the cave 46.8.

#12

 
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you will most likely be well into level 43. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into 43-44 Tanaris section below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are level 43 you can consider doing this section.
43-43 Desolace
01) At Nijel's Point, go up the ramp in the Inn and turn in "Brother Anton" 67.8, accept "Down the Scarlet Path".
02) Drop down and accept "Reagents For Reclaimers Inc" 66.10.
03) Go south to the Kodo Graveyard and accept "Ghost-o-Plasm Round Up" 48.62.
04) Go SW to Uthek the Wise (under the tent 36.79) and turn in "Ongeku", accept "Khan Jehn".
05) As you do the following quests you can start working on "Reagents For Reclaimers Inc" by kill/looting demons (fel hunters, succubuses and Doomwarder Captains) around Mannoroc Coven around 50.77.  Also if you run into a patrolling group of 2 kodos and 2 goblins, from them you can accept and do an easy escort "Gizelton Caravan" (no exclamation mark), which leads you through Mannoroc Coven:
06) Go east to the Magram Village and do "Khan Jehn": kill/loot Khan Jehn (in the tent 66.80).
07) Go SW to the Valley of Bones 64.91 and do "Ghost-o-Plasm Round Up": at the big skeleton bones sticking out of the ground, first clear out the skeletons, then use the Crate of Ghost Magnets quest item there, then kill/loot the Magrami Spectre that spawn in for the plasms.
08) In the immediate area around the Valley of Bones, finish "Down the Scarlet Path": kill 30 Undead Ravagers.
09) Go to the Kodo Graveyard and turn in "Ghost-o-Plasm Round Up" (48.62, #3 on map).
10) Go SW to Uthek the Wise (under the tent 36.79, #4 on map) and turn in "Khan Jehn", SKIP "Khan Hratha".
11) Just east off the main path check to see if Super-Seller 680 /target 40.79 is there so you can get resupplied from it.  Most of the time he is not there.
12) Go east to Mannoroc Coven around 50.77 and finish "Reagents For Reclaimers Inc": kill/loot demons (fel hunters, succubuses and Doomwarder Captains).  These are found around #5 on map.  And again, if you run into a patrolling group of 2 kodos and 2 goblins, from them you can accept and do an easy escort "Gizelton Caravan" (no exclamation mark), which leads you through Mannoroc Coven.  If you cannot find them in time it can be skipped.  Also the Doomwarder Captains /target are only found around (52.84, #5 on map), I recommend going there first.
13) If you did it, turn in "Gizelton Caravan" NE at Scrabblescrew's Camp 61.62.
14) Go back north to Nijel's Point and turn in "Reagents For Reclaimers Inc" (66.10, #1 on map), accept the next part.
15) Go up the ramp in the Inn and turn in "Down the Scarlet Path" 67.8, accept the next part.
16) Hearth to Theramore Isle.
17) Go to the Gryphon Master 67.51 and fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
#13

 
43-44 Tanaris
01) Enter Gadgetzan and at the western side on top of the hill, accept "Gadgetzan Water Survey" 50.27.
02) Next to the crane, turn in (if you have it) "Tran'Rek" 52.27, SKIP "Scarab Shells" (Dungeon).
03) From the Wanted Poster next to the cage entrance 52.27, accept "WANTED: Caliph Scorpidsting" and "WANTED: Andre Firebeard".
04) Just to the east of you, next to the teleporter device, accept "Handle With Care" 52.27.
05) Go in the Inn and make Gadgetzan your new home 53.28.  Get resupplied.
06) Next to the Inn at the windmill 52.28, accept "Water Pouch Bounty" and "Wastewander Justice".
07) Go to the Bank SE at Gadgetzan (hut at 52.29) and get the following items out:  Seaforium Booster.  Put the following items in the bank:  A Carefully-packed Crate.
08) Go north to the Shimmering Flats at the Mirage Raceway and from the dwarf turn in "Rumors for Kravel" (78.77 in Thousand Needles).  Wait a few seconds, then accept "Back to Booty Bay".
09) Next to you from the gnome, turn in "News for Fizzle" 78.77.
10) Go east to the goblin's side of the Mirage Raceway and turn in "Razzeric's Tweaking" 80.76, accept "Safety First".  Next to you, accept "Keeping Pace".
11) At the goblin Zamek 80.77 /target next to the Goblin Racer, click on Complete Quest to setup a distraction, then go in the gnome's hut and click on Rizzle's Unguarded Plans 77.77 to turn in "Keeping Pace", accept "Rizzle's Schematics".
12) Go to the goblin Pozzik and turn in "Rizzle's Schematics" 80.76.  NOTE:  If the Sample of Indurium Ore quest item still remains in your inventory after turning this in, then you can destroy the item (it's no longer needed).
13) Hearth to Gadgetzan (if you can), if not just run back south to get there, then from Shreev at the western side of Gadgetzan, turn in "Safety First" (51.27 in Tanaris), SKIP the next part (for now).
14) Go to the Bank SE at Gadgetzan (hut at 52.29, #7 on map) and put the following item in the bank:  Kravel's Scheme.
#14

 

01) Grind your way east to the Noonshade Ruins around 60.24 and start working on "Wastewander Justice" along with "Water Pouch Bounty": kill/loot the Wastewander mobs.  NOTES:  Do not sell the Wastewander Water Pouches to a vendor!  Just clear the area once and continue on...
02) Go east to Steamwheedle Port and from the female goblin under the red tent, accept "Pirate Hats Ahoy!" 67.22.
03) From the male troll under the green tent, accept "Screecher Spirits" 67.22. 
04) At the other side of the Port, in the hut 67.24, turn in "Stoley's Debt", accept "Stoley's Shipment".  And next to you, accept "Southsea Shakedown".
05) Go SW to the Waterspring Field around 64.30 and finish "Wastewander Justice" along with "Water Pouch Bounty": kill/loot the needed Wastewander mobs.  Also, keep an eye out for Caliph Scorpidsting /target , he patrols around the area and has 2 rouges following him.  This is for "WANTED: Caliph Scorpidsting", and you will have another opportunity later to do this if you cannot find him now.
06) Go to Lost Rigger Cove (entrance at 69.42)...
07) Start working on "Southsea Shakedown" along with "Pirate Hats Ahoy!": kill/loot the Southsea mobs (the Southsea Dock Workers are up at 75.47).  Any of these mobs has a chance to drop a Pirate's footlocker, which could contain an item which starts "Ship Schedules", accept it if you are able to find it, if not at least grind for awhile as it might show up (at least until 6 blocks into lvl 44), otherwise it can be skipped.  While doing this, also go do the following 2 steps below in this same area:
08) "Stoley's Shipment": loot the Stolen Cargo up in the building 72.47.
09) "WANTED: Andre Firebeard": kill/loot Andre Firebeard /target in the middle of the cove 73.47.  Watch out this fight usually involves 3 adds.  (See Video)
10) Once all these are done (including "Southsea Shakedown" along with "Pirate Hats Ahoy!"), hearth to Gadgetzan.
#15

 

01) Next to the Inn at the windmill 52.28, turn in "Water Pouch Bounty" and "Wastewander Justice", accept "More Wastewander Justice".  Also turn in (if you happen to have done it already) "WANTED: Caliph Scorpidsting".
02) At the Forge, get repaired/resupplied 51.29.
03) Go west to Sandsorrow Watch and do "Gadgetzan Water Survey": use the Untapped Dowsing Widget quest item in the pool 39.29.  Watch out, as soon as you fill the widget, a few high level bugs will spawn.  Either run away or try to kill them.
04) Go back to the western side of Gadgetzan and on top of the hill, turn in "Gadgetzan Water Survey" 50.27, SKIP the next part (for now).
05) Go east to Steamwheedle Port and from the female goblin under the red tent, turn in "Pirate Hats Ahoy!" 67.22.
06) At the other side of the Port, in the hut 67.24, turn in "Stoley's Shipment", accept "Deliver to MacKinley".  Next to you, turn in "WANTED: Andre Firebeard", "Southsea Shakedown" and (if you have it) "Ship Schedules".
07) Go SW to the Waterspring Field around 60.38 and do "More Wastewander Justice" along with (if you still need to) "WANTED: Caliph Scorpidsting": kill the needed Wastewander mobs and kill/loot Caliph Scorpidsting /target, he patrols around the area and has 2 rouges following him.  He usually spawns at 59.40.
08) Hearth (or run back) to Gadgetzan and at the windmill next to the Inn (52.29, #1 on map), turn in "More Wastewander Justice" and "WANTED: Caliph Scorpidsting".  NOTE:  You can use up any remaining Wastewander Water Pouches for the repeatable quest here.
09) Go into the bank next to you 52.29 and put the following item in the bank:  Stoley's Bottle.  Also, if you have found the item called OOX-17/TN Distress Beacon, put that in your bank as well (and don't accept the quest from it yet).
10) Go to the Gryphon Master (south of Gadgetzan 51.29) and fly to Thalanaar, Feralas.

11) Travel all the way west through Feralas, go north above the Horde town Camp Mojache to avoid it.  Work your way all the way to Feathermoon and get the Flight Path there from the Hippogryph Master 30.43.
#16

 
44-46 Feralas
01) At Feathermoon, next to you at the gazebo between the sea and the Inn, accept "The Mark of Quality" 31.43.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) Go in the Inn and from the Innkeeper 31.43, make Feathermoon your new home.
03) Go in the big building south of the Inn and accept "The Missing Courier" and "The Ruins of Solarsal" 30.46.
04) Go in the small building just east of you and accept "In Search of Knowledge" and "The High Wilderness" 32.46.
05) Go upstairs and turn in "The Missing Courier" 32.45, accept the next part.
06) Go SW to Ruins of Solarsal and click on the Solarsal Gazebo 26.52 to turn in "The Ruins of Solarsal", accept "Return to Feathermoon Stronghold".
07) Go back NE to Feathermoon and in the big building, turn in "Return to Feathermoon Stronghold" (30.46, #3 on map).  Accept "Against the Hatecrest".
08) Next to you, turn in "Against the Hatecrest" 30.46, accept the next part.
09) Go SW to Ruins of Solarsal around 29.52 and do "Against the Hatecrest": kill/loot the nagas (any of them can drop the item you need).
10) Go back NE to the big building at Feathermoon and turn in "Against the Hatecrest" (30.46, #3 on map), accept "Against Lord Shalzaru".
11) Go straight down to the other big island and enter Shalzaru's Lair (cave entrance at 26.67).
12) In Shalzaru's Lair, do "Against Lord Shalzaru": kill/loot Lord Shalzaru /target in the middle of the cave (lvl 47 blue naga 29.70).  Also keep grinding here until you are at least 2,900 XP from lvl 45.  These naga yield good XP.
13) Die on purpose, then res at spirit.

14) Go north a bit to Feathermoon and in the big building across from the Inn turn in "Against Lord Shalzaru" (30.46, #3 on map), accept "Delivering the Relic".
15) Inside the same building, turn in "Delivering the Relic" 30.45.
16) Go in the Inn and from the Cloth and Armor Merchant /target, make sure you get repaired/resupplied (31.43, #2 on map).  Also make sure you upgrade to the lvl 45 tier food/drink.
#17

 

01) Go up the ramp in the Inn and onto the dock to get on the boat to take you east 31.40.  If the boat is not there or within view that it is almost there, then just swim east to the shore at the next step:
02) Along The Forgotten Coast do "Screecher Spirits": kill the Screecher mobs around 46.38, then use the Yeh'kinya's Bramble quest item on the corpse, then talk to the Screecher Spirit that spawns.
03) Go south along the shore (watch out for high lvl enemies) and under the water click on the small Wrecked Row Boat 45.65 to turn in "The Missing Courier", accept "Boat Wreckage".
04) Hearth to Feathermoon.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) Go up to the 2nd floor in the small building at Feathermoon and turn in "Boat Wreckage" 32.45, accept "The Knife Revealed".
06) Go to the top of the tree tower and turn in "The Knife Revealed" 32.44.  Wait a few seconds, then accept "Psychometric Reading".
07) Go up to the 2nd floor in the small building and turn in "Psychometric Reading" (32.45, #5 on map), accept "The Woodpaw Gnolls".
08) Go through the Inn and onto the dock to get on the boat to take you east (31.40, # 1 on map):
09) If the boat is not there or within view that it is almost there, then just swim east to the shore at 47.44, then go up the path.
10) Go SE to the Feral Scar Vale around 56.56 and do "The Mark of Quality": kill/loot the Feral Scar mobs (Yetis).  NOTE:  While questing in Feralas, you may come across an item called OOX-22/FE Distress Beacon, from it accept "Find OOX-22/FE!" and turn this in through the small cave at Feral Scar 53.56, but do NOT do the follow-up escort quest.
11) Go north to the entrance of Dire Maul at 43.70 only to get it Discovered.  Simply discovering Dire Maul in this area will allow you to accept a future quest.  You need to actually discover the zone, you will know when you achieved that when your general chat switches over to Dire Maul.
12) Go east to the Ruins of Isildien (starting at 61.56 and work your way south) and do "The High Wilderness": kill the needed Gordunni ogres.  The Brutes are north, Shamans are south, and the Warlocks are everywhere.
13) Go down south along the cliffs around 57.77 and loot a Hippogryph Egg in one of the nests.  This will be turned in at Tanaris for a later quest.  And remember to not sell this!
#18

 

01) Go NE to the Grimtotem camp and clear the enemies around the Captured Sprite Darter cage around 67.47 before doing the next step's escort quest:
02) At the Grimtotem camp is a hidden path next to a tree (starting at 66.47).  Go up the path and at the end of the ledge, accept and do the escort "Freedom for All Creatures": escort Kindal Moonweaver /target (you need to lead her) to the Captured Sprite Darter cage and click on the Cage Door (67.47, #1 on map) to open it.  Then follow the Sprite Darters and make sure that 6 survive.
03) Go back up the hidden path next to a tree (starting at 66.47, #2 on map).  Go up the path and at the end of the ledge, turn in "Freedom for All Creatures".  Then next to you, accept "Doling Justice".
04) In the immediate area at the Grimtotem camp, do "Doling Justice": kill the required amount of Grimtotem taurens around the camp.  There is another camp up north around 69.40 for more mobs, especially the Naturalists /target.
05) Go back up the hidden path next to a tree (starting at 66.47, #2 on map).  Go up the path and at the end of the ledge, turn in "Doling Justice".  Accept the next part.
06) Go south to the gnoll camps and click on the Large Leather Backpacks hanging on a big tree 73.56 to turn in "The Woodpaw Gnolls", accept "The Writhing Deep".
07) In your inventory is an Undelivered Parcel (backpack), from it accept "Thalanaar Delivery".
08) Go south to The Writhing Deep and go down into the last tunnel (entrance at 73.64), then keep right in the cave and then click on the Zukk'ash Pod to turn in "The Writhing Deep", accept and do "Freed from the Hive": simply wait until Raschal the Courier appears then wait right there until the quest completes itself.
09) Hearth to Feathermoon.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) Just outside of the Inn at the Gazebo by the sea, turn in "The Mark of Quality" 31.43.
11) Across the Inn, inside the small house, turn in "The High Wilderness" 32.46.
12) Go upstairs and turn in "Freed from the Hive" 32.45, accept "A Hero's Welcome".
13) Go in the big building and turn in "A Hero's Welcome" 30.46, accept "Rise of the Silithid".
#19

 

01) GRIND TIME:  At this point, grind all the way to level 46 !  I recommend grinding on the Hatecrest nagas straight down to the other big island, in and outside of the cave around 26.67.  These nagas are considered one of the best grinding spots in the game, especially for Alliance.  Another alternative is to do the Dungeon Uldaman (found in the Badlands).  There are a lot of quests that were opened for Uldaman from the previous questing you have done through out the guide and the Dungeon can get you to level 46 most likely.  Once you are level 46, then continue on..
02) Hearth to Feathermoon and get repaired/resupplied 31.43.
2a) In the small building, accept "The Sunken Temple" 32.46 (requires lvl 46).

03) Go to the Hippogryph Master 30.43 and fly to Thalanaar, Feralas.
04) At Thalanaar, turn in "Thalanaar Delivery" 90.47.
05) Next to you, at the Hippogryph Master 89.46 fly to Rut'theran Village, Teldrassil.
06) At Rut'theran Village, go up the path through the red portal to get to Darnassus 56.89.
07) Go to the bank in Darnassus 40.42 and get the following item out:  A Carefully-packed Crate.  Put the following item(s) in the bank:  Yeh'kinya's Bramble, Hippogryph Egg and (if you found it) OOX-22/FE Distress Beacon (and don't accept the quest from it yet)..
08) At Darnassus, go south in Temple of the Moon, go up and turn in "Rise of the Silithid" 42.86.
09) Also up in the temple, turn in "Doling Justice" 39.82.
Get New Abilities, 10)   NOTE:  You can learn new weapon possibilities from the Weapon Master 58.47.
11) If you have cloth donations to turn in for XP, you can do so from the Alliance Cloth Quartermaster 64.23.  Requires:  60 Wool Cloth --> 60 Silk Cloth --> 60 Mageweave Cloth.
12) Go through the red portal 29.41..
13) At Rut'theran Village, go down in the house and turn in "Handle With Care" 55.92.  Accept "Favored of Elune?".
14) Go upstairs and turn in "In Search of Knowledge" 55.92.
15) Next to you upstairs by the bookshelves is a green book on the ground called Feralas: A History 55.91, click on it to accept "Feralas: A History".
16) Next to you upstairs, turn in "Feralas: A History" 55.92, accept "The Borrower".
17) Go to the Hippogryph Master 58.94 on the bridge and fly to Forest Song, Ashenvale.

18) At Ashenvale, head east on the main path to enter Azshara (95.48 in Ashenvale)..

46-46 Azshara
01) As soon as you enter Azshara, go south to Talrendis Point and get the Flight Path there from the Hippogryph Master 12.78.

04) Go east to Haldarr Encampment around 19.62 and do "A Land Filled with Hatred": kill the needed Haldarr mobs (satyr demons).
05) Go back SW to Talrendis Point and turn in "Spiritual Unrest" and "A Land Filled with Hatred" (11.78, #1 on map).
06) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied from the Bowyer 12.78.
07) Hearth (if you can) to Feathermoon Stronghold.  Then fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris from the Hippogryph Master 30.43 at Feathermoon.  If hearthstone is still on a cooldown, then go to the Hippogryph Master at Talrendis Point 12.78 and fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
08) Go to the Bank SE at Gadgetzan (hut at 52.29) and get the following items out: Bag of Water Elemental Bracers.
09) At Gadgetzan, next to the teleporter, turn in "The Borrower" 52.27, accept "The Super Snapper FX".
10) Go to the Gryphon Master (south of Gadgetzan 51.29) and fly to Theramore, Dustwallow Marsh.
12) Get on the boat (72.56 on the docks) to take you to Menethil Harbor.

#22

 
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you will most likely be well into level 46. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into 46 ST section below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are early into level 46 you can consider doing this section.  If you are not doing this section then abandon "Favored of Elune?"
46-46 The Hinterlands
01) Follow the path into The Hinterlands, then make a sharp left at Aerie Peak, go up the path and turn in "To The Hinterlands" 12.47, accept "Gryphon Master Talonaxe".
02) Go up the ramps and get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master 11.46.
03) Go in the building next to you and turn in "Gryphon Master Talonaxe" 10.44, accept "Rhapsody Shindigger".  Also accept "Witherbark Cages".
04) Drop down, make a right in the Inn and accept "Skulk Rock Clean-up" and "Troll Necklace Bounty" 15.45.
05) Go up and NE in the Inn to the Innkeeper 14.42 patrols a bit and make Wildhammer Keep (Aerie Peak) your new home.
06) On the same level of the Innkeeper, on the SW side of the Inn, get repaired/resupplied from Harggan 13.44.
07) As you go out questing in The Hinterlands also start working on "Favored of Elune?": loot the Wildkin Feathers on the ground scattered all around The Hinterlands:
08) Go to the path (starting at 21.48).
09) Go up to Shindigger's Camp and turn in "Rhapsody Shindigger" 27.49, accept "Rhapsody's Kalimdor Kocktail".
#23

 

01) Go SW to the troll camp of Zun'watha and do part of "Witherbark Cages": open the First and Second Witherbark Cages at the camp 23.59.  Also start working on "Troll Necklace Bounty" by kill/looting the trolls.
02) Go NE to the other troll camp of Hiri'watha and finish "Witherbark Cages": open the Third Witherbark Cage 32.57.  Also finish "Troll Necklace Bounty" by kill/looting the trolls for the necklaces (and remember not to sell these to a vendor)!
03) Go NE to Agol'watha around 47.42 and do "Skulk Rock Clean-up": kill 10 Green Sludge and 10 Jade Ooze.  There is more of these mobs east at Skulk Rock around 58.43.
04) While questing, you may have found an item called OOX-09/HL Distress Beacon, from it accept "Find OOX-09/HL!".  If so, go turn this in in the middle of the lake on top of the hill 49.38, but do NOT do the escort yet!
05) Make sure "Favored of Elune?" is finished: loot the Wildkin Feathers on the ground scattered all around The Hinterlands.
06) Go east to the shore (path entrance downward starting at 72.66).  Once down to the shoreline, do "The Super Snapper FX": use the Super Snapper FX quest item on Gammerita /target.  This lvl 48 turtle patrols up and down the shore line (see map).  You don't actually need to kill it.
07) Hearth to Wildhammer Keep (Aerie Peak).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  Remember to not sell your necklaces!
08) Down near the SE front of the Inn, turn in "Skulk Rock Clean-up" and "Troll Necklace Bounty" 15.45.
09) Go all the way up at Aerie Peak and turn in "Witherbark Cages" 10.44, accept "The Altar of Zul".
10) Up at Aerie Peak go to the Gryphon Master 11.46 and fly to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.

01) When you land stay up and go over to the boat-deck and from Fleet Master Seahorn accept "The Bloodsail Buccaneers" 27.77.
02) Go to the middle level of the Inn and turn in "Ansirem's Key" 27.78, accept ""Pretty Boy" Duncan".
03) Drop down in the Inn and make BB your new home 27.77.

09) Go east to the shore and attempt to do "The Captain's Chest" (Elite): kill/loot Gorlash /target.  This lvl 42 elite giant patrols up and down the shore around 37.70.   You may need to group up with someone, if you cannot do it, it can be skipped.
10) Search the shore east of BB for Half-Buried Bottles until a Carefully Folded Note shows up, from it accept "Message in a Bottle".
11) Go to the island east of BB (use Swim Speed if you can), plus watch out for the lvl 41 stealthed panthers there, then turn in "Message in a Bottle" 39.81, accept the next part.  NOTE:  If the Carefully Folded Note item remains in your inventory, you can destroy it.

13) Back NW on the island turn in "Message in a Bottle" (#11 on map, 39.81).
#26

 

01) Do "The Bloodsail Buccaneers": kill the 3 pirates in each of the 3 ships.  Also find a scroll laying on the ground (can be in any of the 3 ships) called Cortello's Riddle, and from it accept "Cortello's Riddle".  Sometimes this scroll can be in the basement of the ships, so search there too.  The first pirate to kill is Captain Stillwater down the first flight of stairs in the ship 33.88.  See next steps for the other two:
02) Kill Fleet Master Firallon down the first flight of stairs in the middle ship 31.91.  Watch out for Ironpatch in the room before Firallon, take him on alone first.
03) Kill Captain Keelhaul down the first flight of stairs in the last ship 29.88.  Make sure you find the scroll that starts "Cortello's Riddle" before leaving this area.
04) Die on purpose and res at spirit to get to Booty Bay.
05) At the end of the docks, turn in (if you did it) "The Captain's Chest" 27.74.
06) Go towards the Inn and next to the teleporter at the Blade Trader /target 27.78, get repaired/resupplied.
07) Go up in the middle level of the Inn and turn in ""Pretty Boy" Duncan" 27.78
08) Up in the Inn on the boat-deck, from Fleet Master Seahorn turn in "The Bloodsail Buccaneers" 27.77.
09) This is the last time my guide will tell you to turn in "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" if it is complete.  This quest requires that you have the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.
10) N/A
11) If you have all the Green Hills pages, first fly to Rebel Camp, then go to Nesingwary's Expedition, accept and turn in "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" 36.11.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

#28

 
47-48 Searing Gorge
01) Go east in Searing Gorge and from the Wooden Outhouse 66.62, accept "Caught!".
02) At the Grimesilt Dig Site next to you, start working on "Caught!": kill the Dark Iron Geologists /target.  Also make sure you have 15 Silk Cloth (it can drop from the dwarves at the Dig Site).  Also at the Dig Site, is a dwarf /target 64.61 and from him accept and do the escort "Suntara Stones": simply escort him up north along the path, once he falls down, then next to him turn this quest in from the Singed Letter 74.19 on the ground, accept the next part.
03) Go back south to Grimesilt Dig Site around 62.61 and finish "Caught!": kill the Dark Iron Geologists and obtain 15 Silk Cloth (#2 on map).
04) Go back to the Wooden Outhouse (66.62, #1 on map), and turn in "Caught!", accept "Ledger from Tanaris".  Then pick up the Goodsteel Ledger (it drops on the ground next to the outhouse).
05) Go SE up around the mountains and kill/loot Margol the Rager /target (a lvl 48 grey dinosaur, patrols around 73.76), he drops an item, which starts "The Horn of the Beast", accept it.
06) Do part of "Ledger from Tanaris": kill/loot Glassweb Spiders /target for the Leg Shafts.  These are scattered on the eastern side of Searing Gorge around 62.70.
07) Go up NW and from Kalaran Windblade 39.39 accept "Divine Retribution", then do it by simply talking to him.  Then turn it in.  From him, accept "The Flawless Flame".
08) Make sure your Hearthstone is less than 6 minutes until the cooldown resets (grind on any mobs in the immediate area until it is), then continue on..

09) Go up to Thorium Point entrance 32.33...
10) Accept ALL quests at Thorium Point:  From the WANTED/Missing/Lost & Found sign 38.26 accept "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius", "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass" and "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition".  .
WOTLK, 10b) Next to you accept "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation" 38.28.

11) Next to you 39.28 accept "Curse These Fat Fingers", "Fiery Menace!" and "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It"
12) Next to you by the Forge accept "What the Flux?" 39.29.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied by this NPC as well.
13) Go to the Gryphon Master 38.31 at Thorium Point and fly to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
#29

 

01) Go to the Ironforge Bank 35.62 and put the following items into it:  Goodsteel Ledger and 20 Solid Crystal Leg Shafts.
02) Go in the Inn and make Ironforge your new home from the Innkeeper 18.51.
03) Go NE to Hall of Explorers and from Curator Thorius (he patrols around 70.18), turn in "Suntara Stones", accept "Dwarven Justice".
04) Go to the Gryphon Master 56.48 in the middle of Ironforge and if you have "The Horn of the Beast" previously from killing Margol the Rager at Searing Gorge, then fly to Thelsamar, Loch Modan, otherwise just fly back to Thorium Point, Searing Gorge and skip to the Searing Gorge Grind section below.
#30

 
Loch Modan Door to Searing Gorge
01) If you haven't yet, go to the Gryphon Master 56.48 in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Thelsamar, Loch Modan.
02) At Loch Modan, follow the path SW to the locked door and turn in "The Horn of the Beast" 18.84, accept "Proof of Deed".
03) Hearth to Ironforge.
04) At Ironforge, go NE to Hall of Explorers and from Curator Thorius patrols around 70.18, turn in "Proof of Deed".  Wait a few seconds, then accept "At Last!".
GET NEW ABILITIES, 4b) If you need to, get new spells/abilities in Ironforge. 

05) Go to the Gryphon Master (56.48, #1 on map) in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Thelsamar, Loch Modan.
06) At Loch Modan, follow the path SW to the locked door and turn in "At Last!" (18.84, #2 on map).
07) With the Key you have now, open the gated Door in front of you, then go straight through the tunnel to get back to Searing Gorge.
#31

 

Searing Gorge Grind
NOTE:  Do this section all at once (in any order):

01) "The Flawless Flame": kill/loot Magma Elementals, Tempered War Golems, and Heavy War Golems for the Golem Oil.  See next step for the Heart of Flame:
02) Kill/loot the fire elementals for the Heart of Flame, these include:  Inferno Elementals, Blazing Elementals and Magma Elementals.
03) "Fiery Menace!": kill Greater Lava Spiders around the western side of Searing Gorge.
04) "Curse These Fat Fingers": kill Heavy War Golems around the middle of Searing Gorge.
05) "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass": kill/loot Dark Iron Steamsmith for the Smithing Tuyere around 40.50.  See next step for where to find the Lookout's Spyglass:
06) Kill/loot Dark Iron Lookouts for the Lookout's Spyglass, these mobs are scattered around the watchtowers at Searing Gorge, there is a large amount around 53.59, see map for other locations.
07) Once "The Flawless Flame" is done, turn it in at Kalaran Windblade 39.39 and accept "Forging the Shaft".
#32

 

01) "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition": enter one of the caves down in The Cauldron (middle of Searing Gorge), I recommend going into the eastern most cave entrance 49.45 and then kill the needed Dark Iron dwarves in the cave.
02) "What the Flux?" and "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius": loot the Secret Plans: Fiery Flux 40.36 behind Overseer Maltorius (41.36 in the cave). And kill/loot Overseer Maltorius (lvl 50).
WOTLK, 2b) In the hallways across the room around 45.31 do "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation": loot the pillows along side the walls where the beds are at.  Watch out groups of sleepy dwarves patrol this hall and an elite mob can spawn in the middle of that hall, so be careful!  You can skip this if you don't want to wait for the pillows to respawn or deal with the mobs that are in the way.

03) Work your way in the mine and turn in "Dwarven Justice" 41.26, accept "Release Them".
04) "Forging the Shaft": kill/loot Dark Iron dwarves for the Thorium Plated Daggers.  NOTES:  This quest requires "The Flawless Flame" to be finished and turned in first.  Save any Dark Iron Scraps you find, you will need 30 of them for a later quest, but you will also have another good chance to find a lot more later on in the lvl 53-54 Burning Steppes section as well.
05) "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It": enter the cave 47.46 and kill Incendosaurs.
#33

 

01) Go to Kalaran Windblade 39.39 and turn in "Forging the Shaft", accept "The Flame's Casing".
02) Go up to Thorium Point (entrance at 33.33)...
03) Turn in "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius" 38.27.
WOTLK, 3b) Next to you, turn in (if you did it) "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation" 38.28.

04) Next to you, turn in "Curse These Fat Fingers", "Fiery Menace!" and "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It" 39.28.
05) Next to you, turn in "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass" and "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition" 39.28.
06) At the Forge next to you turn in "What the Flux?" 39.29.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied from this NPC.
07) Go west around 25.35 and do "The Flame's Casing": kill/loot the Twilight mobs until the Symbol of Ragnaros drops (low drop rate).  While doing this do the next steps as well:
08) Go up the path at Firewatch Ridge (starting at 24.33)..
09) Go up the path (at the dead fat tree, make a left) and do "Release Them": in the middle of the lava pit, loot the Twilight Artifact 29.26.
10) Next to you by the lava pit, from the caged NPC, accept "Prayer to Elune" 30.26, then simply talk to the NPC to complete it, turn it in and accept the next part.
11) Do "Prayer to Elune": kill/loot the Twilight mobs to obtain the Prayer to Elune.  There is more mobs to kill down below around the cave entrance (around 24.36, #4 on map).
12) Go east to Kalaran Windblade (39.39, #1 on map) and turn in "The Flame's Casing", accept "The Torch of Retribution".
13) Wait a minute, then turn in "The Torch of Retribution", accept the next part.  Then click on the Torch of Retribution in the air next to you to turn in the next part of "The Torch of Retribution".  Accept "Squire Maltrake".  Next to you, turn in "Squire Maltrake", accept "Set Them Ablaze!".
#34

 

01) Do "Set Them Ablaze!": equip the Torch of Retribution quest item and at the top of the 4 watchtowers, click on the Sentry Braziers.  The Northern Tower is at 33.54.  See next steps for the others:
02) The Western Tower is at 36.61.
03) You can stop at the small camp 42.75 to get repaired/resupplied from Graw Cornerstone.
04) The Southern Tower is at 44.61.
05) The Eastern Tower is at 50.55.  NOTE:  You must cross a bridge to get to it.
06) While doing these quests (specifically from killing the Dark Iron dwarves), you should have obtained an item called Grimsite Outhouse Key, from it accept "The Key to Freedom".  Then go SE and turn this in at the Wooden Outhouse 66.62.
07) Go back north to Kalaran Windblade (39.39, #1 on map) and turn in "Set Them Ablaze!".
08) Wait a minute (grind while waiting), then click on the Hoard of the Black Dragonflight 39.39 chest on the ground next to Kalaran Windblade and accept "Trinkets...".  Then click on the chest again to turn this in.  Loot the chest received.  NOTE:  The Black Dragonflight Molt will be used for a later quest.
09) Drop down (at the eastern big crane) to enter the eastern most cave at The Cauldron 49.45.
10) Go into the cave and click on the Altar of Suntara (in the middle of the big room 41.25) to turn in "Release Them".
11) Next to you accept and do "Rise, Obsidion!": next to you in the room, kill/loot Obsidion (lvl 52 golem) and kill/loot Lathoric the Black (lvl 52 dwarf).
12) Die on purpose, then res at spirit.
13) At Thorium Point, make sure you are repaired/resupplied from Master Smith Burninate 39.28 next to the Forge.
14) N/A
15) Go to the Gryphon Master 38.31 at Thorium Point and fly to Nethergarde Keep, Blasted Lands.
#35

 

01) Go north to enter Swamp of Sorrows, then follow the path NW and click on A Soggy Scrolll under the bridge (23.48 in SoS) to turn in "Cortello's Riddle", accept the next part.
02) Go all the way east to the middle of the shore at SoS and do part of "Ledger from Tanaris": kill/loot Jarquia /target 95.52, he drops the Goodsteel's Balanced Flameberge.
03) Hearth to Ironforge.
04) At Ironforge's Inn, from the Innkeeper 18.51, accept "Assisting Arch Druid Staghelm".
05) Go NE to Hall of Explorers and from Curator Thorius (he patrols around 74.20), turn in (if you did it) "Rise, Obsidion!".
06) Also at the Hall of Explorers, accept "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way" 76.23.  NOTE:  Do not loot the item received (yet).
GET NEW ABILITIES, 07) Get your level 48 spells/abilities.

Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into 49 Dustwallow Marsh section below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are early into level 48 you can consider doing this section.
48-49 Feralas
01) At the gazebo between the Inn and the Sea, accept "Improved Quality" 31.43.

03) Next to you, accept "The Sunken Temple".
04) Go through the Inn and onto the dock to get on the boat to take you east 31.40.  If the boat is not there or within view that it is almost there, then just swim east at 45.43 and accept "Zapped Giants" and "Fuel for the Zapping".  NOTE:  "Zapped Giants" and its quest item Zorbin's Ultra-Shrinker is on a 2 hour timer, so work somewhat quickly, but you should have plenty of time.
05) Do "Fuel for the Zapping" (starting at 44.39): kill/loot water elementals, which include Sea Elementals and Sea Sprays (low drop rate).  Watch out for the elite giants (you can use the Zorbin's Ultra-Shrinker quest item on them to make them non-elite).
06) Go back to the goblin next to the dock and turn in "Fuel for the Zapping" (45.43, #4 on map).  NOTE:  The repeatable quest "Refuel for the Zapping" does not yield much XP.
07) Follow the path up the hill 46.38..
08) Go east to Rage Scar Hold entrance 52.32 and do "Improved Quality": kill/loot the yeti for the hides.  Also keep grinding on the yetis until you get the Pristine Yeti Hide, from it accept "Pristine Yeti Hide".
#37

 

01) As you work your way north (starting around 50.33), do part of "Rhapsody's Kalimdor Kocktail": kill/loot the Ironfur Patriarchs (bears) and the Groddoc Thunderers (gorillas) for their livers.  NOTE:  The Roc Gizzards will be done later in Tanaris.  You can work your way to the next step while working on this:
02) Go NW and from Rockbiter (big giant 42.22), accept "The Giant Guardian".

PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you will most likely be well into level 50. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into the section 51 Ungoro Crator below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are early into level 48 you can consider doing this section.
50-51 Blasted Lands
00) Go up to the Gryphon Master 28.78 and fly to Nethergarde Keep, Blasted Lands.
01) At Nethergarde Keep, go up to the top of the tower and accept "Petty Squabbles" 68.19.
02) Next to you up in the tower, turn in (if you have it) "Thadius Grimshade" 67.19, SKIP "Nekrum's Medallion" (Dungeon).
03) Go north to the border of Blasted Lands and SoS, to the Fallen Hero of the Horde (34.66 in SoS) and turn in "Petty Squabbles", accept and do "A Tale of Sorrow" by simply talking to the NPC.  Then turn it in and accept "The Stones That Bind Us".
04) Go back south to enter the Blasted Lands and there is a small quest hub at 51.14 that gives 5 quests.  These 5 quests requires the following total number of items to complete them, and they can drop without needing to accept the quests.  The color codes below show where the mobs are at on the images and stars represent rare mobs that may not be there.  Work on the rest of this section all at once):
Mob - Total Items Needed - After "The Decisive Striker" - And then "Vulture's Vigor"
05) Vulture Gizzards     =14 - 12 - 2
06) Basilisk Brain         =11 - 11 - 11
07) Blasted Boar Lungs =6 - 5 - 5
08) Scorpok Pincers      =6 - 3 - 3
09) Snickerfang Jowls   =5 - 5 - 3
10) 2+ PLAYERS (or could be soloable):  While grinding for these items, try to do "The Stones That Bind Us": drag the different servants close to their Stone of Binding crystal next to each of them, then kill them along with attacking the crystal as well.  This allows you to actually kill the mob and get quest credit.  Each of these different servants are located at different parts of the Blasted Lands.  See map for locations.  If for some reason you cannot solo this quest (or find help), it can be skipped.
11) While grinding make sure you get everything discovered for XP (including The Tainted Scar at 44.57).
12) As you get these items, you can turn the quests in for a 60 min stat reward that will help you grind faster in Blasted Lands or even for the next section Un'Goro Crater (although they don't stack).  Go to the quest hub at 51.14 and turn in all 5 of these quests:  "Snickerfang Jowls" (+25 Strength), "A Boar's Vitality" (+25 Stamina), "The Decisive Striker" (+25 Agility), "The Basilisk's Bite" (+25 Intellect) and "Vulture's Vigor" (+25 Spirit).  NOTE:  The follow up quests do NOT yield XP: "Rage of Ages", "Spirit of the Boar", "Salt of the Scorpok", "Infallible Mind", and "Spiritual Domination".
13) While questing in Blasted Lands, you may have came across an item called Imperfect Draenethyst Fragment, turn these in at 52.36 for the quest "Everything Counts In Large Amounts".  NOTE:  I have NEVER found a Flawless Draenethyst Sphere for the quest "To Serve Kum'isha" ..good luck on that.  Also, the follow up quest "One Draenei's Junk" is repeatable but yields no XP.
14) Go to the border of Blasted Lands and SoS, to the Fallen Hero of the Horde (34.66 in SoS) and turn in (if you did it) "The Stones That Bind Us", SKIP "Kirith".  Accept "Heroes of Old".
15) Next to you on the other side of the path, turn in (if you have it) "Heroes of Old" (33.66 in SoS), accept the next part.  Then click on the Spectral Lockbox on the ground next to you to turn it in.
16) Hearth to Theramore.  NOTE:  If you did not do "The Stones That Bind Us", then abandon the quest.
18) From the Gryphon Master 67.51, fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.

04) NOTE:  The Noggenfogger Elixir item is used for fun and can be stored in the bank 52.29when you want to use it.
