#06

 
(log)
01) At the bottom of the path into Un'Goro Crater, go up the hill next to the waterfall and accept "The Apes of Un'Goro" and "The Fare of Lar'korwi" 72.76.
02) Go north a bit and do "The Fare of Lar'korwi": loot the big Fresh Threshadon Carcass lying on the ground 69.57.
03) Grind north to the entrance of Marshal's Refuge and accept "Chasing A-Me 01" 46.13.  NOTE:  On the way to Marshal's Refuge, grind on Bloodpetal mobs (plants) to start collecting Savage Fronds for a later quest.  Also start collecting crystals (the red, blue, green and yellow) scattered everywhere.
04) Go up into Marshal's Refuge and accept ALL quests there:  From the goblin on top of the hill, accept "Shizzle's Flyer" 44.12.
05) Go up the hill a bit to Muigin and accept "Muigin and Larion" 43.10.
06) Move into Marshal's Refuge 44.8 and accept "Lost!", (from the Wanted Sign Beware of Pterrordax) "Beware of Pterrordax".
07) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.
08) Next to you accept "Roll the Bones" 43.7.
09) Next to you 44.7 accept "Alien Ecology" and "Expedition Salvation".  NOTE:  If you have already found A Mangled Journal, accept and turn in "Williden's Journal".
10) Time to do the Un'Goro Grind!  NOTES For the Grind:
  - Don't worry about looting the Un'Goro Dirt Piles, you will find enough Un'Goro Soil from mob drops.  Also do not sell the Un'Goro Soil to vendors, you will need these for quest turn-ins!
  - Don't worry about looting the Bloodpetal Sprouts, as they are not turned in for any XP.
  - The Dinosaur Bones you need for the quest "Roll the Bones" will drop from many different mobs as you do the other quests.
  - Also while questing, loot 7 crystals of each color (red, blue, green, and yellow) for a later quest.  These crystals are scattered all around Un'Goro Crater.
  - As you see the Bloodpetel mobs (plants spread all around Un'Goro) along the way kill/loot them for the quest "Muigin and Larion".  These plants also drop Savage Fronds, you could collect 30 of these for a later quest at lvl 59.  As you are doing the Un'Goro quests associated with these mobs you should get about half, so grind on these that you see along the way and see what you can get before leaving Un'Goro Crater, and you could always check the AH for more of these, or just skip this entirely.
- Watch out for the Elite Devilsaurs patrolling around this zone.  I highlighted them through out the images so you can be alerted if you are in a danger zone for them.
#07

 

LET THE GRIND BEGIN:
01) Just south of Marshal's Refuge around 46.16 do "Super Sticky": kill/loot the Tar mobs around the tar pits.
02) Go NE to Fungal Rock and start working on "The Apes of Un'Goro": kill/loot the apes (in and outside of the cave).  Also go in the cave entrance 64.16 and then make a right and turn in "Chasing A-Me 01", then if you have a Mithril Casing with you, accept and turn in "Chasing A-Me 01".  Then accept and do the escort "Chasing A-Me 01": escort A-Me 01 to Marshal's Refuge, and then turn it in once you get there.
03) Go up on the mountain ledge (ramp entrance at 55.12) and start working on "Shizzle's Flyer": kill/loot the Pterrordax /target.  There are only 6 mob spawns here, so clear the area once and move on to the next step..
04) Make sure "The Apes of Un'Goro" is finished: kill/loot the apes (in and outside of the cave around 64.16, #2 on map).
05) Go SE and do part of "Expedition Salvation": under the tent, loot the Crate of Foodstuffs 69.37.
06) Kill/loot Bloodpetal mobs (plants) starting below the river around 61.48 and work you way south.  Kill/loot any Diemetradon you see as well for "Shizzle's Flyer".
07) Grind your way SE to Torwa Pathfinder on top of the hill by the waterfall and turn in "The Apes of Un'Goro" 72.76.  Accept "The Mighty U'cha".  And turn in "The Fare of Lar'korwi", accept "The Scent of Lar'korwi".
#08

 

01) Just to the west of you at The Marshlands around 67.73 do "The Scent of Lar'korwi": run across the raptor egg nests (there is 5 total: 67.73 63.77 67.67 61.72 62.66) and then kill/loot the Lar'korwi Mates that spawn in.  Keep the next step in mind:
02) At The Marshlands, at the western most pond, north on it's shore, click on A Wrecked Raft 63.69 to accept "It's a Secret to Everybody".  Then right next to the wrecked raft, under the water, click on A Small Pack 63.69 to turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", accept the next part.  NOTE:  To free up bag space, do NOT loot A Small Pack yet!
03) Go east to Torwa Pathfinder on top of the hill by the waterfall and turn in "The Scent of Lar'korwi" 72.76, accept "The Bait for Lar'korwi".  NOTE:  Do NOT loot Torwa's Pouch yet!
04) Go west to The Slithering Scar and do "Alien Ecology": enter the cave entrance 50.81.  Make a left in the cave and then use your Unused Scraping Vial quest item on the mound in the center of the room 49.85.
05) Go south up at the ledges around 56.90 to continue obtaining the Webbed Pterrordax Scales (you will be able to get more of these later) for "Shizzle's Flyer".  There is another spot of these mobs around 45.90.
06) Grind NW a bit to the old camp site 38.66 and do the last part of "Expedition Salvation": loot the Research Equipment (looks like a stack of brown boxes next to a tent).
07) All around the western side of Un'Goro Crater, finish up "Muigin and Larion" (kill Bloodpetal Trappers) along with finishing up "Beware of Pterrordax", "Shizzle's Flyer" and "Roll the Bones" (kill/loot Frenzied Pterrordax).  The Dinosaur Bones can also drop from Elder Diemetradon and regular Diemetradon mobs (found south-middle).  Keep the next step in mind:
08) At the Golakka Hot Springs (below the hotspring mountain), accept "Finding the Source" 31.50.
#09

 

01) Do "Volcanic Activity" along with "Finding the Source".  First go to the north entrance at Fire Plume Ridge (spiky bridge entrance at 53.43).  Follow the path upwards to the top and use Krakle's Thermometer quest item at the Fire Plume Ridge Hot Spot (the one at 50.46).  Watch out for the lvl 56 Elite mob in the cave at the top of the Ridge.  Also kill/loot the fire elementals around Fire Plume Ridge for the 9 Un'Goro Ash and to start collecting Core of Elements for a future quest (I recommend at least getting about 10 for now).
02) Make sure all the quests above are done, including obtaining 7 crystals of each color (red, blue, green, and yellow), then go to the small cave at the eastern side of Fire Plume Ridge and turn in "Lost!" 52.50.
03) Accept and do the escort "A Little Help From My Friends": escort Ringo /target back to Marshal's Refuge 44.9.  You will need to use the quest item Spraggle's Canteen on Ringo to wake him up if he faints.
04) turn in "A Little Help From My Friends" and "Beware of Pterrordax" 44.9.
05) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.
06) Next to you turn in "Roll the Bones"43.7.
07) Next to you 44.7 turn in "Alien Ecology", "Expedition Salvation" and (if you haven't yet) accept and turn in "Williden's Journal" from the item A Mangled Journal (this item/quest is obtained while killing any mob in Un'Goro Crater that should be in your inventory by now).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
08) Open and loot A Small Pack in your inventory and then go to Linken (in the middle of Marshal's Refuge behind some trees 45.8) and turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", accept the next part.  NOTE:  The Faded Photograph is not needed and will remain in your inventory forever unless you destroy or bank it.
09) At the western side of Marshal's Refuge up on the hill, turn in "Muigin and Larion" 43.10, SKIP "A Visit to Gregan".
10) Enter the cave at Marshal's Refuge entrance 43.6 and at the very end of the cave accept and turn in "Crystals of Power" 42.3.  Then accept the 3 Pylon quests:  "The Northern Pylon", "The Eastern Pylon" and "The Western Pylon".  You can destroy any remaining crystals in your inventory (to free bag space).
11) Leave the cave, and then south in Marshal's Refuge on top of the hill, turn in "Shizzle's Flyer" 44.12.
#10

 

01) Go east up on the ledges and do "The Northern Pylon": click on the Northern Crystal Pylon 57.13.
02) Go east to Fungal Rock and do "The Mighty U'cha": enter the cave entrance 65.16, then keep left in the cave until you get to U'cha (lvl 55 red colored ape) and kill/loot him.
03) On the eastern side of Un'Goro Crater, go up on the ledges and do "The Eastern Pylon": click on the Eastern Crystal Pylon 77.50.
04) Just to the east of you on the mountain ledges there, do "The Bait for Lar'korwi": at the old dinosaur bones, there is a gray flat rock on the ground at 80.50.  At this spot open your Torwa's Pouch quest item and first use the Preserved Threshadon Meat quest item, then use the Preserved Pheromone Mixture at this spot, then kill/loot Lar'korwi that spawns in (See Video).
05) Go south to Torwa Pathfinder 72.76 on top of the hill by the waterfall and turn in "The Bait for Lar'korwi" and "The Mighty U'cha".
06) Go west up on the mountain ledges and do "The Western Pylon": click on the Western Crystal Pylon 24.59.
07) Go NE to Golakka Hot Springs (below the hotspring mountain) and turn in "Finding the Source" 31.50, accept "The New Springs".
08) Grind your way back north to Marshal's Refuge entrance 46.13.  Also at this time see if you can obtain 30 Savage Fronds from the Bloodpetel mobs (plants all around Un'Goro), 30 of these can be used for a later quest at lvl 59.  If you are still far behind on that then don't worry about it, you may find more in the AH (or just skip it).  Also remember to not sell your Un'Goro Soil!
09) Enter the cave at Marshal's Refuge entrance 43.6 and work your way to the end of the cave and turn in at 42.3 "The Northern Pylon", "The Eastern Pylon" and "The Western Pylon".  Then accept and turn in "Making Sense of It".  NOTE:  The Crystal Pylon User's Manual can be destroyed or saved in your bank for later reference and you can always get a new one from this NPC in the cave.
10) Leave the cave, make sure you are repaired/resupplied 44.7.
11) Go up on the eastern side of Marshal's Refuge, to the Flight Master 45.6 and fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
#11

 

01) Go to the Bank SE at Gadgetzan (hut at 52.29) and put the following items in the bank:  9 Un'Goro Ash, Savage Fronds, Core of Elements.  Get the following items out of the bank:  Snapshot of Gammerita and Super Snapper FX.
02) North in Gadgetzan (next to the crane) turn in "Super Sticky" 52.27.
03) If you are not level 52 then grind on any mob in Tanaris until you are.
04) Hearth to Theramore.
05) N/A
08) At Theramore go to the Gryphon Master 67.51 and fly to Rut'theran Village, Teldrassil.
09) At Rut'theran Village, go in the house 55.92 and turn in "Favored of Elune?", accept "Moontouched Wildkin".
09b) Go upstairs in the house and turn in "The Super Snapper FX" 55.92, SKIP "Return to Troyas".

10) Go up the path through the red portal 56.89 to get to Darnassus.
#12

 

01) While in Darnassus, if you run across Herald Moonstalker /target (she patrols all around the city), from her, accept "A Call to Arms: The Plaguelands!".  If you cannot find her, then don't worry you will have another chance later:
02) North at the Cenarion Enclave, go to the top of the tree tower and turn in "Assisting Arch Druid Staghelm" 35.9, accept "Un'Goro Soil".
03) Drop down behind the tree tower and turn in "Un'Goro Soil" 31.8.
04) Go back up to the top of the tree tower and accept "Morrowgrain Research" (35.9, #2 on map).  NOTE:  It's a long walk around unless you go through the shortcut right behind you.
05) Go down to the middle level in the tree tower and turn in "Morrowgrain Research", SKIP the next part.
06) You can sell any remaining Packet of Tharlendris Seeds and Un'Goro Soil to this vendor.
07) N/A
09) Go to the Bank 40.42 and get the following items out:  Package of Empty Ooze Containers, Insect Analysis Report and (if you have it) Prayer to Elune.
10) Go south to Temple of the Moon and turn in "Rise of the Silithid" up at 42.86 , SKIP "March of the Silithid" (for now).
11) If you have "Prayer to Elune", then turn this in as well (up in the Temple of the Moon 38.81).
12) Exit Darnassus by going through the red portal 29.40.
13) On the docks, go to the Hippogryph Master 58.94 and fly to Astranaar, Ashenvale.
#13

 
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you will most likely be well into level 52. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into the section 53-54 Felwood below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  Also its not wise to even do this zone if you wanted to since we skipped getting the quests for this zone earlier.
52-53 Azshara
01) At Azshara, go east to Ruins of Eldarath and start working on "Arcane Runes": loot 3 of 4 Runes at the ruins.  First one is Rune of Beth'Amara 37.53.  See next steps for 2 more (for now):
02) Go SE a bit and loot Rune of Markri 39.55.
03) Go north a bit and loot Rune of Jin'yael 40.50.
04) Exit Ruins of Eldarath by heading east through The Shattered Strand, and then exit north 46.38..
05) Go NE to the hill entrance 53.20...
06) Go up to the top of the hill and accept "Kim'jael Indeed!" 53.22.
07) Go south to Thalassian Base Camp around 58.28 and do "Kim'jael Indeed!": loot Kim'jael's Equipment boxes on the ground around the camp.
08) Go NE to the hill entrance (53.20, #5 on map)...
09) Go up to the top of the hill and turn in "Kim'jael Indeed!" 53.22.  Accept "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment".
10) Drop down to The Shattered Strand 51.36 (be careful, its a long drop), then do "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment": kill/loot Spitelash mobs (naga) until Some Rune drops (low drop rate).
11) Exit The Shattered Strand by heading north (at 46.38, #4 on map)..
12) Head east to the hill entrance (53.20, #5 on map)...
13) Go up to the top of the hill and turn in "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment" 53.22.
#14

 

01) GRIND TIME:  At this point you should be about level 53.  Grind all the way to level 53 and a half (53.5) on Blood Elves around Thalassian Base Camp around 58.28.  NOTE:  Blood Elves are squishy mobs (mobs that have 30% fewer health/armor, than the average mob that level), so they die relatively fast and yield good XP/hour. Another decent grinding spot is the Satyrs at the Legash Encampment around 61.25.  Also make sure "Morrowgrain Research" is finished too.  OPTIONAL:  Instead of the grinding, you could go do BRD Dungeon instead.
02) Go SW to Ruins of Eldarath and do the last part of "Arcane Runes": loot Rune of Sael'hai 42.64.
03) Go all the way SE in Azshara at the little island 78.91 and use your Standard Issue Flare Gun quest item at the helicopter pad, then from the NPC that spawns, turn in "Arcane Runes", accept "Return to Tymor".
04) Hearth to Craftsmen's Terrace (Darnassus).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  NOTE:  If the Standard Issue Flare Gun is still in your inventory you can destroy it. 
05) Go west to the middle level of the tree tower 35.8 and turn in "Morrowgrain Research".  NOTES:  The quest is repeatable but you only get 550 XP on the next turn in, then no more afterwards.  After turning this in, you can sell any remaining Packet of Tharlendris Seeds, Morrowgrains and Un'Goro Soil to a vendor.  And you can destroy the Evergreen Pouch as well. 
06) Go to the bank at Darnassus 40.42 and
07) Exit Darnassus by going through the red portal 29.41.
08) On the docks, go to the Hippogryph Master 58.94 and fly to Astranaar, Ashenvale.
#15

 
53-54 Felwood
02) Head east on the main path, then north to Felwood (entrance at 56.29 in Ashenvale)..
03) As you enter Felwood, and off to your right of the main path, accept "Cleansing Felwood" (54.87 in Felwood).
04) Go north a bit to Emerald Sanctuary and accept "Forces of Jaedenar" 51.82.  And next to you in the tent 51.82, accept "Verifying the Corruption" and (if you are lvl 53) "To Winterspring!".  And in the house, accept "The Corruption of the Jadefire" 51.82. Also, get the FP from the Flight Master 52.82.
05) Just south a bit next to the main path, from Grazle /target (furblog 51.85), accept "Timbermaw Ally".
06) Go south to Deadwood Village around 49.91 and do "Timbermaw Ally": kill the required amount of Deadwood mobs.
07) Go north a bit along the main path to Grazle (51.85, #5 on map) and turn in "Timbermaw Ally".  Accept "Speak to Nafien".
08) Go up north to the two small ponds on your left around 41.71 and do part of "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way": first loot your Package of Empty Ooze Containers quest item, then use the Empty Cursed Ooze Jar quest item on Cursed Ooze corpses.
09) Just west of you do "The Corruption of the Jadefire": kill the required amount of Jadefire Satyrs and Xavathras /target 32.67 is at the west end of the camp by the green well.
10) Go up north and start working on the last part of "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way" around 41.59: use the Empty Tainted Ooze Jar quest item on Tainted Ooze corpses.  When you run out of mobs, just continue on:
11) Go west into Jaedenar around 39.59 and do "Forces of Jaedenar": kill the required amount of mobs in the area.
#16

 

01) Back south a bit off the main path around 41.59 finish "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way": use the Empty Tainted Ooze Jar quest item on Tainted Ooze corpses.  NOTE:  There is even more of these slimes up north a bit around around 40.56.
02) Go south to Emerald Sanctuary and turn in "Forces of Jaedenar" 51.82, accept "Collection of the Corrupt Water".
03) Go in the house and turn in "The Corruption of the Jadefire" 51.82.  Accept "Further Corruption".
04) Go north in Jaedenar and do "Collection of the Corrupt Water": use your Empty Canteen quest item in the green moonwell 35.60.
05) Go back south to Emerald Sanctuary and turn in "Collection of the Corrupt Water" (51.82, #2 on map), accept "Seeking Spiritual Aid".  If you are level 53, accept (if you haven't yet) "To Winterspring!" 51.82.  If not don't worry (it's not needed).
06) Go up north and on the east side of the path to Shatter Scar Vale around 42.43 do "Verifying the Corruption": walk across one of the craters in the Vale to get it explored, and kill the required amount of fire elementals.
07) Go north to enter Jadefire Run 42.20..
08) Grind south in Jadefire Run and kill/loot Xavaric 39.22 /target, he drops an item, which starts "Flute of Xavaric", accept it.
09) In the immediate area around Jadefire Run, do "Further Corruption" along with "Flute of Xavaric": kill the required amount of Jadefire Satyrs (demons) and loot them for the 5 Jadefire Felbinds.
#17

 


01) Follow the main path up NE to Irontree Woods and do "Cleansing Felwood": kill/loot the Warpwood elementals.  There is more mobs in the Irontree Cavern entrance 56.17.
02) Go east to Talonbranch Glade and get the Flight Path from the Hippogryph Master 62.24.  NOTE:  You can get repaired/resupplied at the NPCs by the gazebo 62.26 .
03) Follow the main path north (right before the Timbermaw Hold entrance) and from Nafien 65.8, turn in "Speak to Nafien".  And accept "Deadwood of the North".
04) Drop down west to Felpaw Village around 63.9 and do "Deadwood of the North": kill the required amount of Deadwood furbolgs.
05) Go back up to Nafien by the Timbermaw Hold entrance (65.8 #3 on map) and turn in "Deadwood of the North".  Accept "Speak to Salfa".  Also turn in any feathers you may have (repeatable quest to increase rep).  NOTE:  Becoming friendlier with Timbermaw Hold will allow you to enter their cave without them attacking you and allowing you to get additional quests and items from them.
06) Enter Timbermaw Hold next to you entrance 65.7 and go through the cave , make a right in the middle after the bridge to enter Moonglade up north.

07) Once in Moonglade, go east and just right after the first lake you see, get the Flight Path from the Hippogryph Master 48.67.

08) Exit Moonglade back to Timbermaw Hold (36.73 in Moonglade).

09) In Timbermaw Hold, make a right after the stairs to go into Winterspring..
#18

 
54-54 Winterspring (log)
01) Right outside of the cave, turn in "Speak to Salfa" (28.34 in Winterspring).  And accept "Winterfall Activity".
02) Follow the main path south to Donova Snowden 31.45 and accept "Threat of the Winterfall".  Turn in "The New Springs", accept "Strange Sources".  Also turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", SKIP "The Videre Elixir".  NOTE:  You do not have to wait for the NPC, just leave..
03) As you go east through the open fields of Winterspring start working on "Moontouched Wildkin": loot the Moontouched Feathers on the ground (south of the main path).  While doing this, head east to the next step:
04) Head NE to Starfall Village and turn in (if you have it) "To Winterspring!" 52.30, accept "The Ruins of Kel'Theril".
05) Next to you, turn in "The Ruins of Kel'Theril" 52.30, accept "Troubled Spirits of Kel'Theril".  Also accept "Enraged Wildkin".
06) Make sure you are resupplied at Food & Drink 52.29 or General Goods 51.31.
07) Go SE and just above Everlook go to the Hippogryph Master 62.37 to get the Flight Path.
08) Go around the west side of Everlook to enter it on the south side.  As you enter Everlook, on your left, accept "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38.
09) Go in the Inn and from the Innkeeper, make Everlook your new home 61.39.
TBC, 9b) Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, accept "Ursius of the Shardtooth".

10) Go to Everlook's Bank 61.37 and put the following items into it:  Flute of Xavaric, 5 Jadefire Felbind, Corrupt Moonwell Water, 15 Blood Amber, 6 Filled Cursed Ooze Jar, 6 Filled Tainted Ooze Jar, any Core of Elements you may have, any Deadwood Headdress Feathers you may have, and (if you have it) Deadwood Ritual Totem.
#19

 

01) As you work on the following quests also start working on "Are We There, Yeti?" by kill/looting yeti (any will do), but don't make it your main priority:
02) Go SW to The Ruins of Kel'Theril and do "Troubled Spirits of Kel'Theril": loot the 4 Relic Fragments on the ground.  Watch out for the high level water elementals. Just run in, loot them and run out. First one to loot is First Relic Fragment 55.43.  See next steps for the other 3:
03) Go west a bit and loot the Third Relic Fragment 53.43.
04) Go east a bit and loot the Fourth Relic Fragment 52.41.
05) Go NW a bit and loot the Second Relic Fragment 51.42.
06) Go south to the small wrecked camp and click on the Damaged Crate 59.60 to turn in "Enraged Wildkin", accept the next part.
07) Go SE a bit and click on Jaron's Wagon 61.61 to turn in "Enraged Wildkin", accept the next part.
08) Do part of "Enraged Wildkin": right next to the wagon, loot Jaron's Supplies (small crate 61.61).
09) Go east to Owl Wing Thicket entrance 64.59 and finish "Enraged Wildkin": kill/loot the owls until you get the Blue-feathered Amulet (high drop rate).  Try to avoid the higher level owls, you can find more owls up around the hills at the Owl Wing Thicket and more owls around 59.60.
10) Follow the main path south to discover Darkwhisper Gorge at 60.74 for "Strange Sources".  You actually need to go further south past the bridge to get this discovered.
11) Hearth to Everlook.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 62.38.
12) Go to Everlook's Bank 61.37 and put the following items into it:  Jaron's Pick, First Relic Fragment, Second Relic Fragment, Third Relic Fragment, and Fourth Relic Fragment.
#20

 

TBC, 00) As you go questing, keep an eye out for a bear named Ursius /target (he looks just like all other other ones), if so, kill him for "Ursius of the Shardtooth", but don't intentionally try to find him now:

01) Exit Everlook to the right, then go east to Winterfall Village around 67.35 and do "Winterfall Activity": kill the required amount of Winterfall furbolgs.  NOTE:  This place is usually busy with lvl 60+ players farming Winterfall Firewater here.  If that's the case then this quest can be skipped (there is no follow up).  You can also skip this if you are only level 53 right now as it is kind of a higher level quest.
02) Go west to Starfall Village and turn in "Enraged Wildkin" 52.30.  Make sure you are resupplied.
03) Go west and do "Threat of the Winterfall": kill the required amount of Winterfall furbolgs.  There are two camps of these mobs (one at 42.43 and one at 31.36).  While doing this quest you should find an item, which starts "Winterfall Firewater" (accept it).
04) Go SW to Donova Snowden 31.45 and turn in "Strange Sources", "Threat of the Winterfall" and "Winterfall Firewater", accept "Falling to Corruption".
05) Make sure "Moontouched Wildkin" is finished now: loot the Moontouched Feathers on the ground (south of the main path).
06) Hearth (or run back if its still on a cooldown) to Everlook.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 62.38.
TBC, 6b) IF YOU DID IT:  Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, turn in "Ursius of the Shardtooth".

07) Go SE to Ice Thistle Hills around 66.41 and finish "Are We There, Yeti?": kill/loot yeti (any will do).
08) Hearth (or go back) to Everlook and turn in "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38, accept the next part.
#21

 

01) Go SE back to Ice Thistle Hills around 66.41 and do "Are We There, Yeti?": kill/loot Ice Thistle Matriarchs and Ice Thistle Patriarchs for the 2 horns (low drop rate).  There is a higher concentration of these mobs in the cave entrance up at 68.42.
02) Hearth if you can .  If you can't hearth, then go back to Everlook if you are close, or die on purpose and res at spirit if you are deep in the cave.  Once at Everlook turn in "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38, accept the next part.
03) Do part of "Are We There, Yeti?": at Legacki 62.39 /target (next to the Inn), use Umi's Mechanical Yeti quest item to scare her.
04) In the Inn 61.39 accept "The Everlook Report", "Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff" and (next to you) "Sister Pamela".
05) Go to Everlook's Bank 61.37 and put the following items into it:  10 Moontouched Feathers, Umi's Mechanical Yeti, Everlook Report, Studies in Spirit Speaking and (if you have it) Winterfall Ritual Totem.  The totem could have dropped by one of the Winterfall furbolgs.  And get the following items out:  15 Blood Amber, 9 Un'Goro Ash, Flute of Xavaric, 5 Jadefire Felbind and Corrupt Moonwell Water.
06) Exit Everlook to your right and go to the Hippogryph Master (north of Everlook 62.37) to fly to Talonbranch Glade, Felwood.
08) Go north to Felpaw Village and clear the mobs around the Deadwood Cauldron at 60.6, or distract the mobs some how (See Video), then click on the Cauldron to turn in "Falling to Corruption", accept "Mystery Goo".
09) Go through the cave NE entrance 65.7 to get to Winterspring and right outside of the cave, turn in "Winterfall Activity" (28.34 in Winterspring).
10) Follow the main path south to Donova Snowden 31.45 and turn in "Mystery Goo", accept "Toxic Horrors".
11) Hearth (or go east) to get to Everlook.
12) Go to the Hippogryph Master (62.37, #6 on map) to fly to Ratchet, The Barrens.
#22

 
54-55 Felwood (log)
01) At Ratchet, In Liv Rizzlefix's hut (62.39 in The Barrens), turn in "Volcanic Activity".
02) Go up to the Inn and make Ratchet your new home 62.39.
03) Go south of Ratchet to The Tidus Stair (small Tauren camp 66.44) and turn in "Seeking Spiritual Aid".  Wait a minute, then accept "Cleansed Water Returns to Felwood".
04) Go north to the Flight Master 63.37 at Ratchet and fly to Emerald Sanctuary, Felwood.
10) At Emerald Sanctuary turn in "Cleansed Water Returns to Felwood" 51.82, accept "Dousing the Flames of Protection".

11) In the house 51.82, turn in "Flute of Xavaric", accept "Felbound Ancients".  Also turn in "Further Corruption"

12) In the tent, turn in "Verifying the Corruption" 51.82.

13) Go SE, and just off the main path, turn in "Cleansing Felwood" (54.87 in Felwood).  Then click the NPC again and talk to her to get a Cenarion Beacon.
#23

 

01) Go north to Jaedenar, and enter Shadow Hold entrance 35.58, then do "Dousing the Flames of Protection": click on the 4 Braziers.  The cave is relatively linear and if you keep following it you will see the 4 Braziers to click on.  The first one is at the bottom of the ramp that enters the cave 36.56.  Go up a bit and you will see the 2nd one 36.55.  Keep moving in and you will run into a big room that has the last two (37.53, 38.53).  Keep the next step in mind:
01b) Exit the cave grinding backwards out of it.  Also if you found a Blood Red Key (in keyring), then turn it in near the entrance of the cave from the caged night elf 36.56.  Then go ahead and do the escort "Rescue From Jaedenar" which leads back into the cave and then back out of it.
02) Go back south to Emerald Sanctuary and turn in "Dousing the Flames of Protection" 51.82.  Accept "A Final Blow" (this will be done later at level 58).  Also, next to you, turn in (if you did it) "Rescue From Jaedenar", accept "Retribution of the Light" (this would also be done later at level 58).
03) Go south and just to the east of the main path, turn in "Salve via Hunting" 54.87.  NOTES:  If for some reason you do not have the 6 Corrupted Soul Shards, grind on any mob in Felwood until you get them.  The quest is repeatable, but doesn't yield additional XP.  The reward item Cenarion Plant Salve is used to cleanse the various corrupted plants around Felwood and the reward you get from them is decent (but no XP).
04) Go south and grind on Deadwood furblogs around 49.89 until you are able to hearth.
05) Hearth to Ratchet .  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied. 
06) Go north to the Flight Master 63.37 at Ratchet and fly to Theramore, Dustwallow Marsh.
07) N/A
08) At Theramore, go SE at the end of the docks to get on the boat to take you to Menethil Harbor 72.56.
09) At Menethil, go to the Gryphon Master 9.60 and fly to Ironforge, Dun Morogh.
#24

 

01) At Ironforge, go west to The High Seat and accept "The Smoldering Ruins of Thaurissan" 38.55.  Then do it by talking to the NPC.  Turn it in and accept the next part.
02) Go west to the Inn and make Ironforge your new home from the Innkeeper 18.51.
03) Go to the bank 35.62 and get the following items out:  current stack of Dark Iron Scraps, Black Dragonflight Molt, 6 Filled Cursed Ooze Jar, 6 Filled Tainted Ooze Jar and any cloth for cloth donations.  Put in the bank:  Eridan's Vial, your Cenarion Beacon, any Cenarion Plant Salves and any Corrupted Soul Shards you may have.
04) You can turn in any cloth donation for the Dwarf Faction north from Mistina Steelshield 43.32.
05) N/A
GET NEW ABILITIES, 5b) Get your level 54 spells/abilities (if you haven't yet). 

06) Go east to Hall of Explorers (at The Library) and turn in "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way" 76.23.  Accept the next part.
07) You can go south to Tinker Town and turn in any cloth donations for the Gnome faction from Bubulo Acerbus 74.48.
GET NEW ABILITIES, 7b) Get your level 54 spells/abilities (if you haven't yet). 

08) Go to the Bank (35.62, #3 on map) and put the following item into it:  Bag of Empty Ooze Containers.
09) Go to the Gryphon Master 56.48 in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Morgan's Vigil, Burning Steppes.
#25

 
55-56 Burning Steppes
01) Next to you when you land at Morgan's Vigil 85.69, accept "Extinguish the Firegut" and "FIFTY! YEP!".
03) Grind your way north to Flame Crest ramp entrance 64.28..
04) Go up to Flame Crest and accept "Broodling Essence" and "Tablet of the Seven" 65.24.
05) Go in the small cave there and turn in "Yuka Screwspigot" 66.22, SKIP the next part (Dungeon).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
06) Go to the east side of Burning Steppes (starting around 73.28, #8 on map) and work on the following 4 steps together:
07) Start working on "FIFTY! YEP!" by kill/looting any Worgs you see.  Don't make this a main priority now!
08) Start working on "Broodling Essence": use the Draco-Incarcinatrix 900 quest item on Broodlings (dragon whelps), then kill them and loot the Broodling Essence crystal that drops.  Also throughout questing in Burning Steppes, kill any dragon whelps you see, they are squishy mobs that die real fast.
09) N/A
10) If you have the Black Dragonflight Molt with you, work your way east to the small cave 95.32 then accept and turn in "A Taste of Flame" (you will need to talk to him first after getting the quest), SKIP the next part (Dungeon quest).
11) At Dreadmaul Rock (in the middle of the east side of Burning Steppes) do "Extinguish the Firegut": kill the required amount of Firegut ogres.  Also finish "Broodling Essence": use the Draco-Incarcinatrix 900 quest item on Broodlings (dragon whelps), then kill them and loot the Broodling Essence crystal that drops.  
#26

 

01) Go west to Ruins of Thaurissan (starting at 69.38) and do "The Smoldering Ruins of Thaurissan": loot the Thaurissan Relics (small stones).  Keep the next step in mind:
02) Work west in Ruins of Thaurissan and do "Tablet of the Seven": loot the Tablet of the Seven next to the dwarf statue 54.41.
03) Grind your way north to Flame Crest ramp entrance 64.29..
04) Go up to Flame Crest and turn in "Broodling Essence" 65.24, accept "Felnok Steelspring". And turn in "Tablet of the Seven".
05) At Flame Crest make sure you are repaired/resupplied from Yuka Screwspigot 66.22 in the small cave.
06) Grind south to Morgan's Vigil and turn in "Extinguish the Firegut" 85.69, accept "Gor'tesh the Brute Lord".
07) Go west to The Pillar of Ash (starting at 53.53) and finish "FIFTY! YEP!": kill/loot the Blackrock orcs.  Keep the next step in mind:
08) Work your way west in The Pillar of Ash and do "Gor'tesh the Brute Lord": kill/loot Gor'tesh /target (ogre that patrols around the bunker entrance 39.55).
09) Grind back east to Morgan's Vigil and turn in "Gor'tesh the Brute Lord" (85.69, #6 on map), accept "Ogre Head On A Stick = Party".  Also turn in "FIFTY! YEP!".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  If you have any Blackrock Medallions left over after turning in "FIFTY! YEP!", they can be destroyed.
10) Go up to the top of Dreadmaul Rock (bridge entrance at 76.38)..
11) Once at the top do "Ogre Head On A Stick = Party": next to the bonfire, click on the Soft Dirt Mound 81.47.
12) While at Dreadmaul Rock, make sure you collect a total of 30 Dark Iron Scraps, this will be used for a later quest.  All the Ogres in Burning Steppes can drop them.

13) N/A
14) Go back south to Morgan's Vigil and turn in "Ogre Head On A Stick = Party" (85.69, #6 on map).
#27

 

16) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 85.68.  NOTE: Steps 1 - 16 are taken out for WoTLK due to the Onyxia key quest chain was taken out for it!

#28

 

01) At Ironforge, go to the bank 35.61 and get the following item out:  Everlook Report..  Put the following items in the bank:  stack of 30+ Dark Iron Scraps and Tinkee's Letter.
02) If you have not accepted "The New Frontier" and "A Call to Arms: The Plaguelands!" from Stormwind previously, then find Courier Hammerfall /target (he patrols around Ironforge, See Map) and accept these quests from him.  As you work on the following steps you might run into him, but make sure these are accepted before flying out of Ironforge:
03) You can turn in any cloth donation for the Dwarf faction from Mistina Steelshield 43.32 and cloth donations for the Gnome faction from Bubulo Acerbus 74.48.

05) If you turned level 56 then get new spells/abilities for your class.

#29

 
56-56 Western Plaguelands (log)
01) At Southshore, go NE (follow path to take on map) to get to Chillwind Camp, Western Plaguelands...
02) At Chillwind Camp, right as you get there and next to the shack/torn down house, accept "A Plague Upon Thee" (43.85 in Western Plaguelands).
03) Behind the shack, get the Flight Path from the Gryphon Master she patrols a bit around 43.85.
TBC, 3a) From the male Draenei 43.84 accept "The Mark of the Lightbringer".

04) From the bald human male by the tent, turn in "A Call to Arms: The Plaguelands!" 43.84, accept "Clear the Way".
05) In front of the other tent, from The Argent Dawn female human 43.84, turn in "The Everlook Report".  Also complete "Argent Dawn Commission".  NOTE:  The trinket received called Argent Dawn Commission will allow you to collect Scourgestones from undead mobs in the Plaguelands (both eastern and western).  Right-click it in your inventory to wear it.  As long as you have it on, this will allow you to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn, which will allow you to buy items such as Enriched Manna Biscuits later.
06) Go east to Sorrow Hill around 48.81 and do "Clear the Way": kill 10 Skeletal Flayers and 10 Slavering Ghouls.
07) Go back west to Chillwind Camp and from the bald human male by the tent (43.84, #2 on map), turn in "Clear the Way".  Accept "All Along the Watchtowers" and "The Scourge Cauldrons".
08) Next to you by the wagon 43.85, turn in "The Scourge Cauldrons", accept "Target: Felstone Field".
09) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied /target 43.84.
#30

 


GROUP IF YOU CAN: The rest of the entire 56-56 WPL section is soloable but is probably the hardest section to do in the entire guide and I recommend seeing if you can group with someone to do the rest of this section, if not you should be able to solo it, some sections will be a bit rough.  Proceed with caution
 
01) Follow the main path NW to get into Ruins of Andorhal and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower One 40.72.  NOTE: You must use the torch item right in the doorway of these towers in order for it to work, don't waste your charges!
02) Go north and enter the Andorhal Inn entrance 40.68, go upstairs and accept "A Matter of Time" 39.67.
03) Go NE and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Two 42.66.
04) Go NE and do the last part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Three 44.63.
05) Go east a bit and do "A Matter of Time": use the Temporal Displacer quest item near the silos around 47.63 then kill the Temporal Parasites that spawn.  NOTE:  The quest item only works on the silos emitting blue beams of light.
06) Go south and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Four 47.71.
07) Go NW and enter the Andorhal Inn (entrance 40.68, #2 on map), go upstairs and turn in "A Matter of Time".  Accept "Counting Out Time".
08) All around Ruins of Andorhal do "Counting Out Time": loot the Small Lockboxes all around the broken down houses.  There is usually one at each broken down house.
09) Enter the Andorhal Inn (entrance 40.68, #2 on map), go upstairs and turn in "Counting Out Time".  NOTES:  This quest is repeatable, but you will not get additional XP from it.  The quest reward Attuned Dampener can only be used on Araj the Summoner in the middle of Andorhal, and there will be quests later associated with killing him.
10) Cauldron Quest Chain Info:  You need to kill the Cauldron Lord at each targeted farm field (listed below), grab their key and use it at the big cauldron in the middle of the farm fields.  You will be going back and forth from Chillwind Camp to the next field.  As you enter each field, the Cauldron Lords spawns near the Scourge Cauldron in the middle of each field, if the Cauldron Lord is not there, leave the field and come back.
11) At Felstone Field, do "Target: Felstone Field": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Bilemaw /target 37.57.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron 37.57 to turn in "Target: Felstone Field", accept "Return to Chillwind Camp".
12) North in Felstone Field, go up in the house and accept "Better Late Than Never" 38.54.
13) Go in the barn next to the house and click on Janice's Parcel 39.55 to turn in "Better Late Than Never".  Then click Janice's Parcel again to accept the next part of "Better Late Than Never".
14) Make sure you are at least 10,000 XP away from 56 (if you aren't yet).  Keep grinding on any undead mobs to increase rep with The Argent Dawn until you are.
#31

 

01) Hearth to Ironforge.
02) At Ironforge, go to the Bank 35.61 and get the following items out:  First Relic Fragment, Second Relic Fragment, Third Relic Fragment, Fourth Relic Fragment, Studies in Spirit Speaking, (if you can) Swim Speed Potion, (if you have them) 30+ Dark Iron Scraps and 30+ Savage Fronds.  There is a quest in Eastern Plaguelands that wants these items:  2 Thorium Bar, Golden Rod, 8 Hi-Explosive Bomb, 8 Unstable Trigger (get these out, either from the AH or bank if you can get all of them).  You can then mail these items (including the 30+ Dark Iron Scraps and 30+ Savage Fronds) to yourself and retrieve them from the mailbox at Light's Hope in EPL):
03) Also, put in the bank:  Attuned Dampener.
04) Go to the Gryphon Master 55.48 in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Stormwind, Elwynn.
05) At the side entrance of the Cathedral Square, go up in the City Hall building and turn in "Better Late Than Never" 57.48, accept "Good Natured Emma".
06) Find Ol' Emma /target, she patrols around the northern part of The Trade District.  She patrols from up in the house at 60.57 to 67.72.  From her, turn in "Good Natured Emma", accept "Good Luck Charm".
07) Go NE to Stormwind Keep and accept "The First and the Last" 80.38.
GET NEW ABILITIES, 7b) You should be level 56 now.  Get new lvl 56 spells/abilities if you haven't yet.

08) Go south to Old Town and up in SI:7, turn in "The First and the Last" 78.71, accept and turn in "Honor the Dead", then accept "Flint Shadowmore".
09) Hearth if you can, then fly to Chillwind Camp, WPL. If you can't hearth then just go to the Gryphon Master 71.73 and fly to Chillwind Camp, Western Plaguelands.
10) When you land at Chillwind Camp, next to you by the wagon, turn in "Return to Chillwind Camp" 43.85, accept "Target: Dalson's Tears".
11) From the bald human male by the tent 43.84, turn in "All Along the Watchtowers".  Accept "Scholomance".  You can then destroy the Beacon Torch item.
12) Next to you, turn in "Scholomance" 43.84, accept "Skeletal Fragments".  NOTE:  "Skeletal Fragments" quest involves kill/looting skeleton type mobs around Ruins of Andorhal, Felstone Field and Dalson's Tears.  Start working on this quest as you continue working on other WPL quests:
13) In front of the torn down house, turn in "Flint Shadowmore" 44.85, accept "The Eastern Plagues".
#32

 

01) Go north to Felstone Field and up in the house, turn in "Good Luck Charm" 38.54, accept "Two Halves Become One".
02) Go south in Felstone Field and do "Two Halves Become One": kill/loot the Jabbering Ghoul /target (only one mob spawn that patrols around the farm).  This mob drops Good Luck Other-Half-Charm.  Right-click this item in your inventory.
03) Go back up in the house at Felstone Field and turn in "Two Halves Become One" (38.54, #1 on map).
04) Go east to Dalson's Tears farm and do "Target: Dalson's Tears": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Malvinious /target 46.52.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Dalson's Tears", accept "Return to Chillwind Camp".
05) There is a small quest chain here at this farm that yields good XP:  Go in the Barn at Dalson's Tears and click on Mrs. Dalson's Diary laying on the ground 48.51 to complete "Mrs. Dalson's Diary".
06) Next, find and kill/loot the Wandering Skeleton /target.  This one skeleton wanders around the NE side of Dalson's Tears.  He circles around the barn as well.
07) Click on the Outhouse 48.50 behind the barn to complete "Locked Away".  Then Farmer Dalson will spawn next to the Outhouse, kill/loot this zombie.
08) Go up in the house (next to the barn) and click on the Locked Cabinet 47.50 to turn in "Locked Away".
09) Go back south to Chillwind Camp and turn in "Return to Chillwind Camp" 43.85, accept "Target: Writhing Haunt".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) Go NE to The Writhing Haunt and do "Target: Writhing Haunt": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Razarch /target 53.66.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Writhing Haunt", accept "Return to Chillwind Camp".
#33

 

01) Go back to Chillwind Camp and turn in "Return to Chillwind Camp" 43.84, accept "Target: Gahrron's Withering".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) From the The Argent Dawn female human 43.84 by the tent, turn in any Scourgestones you may have to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn.   Also, it's important to make sure you save at least one Argent Dawn Valor Token for a later quest.
03) Go NE to Gahrron's Withering and do "Target: Gahrron's Withering": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Soulwrath /target 63.59.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Gahrron's Withering", accept "Return to Chillwind Camp".
04) Make sure "Skeletal Fragments" is finished:  kill/loot skeleton type mobs.  You can find a large amount at Ruins of Andorhal (starting at 49.67).
05) Go back to Chillwind Camp (#1 on map) and turn in "Skeletal Fragments" 43.84, SKIP "Mold Rhymes With...".
06) At the wagon, turn in "Return to Chillwind Camp" 43.85.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  You can also turn in Scourgestones to increase rep with The Argent Dawn, but it's important to make sure you save at least one Argent Dawn Valor Token for a later quest.
07) From the bald human male by the tent 43.84, complete "Mission Accomplished!".
08) If for some reason you are already friendly with The Argent Dawn, then stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits next to you from Argent Quartermaster Lightspark (gnome 43.84).  These biscuits will help you level faster by receiving mana faster from consuming these biscuits.

09) Go east into Eastern Plaguelands (follow path on map)..
#34

 
56-57 Eastern Plaguelands (log)
01) Go north along Thondoril River to Tiron Fordring 5.38 and accept "Demon Dogs", "Blood Tinged Skies" and "Carrion Grubbage".
02) Grind SE to The Marris Stead and do "The Eastern Plagues": loot the 3 skeleton remains.  First one to loot is Mangled Human Remains [Turyen] (up on a hill 25.68).  See next steps for the other two:
03) Go west a bit (watch out for the elites) and loot the Mangled Human Remains [Fredo] (up on a hill 24.68).  Getting this also gets The Blightcaller Uncovered as well.
04) South a bit click on Mangled Human Remains [Rutger] (just north of the road 25.73).
05) Go south to The Undercroft and down in the crypt, from the Torn Scroll laying on the ground, accept "Hameya's Plea" 24.78.
06) Grind east to Darrowshire (kill/loot every mob you see along the way) to Pamela Redpath and turn in "Sister Pamela" 32.84, accept "Pamela's Doll".
07) At Darrowshire do "Pamela's Doll": find the 3 parts to Pamela's Doll:  the head, the left side and right side.  These parts have multiple spawn locations around Darrowshire in each of the 3 buildings in the area (excluding the building where Pamela is at).  They look like little yellow things.  Search the buildings for the 3 parts: big house 34.85, torn down house 35.85, small house 35.83, then click one of them to combine them to make Pamela's Doll.  (See Video)
08) Go back to Pamela Redpath at Darrowshire and turn in "Pamela's Doll" ( 32.84, #6 on map).  Accept "Auntie Marlene" and "Uncle Carlin".
09) Around southern EPL around 36.76, finish "Blood Tinged Skies": kill 30 Plaguebats.  Also do part of "Demon Dogs": kill 20 Plaguehound Runts.  And start working on "Carrion Grubbage": kill/loot Carrion mobs for the worm meat.  All of these mobs are found in southern Eastern Plaguelands and the Carrion worm mobs are all over Eastern Plaguelands.  Once you are nearing completion, work your way east to the next step:
10) Grind to Light's Hope Chapel 74.58 and accept "The Restless Souls" and "Zaeldarr the Outcast".  Get resupplied here.
11) Go north a bit and turn in "Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff" 76.54.
#35

 

01) Next to you, turn in "Uncle Carlin" 76.54, accept "Defenders of Darrowshire".
02) Next to you from the Gryphon Master 76.53 get the Flight Path.
03) Get repaired from Craftsman Wilhelm 75.54 next to the Forge.

04) Right in front of the Inn's Entrance, go to Rayne and if you have 30 Savage Fronds accept and turn in "Savage Fronds" 75.53.  NOTE:  This and all 30 item turn-in quests here at Light's Hope requires you to be friendly with The Argent Dawn.  If you are not friendly yet, ignore these for now and you will be able to turn these in a bit later once you are.

05) Go in the Inn and in the back from the human with black hair 76.52, accept and turn in "Binding the Dreadnaught" (requires the 30 Dark Iron Scraps).

06) If you have all 30 Bone Fragments, then you can accept and turn in "Bonescythe Digs" 76.53 (if not you can have more opportunities later).

07) If you were able to obtain the items needed for "That's Asking A Lot", then turn it in (at the dwarf by the Campfire, 75.52 ), accept "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent".
08) Next to you at the human on the horse and with your one Argent Dawn Valor Token you saved earlier, accept and turn in "They Call Me "The Rooster" 75.52.

#36

 

01) Do part of "Demon Dogs": kill 5 Plaguehounds /target.  These evil dogs are found in the middle-eastern side of Eastern Plaguelands around 66.50.
02) Go NE a bit around 61.35 and start working on "Defenders of Darrowshire": kill any Gibbering Ghouls or Diseased Flayers and then talk to the spirit that spawns.  If the area is cleared, you can continue on and revisit this place later:
03) Grind north to Zul'Mashar (ramp entrance at 59.19)..
04) Go up into Zul'Mashar and do "Hameya's Plea": kill/loot Infiltrator Hameya /target.  This lvl 60 troll patrols around in front of the temple around 64.13.
05) Finish up "Carrion Grubbage" and "Demon Dogs": kill/loot Carrion mobs for the worm meat and kill 5 Frenzied Plaguehounds.  These mobs are found NE around the open fields of Eastern Plaguelands around 52.26.  Keep the next step in mind:
06) Go north (just below Quel'Lithien Lodge) and turn in "Troubled Spirits of Kel'Theril" 49.18 , accept "Fragments of the Past".
07) Go west to Plaguewood (starting at 41.29 ) and do the following 3 quests together:
08) "A Plague Upon Thee": loot the Large Termite Mounds, these are spaced all around Plaguewood (see map).
09) "Defenders of Darrowshire": continue working on this by killing Cannibal Ghouls and talk to the spirits that spawn.  You will have more opportunities later to finish this.
10) If you have it do "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent": go in the various ziggurat buildings around Plaguewood and use Smokey's Special Compound quest item inside the buildings.
#37

 

01) Go west to Terrordale and in the hut turn in "The Restless Souls" 11.29 , SKIP the next part (dungeon).  And next to you, accept "Augustus' Receipt Book".
02) Do "Augustus' Receipt Book": go NE a bit to the Terrordale Inn (the largest building in the area entrance 14.28 ) and go up in the Inn, make a right and between two split open barrels loot Augustus' Receipt Book 14.26 on the ground.  NOTE:  Kill/loot all Crypt Fiends you see to start obtaining Crypt Fiend Parts (used for a later quest).
03) Go back to the hut in Terrordale and turn in "Augustus' Receipt Book" ( 11.29 , #1 on map).  NOTE:  You can now get resupplied from this NPC.
04) Grind on Crypt Fiend mobs all around Terrordale to potentially collect 30 Crypt Fiend Parts.  This can be used for a later quest.  There is more in the next step as well (but watch out for the Elite):

05) Go through the long cave entrance 12.25.  NOTES:  This cave journey is not easy.  There is a lvl 60 elite spider /target (with a 1 hour respawn) that patrols back and forth to the 2 entrances of this cave.  Try to sneak past it if you can.  If this cave is too hard for you, then another possible quick alternative is to take the Tower Gryphon ride from the Plaguewood Tower 19.27 to the Crown Guard Tower, as long as the Alliance has control over the towers that is.  Try to get all 30 Crypt Fiend Parts before leaving this area.  If you are still far off from that, then you can skip getting the remaining ones.
06) Once you get out to the other side of the cave (or take the tower ride), north along Thondoril River at Tiron Fordring 5.38 , turn in "Demon Dogs" "Blood Tinged Skies" and "Carrion Grubbage".  Accept "Redemption".  Simply talk to Tiron Fordring to complete "Redemption", accept "Of Forgotten Memories".
07) Go south to The Undercroft, go down in the crypt and then do "Zaeldarr the Outcast": kill/loot Zaeldarr the Outcast 24.78.  (See Video)
08) Behind the crypt, next to the wooden wagon, click on the Mound of Dirt to turn in "Hameya's Plea" 24.79.
09) Next to you, do "Of Forgotten Memories": click on the Loose Dirt Mound 25.80 that is next to Tirion Fordring's Grave, then kill/loot Mercutio Filthgorger (he has 3 bodyguards).  IMPORTANT TIP:  These mobs spawn between the two dirt mounds (from the previous step).  Mercutio Filthgorger spawns in first, then his bodyguards soon after.  So act fast and immediately pull Mercutio Filthgorger away from his spawn point to avoid fighting his guards too, as this fight can be really tough to do solo without this tactic.
#38

 

01) Go NW to Tiron Fordring, 5.38 and turn in "Of Forgotten Memories", accept "Of Lost Honor".
02) Go to the south side of Light's Hope Chapel (grind on all mobs along the way), then turn in "Zaeldarr the Outcast" 74.58.
03) Go north in Light's Hope Chapel and turn in (if it's finished) "Defenders of Darrowshire" 76.54.
04) Next to you at Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff 76.54, turn in any Scourgestones you have.
05) Get resupplied/repaired from Craftsman Wilhelm 75.54 next to the Forge.  NOTE:  If for some reason you are already Friendly with The Argent Dawn, then stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits from the female dwarf Miranda Breechlock 76.54 by the tent.
06) Go in the Inn and with your 30 Crypt Fiend Parts, accept and turn in "Cryptstalker Armor Doesn't Make Itself..." 76.53.

07) If you have all 30 Bone Fragments, then you can accept and turn in (if you haven't yet) "Bonescythe Digs" 76.53 (if not you can have another opportunity later).

08) Go north a bit more in Light's Hope Chapel and turn in (if you did it) "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent" 75.52.  If you turned this in, you can then destroy Smokey's Special Compound.
Phase6, 8b) If you still need to:  Next to you at the human on the horse and with your Argent Dawn Valor Token, accept and turn in "They Call Me "The Rooster" 75.52.

09) Go NE a bit around 61.35 and finish (if you still need to) "Defenders of Darrowshire": kill any Gibbering Ghouls or Diseased Flayers and then talk to the spirit that spawns.
10) Go north to Northdale and do "Of Lost Honor": loot the Symbol of Lost Honor (under the lake 66.29).
11) IF YOU STILL NEED TO: Go SE to Light's Hope Chapel and turn in "Defenders of Darrowshire" (#3 on map) 76.54.
12) Grind all the way back west to Tiron Fordring, ( 5.38, #1 on map) and turn in "Of Lost Honor", accept "Of Love and Family".
13) Go down south at the island of Caer Darrow 66.75 in WPL (use a Swim Speed Potion if you have one) and turn in "Of Love and Family".  SKIP the next part (Dungeon).
#39

 
57-58 Western Plaguelands (log)
01) Go west to Sorrow Hill and in the house turn in "Auntie Marlene" 49.79, accept "A Strange Historian".  NOTE: She could be upstairs as well.
02) Do "A Strange Historian": next to the house your at, loot Joseph Redpath's Monument in the graveyard 50.77.  (See Video)
03) Go west to Chillwind Camp and in front of the torn down house, turn in "The Eastern Plagues" 44.85, accept "The Blightcaller Cometh".
04) Next to you at the shack, turn in "A Plague Upon Thee" 43.85, accept the next part.
05) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.84.  NOTE:  If you have any remaining Plagueland Termites in your inventory, you can destroy them.
06) Enter Ruins of Andorhal on the west side and go upstairs in the Andorhal Inn entrance 40.68 and turn in "A Strange Historian", accept "The Annals of Darrowshire".
07) Enter the City Hall building in the middle of Andorhal entrance 44.69 and do "The Annals of Darrowshire": loot the Annals of Darrowshire (blue book).  The actual Annals of Darrowshire book looks different from the others, it has a lighter tint on the top portion of the pages in the book, unlike the rest which has a darker tint on the top portion of the pages. (See Video)
08) Go back west to the Andorhal Inn entrance 40.68 and turn in "The Annals of Darrowshire" (#6 on map).  Accept "Brother Carlin".
09) Go east to The Writhing Haunt and in the small house accept "The Wildlife Suffers Too" 54.65.
10) Work your way north (to the next step) while starting on "The Wildlife Suffers Too": kill Diseased Wolves /target:
11) Go north to Northridge Lumber Camp and do "A Plague Upon Thee": clear the mobs at the lumber camp, click on the Northridge Lumber Mill Crate 48.32 to place the termite barrel.  Then click on the spawned Termite Barrel on top of the crate to turn in "A Plague Upon Thee", accept the next part.  (See Video)
12) Go down the long slope in Northridge Lumber Camp and accept "Unfinished Business" 52.28.
#40

 

01) Go south a bit and do "Unfinished Business": kill the needed Scarlet human mobs in the area.  The mages and knights (are around 50.41).  NOTE: They share spawns with each other.  See next step for the medics and hunters:
02) Go south a bit to the small Scarlet camp and kill the needed medics and hunters around 52.44.
03) Go back up NE in Northridge Lumber Camp, down the long slope and turn in "Unfinished Business" 52.28, accept the next part.
04) Go east a bit and do "Unfinished Business": kill Huntsman Radley /target 58.36.  See next step for the other named mob to kill:
05) Kill Cavalier Durgen /target.  He patrols around in the tower 55.24.   Also go up this tower and do "The Mark of the Lightbringer" by looting the Holy Coffer.
06) Go back down in Northridge Lumber Camp and turn in "Unfinished Business" (52.28, #3 on map), accept the next part.
07) Go north to Hearthglen and do the last part of "Unfinished Business": go to the top of the watchtower 46.18 to complete the quest.  Just mount up and avoid the mobs.  Having a healing potion on hand helps.  (Watch This Video to see a shortcut and how to do this quest).
08) Go back down in Northridge Lumber Camp and turn in "Unfinished Business" (52.28, #3 on map).
09) Make sure "The Wildlife Suffers Too" is finished: kill 8 Diseased Wolves /target.  These wolves are found mostly above Dalson's Tears farm around 47.45.  They share spawns with the spiders in the area, so kill them if not enough wolves.  Work your way to the next step:
10) Go south to the small house at The Writhing Haunt and turn in "The Wildlife Suffers Too" 54.65, accept the next part.
11) Do "The Wildlife Suffers Too" (part 2): kill 8 Diseased Grizzly /target.  These bears are found north and east of The Writhing Haunt.  They share spawns with the spiders, so kill spiders if not enough bears.
12) Go back to the small house at The Writhing Haunt and turn in "The Wildlife Suffers Too" (54.65, #10 on map).  Accept "Glyphed Oaken Branch".
13) At this point make sure you have at least 30 Bone Fragments (if you still need to turn in The Argent Dawn EPL quest).  You can farm this item at Ruins of Andorhal (starting at 49.67 ).
14) Go to Chillwind Camp and in front of the shack, turn in "A Plague Upon Thee" 43.85.

15) Also turn in "The Mark of the Lightbringer" 43.84, accept "Tomb of the Lightbringer". NOTES:  Accepting this starts an escort, which we will do on next step:  Also if this male Draenei is not there, it means someone else is currently doing the escort, if so just skip it then, or consider doing it later.

16) Do "Tomb of the Lightbringer": escort the quest giver to Uther's Tomb 52.83 to complete it.

17) Go to Chillwind Camp and turn in "Tomb of the Lightbringer" 43.84.  
#41

 

01) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  You can stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits from the gnome 43.84 (I get about 300 biscuits).
02) You should be level 58 by now, if not, grind on any mobs until you are.
03) Hearth to Ironforge.
04) At Ironforge, go to the Bank 35.61 and put the following items in the bank:  Extended Annals of Darrowshire, any remaining Crypt Fiend Parts, Bone Fragments, Savage Fronds, Dark Iron Scraps, any Insignias and any Scourgestones you may have.  Get the following items out:  Eridan's Vial and 10 Moontouched Feathers.
05) Get your level 58 spells/abilities for your class. 

06) Near the Gryphon Master accept "Taking Back Silithus" 59.47.

07) Go to the Gryphon Master 55.48 in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Stormwind, Elwynn.
08) At Stormwind, go NE to Stormwind Keep and turn in "The Blightcaller Cometh" 80.38, accept "Order Must Be Restored".  NOTE:  "Order Must Be Restored" can be kept in your quest book until you want to do this in EPL with a raid, but my guide will not cover it.
09) Go to the docks and take the southern boat 22.56to take you to Auberdine.
#42

 

01) At Auberdine, on the small dock next to the Inn, go to the Hippogryph Master 36.46and fly to Rut'theran Village, Teldrassil.

02) At Rut'theran Village, go in the house and turn in "Moontouched Wildkin" 55.92, accept "Find Ranshalla".
03) Go up the path through the red portal 56.89 to get to Darnassus.
04) At Darnassus, go north to Cenarion Enclave and at the middle level of the tree tower 35.8, turn in "Glyphed Oaken Branch".
05) Go to the top of the tree tower and turn in "The New Frontier", accept the next part.
06) Go down to the middle level of the tree tower and turn in "The New Frontier" 35.8, accept "Rabine Saturna".
07) Go south to Temple of the Moon and in the water fountain in the middle 39.85 do "Felbound Ancients": use the Eridan's Vial quest item in the waterfall.
08) Go up in Temple of the Moon (on the east side) and from Gracina Spiritmight /target accept (no exclamation mark) "March of the Silithid" 42.86.
09) Any cloth donations are turned in at 64.23 for the Night Elf faction.
10) Exit Darnassus by going through the red portal 29.41.
11) On the docks, go to the Hippogryph Master 58.94 and fly to Moonglade.
12) Once in Moonglade, go to Nighthaven and turn in "Rabine Saturna" 52.45, accept "Wasteland".  Also accept "A Reliquary of Purity". NOTE:  In order to accept the purity quest, you need to have been in the Dire Maul zone in Feralas before talking to Rabine Saturna in order to unlock the dialog option (which you also need to read) in order to get this quest.  If you can't get it, you can just skip it and ignore any future notations of it.
13) Go south (just east of the small lake) to the Hippogryph Master 48.67 and fly to Talonbranch Glade, Felwood.
#43

 
58-58 Felwood / Azshara (log)
01) Go SW to Irontree Woods (around 50.23 in Felwood) and do "Toxic Horrors": kill/loot Toxic Horrors (green water elementals).
02) Go south to Jaedenar and enter Shadow Hold, entrance 35.58 .  Keep going through the cave then do (if you have it) "Retribution of the Light" by killing Rakaiah 38.51, then turn it in on the slab next you in that room, accept the next part.  Then do "A Final Blow": keep going through the cave (it is relatively linear), then kill/loot Shadow Lord Fel'dan /target and his two succubus minions (in the cave at 39.47).  Then exit the cave going backwards.
03) Go south to Emerald Sanctuary and turn in "A Final Blow" 51.82.  And turn in (if you have it) The Remains of Trey Lightforge.
04) In the small house, turn in "Felbound Ancients" 51.82.  Accept "Purified!".  Wait a few seconds, then turn in "Purified!".  NOTE:  Hold onto the item received Flute of the Ancients.  It involves an escort quest that can be done later (but my guide wont cover it).  You can do that escort if you run out of quests to do at the very end of the guide.  There is also a trick you can do with the flute, as it spawns in extra Toxic Horrors during the escort which can be used to help you farm a valuable item.
05) Go south into Ashenvale, make a right off the main path and die on purpose (around 55.34 in Ashenvale).  Res at spirit.
06) NW in Astranaar, go to the Hippogryph Master 34.48 and fly to Talrendis Point, Azshara.
07) Go east inside Temple of Zin-Malor and down below loot the Sacred Highborne Writings (light green book on the ground 42.53).
09) Go west to Talrendis Point and you can get repaired/resupplied from the Bowyer 12.78.
10) Go to the Hippogryph Master 12.78 at Talrendis Point and fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
#44

 
58-58 Tanaris / Un'Goro (log)
01) SE in Gadgetzan, go to the Bank 52.29 and get the following items out:  Bag of Empty Ooze Containers, Umi's Mechanical Yeti and your stack of Core of Elements.  Put the following items in the bank:  3 Toxic Horror Droplet and Sacred Highborne Writings.
02) NW in Gadgetzan, do part of "Are We There, Yeti?": use Umi's Mechanical Yeti quest item to scare Sprinkle /target 51.27.
03) In the hut next to you, turn in "March of the Silithid" 51.27, accept "Bungle in the Jungle".
04) Go SW into Un'Goro Crater (entrance 27.54 in Tanaris).
05) At Un'Goro Crater, go west to The Slithering Scar around 50.81 and do "Bungle in the Jungle": keep kill/looting the Gorishi mobs (bugs) until you get the Gorishi Scent Gland (can drop from any of them, but has a low drop rate) and also 5 Un'Goro Soil.  These bugs can be found in and outside of the cave.  You can find more soil from the slimes on the next step.
06) Do "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way".  First loot your Bag of Empty Ooze Containers, then use the Empty Pure Sample Jar quest item on the corpses of slimes through out Un'Goro Crater (any slimes will do).  Work your way to the next step:
07) Go north to Marshal's Refuge and do the last part of "Are We There, Yeti?": use the Umi's Mechanical Yeti quest item to scare Quixxil /target 44.9.
08) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.  Remember to not sell your Un'Goro Soils!
09) Go west to enter Silithus (entrance 29.22 in Un'Goro Crater)..
#45

 
58-59 Silithus (log)
01) As you enter Silithus go to the small camp on your left (Valor's Rest) and turn in "Wasteland" 82.19, accept "The Spirits of Southwind".
02) Follow the main path west to Cenarion Hold and turn in "Taking Back Silithus" 51.38, accept "Securing the Supply Lines".

03) Next to you at the Wanted Poster: Deathclasp 51.38, accept "Wanted - Deathclasp, Terror of the Sands" (Elite).

04) Go up in the Inn next to you and accept "Deadly Desert Venom" 52.39.

05) Go up in Cenarion Hold and accept "The Twilight Mystery" 50.37.

06) Behind the moonwell, accept "Secret Communication" 49.38.  And IF YOU ARE LVL 60 accept: "Abyssal Contacts", and (next to you) "Twilight Geolords".

07) Go in the building next to you and accept "Dearest Natalia" 49.34.

08) Exit the building and make a left to go up the dirt ramp and accept "Report to Marshal Bluewall" 51.35.

09) At the Hippogryph Master 51.34 in Cenarion Hold get the FP.
10) Go south to Southwind Village around 62.52 and do "The Spirits of Southwind" along with "A Reliquary of Purity": kill 8 Tortured Druids and 8 Tortured Sentinels around the village and work your way up in the village into the building and loot the Dusty Reliquary 63.55.
11) Grind/loot all mobs as you go NE to Valor's Rest and turn in "The Spirits of Southwind" (82.19, #1 on map), accept "Hive in the Tower".
12) Grind back down SW to Southwind Village and do "Hive in the Tower": go to the top of the swarming bug tower 60.53 and click on the Hive'Ashi Pod, then kill/loot the Hive'Ashi Ambushers until you get the Encrusted Silithid Object.  Also while doing Southwind Village quests you should find an item (which drops from the bugs), which starts "Brann Bronzebeard's Lost Letter", accept it.  And keep grinding until you get it.
13) Go NE to Valor's Rest and turn in "Hive in the Tower" (82.19, #1 on map), accept "Umber, Archivist".
#46


01) Go to the small camp above Staghelm Point around 66.21 and do "Secret Communication": kill/loot the Twilight mobs.  NOTE:  IF YOU ARE LVL 60 the quest "Abyssal Contacts" can be done here and multiple times later in this zone but my guide will not cover it or the follow ups, but its worth doing.  And keep the next step in mind:
02) All around NE Silithus do "Deadly Desert Venom" along with "Securing the Supply Lines": kill/loot Stonelash Scorpids (scorpids), kill/loot Sand Skitterers (spiders), and kill Dredge Strikers (snakes).
03) Go to Cenarion Hold and turn in "Securing the Supply Lines" 51.38, accept "Stepping Up Security".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
04) Go up in the Inn next to you and turn in "Deadly Desert Venom" 52.39.  Accept "Noggle's Last Hope".
05) Go up in Cenarion Hold and behind the moonwell, turn in "Secret Communication" 49.38.  NOTE:  The repeatable quest "Encrypted Twilight Texts" does not yield XP, only rep with the Cenarion Circle.  But save your additional Encryped Twilight Texts for a future quest.
06) Go to the NW end of Silithus around 27.15 and do "The Twilight Mystery": loot the tablets on the ground.  And grind on Dust Stormers & Whirling Invaders (wind elementals) until you get a total of 30 Core of Elements (used for a later quest), however if you are far off from the 30, you can optionally skip this.
07) All around the middle of Silithus do "Stepping Up Security": kill 20 Dredge Crushers.  Also do part of "Noggle's Last Hope": kill/loot Stonelash Pincers.  Work your way to the next step:
08) South at the small Alliance camp, turn in "Report to Marshal Bluewall" 33.51, accept "Scouring the Desert".  NOTE:  For "Scouring the Desert", keep an eye out for a Silithyst Geyser which looks like a glowing red thing all around Silithuis, click on it and you will get a buff, maintain the buff while bringing it back to the PVP Alliance base and stand in the teleporter looking thing 33.51, then turn the quest in next to you at Marshal Bluewall 33.51.

09) Grind south to Bronzebeard Encampment and turn in "Brann Bronzebeard's Lost Letter" 41.88.  Also at this same NPC, talk to him to do part of "Dearest Natalia".
10) Next to you at Frankal Stonebridge 41.89 (sitting down at a tent), finish "Dearest Natalia" by talking to this NPC.
#47


01) All around southern Silithus finish "Noggle's Last Hope": kill/loot Stonelash Flayers (scorpids) and Rock Stalker (spiders).
02) Grind north to Cenarion Hold and just outside of the Inn, turn in "Stepping Up Security" 51.38.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) Next to you, go up in the Inn and turn in "Noggle's Last Hope" 52.39.  Then next to you, accept "Noggle's Lost Satchel".
04) Go up in Cenarion Hold and turn in "The Twilight Mystery" 50.37, accept "The Deserter".
05) Go in the building next to you and turn in "Dearest Natalia" 49.34, SKIP "Into The Maw of Madness" (group quest).
06) Go SE to Ortell's Hideout (small cave) and turn in "The Deserter" 67.70, accept "The Twilight Lexicon".
07) Grind NW to Twilight Base Camp and do "The Twilight Lexicon": kill/loot the 3 named mobs.  First one is Twilight Keeper Havunth /target.  This orc patrols around the Twilight Base Camp 42.44.  Also IF YOU ARE LVL 60 start working on "Twilight Geolords" by killing the Twilight Geolords (and you will see more of these mobs in the next 2 camps coming up).  See next steps for the other two:
08) Grind west to Twilight Post and kill/loot Twilight Keeper Mayna /target (female human 26.37).  NOTE:  Feel free to grind on the Twilight mobs here to obtain Encryped Twilight Texts.  You will need 10 for a future quest and you will have one more opportunity in the next step to get some more:
09) Go south to Twilight Outpost and kill/loot Twilight Keeper Exeter /target (red armored human with fiery sword 16.86).  NOTES:  You can avoid Hive'Zora & get there quicker by hugging the west side of Silithus mountains.  Also keep grinding on the Twilight mobs until you get 10 Encryped Twilight Texts, this will be used for an easy turn-in quest later.  And IF YOU ARE LVL 60 make sure "Twilight Geolords" is finished now.
10) Go right behind Bronzebeard Encampment to the mountain entrance 43.90 and try to do "Wanted - Deathclasp, Terror of the Sands" (Elite): kill/loot Deathclasp /target.  This level 59 Elite scorpid is soloable but could give you some troubles.  If you cannot solo it, or find a group to help you, then it can be skipped.  (See Video)
11) Also at the same area of Deathclasp, do "Noggle's Lost Satchel": loot Noggle's Satchel (yellow sack on ground 45.91).  (See Video)
#48


01) Grind east to Ortell's Hideout (small cave) and turn in "The Twilight Lexicon" 67.70, accept "A Terrible Purpose".  Also accept, and then turn in "True Believers".  Also this quest is repeatable, but no additional XP.

02) Grind back to Cenarion Hold and up in the Inn turn in "Noggle's Lost Satchel" 52.39.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

03) Go up in Cenarion Hold and in the building, turn in "A Terrible Purpose" 49.34, SKIP "Into The Maw of Madness" (requires group).  Also, IF YOU ARE LVL 60 turn in "Twilight Geolords" 49.38 (by the moonwell), NOTE: "Vyral the Vile" is a solable quest that can be done, but wont be covered here.

04) As you exit the building, make a left and go all the way to the top of the tower and turn in (if you did it) "Wanted - Deathclasp, Terror of the Sands" 51.34.

05) Drop down and at the Hippogryph Master 51.34 fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
06) NW in Gadgetzan, in the hut, turn in "Bungle in the Jungle" 51.27.
07) Hearth to Ironforge.
08) At Ironforge, go to the bank 35.61 and get the following items out: Sacred Highborne Writings, 3 Toxic Horror Droplet, Tinkee's Letter, Extended Annals of Darrowshire and (if you still need to) 30+ Bone Fragments.  NOTE:  30 Core of Elements could already be in your inventory, if not get them out too (if you have them).  You can put into the bank any remaining Encrypted Twilight Text you may have.
09) This is the last time I will remind you to turn in any cloth donations for Ironforge that you may have.  These are found from the Dwarf faction from Mistina Steelshield 43.32 and cloth donations for the Gnome faction from Bubulo Acerbus 74.48.
10) Go north to Hall of Explorers (at The Library) and turn in "A Little Slime Goes a Long Way" 76.23.
11) Go to the Gryphon Master 55.48 in the middle of Ironforge and fly to Light's Hope Chapel, Eastern Plaguelands.
#49

 
59-59 Eastern Plaguelands (log)
01) At Light's Hope Chapel, next to you when you land, by the tent, turn in "Brother Carlin" 76.54 , accept "Villains of Darrowshire".  NOTE:  "Heroes of Darrowshire" (and all follow up quests) is a quest chain you could do if you have a group, and it's worth doing, especially later when you get the chance.
02) Next to you from Quartermaster Miranda Breechlock <The Argent Dawn> 76.54, you can re-stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits if you need to.

03) Go in the Inn and from the female human 76.52 sitting at the table, (if you have the 30 Core of Elements) complete The Elemental Equation.

04) (If you still need to)  Next to you by the entrance, (with 30 Bone Fragments), accept and turn in "Bonescythe Digs" 76.53.

05) Go NW (just below Quel'Lithien Lodge 49.18) and turn in "Fragments of the Past", SKIP the next part.
06) Go south to Blackwood Lake and do part of "Villains of Darrowshire": loot Horgus' Skull under the water (look for the purple glow 47.44).
07) Go south to The Infectis Scar and do the last part of "Villains of Darrowshire": loot Shattered Sword of Marduk 49.60.  Look for the pink glow.
08) Go back to Light's Hope Chapel at Carlin Redpath and turn in "Villains of Darrowshire" ( 76.54, #1 on map).

09) Go to the Gryphon Master 76.53 and fly to Menethil Harbor, Wetlands.  NOTE:  If your Hearthstone cooldown is almost up (less than 15 minutes), then go grind on any mobs in EPL (or take a break) until you are able to hearth to Ironforge, then fly to Menethil Harbor.
#50

 

01) At Menethil Harbor, go on the docks and get on the southern boat 5.64 to take you to Theramore.
02) At Theramore go to the Gryphon Master 67.51 and fly to Moonglade.
03) Once in Moonglade, go to Nighthaven and at Umber, turn in "Umber, Archivist" 45.36 or could be at 48.40, accept "Uncovering Past Secrets".
04) Go into Nighthaven and turn in (if you have it) "A Reliquary of Purity" 52.45, SKIP "Shards of the Felvine" (Dungeon).  Also turn in "Uncovering Past Secrets".
05) Go back to Umber at Nighthaven and complete "Under the Chitin Was..." (48.40 or could be at 45.36, #3 on map).
06) Exit Moonglade at the SW path to go to Timbermaw Hold (36.72 in Moonglade).
07) In Timbermaw Hold, make a right after the stairs (NOT the bridge) to go into Winterspring..
#51

 
59-60 Winterspring (log)
01) Go south to Donova Snowden (31.45 in Winterspring) and turn in "Toxic Horrors", accept "Winterfall Runners".
02) Try to do "Winterfall Runners": try to find the pack of Winterfall Runners /target (three lvl 57s) that patrols the main path from the Timbermaw Hold cave 28.34 to Winterfall Village 68.38 and kill/loot them for the Winterfall Crate.  See map for exact patrol path.  If after making one pass and you cannot find these mobs, then this can be skipped for now.
03) If (or when ever) you have completed "Winterfall Runners", go back to Donova Snowden (31.45, #1 on map) and turn it in.  Accept "High Chief Winterfall" .  NOTE:  My guide will not cover "High Chief Winterfall".
04) Frostsaber Mounts Only (NO XP):  Follow the main path east, then north to Frostsaber Rock and up on the ice ledge, accept "Frostsaber Provisions" 50.10.  NOTE:  This is a repeatable quest that only yields rep points that can eventually allow you to purchase unique Frostsaber mounts from him.
05) Go to Everlook while starting on (if you have it) "Frostsaber Provisions": kill/loot bears and chimera.
06) Just to your left as you enter Everlook (next to the hut) turn in "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38.  Then complete (if you have the engineering profession) "A Yeti of Your Own".
07) From Felnok Steelspring under the blue tent 62.39, turn in "Felnok Steelspring", accept "Chillwind Horns".
08) Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, accept "Luck Be With You" (Elite).  And accept (if you don't have it) "Ursius of the Shardtooth" OR (if available) "Brumeran of the Chillwind".
09) Go in the Inn and make Everlook your new home 61.39.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) You can Bank 61.37 any remaining Core of Elements, Bone Fragments and Insignias you may have.
#52

 

01) As you do the rest of this section, start working on "Chillwind Horns" (kill/loot chimera) and continue working on (if you have it) "Frostsaber Provisions" (kill/loot bears & chimera) along the way, also (if you still need to) keep an eye out for a bear named Ursius /target (he looks just like all other other ones), if so, kill/loot him for "Ursius of the Shardtooth" OR if you have (instead) "Brumeran of the Chillwind" then keep an eye out for Brumeran /target (lvl 58 Chimera) and kill it.:
02) Go to the entrance of the Owl Wing Thicket and from Ranshalla 63.59, turn in "Find Ranshalla".  Then accept and do the escort "Guardians of the Altar": follow Ranshalla through the Owl Wing Thicket and click on the 5 Fire of Elune torches (one in each cave).  Then click on the Altar of Elune at the end of the escort.
03) Go south to Frostwhisper Gorge (entrance 59.66, you can even just go directly south from the escort) and start working on "Luck Be With You" (Elite): loot Frostmaul Shards.  Just try to distract the giants to loot the shards.  Try to sneak in a take as much as you can, you can return here later to get more.
04) Grind north to Everlook.
05) In Everlook, by the cage 62.38, and turn in (if its done) "Luck Be With You", then accept and turn in "Cache of Mau'ari".  NOTE:  The Cache of Mau'ari allows you to turn in E'kos for a temporary stat boost as long as the item remains in your bags.  Also turn in (if you did any of them) "Ursius of the Shardtooth" OR "Brumeran of the Chillwind". 
06) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 62.38.
