07) North of Everlook around 56.21 finish "Chillwind Horns" (kill/loot chimera) and (if you have it) "Frostsaber Provisions" (kill/loot bears and chimera).
08) Frostsaber Mounts Only (NO XP):  Go north to Frostsaber Rock and up on the ice ledge, turn in "Frostsaber Provisions" 50.10.  NOTE:  This is a repeatable quest that only yields rep points that can eventually allow you to purchase unique Frostsaber mounts from him.
#53

 

01) Die on purpose to get back to Everlook.
02) In Everlook, from Felnok Steelspring under the blue tent 62.39, turn in "Chillwind Horns", accept "Return to Tinkee".  NOTE:  My guide will not cover "Return to Tinkee", but you can turn this in next time you head to Burning Steppes.
03) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 62.38.

04) Exit Everlook to your right and go to the Hippogryph Master 62.37 to fly to Rut'theran Village, Teldrassil.
05) At Rut'theran Village, go in the house and turn in "Guardians of the Altar" 55.92, accept "Wildkin of Elune".
06) Go up the path through the red portal 56.89 to get to Darnassus.
07) Go north to Cenarion Enclave to the top of the tree tower and turn in "Wildkin of Elune" 35.9.
08) While in Darnassus, any cloth donations are turned in at 64.23 for the Night Elf faction.
09) Hearth to Everlook.
10) If you still need to, finish "Luck Be With You": go south to Frostwhisper Gorge entrance 59.66 and loot Frostmaul Shards.  Distract the elite giants and sneak in to loot the shards.  Then turn this in at Everlook once finished.
11) At this point you should be very close to level 60.  I usually grind the rest of the way to level 60 at the Owl Wing Thicket entrance 64.59.  But here is a list of other things you could do instead of the owl grinding:
#54

 
Here are the things you can do to level from 59-60:
-->  You can go back into Eastern Plaguelands and do the many group quests that you couldn't solo.
-->  You can grind away at mobs in Andorhal to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn.
-->  You can grind away at Deadwood furbolgs up north in Felwood to gain rep with the Timbermaw.
-->  You can do Black Rock Depths Instance and all the quests associated with it.
-->  You can also do Stratholme Instance and all the quests associated with that.
-->  You can simply grind on any mob you like until you hit 60.
HORDE
Orc-Troll

1-6 Valley of Trials (Durotar)
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
And pick the Version you are playing on (Era, WotLK, or SoM)
01) Right in front of you, accept "Your Place In The World" 43.69.
02) West at The Den entrance, turn in "Your Place In The World" 42.68, accept "Cutting Teeth".
03) Start working on "Cutting Teeth" by killing the Mottled Boars all around the area.  Work your way to accept "Sarkoth" 41.63.
04) Go south a bit and do "Sarkoth": kill/loot the scorpid Sarkoth /target patrols a bit 41.67.
05) Turn in "Sarkoth" (41.63 #3 on map), accept the next part.
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
06) Make sure "Cutting Teeth" is complete (kill Mottled Boars, #3 on map), then go back to Valley of Trails and get resupplied 43.67.
07) Go to The Den entrance and turn in "Cutting Teeth" and "Sarkoth" (42.68, #2 on map), accept "Sting of the Scorpid" and .
08) Just outside of The Den, next to the wagon, accept "Vile Familiars" 43.69.
09) By the cauldron / cooking area, accept "Galgar's Cactus Apple Surprise" 43.67.  Make sure you get resupplied here.
10) East a bit, accept "Lazy Peons" 45.69 (requires lvl 3).
11) Around the starting area, do:  "Galgar's Cactus Apple Surprise", "Sting of the Scorpid", "Lazy Peons" .  Pick up Cactus Apples, kill/loot Scorpid Workers, use the Foreman's Blackjack quest item on the Lazy Peons /target. 
12) Once everything is done, go turn in "Galgar's Cactus Apple Surprise" (at the cooking area 43.67, #9 on map).  Then make sure you get resupplied (next to you).
13) In The Den entrance, turn in "Sting of the Scorpid" (42.68, #2 on map).
14)
15) Go east a bit and turn in "Lazy Peons" (45.69, #10 on map), accept "Thazz'ril's Pick".
16) Go north inside the cave entrance at 45.56 and do "Burning Blade Medallion" and "Thazz'ril's Pick": keep hugging the right side of the cave until you get to Yarrog Baneshadow /target 43.53 and kill/loot him.  Then drop down and keep hugging the left side of the cave until you get to Thazz'ril's Pick 44.54, and loot it.
17) Make sure you are at least level 5, grind in the cave until you are, then use your Hearthstone to go back to the Valley of Trials and then by the wagon, turn in "Burning Blade Medallion" (43.69, #8 on map), accept "Report to Sen'jin Village".
18) Go east a bit and turn in "Thazz'ril's Pick" (45.69, #10 on map).
19) Leave starting noob zone by heading east on the path...
#01

 
6-8 Durotar (log)
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Follow the main path just east and accept "A Peon's Burden" 52.68.
02) Go SE towards Sen'jin Village and then accept "Thwarting Kolkar Aggression" (NPC /target patrols around 54.74 and hides behind rocks and trees).
03) At Sen'jen Village 56.75, turn in "Report to Sen'jin Village", accept all quests there: "Minshina's Skull", "Report to Orgnil" and "Zalazane".  Next to you, accept "A solvent Spirit" 56.74.  Then next to you up in the little hut, accept "Practical Prey" 56.74.  You can get resupplied by the dancing male troll 56.74 by the Raging Bonfire.

04) Run up north to Razor Hill.  Once there, on your left, turn in "Report to Orgnil" 52.43, accept "Dark Storms".  In the burrow next to you 52.43, accept "Vanquish the Betrayers" and "Encroachment".
05) Go up to the top of the watchtower NW of Razor Hill, (up at 50.40) and accept "Carry Your Weight".
06) Drop down SE and accept "Break a Few Eggs" (at the cooking area, 51.43).
07) Go in the Inn next to you and turn in "A Peon's Burden" 52.42.  And also make Razor Hill your new home for your Hearthstone.  Make sure you are resupplied.
08) If you have an extra silver to spare:  in the Barracks, get Apprentice First Aid from Rawrk 54.42.
09) Go SE to Tiragarde Keep and do "Vanquish the Betrayers" along with "Carry Your Weight": kill/loot the humans around and in the fortress, also work your way to the top of the fortress and kill/loot Lieutenant Benedict 60.58.  Once you loot the key from his corpse, then go up the other steps to open Benedict's Chest up at 59.58, to accept "The Admiral's Orders" from the Aged Envelope inside.
10) Once done, die on purpose at the NW side of the fortress and res at spirit to get back to Razor Hill and up in the burrow, turn in "Vanquish the Betrayers" (52.44, #4 on map), accept "From The Wreckage....".  Also turn in "The Admiral's Orders", accept the next part.
11) Go up to the top of the watchtower NW of Razor Hill, (up at 50.40, #5 on map) and turn in "Carry Your Weight".
12) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 52.41.
13) Go east to the beach and under the water around 60.44 do "A solvent Spirit" along with "From The Wreckage....": kill/loot the crabs and makrura mobs and loot the Gnomish Toolboxes.
14) Go just SW of Razor Hill around 49.49 and do the first half of "Encroachment": kill the Razormane Quilboars and Razormane Scouts.
15) Then go to Razor Hill and at the top of the burrow turn in "From The Wreckage...." (52.44, #4 on map).   Also make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

17) If you have not learned Apprentice First Aid, then get that now in the Barracks from Rawrk 54.42.
#02

 
8-10 Durotar (log)
01) Run way down south to Kolkar Crag (entrance at 51.79) and do "Thwarting Kolkar Aggression": click on the attack plans (scrolls) on the ground.  Follow the steps below to find all the scrolls...
02) Click on Attack Plan: Valley of Trials 50.81.
03) Click on Attack Plan: Sen'jin Village 48.77.
04) Click on Attack Plan: Orgrimmar 46.79.
05) Go east towards Sen'jin Village to the NPC /target that patrols and hides around tress/rocks around 54.76 and turn in "Thwarting Kolkar Aggression".
06) Go to Sen'jin Village and turn in "A solvent Spirit" 56.74.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
07) Go to Echo Isles (the islands SE of Durotar) and do the following 4 quests together "Break a Few Eggs", "Practical Prey", "Minshina's Skull" and "Zalazane": kill/loot Durotar Tigers, pickup Taillasher Eggs, and (at 67.88) pickup Minshina's Skull (on the little hill with candles around it) and in that same area kill the needed trolls and kill/loot Zalazane /target (See Video).
08) After completing all these quests, die on purpose (then res) to get to Sen'jin Village quickly, and turn in "Minshina's Skull" and "Zalazane" 56.75.  NOTE:  Save the quest reward item Faintly Glowing Skull for a later quest.
09) In the little hut next to you, turn in "Practical Prey" 56.74.
10) Hearth to Razor Hill and turn in "Break a Few Eggs" (by the cooking area next to the Inn, 51.43).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
11) Go west of Razor Hill around 44.40 and do the second half of "Encroachment": kill the Razormane Dustrunners and Razormane Battleguards.
12) Grind north to the little house 43.30 and accept "Lost But Not Forgotten".
13) Go NE to the goblin and accept "Winds in the Desert" 46.23.
14) Go east a bit 49.22 and do "Winds in the Desert": loot Stolen Supply Sacks around the harpies.
15) Go back to the goblin and turn in "Winds in the Desert" (46.23, #13 on map), accept "Securing the Lines".
16) Keep grinding on any mob in the area until you are at least 1.5 bars away from level 10, then run back to Razor Hill [Don't Hearth] (or you can die on purpose south in the Razorwind Canyon harpy camp at about 48.34).

17) At the top of the burrow turn in "Encroachment" 52.43.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
18) On the west side in Razor Hill, accept "Conscript of the Horde" 51.44.
#03

Crossroads (The Barrens)
01) Go west across the river and next to the path, turn in "Conscript of the Horde" (62.19 in The Barrens), accept "Crossroads Conscription".

03) Head safely to XRs and accept "Meats to Orgrimmar" 53.30.
04) At Sergra Darkthorn, turn in "Crossroads Conscription" 52.31.  Accept "Plainstrider Menace".
05) South in XRs accept "The Forgotten Pools" 52.32.  Then next to you, get repaired/resupplied from the Clothier.
06) Right outside the Inn and next to the mailbox, accept "Raptor Thieves" 52.30.
07) At the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower accept "Disrupt the Attacks" 52.31, and "Supplies for the Crossroads".
08) Go north a bit to the undead under the red canopy and accept "Fungal Spores" and "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" 51.30.
09) Next to you at the Wind Rider Master 52.30 turn in "Meats to Orgrimmar", accept "Ride to Orgrimmar".  Then take a flight to Orgrimmar, Durotar.
#05

Orgrimmar
02) Stay up (cross the bridge), then Go north to Thrall and accept "Hidden Enemies" 32.38.  In that room turn in "The Admiral's Orders".
#06

10-12 Durotar
01) and do "Securing the Lines": kill the harpies (through the other side of the small cave 52.28 in Durotar).
02) At the goblin, turn in "Securing the Lines" 46.23.
03) Along the river west of Durotar 37.21 do "Lost But Not Forgotten": kill/loot the Dreadmaw Crocolisk until Kron's Amulet drops.
04) Enter Thunder Ridge (at 39.31)...
05) In Thunder Ridge, do "Dark Storms": work your way to Fizzle Darkstorm (a goblin at 42.27, /target) and kill/loot him.  Be careful he is heavily guarded..
06) Exit Thunder Ridge, then go east to the little house and turn in "Lost But Not Forgotten" 43.30.
07) Go SE to Razor Hill and turn in "Dark Storms" (52.43).  Accept "Margoz".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  ALSO:  If you destroyed your Hearthstone earlier, then get a new one in Razor Hill's Inn 52.42 by making it your new home.  If for some reason you are already level 12, get new spells/abilities while in town.
08) Go NE and turn in "Margoz" 56.20, accept "Skull Rock".
09) Go north to enter Skull Rock (cave entrance at 55.10) and do "Skull Rock" along with "Hidden Enemies": kill/loot the mobs in the cave.  If you can, try to kill Gazz'uz /target (has multiple spawn locations in cave), he drops Eye of Burning Shadow item which starts "Burning Shadows". TIPS: use the quest reward item Faintly Glowing Skull to help you kill him, he's tough without it.  Also use a Sticky Glue on his voidwalker, or pull Gazz in a water pool since his voidwalker cant swim.  If you are an Orc, don't use Blood Fury before you have used your healing items.  Try to party up with somebody if you can. (See Video on how I killed Gazz'uz).  Also don't leave this cave until you have grinded out First Aid (50) to learn Journeyman First Aid soon.
10) Exit the cave and go directly south to turn in "Skull Rock" (56.20, #8 on map).  Accept "Neeru Fireblade".
#10

 
Orgrimmar
01) Go into Orgrimmar and go to the Inn and turn in "Ride to Orgrimmar" 54.68, accept "Doras the Wind Rider Master".

02) Go up to the Wind Rider Master 45.64 and turn in "Doras the Wind Rider Master", accept "Return to the Crossroads.".

03) Go north to Thrall and turn in "Hidden Enemies" 32.38.  Accept the next part.
04) Go to Cleft of Shadows (entrance at 42.57 in Orgrimmar).
05) In Cleft of Shadows 50.51, turn in "Neeru Fireblade" (accept "Ak'Zeloth") and (if you have it) "Burning Shadows".  Also keep talking to this NPC to complete "Hidden Enemies".
06) Go back to Thrall (32.38, #3 on map) and turn in "Hidden Enemies", SKIP the next part (Dungeon).  NOTE: You can destroy the Lieutenant's Insignia, as its not used for anything else.
07) Hearth (or run) to Razor Hill. Make sure you are level 12 (grind on any mobs until you are).  Make sure you get repaired/resupplied.  Upgrade First Aid in the Barracks from Rawrk 54.42 to learn Journeyman First Aid. 
08) Run west into the Barrens...
#11

Levels 12-15 Barrens (log)
01) Over the bridge, next to the watchtower, turn in (if you have it from the Durotar section) "Ak'Zeloth" 62.20, accept "The Demon Seed".  Then loot a Flawed Power Stone from the table next to you.  NOTE: The Flawed Power Stone item received only lasts 30 minutes, so you need to do the next few steps somewhat quickly (but you should have plenty of time).
02) While working on the following steps, also start working on "Plainstrider Menace" and "Raptor Thieves" by killing plainstriders and raptors all around the fields of the Barrens, but don't make it your main priority:
03) Go west across the main path, then go up the big mountain ramp (begins at 51.22).
04) Once you get at the top do "The Demon Seed": go in the small cave and use your Flawed Power Stone quest item on The Altar of Fire 48.19.  NOTES:  Watch out for a rare elite named Rathorian, he is very dangerous.  If he is in the way, then either try to team up with someone to help you kill him, or this quest can be skipped.  His respawn is every 5.5 hours.
05) Go east and do "Disrupt the Attacks": kill the required amount of Razormane quilboars around 54.27.  ALSO: Back in the Razormane camp is a barrel called Chen's Empty Keg keg 56.27, loot it and accept the quest from the item received.
06) Go to the NE side of XRs and turn in "Return to the Crossroads." 53.30.
07) Turn in (if it's complete) "Plainstrider Menace" (from Sergra Darkthorn 52.31), accept "The Zhevra".
08) Go in the Inn and make XRs your new home 52.30.
09) At the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower, turn in "Disrupt the Attacks" (52.31, #7 on map).  Accept "The Disruption Ends".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) Go up the watchtower next to you and accept "Harpy Raiders".
#12

Tauren


1-6 Mulgore
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, WotLK, or SoM)
01) Right in front of you, accept "The Hunt Begins" 45.77.
02) In the big hut, accept "A Humble Task" 44.76.
03) Start working on "The Hunt Begins" by kill/looting Plainstriders as you go SE to turn in "A Humble Task" 50.81, accept the next part.
04) Do "A Humble Task": next to you pick up a Water Pitcher 50.81 on the well (next to the quest giver).
05) Finish "The Hunt Begins" by kill/looting Plainstriders (#3 on map) as you head back to Camp Narache 45.77.
06) Go back to Camp Narache and turn in "The Hunt Begins" 45.77, accept "The Hunt Continues" and Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
07) Stop at the General Goods 45.77 vendor (in small hut) to sell junk to gain money.
09) In the big hut, turn in "A Humble Task" 44.76, accept "Rites of the Earthmother".
10) Go south and turn in "Rites of the Earthmother" 43.92, accept "Rite of Strength".  Also start working on "The Hunt Continues" by kill/looting any Mountain Cougars (cats) you see along the way.
11) Do "The Hunt Continues": kill/loot Mountain Cougars around the mountain slopes around 45.91.
12) Grind your way back to Camp Narache (make sure you are at least 3 bars away from level 4) and then turn in "The Hunt Continues" 45.77, accept "The Battleboars".  Also accept (from the NPC patrolling around) "Break Sharptusk!".
13) Stop at the General Goods 45.77 vendor (in small hut) to sell junk to gain money.
14) Get new spells/abilities.
15) Go to the area around 57.86 and do most of "The Battleboars" by kill/looting Battleboars.  Get about half (4/8 of each item needed), then continue on..
16) Go to the cave at entrance 58.85 while continue killing Battleboars along the way for "The Battleboars".  Once you get to the cave, then go to the next step..
17) Grind your way to the big tent while kill/looting Bristlebacks for "Rite of Strength".  Once you get to the tent, kill/loot Chief Sharptusk Thornmantle /target 65.78 for "Break Sharptusk!". 
18) Then grind to the small cave and on the inside is a Dirt-stained Map 63.83.  Loot it, then use the item to start "Attack on Camp Narache".
19) Make sure all other quests are complete then Hearth to Camp Narache and turn in all quests: (in front of you) "The Battleboars" 45.77, (from patrolling NPC) "Break Sharptusk!", (in hut 44.76) "Attack on Camp Narache" and "Rite of Strength" then accept "Rites of the Earthmother" (part 2).
20) In the tent, get repaired/resupplied 44.77.
21) Go west on the path and accept "A Task Unfinished" 39.82.
22) The starting noob zone is complete, continue following the road to Bloodhoof Village 47.62 while grinding mobs along the way and make sure you are level 6 before getting there for new spells/abilities.  Once you hit level 6, you can then die on purpose and res at spirit healer to get you to Bloodhoof Village faster (or just run there if you are close).
#01

 
6-8 Mulgore (log)
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Once at Bloodhoof Village accept "Dangers of the Windfury" 47.62.
02) Go into the Inn (entrance is at 47.61) and from the First Aid Trainer train Apprentice First Aid.  And talk to the Innkeeper 47.61 to turn in "A Task Unfinished" and to make Bloodhoof Village your new home for your hearthstone.
03) Just outside of the inn 48.60, turn in "Rites of the Earthmother" and accept "Sharing the Land", "Dwarven Digging" and "Rite of Vision".
04) East a bit accept "Poison Water" 49.60.
05) Go in the hut (next to you) and accept "Swoop Hunting" 49.59.
06) North a bit by the fire turn in "Rite of Vision" 48.58, accept the next part.
07) West a bit accept "Mazzranache" 47.57.
08) Get new spells/abilities for your class. Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!.
09) Go east of the main path and do "Rite of Vision": loot Ambercorns around the trees and loot Well Stones (around the well at 54.66).  Grind any mobs you see along the way.
10) All around the area, start doing "Poison Water", "Swoop Hunting" and "Mazzranache": kill & loot Swoops, Wolves, Plainstriders and Cougars.  Work your way south to do the next steps as well:
11) Go south a bit and do "Sharing the Land" around 52.70.  NOTE: There are several camps of these mobs and the Poachers are only in certain camps.
12) Go east a bit and do "Dangers of the Windfury" 62.71: kill and loot the harpies.
13) Find Morin Cloudstalker /target along the path (patrols around 54.60) and accept "The Ravaged Caravan".
14) Go north to the ravaged caravan 54.48 and do "The Ravaged Caravan": open the Sealed Supply Crate to turn the quest in, accept the next part.
15) Make sure all quests are complete (besides "Dwarven Digging" and "Swoop Hunting"), then Hearth (or die on purpose) to go back to Bloodhoof Village and turn in "Sharing the Land" 48.60.
16) South a bit turn in "Dangers of the Windfury" 47.62.
17) NE a bit turn in "Poison Water" 49.60, accept "Winterhoof Cleansing".
18) If you completed "Swoop Hunting" turn it in next to you in the hut 49.59.
19) North a bit by the fire and turn in "Rite of Vision" 48.58, accept the next "Rite of Wisdom".  NOTE:  Don't bother using  Water of the Seers quest item.
20) West a bit and turn in "Mazzranache" 47.57.  Make sure you are all repaired, supplied and new spells/abilities. Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
#02

 
8-10 Mulgore (log)
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Go to Morin Cloudstalker /target along the main path going east around 54.60 and turn in "The Ravaged Caravan", accept "The Venture Co." and "Supervisor Fizsprocket".
02) Go south a bit and do "Winterhoof Cleansing": use the Winterhoof Cleansing Totem quest item on the well 54.66.
03) Go NW to Bloodhoof Village and turn in "Winterhoof Cleansing" 49.60, accept "Thunderhorn Totem".
04) While focusing on the next steps, also start working on "Thunderhorn Totem" by kill/looting Prairie Stalkers (wolves) and Flatland Cougars (cats)...
05) Go NW and start working on "Dwarven Digging" around 33.49: find Prospector's Picks and use them at a Forge.  NOTE:  Without grouping, it's hard to get to the Forge there 31.50.  Instead, there will be other easier Forges later on in the guide you can use.  For now, just get 5 Prospector's Picks.  The diggers are easier to kill solo, and taurens can use their War Stomp ability to prevent the Appraiser's from healing themselves.
06) Go up north (past the harpy camp) to the cave 33.36 and turn in "Rite of Vision", accept "Rite of Wisdom".
07) Work your way east and accept "A Sacred Burial" 60.26.
08) Work your way to the Ancestral Spirit 61.21 and turn in "Rite of Wisdom" accept "Journey into Thunder Bluff".  Keep the next step in mind:
09) In the Immediate area around Red Rocks do "A Sacred Burial": kill 8 Bristleback Interlopers (#8 on map).
10) Go SW a bit and turn in "A Sacred Burial" (60.26, #7 on map)
11) Finish up "Thunderhorn Totem": kill/loot Prairie Stalkers and Flatland Cougars (around the middle of Mulgore around 52.35).
12) If you are not level 10 (or at least 1 bar away from it), grind until you are,
13) Right outside of the Inn, accept (if you are lvl 10) "The Hunter's Way" 47.60.  If you're not lvl 10 do the next step before this one:
14) East turn in "Thunderhorn Totem" 49.60, accept "Thunderhorn Cleansing".  Also turn in (if you need to) "Swoop Hunting" (in the tent next to you 49.59).
15) Get new spells/abilities and accept your class's level 10 quests.  NOTE:  You can destroy the  Water of the Seers item to free bag space. 
16) Go north and do "Thunderhorn Cleansing": use the Thunderhorn Cleansing Totem on the well 45.45.
17) Go north to Thunder Bluff (a Lift is at 36.30 in Mulgore), and build up First Aid while waiting for the Lift.
#03

Thunder Bluff (TB) (log)
01) In TB, accept "Preparation for Ceremony" 38.60.
02) Finish up "Dwarven Digging" by using your 5 Prospector's Picks at the Thunder Bluff Forge 40.56.
03) Go up east and turn in "Journey into Thunder Bluff" 60.52, accept "Rites of the Earthmother".
04) Go north and leave TB by taking the Lift down 54.28.
#04

10-12 Mulgore (log)
01) Go SE and do "Supervisor Fizsprocket" along with "The Venture Co.": kill/loot Supervisor Fizsprocket in the cave (entrance 62.47, /target).  Keep hugging the right side of the cave straight through to get to him.
02) Make sure "The Venture Co." is finished before continuing, then die on purpose to get to Bloodhoof Village and turn in "Dwarven Digging" (by the big totem, 48.60).
03) Go east a bit and turn in "Thunderhorn Cleansing" 49.60, accept "Wildmane Totem".
04) Go east and find Morin Cloudstalker /target along the path around 54.60 and turn in "The Venture Co." and "Supervisor Fizsprocket".
05) While doing the following steps up north also focus on doing "The Hunter's Way" along with "Wildmane Totem" by kill/looting the Flatland Prowler & Prairie Wolf Alphas (mostly north of TB):
06) Do "Rites of the Earthmother": kill Arra'chea and loot his horn.  Arra'chea /target is a kodo that patrols clockwise just to the right of TB.  His original spawn point is at 49.19.  If you can't find him in time, then it can be skipped.
07) Work your way to the very north of Mulgore around 55.17 and do "Preparation for Ceremony": kill/loot the harpies (These harpies are in many camps north and NW of Mulgore).
08) Make sure "The Hunter's Way" along with "Wildmane Totem" are finished: kill/loot Flatland Prowlers & Prairie Wolf Alphas (mostly north of TB).
09) Go to the Lift 41.22 to go into TB.  NOTE:  If you are real far away from that, you can die on purpose to get right to the Lift.
#06

 
Thunder Bluff (log)
01) Go to the Elder Rise and accept "The Barrens Oases" 70.29.
02) Turn in (if you did it) "Rites of the Earthmother" 60.52.
03) Go to the Hunter Rise and turn in "The Hunter's Way" 62.81, accept "Sergra Darkthorn".
04) Turn in "Preparation for Ceremony" 38.60.
#07

11-12 Mulgore (log)
01) Hearth to Bloodhoof Village.
02) East a bit and turn in "Wildmane Totem" 49.60, accept "Wildmane Cleansing".
03) If you turned level 12, get your lvl 12 spells/abilities. Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
04) Grind your way north to 47.33 then die on purpose and res at spirit.

05) Go north a bit and do "Wildmane Cleansing": use the Wildmane Cleansing Totem quest item at the well 43.14.
06) Hearth or grind south to 47.38 and die on purpose to get back to Bloodhoof Village.
07) Turn in "Wildmane Cleansing" (49.60, #2 on map).
08) If you haven't got your lvl 12 spells/abilities yet then make sure you are lvl 12 (grind until you are) and get them at Bloodhoof Village. Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
#08

12 The Barrens (log)
01) Run east into the Barrens and get the Flight Path in Camp Taurajo from the Wind Rider Master (44.59 in the Barrens).
02) In the middle of CT accept "Journey to the Crossroads" 45.59.
03) Follow the path north to the Crossroads (XRs) and turn in "The Barrens Oases" (south in XRs, 52.32), accept "The Forgotten Pools".
04) At Sergra Darkthorn, turn in "Sergra Darkthorn" 52.31, accept "Plainstrider Menace".
05) Go in the Inn and make XRs your new home 52.30.
06) Right outside the Inn on your right accept "Raptor Thieves" 52.30.
07) At the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower, turn in "Journey to the Crossroads" 52.31, accept "Disrupt the Attacks" and "Supplies for the Crossroads".
08) Go up the watchtower next to you and accept "Harpy Raiders" 52.31.
09) Drop down, and right next to the Wind Rider Master 51.30 accept "Fungal Spores" and "Wharfmaster Dizzywig".
10) NW in XRs, accept "A Bundle of Hides" 51.29.
11) At the Wind Rider Master 51.30, turn in "A Bundle of Hides", SKIP "Ride to Thunder Bluff" (for now).
12) While working on the following steps, also start working on "Plainstrider Menace" and "Raptor Thieves" by killing plainstriders and raptors all around the fields of the Barrens, but don't make it your main priority:
13) Go NE and do "Disrupt the Attacks": kill the needed Razormane mobs around 54.27.  ALSO: Back in the Razormane camp is a barrel called Chen's Empty Keg 56.27, loot it and accept the quest from the item received.

14) Go back SW to XRs and at the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower turn in "Disrupt the Attacks" (52.31, #7 on map).  Accept "The Disruption Ends".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
#10


Undead – Blood Elf

1-6 DeathKnell (Tirisfal Glades)
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, WotLK, or SoM)
00) First thing to do is to destroy your Hearthstone in your Backpack.  NOTE: The Hearthstone is not needed right now and you will get a new one later.  This will give you an extra bag space.
01) Right in front of you accept "Rude Awakening" 30.72.

02) Go in the church building and turn in "Rude Awakening" 31.66, accept "The Mindless Ones".

03) Go down the hill a bit around 32.63 and do "The Mindless Ones": kill the needed zombies.
04) Go back to the church (31.66, #2 on map) and turn in "The Mindless Ones".  Accept and "Rattling the Rattlecages".  Next to you accept "The Damned" 31.66.
05) Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!".

06) Do "The Damned" along with "Rattling the Rattlecages": kill Rattlecage Skeletons /target (in the village area) and kill/loot wolves and bats all around the outside of the village area.  Both are around 32.60.
07) Go back to the church and turn in "The Damned". (31.66, #2 on map).  Accept "Marla's Last Wish".  Next to you, turn in "Rattling the Rattlecages".
08) Right outside the church (from the patrolling NPC) accept "Scavenging Deathknell" patrols a bit 32.66, and "Night Web's Hollow" 32.66.
09) Do "Scavenging Deathknell": pick up the Equipment Boxes in and around the village buildings around 32.64.
10) Go NW and do "Night Web's Hollow": kill the Young Night Web Spiders outside of the mine and the Night Web Spiders inside the mine (mine entrance at 27.59).
11) Die on purpose and res at spirit.

12) Turn in "Scavenging Deathknell" 32.66.
13) Make sure you are resupplied 32.65.
14) Turn in "Night Web's Hollow" 32.66, accept "The Scarlet Crusade".
15) Go east of the village around 37.67 and do "The Scarlet Crusade": kill/loot the Scarlet humans.
16) Go north a bit and do part of "Marla's Last Wish": kill/loot the zombie Samuel Flipps 37.62 /target.
17) Die on purpose (res at spirit), then next to you in the graveyard do the last part of "Marla's Last Wish": click on Marla's Grave 31.65 (dirt mound).
18) In the church, turn in "Marla's Last Wish" (31.66, #2 on map).
19) Just outside, turn in "The Scarlet Crusade" 32.66, accept "The Red Messenger".
20) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 32.65.
21) Go east and do "The Red Messenger": kill/loot Meven Korgal 37.69 /target.  He is guarded by a few other mobs, but it can be soloed.
22) Back west, turn in "The Red Messenger" 32.66, accept "Vital Intelligence". 
23) Follow the path NE out of Deathknell and accept "A Rogue's Deal" 38.57.  The starting noob zone is complete.
#01

 
6-10 Tirisfal Glades (log)
Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
01) Down the path at the intersection, accept "Fields of Grief" 41.54.
02) Run east to Brill 58.51 along the main path, grinding mobs along the way, and make sure you get to level 6 before you get there (for new spells/abilities).  Along the way on the path accept "Gordo's Task" (from the abomination Gordo /target).
03) Once at Brill, in front of the graveyard entrance, accept "A Putrid Task" 58.51.
04) Go in the first house on your right and accept "A New Plague" 59.52.
05) Just outside of the house pick up Apprentice Herbalist from the Herbalism Trainer /target 60.52.  NOTE: I recommend getting this at least temporarily for helping you find the Gloom Weed and Doom Weeds later in this zone.
06) In front of the Town Hall building, turn in "Vital Intelligence" 61.52.  Accept "At War with the Scarlet Crusade".
07) Right behind this NPC, accept "Wanted: Maggot Eye" 61.51 (from the Wanted! sign).
08) Go in the Town Hall building right next to you and accept "Graverobbers" 61.51.
09) Enter the Inn entrance 62.53 and at the Innkeeper 62.52 turn in "A Rogue's Deal".  Make this Inn your new home for your Hearthstone (by talking to the Innkeeper).  Make sure you are resupplied.
10) In Brill's Inn, get your level 6 spells/abilities.
11) Go just west of Brill and do "Gordo's Task" along with "A Putrid Task": pick up Gloom Weed and kill/loot zombies.  Also, start working on "A New Plague" (kill/loot darkhounds).  TIPS:  There is a merchant in the small house 53.56 that you can sell items to.  Don't forget to turn Find Herbs on to spot the Gloom Weed on mini map.
12) Finish "A New Plague": kill/loot darkhounds (blue dots on map).  Focus on finishing this west around 42.45.
13) Go west and do "At War with the Scarlet Crusade": kill/loot the Scarlet Warriors.  Also start working on "Fields of Grief" by looting Tirisfal Pumpkins you see along the way.  TIP: Work your way along the main path, looting pumpkins you see along the north side of the farm, you will find a few more around the tower around 32.47.  The Scarlet Warriors are north and west of the farm.
14) Finish "Fields of Grief": pickup Tirisfal Pumpkins around the farm 36.51.
15) Die on purpose and res at spirit.

16) In the graveyard next to you around 58.49 turn in "Gordo's Task" (the NPC /target patrols around the grave), accept "Doom Weed".
17) In front of the graveyard entrance turn in "A Putrid Task" (58.51, #3 on map).  Accept "The Mills Overrun".
18) In the small house 59.52 (#4 on map), turn in "Fields of Grief" and "A New Plague", accept the next part to both of these quests.
19) In front of the Town Hall, turn in "At War with the Scarlet Crusade" 61.52 (#6 on map), accept the next part.
20) Next to you accept "Proof of Demise" 61.52.
21) Get resupplied 61.53.
22) Just inside of the Inn entrance 62.53, stop at the First Aid Trainer /target 62.53 and get Apprentice First Aid.  NOTE: If you could not afford this, then get this when you can.
23) Inside the Inn, accept "Deaths in the Family" and "The Haunted Mills" 62.52.
24) Go downstairs in the Inn and turn in "Fields of Grief" 62.51.
25) Go upstairs in the Inn and accept "The Chill of Death" 62.53.
#02

 

01) If you have an extra silver to spare, it's wise to check to see if you can buy any health potions from Selina Weston /target 62.50 behind Brill.  It will come in handy for tough solo quests soon.
02) Go just north above Brill and do "Graverobbers" along with "Doom Weed": kill/loot the Rot Hide Graverobbers, and pickup Doom Weeds all around the area around 55.42.  You will find the Rot Hide Mongrels /target north around the farm 59.36.  Don't forget to turn Find Herbs on to spot the Doom Weeds on mini map.  Keep the next step in mind:
03) First make sure you are level 8, then do "Wanted: Maggot Eye": kill/loot Maggot Eye in the hut 59.31.  TIPS:  This can be hard to solo, make sure you are prepared and use a potion if you have one.  If you cant do it then skip it or try later.
04) Go north to the beach around 60.28 and do "A New Plague": kill/loot the murlocs.  NOTE:  Be careful, these murlocs are not easy.
05) Hearth to Gallows' End Taven (Brill).  Get resupplied, then get your level 8 spells/abilities. Remember to Choose Your Class At Top of Screen!!!
06) In front of the Town Hall, turn in (if you did it) "Wanted: Maggot Eye" 61.52.
07) Go in the Town Hall and turn in "Graverobbers".  Accept "Forsaken Duties" 61.51.
08) Make sure you are all repaired/resupplied 60.53.
09) In the small house 59.52, turn in "A New Plague", accept the next part.
10) Go to the grave and turn in "Doom Weed" (NPC /target patrols around 58.49).
#03

 
NOTE:  While doing this section start working on "The Chill of Death" by kill/looting Duskbats you see along the way:

01) Go NW to Agamand Mills and do "The Mills Overrun" (kill/loot the skeletons around the mill) while also killing the named mobs for "Deaths in the Family" and "The Haunted Mills". Follow the steps below to kill the named mobs for these quests:
02) Kill/loot Devlin Agamand /target, a skeleton warrior patroling around the grassy area around 47.41.
03) Kill/loot Nissa Agamand /target, she is a ghost that patrols around in the house entrance at 50.36.
04) Kill/loot Gregor Agamand /target , a ghoul that patrols around the windmill around 46.29.
05) Kill/loot Thurman Agamand /target, a zombie that patrols between the two windmills next to the cliff around 44.34.
06) Make sure "The Mills Overrun" is finished, then hearth to Gallows' End Taven (Brill).  If you cannot hearth, then die on purpose and res at spirit.  If you died on purpose then do step #10 next before continuing on.
07) In the Inn 62.52 turn in "Deaths in the Family" and "The Haunted Mills", accept "Speak with Sevren".
08) NOTE: While doing the Agamand quests, you may have received an item called A Letter to Yvette, accept "A Letter Undelivered" from it, then turn it inside the Inn, near the entrance 62.53.
09) In the Town Hall turn in "Speak with Sevren" 61.51, accept "The Family Crypt".
10) At the graveyard entrance, turn in "The Mills Overrun" 58.52.
11) Make sure you are now level 10, if not grind until you are.  You can continue to work on "The Chill of Death" by kill/looting bats around Brill until you are.  If you happen to get all 5 Pelts before hitting level 10, then go to the Trade Supplies /target vendor (under the red tent 61.52) and purchase a Coarse Thread, then go upstairs in the Inn and turn in "The Chill of Death" (but it doesn't need to be completed now).
#04

 
10-12 Tirisfal Glades (log)
01) In front of the Town Hall, turn in "At War with the Scarlet Crusade" 61.52, accept the next part.  Also turn in next to you (if it is done) "Proof of Demise".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) If you are still working on "The Chill of Death", then go to the Trade Supplies /target vendor (under the red tent 61.52) and purchase a Coarse Thread (remember not to sell this!)
03) Go SE to the small camp along the main path and turn in "Forsaken Duties" 66.60, accept "Return to the Magistrate" and "Rear Guard Patrol".
04) Go east to Balnir Farmstead around 76.61 and do "Rear Guard Patrol": kill the zombies and spirits around the farm.
05) Go NE a bit and do "At War with the Scarlet Crusade": kill 5 Scarlet Friars, and kill Captain Vachon (in the watchtower 79.56).  NOTE:  "Proof of Demise" does not need to be completed now.
06) Go east and do "A New Plague": kill/loot the spiders around 84.54.  Also, make sure you have all 5 of the Duskbat Pelts for "The Chill of Death".
08) In front of the Town Hall, turn in "Proof of Demise" (if it is done) 61.52, and next to you "At War with the Scarlet Crusade", accept the next part.
#09

 

01) In the small house 59.52, turn in "A New Plague", accept the next part.  Also accept "Delivery to Silverpine Forest".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) Above Agamand Mills, go down in the crypt (entrance at 52.27) and do "The Family Crypt": kill the zombies and ghosts, and kill/loot Captain Dargol, he is at the very bottom of the crypt.  NOTE:  This quest is not easy, but every class should be able to solo it at level 11 with good skill.
03) Die on purpose and res at the spirit, then go in the Town Hall 61.51 and turn in "The Family Crypt" and "Return to the Magistrate".
04) Go in the basement of the Inn and turn in "A New Plague".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) Do "At War with the Scarlet Crusade": kill Captain Melrache and his two bodyguards in the watchtower 79.25.  Also make sure "Proof of Demise" is now finished.
06) Hearth, or run back to Brill and in front of the Town Hall, turn in "At War with the Scarlet Crusade" 61.52 and (if you still need to) "Proof of Demise".
07) At the First Aid Trainer 62.53 just in the entrance of the Inn, learn Heavy Linen Bandage (skill 40), and if possible Journeyman First Aid (skill 50).
09) Make sure you are level 12, grind on any mobs around Brill until you are.  Then go in Brill's Inn and get your level 12 spells/abilities.
#10

 
Levels 12+ Options
Now you have two options on where to go.  You can go to The Barrens, or you can go to Silverpine Forest (next section below).  Overall with WotLK 50% faster quest XP rate, I recommend skipping Silverpine Forest and just jump right into The Barrens guide, otherwise you will be too far ahead after doing Silverpine.  So for WotLK 50% XP rate, I would highly recommend skipping Silverpine and going to The Barrens.

If you decide to go to The Barrens right away, then abandon "Delivery to Silverpine Forest" and follow the steps in this section to get to The Barrens.  Otherwise, skip to the Silverpine Forest 12-15 section below.

To The Barrens Only:
01) Get on the Zeppelin to go to Orgrimmar (61.59 in Tirisfal Glades).   NOTE:  There are two Zeppelins, the eastern one takes you to Stranglethorn Vale and the western one to Orgrimmar.
02) Once in Orgrimmar, get the Flight Path from the Wind Rider Master (up at 45.64).
03) Leave Orgrimmar (south) and run to Razor Hill and on the west side of the town, accept "Conscript of the Horde" (51.44 in Durotar).
04) Then go to the Levels 15 Barrens (Undead) section down below in this guide.
#11

 
Silverpine Forest 12-15 (log)
01) Head south on the main path to enter Silverpine Forest (54.76 in Tirisfal Glades).
02) Just to your right off the main path at Malden's Orchard, in the small house house 56.9, accept and do the escort "Escorting Erland" by escorting Deathstalker Erland SW a bit to The Ivar Patch.  Then turn it in from the NPC 53.13 in front of the big house and accept "The Deathstalkers' Report".  Do NOT accept "Wild Hearts" yet.
03) Die on purpose and res at spirit to get to The Sepulcher.  NOTE:  Save any Discolored Worg Hearts you may have.
04) At the Innkeeper just outside of the crypt/Inn 43.41, make The Sepulcher your new home.
05) Go down in the Inn/crypt and turn in "The Deathstalkers' Report" (down at 43.41), accept "Speak with Renferrel".  Also accept "Lost Deathstalkers" and "The Dead Fields".
06) Exit the crypt and make a sharp left to the NPC 43.41 under the canopy and turn in "Delivery to Silverpine Forest" and "Speak with Renferrel", SKIP "Zinge's Delivery" (for now).  Also accept "A Recipe For Death".
07) At the largest tombstone in the graveyard, accept "Border Crossings" 44.41.
08) Just north from the NPC in purple, accept "Prove Your Worth" 44.40.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
#12

 

01) As you do the following steps also start working on part of "A Recipe For Death" by kill/looting any bears you see along the way:
02) Work your way north and east of the main path around Valgan's Field around 52.25 do "Prove Your Worth": kill 5 Moonrage Whitescalps (worgen).
03) Work your way north to The Ivar Patch and from the NPC 53.13 in front of the big house, turn in "Lost Deathstalkers".  Also accept "Wild Hearts".
04) Around the immediate area do "Wild Hearts": kill/loot worgs.
05) Grind south to The Dead Fields and do "The Dead Fields": keep killing the gnolls until Nightlash /target spawns in the middle of the field 45.21, then kill/loot her.
06) Grind west to The Skittering Dark (watch out for the lvl 24 elite) and do part of "A Recipe For Death": kill/loot the spiders (in and outside of the cave, 36.14, #1 on map).  Keep the next step in mind:
07) You can get resupplied from the Fisherman 33.18 by the shore.  Be careful not to sell your Discolored Worg Hearts!
08) Finish "A Recipe For Death" by kill/looting the rest of bears you need.  You can find a lot of Giant Grizzled Bears around where you are at and Ferocious Grizzled Bears a bit more east.  #1 on map.
09) Die on purpose to get to The Sepulcher.
10) Down in the crypt/inn turn in "The Dead Fields" 43.41, accept "The Decrepit Ferry".
11) Exit the crypt and make a sharp left to the NPC 43.41 under the canopy and turn in "Wild Hearts", accept "Return to Quinn".
12) Just north from the NPC in purple, turn in "Prove Your Worth" 44.40, accept "Arugal's Folly".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
#13

 

01) Go east to the shore and click on the Corpse Laden Boat 58.35 to turn in "The Decrepit Ferry", accept "Rot Hide Clues".
02) Go north to Valgan's Field and do "Arugal's Folly": upstairs in the big house, loot the Dusty Spellbooks 53.29 on the ground.
03) Go north to The Ivar Patch and upstairs in the big house, turn in "Return to Quinn" 53.13.
04) Just outside the house, accept "Ivar the Foul" 53.13.
05) Go in the barn next to you and do "Ivar the Foul": kill/loot Ivar the Foul 51.14.  Be careful there is a few other mobs around this ghoul.
06) Go back to Rane in front of the big house and turn in "Ivar the Foul" (53.13, #3 on map).
07) Die on purpose to get to The Sepulcher.
08) Down in the crypt/inn, turn in "Rot Hide Clues" 43.41, SKIP "Rot Hide Ichor".
09) Exit the crypt, and just north from the NPC in purple, turn in "Arugal's Folly" 44.40, accept the next part.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) Go east to Deep Elem Mine around 56.46 and do "Arugal's Folly": kill/loot Moonrage Glutton and Darksoul (worgen).  You will find more Darksouls in the mine.
11) Go SW to the small Dalaran camp and click on the Dalaran Crate (next to a canopy, 50.60) to turn in "Border Crossings", accept "Maps and Runes".
#14

 

01) At The Sepulcher, outside at the largest tombstone in the graveyard, turn in "Maps and Runes" 44.41, accept "Dalar's Analysis".
02) Just north from the NPC in purple, turn in "Arugal's Folly" 44.40, accept the next part.  And turn in "Dalar's Analysis", accept "Dalaran's Intentions".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) Back at the largest tombstone in the graveyard, turn in "Dalaran's Intentions" 44.41, accept "Ambermill Investigations".
04) Go SE back to Deep Elem Mine and do "Arugal's Folly": enter the mine (entrance at 57.46) and at the end of the mine kill/loot Grimson the Pale.
05) Go south to Ambermill around 56.63 and do "Ambermill Investigations": kill/loot the Dalaran Protectors, Mages and Conjurors for the 8 pendants.
07) At The Sepulcher, next to the Inn/crypt/mailbox, accept "Supplying the Sepulcher" 43.42.
08) Next to the crypt/inn, from the NPC 43.41 under the canopy, accept "Zinge's Delivery".
09) At the largest tombstone in the graveyard, turn in "Ambermill Investigations" 44.41, SKIP "The Weaver".
10) Just north from the NPC in purple, turn in "Arugal's Folly" (44.40, #2 on map), SKIP the next part.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
11) SE in town from the Bat Handler 46.43, turn in "Supplying the Sepulcher", accept "Ride to the Undercity".  Then take a bat ride to Undercity, Tirisfal.
#15

 

01) When you land at the UC, go across the room in the Trade Quarter and turn in "Ride to the Undercity" 61.42, accept "Michael Garrett".
02) Go to the Bat Handler 63.49 and turn in "Michael Garrett", accept "Return to Podrig".  Skip the ride for now.
03) Go SW down in The Apothecarium and turn in "A Recipe For Death" 49.69, SKIP the next part.
04) Next to you in the room, turn in "Zinge's Delivery" 50.68, accept "Sample for Helbrim".
05) Hearth to The Sepulcher.
06) Next to the crypt/Inn/mailbox, turn in "Return to Podrig" (43.42 in Silverpine Forest).
07) SE at the Bat Handler 46.43, take a bat ride to Undercity, Tirisfal.
08) Once at the UC, exit it and head north to the Zeppelin to go to Orgrimmar (61.59 in Tirisfal Glades).  NOTE:  There are two Zeppelins, the eastern one takes you to Stranglethorn Vale and the western one to Orgrimmar.
09) Once in Orgrimmar, get the Flight Path from the Wind Rider Master (up at 45.64).
10) Leave Orgrimmar (south) and run to Razor Hill (in Durotar) to get new spells/abilities.  You can also upgrade First Aid if you need to in the Barracks from the First Aid Trainer 54.42. 
11) At the west entrance of Razor Hill accept "Conscript of the Horde" 51.44.
12) Head west on the main path to enter The Barrens..
#16

 
Levels 15 Barrens (Undead) (log)
01) Just over the bridge, next to the path, turn in "Conscript of the Horde" (62.19 in the Barrens), accept "Crossroads Conscription".
02) Go SW to XRs and in the Inn, make XRs your new home from the Innkeeper 52.30.
03) Right outside the Inn on your right accept "Raptor Thieves" 52.30.
04) At the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower accept "Disrupt the Attacks" 52.31, and "Supplies for the Crossroads".
05) Go up the watchtower next to you and accept "Harpy Raiders" 52.31.
06) Drop down and get the FP from the Wind Rider Master 52.30.
07) Right next to you under the red canopy, turn in (if you have it) "Sample for Helbrim" 51.30.  Also accept "Fungal Spores" and "Wharfmaster Dizzywig".
08) At Sergra Darkthorn, turn in "Crossroads Conscription" 52.31.  Accept "Plainstrider Menace".
09) Go south in XRs and accept "The Forgotten Pools" 52.32.  Then next to you, get repaired/resupplied from the Clothier.
10) While working on the following steps, also start working on "Plainstrider Menace" and "Raptor Thieves" by killing plainstriders and raptors all around the fields of the Barrens, but don't make it your main priority:
11) Go NE and do "Disrupt the Attacks": kill the needed Razormane mobs around 54.27.  ALSO: Back in the Razormane camp there is a barrel called Chen's Empty Keg 56.27, loot it and accept the quest from the item received.
12) Go back SW to the XRs and at the orc standing at the bottom of the watchtower, turn in "Disrupt the Attacks" (52.31, #4 on map).  Accept "The Disruption Ends".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

NOTE:  As you continue on in The Barrens, you might be a few levels above what the guide indicates, but that is ok, simply follow The Barrens guide entirely (whether you did the Silverpine section or not).
#17

First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, SoM, TBC or WotLK)
(log)
01) Go back to the other Razormane camps up NE around 57.25 and do "The Disruption Ends" and "Supplies for the Crossroads": kill the required amount of Razormane quilboars and loot a Crossroads' Supply Crates (there are multiple locations of these crates).  Kreenig Snarlsnout is at 59.27 /target.
02) Make sure you are level 13 as you work your way SE to Ratchet (stay next to the river for mobs more your level) and then go up in the engineering building and accept "Southsea Freebooters" 63.36.
03) Go to the Flight Master 63.37 to get the FP, then next to you accept "Samophlange".  NOTE:  To free up bag space you can destroy the Control Console Operating Manual as it is not needed.
04) Go to the goblin on the docks and turn in "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" 63.38.  Accept "Miner's Fortune".
05) At the WANTED sign next to the bank, accept "WANTED: Baron Longshore" 63.38.
06) Accept "Raptor Horns" (next to Ratchet's bank, 62.38).
07) Turn in "Chen's Empty Keg" (male orc next to the Plate-n-Chain hut, 62.38), accept the next part.  Also focus on doing this quest while throughout The Barrens by kill/looting cats, plainstriders and Thunderlizards...
08) NOTE:  At the Ratchet's Inn 62.39 you can buy Longjaw Mud Snapper food for really cheap.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
09) Follow the path south of Ratchet to The Merchant Coast and do "Southsea Freebooters" along with "WANTED: Baron Longshore": kill the needed Southsea pirates and kill/loot Baron Longshore /target, he has 3 possible spawns at the southern most tents (starting at 64.47).
10) Go back to Ratchet, and in the engineering building turn in "Southsea Freebooters" (accept "The Missing Shipment") and "WANTED: Baron Longshore" 63.36.
11) Go to the goblin at the docks and turn in "The Missing Shipment" (63.38, #6 on map), accept the next part.
12) Go back in the engineering building and turn in "The Missing Shipment" 63.36, accept "Stolen Booty".
#01

 

01) South of Ratchet again at The Merchant Coast, do "Stolen Booty": loot the Fragile - Do Not Drop box for the Lens 64.49.  For the boots, see next step:
02) Go SW a bit to the other small pirate camp and loot Drizzlik's Emporium box for the boots 63.50.
03) Hearth to XRs.
04) Go to the orc at the bottom of the watchtower and turn in "The Disruption Ends" and "Supplies for the Crossroads" 52.31.
05) Turn in (if you haven't yet) "Plainstrider Menace" (it should be done by now) 52.31, accept "The Zhevra".  NOTES:  If "Plainstrider Menace" is not done yet, you can find a lot of Plainstriders just around the outskirts of the XRs.  Also make sure you are level 14 before continuing.
06) South in XRs, accept "Consumed by Hatred" and "Lost in Battle" 52.32.
07) At this point, I make sure I have 3/4 six slot bags, if I don't, I buy them at the bag vendor /target (the southern most vendor in XRs, 52.32).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
08) NW in Crossroads 51.29, you can possibly upgrade your weapons from the Weaponsmith.
#02

 

01) Go west of XRs (grinding on zhevra, plainstriders and raptors along the way), to the orc 45.28 in the burrow and accept " Kolkar Leaders" and "Centaur Bracers".
02) While focusing on the following quests, also kill any Plainstriders, Centaur, Raptors and Zhevra along the way, but don't make them your main focus...
03) Grind your way to The Forgotten Pools and do "The Forgotten Pools": discover the fissure under the water 45.23.  Also start working on (or finish?) "Fungal Spores": loot the Laden Mushrooms around The Forgotten Pools.    ALTERNATE ROUTE CHOICE:  Some classes can get away without needing to get all the shrooms for "Fungal Spores" right now, or for other classes that can get away without needing to go to TB at lvl 16 for new spells/abilities and wait to lvl 20 to get them instead.  This choice dramatically changes the route below for the 16-20 sections.  Speedlevelers may want to skip going to TB at 16 and get their spells/abilities instead at lvl 20 to save travel time.  With all that being said, choose your route:
Choose Your Route: Fungal Spores Incomplete / TB at 20


04) Do "Kolkar Leaders": grind your way to Barak Kodobane 43.23 /target, and kill/loot him.  NOTE:  "Centaur Bracers" does not need to be completed now.
05) Go NW to The Dry Hills and do "Harpy Raiders": kill/loot the harpies around 41.19.  Watch out there is an Rare Elite /target that wanders around in the area.
06) Grind your way west (at 35.28 in The Barrens) and accept "Goblin Invaders" and "Avenge My Village".
#03

 
15-16 Stonetalon Mountains (log)
01) Go west into Stonetalon Mountains and do "Avenge My Village": kill the required amount of Grimtotem taurens around 81.90.  TIP:  Kill the Grimtotem Sorcerers first as they do a lot of damage.
02) Go back and turn in "Avenge My Village" 35.28, accept "Kill Grundig Darkcloud".
03) Go back into Stonetalon, to the path that goes up into Grimtotem post hill entrance 76.89.   NOTE:  If this entrance is to tough for you then a safer entrance is at 72.87 (which will also clear the path for the future escort coming up).
04) Do "Kill Grundig Darkcloud": kill 6 Grimtotem Brutes and also kill Grundig Darkcloud /target, he is standing on the steps outside the hut 74.86.
05) Accept and do the escort quest there ("Protect Kaya" 73.86 in the hut). TIPS:  Make bandages during the escort.  When reaching down to Camp Aparaje, kill the Ruffian behind the big totem before the final fight starts.  Try to keep aggro off of Kaya Flathoof, as she has low HP.   Kill the Sorcerer first that pops out during the one battle you have to fight.  If it's too hard for you, skip it.
06) Once done, go back and turn in (if you did it) "Protect Kaya" (at 35.28 in The Barrens, #2 on map), accept "Kaya's Alive".  Also turn in "Kill Grundig Darkcloud".
#04

 
16-20 Barrens (log)
01) Grind your way to the orc in the burrow and turn in "Kolkar Leaders" 45.28.  Accept "Verog the Dervish".  Also turn in (if it's done) "Centaur Bracers" if not don't worry.
02) At this point, make sure "The Zhevra" is finished, kill/loot Zhevra while you work your way to the XRs...
03) Go to XRs and turn in all quests and accept new ones:  Turn in (If it's complete) "Fungal Spores" 51.30 (next to Wind Rider Master).  SKIP (if it's available) "Apothecary Zamah" (for now).

04) Go up in the watchtower and turn in "Harpy Raiders" 52.31, accept "Harpy Lieutenants".
05) If you finished "Raptor Thieves", turn it in next to the Inn 52.30, accept "Stolen Silver".  If not, don't worry, I will mention when it needs to be finished.
06) At Sergra Darkthorn turn in "The Zhevra" 52.31, accept "Prowlers of the Barrens".
07) South in XRs, turn in "The Forgotten Pools" 52.32, accept "The Stagnant Oasis".  Then next to you, get repaired/resupplied from the Clothier.
#05


01) Make sure you are upgraded to lvl 15 food/water!
02) Go NW of XRs and do "Prowlers of the Barrens" along with "Raptor Thieves" (if you still need to): kill/loot raptors and Savannah Prowlers starting at 42.29, then work your way up north.  Look for the green grassy areas for the Prowlers.  Work your way to the next step:
03) Go up north to The Dry Hills (harpy camp) and do "Harpy Lieutenants" around 40.17: kill/loot the Witchwing Slayers (they are the only ones that drop the rings).  Watch out there is an Rare Elite /target that wanders around in the area.
04) Grind east and you can get repaired/resupplied from Vrang Wildgore next to the hut 44.12.
05) Grind your way east and do "Samophlange": clear the Venture Co. mobs out of the area, then click on the Control Console 52.12 to turn in the quest and accept the next part.  Do the next part by clicking on the 3 Valves around the research facility.  Then click on the Control Console again to turn it in and accept the next part. (See Video of whole quest chain) See next step:
06) Go NE a bit and do the next part of "Samophlange": kill/loot Tinkerer Sniggles /target (in the hut, 53.10).
07) Go back to the research facility's Control Console 52.12 to turn in "Samophlange" (#5 on map), accept the next part.
08) Go NE to The Sludge Fen at Wizzlecrank's Shredder 56.7 and accept "Ignition".  NOTE:  This shredder may currently be in an escort, if this is the case keep grinding on mobs until the escort completes then accept this.
09) Go up to the top of the building in the middle of the pond and do "Ignition": kill/loot Supervisor Lugwizzle /target (he patrols around at the top around 56.9).
10) Go back down to Wizzlecrank's Shredder (56.7, #8 on map) and turn in (as long as he is currently not in an escort) "Ignition".  Then accept and do the escort "The Escape" by escorting the shredder out of The Sludge Fen.  (See video on how I do Ignition and Escape)
11) Go NE to Boulder Lode Mine and do "Miner's Fortune" 62.6: kill/loot the mobs there until the Cats Eye Emerald drops.  NOTE:  The mobs outside of the mine can drop the emerald as well (it's a low drop rate).
#08

 

01) ORC & TROLL ONLY:  Grind your way south to the watchtower and turn in (if you have it) "The Demon Seed" 62.20.

02) Continue south to Ratchet and in the engineering building turn in "Stolen Booty" 63.36.
03) Near the Flight Master 63.37, turn in "The Escape" and "Samophlange".  Accept "Ziz Fizziks" , SKIP "Wenikee Boltbucket".
04) At the goblin at the docks, turn in "Miner's Fortune" 63.38.
05) By the Bank 62.38 accept "Deepmoss Spider Eggs".  And turn in (if it is complete) "Raptor Horns".  Then in the hut next to you, make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
06) Turn in (if it's done) "Chen's Empty Keg" (male orc next to the Plate-n-Chain hut, 62.38), accept the next part.  If it's not done yet, don't worry.
07) Go west of Ratchet, and do "The Stagnant Oasis": click on the Bubbling Fissure down in the lake 56.43.  Also continue working on "Fungal Spores" (if you still need to): loot Laden Mushrooms around the lake (and it doesn't need to be completed now).
08) Go west a bit and do "Verog the Dervish": by kill/looting Verog the Dervish (spawns at 53.42 /target).  NOTE:  Verog spawns in randomly when a centaur has been killed around the area, and there will be a yell notice to let you know he spawns in.  Also, make sure "Centaur Bracers" is finished.
09) Go to XRs and turn in "The Stagnant Oasis" (south in XRs, 52.32), accept "Altered Beings".  NOTE:  If the Dried Seeds is still in your bags, you can destroy it, as it now has no use.
10) At Sergra Darkthorn turn in "Prowlers of the Barrens" 52.31, accept "Echeyakee".

11) Next to the Inn, turn in (if it's complete) "Raptor Thieves" 52.30, accept "Stolen Silver".
12) At the bottom of the watchtower, accept "Report to Kadrak" 52.31.
13) Go up the watchtower and turn in "Harpy Lieutenants" 52.31, accept "Serena Bloodfeather".

13b) Drop down and turn in (If it's complete) "Fungal Spores" (next to Wind Rider Master, 51.30).  SKIP (if it's available) "Apothecary Zamah" (for now).

14) Get repaired/resupplied NW in XRs at the Forge/blacksmith area 51.29.
#12

 

01) Go west from XRs to the orc 45.28 at the burrow and turn in "Verog the Dervish" and (if you still need to) "Centaur Bracers", accept "Hezrul Bloodmark".
02) Grind your way north to The Dry Hills (harpy camp) and do "Serena Bloodfeather": far back in the camp, kill/loot Serena Bloodfeather 39.12 /target.
03) Go east (grind mobs along the way) and do "Echeyakee": use your Horn of Echeyakee quest item at the kodo bones 56.17, then kill/loot Echeyakee /target (See Video).  Also make sure "Raptor Thieves" is finished now (kill/loot raptors if you still need to).
04) Hearth to XRs.  Get resupplied in the Inn.
05) At Sergra Darkthorn, turn in "Echeyakee" 52.31, accept "The Angry Scytheclaws".
06) Next to the Inn, turn in (if you still need to) "Raptor Thieves" 52.30, accept "Stolen Silver".
07) Go to the top of the watchtower and turn in "Serena Bloodfeather" 52.31.  Accept "Letter to Jin'Zil".
08) Go SW to The Lushwater Oasis and do "Altered Beings" along with "Hezrul Bloodmark": kill/loot the Oasis Snapjaw (turtles) in and around the lake around 46.38.  Also kill/loot Hezrul Bloodmark /target, he patrols clockwise around the lake.  TIPS:  Try to avoid the gray lvl mobs.  If you can't find Hezrul in time, the quest can be skipped.  Also finish "Fungal Spores" (if you still need to): loot Laden Mushrooms around the lake.
09) Go SE to the Raptor Grounds and do "Stolen Silver" along with continuing (if you still need to) "Raptor Horns": kill/loot raptors and loot the Stolen Silver chest 58.54. (See Video)
10) Go NW and do "The Angry Scytheclaws" around 52.46: kill/loot raptors for Sunscale Feathers, then use them at the 3 Raptor nests.  Make sure "Raptor Horns" is complete now as well.
#13


01) Go SW across the main path/bridge (to 49.50) and do "Lost in Battle" (Manrik's Wife): She's laying dead by the hut. Simply examine the Beaten Corpse.
02) Go west around 50.53 and see if a lvl 22 gray kodo named Lakota'mani /target is there and kill/loot him.  He might also be on the right side of the main path around 47.51.  He drops Hoof of Lakota'mani which starts "Lakota'mani", accept it if you can spot him soon (if not you can try again later)..
03) Grind south to Camp Taurajo (CT) and accept "Weapons of Choice" (by the Forge, 45.58).
04) Turn in (if you have it) "Lakota'mani" 45.59.
05) At the quilboar in the cage accept "Tribes at War" 45.59. And get the FP there next to you from the Wind Rider Master.  Get resupplied while in town.  NOTE:  Don't sell Blood Shards to a vendor.
06) Go north to Agama'gor and do "Tribes at War" along with "Consumed by Hatred" around 43.46: kill/loot the Bristlebacks (Quilboar).  The Geomancers are north in the area around 43.47, Thornweavers are in the middle around 44.52 and Water Seekers are south around 45.54.  If you go to the area east of the main path around 53.54 they are evenly spaced out there.
07) Go south to CT and turn in (if you have it) "Lakota'mani" (45.59, #4 on map).
08) Go to the quilboar in the cage and turn in "Tribes at War" (45.59, #5 on map).  Accept "Blood Shards of Agamaggan" and then talk to the NPC to turn it in. Then accept "Betrayal from Within"  NOTE:  You can turn in Blood Shards for ability boosts here.  You can turn in multiple and they will stack.  Save Blood Shards for later (I recommend storing them in the mailbox and use some for the next step).
09) At this point, make sure you are at least 5 bars away from level 20.  If you aren't then I recommend grinding on Bristlebacks (Quilboar, #6 on map) to obtain Wool Cloth so you can get skill 115 for Heavy Wool Bandage sooner.  You can also grind on beasts around where Lakota'mani is at (around 47.51, #2 on map).  You can use some Blood Shards to help the grind.  NOTES:  Instead of the grinding, you could get a 5 man group and do the Wailing Caverns dungeon 46.36.  Also make sure "Chen's Empty Keg" (part 2 is finished by now if you still need to).
10) From CT, fly to XRs (or run there if you are close by).

06) At the Wind Rider Master next to you, fly to Camp Taurajo, The Barrens.

08) Go south and do "Egg Hunt": loot the Silithid Mounds.   NOTE:  There is a rare mob called Silithid Harvester (at around 43.70 or around 48.70, /target) that drops an item which starts "The Harvester", accept it if you are able to kill this mob.  This rare has a 2 hour 45 min respawn, so he may not be there.  You can try again later at the level 25 Southern Barrens section below.
09) As you focus on the follow quests below, also start working on "Chen's Empty Keg" by kill/looting the Stormhides, Greater Thunderhawks and kodos along the way.  Also if you run across a reddish thunderhawk named Washte Pawne /target, kill/loot him, he drops Washte Pawne's Feather, which starts "Washte Pawne", accept it.  Washte Pawne randomly wonders around Blackthorn Ridge (or to the east of the main path).
10) Go south to Blackthorn Ridge and do "Betrayal from Within" along with "Weapons of Choice": kill the Razormane Seers for the Wand, Razormane Warfrenzy for the Shield, and the Razormane Pathfinder for the Backstabber.  Also, kill/loot the 3 named mobs, Kuz wonders around (around 45.80 /target), see below for the other named mobs to kill...
11) Kill/loot Lok Orcbane up in the tent (40.81 /target).
12) Kill/loot Nak in the middle of the field (around 44.83 /target).  Watch out for the stealthed mobs in the area!

12) Do "Alien Egg": loot an Alien Egg.  This egg has several possible spawn points (either: 56.50 52.55 38.56).

26-27 Ashenvale (log)
01) Go to Splintertree Post and turn in (if you have the initial quest) "The Ashenvale Hunt" 74.61.  Then accept and turn it in again.

TBC, 6b) At the outskirts of Splintertree Post, turn in (if you have it) "Report to Splintertree Post" 71.68.

07) Accept "Ashenvale Outrunners" 71.68.
08) Just south of Splintertree Post, kill the first of the three mobs related to the Ashenvale hunt: Sharptalon /target.  This lvl 31 blue hippogryph (patrols around and spawns at around 75.70).  This mob drops an item, which starts "Sharptalon's Claw", accept it.  TIP:  An easy way to kill this mob is to bring his health to a 3rd left, then drag him into the guards at Splintertree Post, they will finish em.
09) Just south of Splintertree Post, do "Ashenvale Outrunners": kill 9 Ashenvale Outrunners /target.

11) Go west to Mystral Lake and do "Stonetalon Standstill": kill 12 Befouled Water Elementals around the lake and discover the gazebo on the small island in the middle of the lake 49.70.  Also, make sure you find and kill/loot Tideress /target, this water elemental patrols around under the lake, he drops an item which starts "The Befouled Element", accept it.
12) Kill the next mob related to the Ashenvale Hunt: Ursangous /target, this bear patrols clockwise around the mountain and trees around 42.68.  He drops an item, which starts "Ursangous's Paw", accept it.
13) Kill the last mob related to the Ashenvale Hunt: Shadumbra /target, this black panther patrols around around 57.56.  This cat drops an item, which starts "Shadumbra's Head", accept it.
14) Do "The Sacred Flame".  First kill/loot the Laughing Sisters around Raynewood Retreat around 59.55 until you get an Etched Phial (low drop rate), then see next step...
15) Use the quest item Etched Phial at the Moonwell down south 60.73.
#15

 

01) Hearth (or run back) to Splintertree Post.
02) Right outside the Inn on your left turn in the 3 Ashenvale Hunt quests: "Sharptalon's Claw", "Ursangous's Paw" and "Shadumbra's Head" 74.61.  Then accept and complete "The Hunt Completed".
03) Next to the Inn, turn in "Stonetalon Standstill" and "The Befouled Element" 74.60, accept "Je'neu of the Earthen Ring".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
04) At the orc next to the western watchtower, turn in "Torek's Assault" 73.62.
05) At the outskirts of Splintertree Post, turn in "Ashenvale Outrunners" 71.68.

06) The goal now is to go all the way to the other side of Ashenvale, to Zoram Strand (follow path on map) and then get the Flight Path there from the Wind Rider Master 12.34.  NOTE: Once you get to Astranaar 37.55(alliance town), go south of it to get around it.

07) At Zoram 'gar Outpost, first accept "Vorsha the Lasher" (starts in front of the bigger tent 12.35) (IMPORTANT:  This starts an escort.  If he is not there you can try later in the guide.  Do the next few steps quickly in the village before following Muglash for the escort:
08) turn in "Je'neu of the Earthen Ring" 12.34 (up in hut).   Also turn in "Trouble in the Deeps" 12.34, SKIP "The Essence of Aku'Mai" (Blackfathom Deeps dungeon).
09) From the troll up in the other tent, accept "Troll Charm" 12.35.   From the female troll next to you, accept "Naga at the Zoram Strand" 12.35 (no exclamation mark).
10) From the orc next to the Cauldron accept "Between a Rock and a Thistlefur" 12.35.
11) Do the escort you previously accepted (if you were able to) "Vorsha the Lasher": follow and protect Muglash /target until you can click on the Naga Brazier, then after a few waves of Naga mobs, kill Vorsha the Lasher. (See Video).   Do this along with "Naga at the Zoram Strand" by kill/looting Naga along the coast.
12) Grind your way to the Thistlefur Hold cave (entrance is 39.31) killing mobs for "Between a Rock and a Thistlefur".  Also, before getting to the cave, make sure Rorgish Jowl /target is dead.  He is a rare mob that patrols around (before the cave entrance) making your future escort quest difficult.  He has a 5.5 hour respawn.
13) In the cave do "Troll Charm": loot the Troll Chests on the ground.  Then, in the back of the cave, from the white bear in the cage 42.35, accept and try to do the escort quest "Freedom to Ruul".  You may have to fight 3-5 mobs at once several times, if you fail then it can be skipped.

27-28 Stonetalon Mountains (log)

#18

 
28-29 Thousand Needles (log)
01) In the Inn, turn in "The Sacred Flame" (46.52 in Thousand Needles), accept the next part.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) Take the Lift down from Freewind Post and then go to the cave north (entrance is at 44.38) and do "The Sacred Flame": keep hugging the left side of the cave until you get to the Ancient Brazier 42.31, then loot it (See Video).
03) REMINDER:  Keep an eye out for the Galak Messenger /target and kill/loot this centaur when you see him in 1k Needles.  He has a very large patrol path.  He drops an item which starts "Assassination Plot", accept it (if you can).  But, don't intentionally do this yet, continue on with the next step...
04) Grind west to Whitereach Post 21.32 and turn in "Serpent Wild", accept "Sacred Fire".  And next to you, accept "Hypercapacitor Gizmo".  Also turn in "A Different Approach", accept "A Dip in the Moonwell".  NOTES:  If you have already killed Galak Messenger /target and accepted the quest from the item he dropped, then turn in "Assassination Plot", then accept, do & turn in the follow up escort quest.  You can also get resupplied here from Laer Stepperunner /target.
05) Go east to the Boiling Pool around 35.35 and do "Sacred Fire": loot 10 Incendia Agave from the ground and under the water. TIP: Use Find Herbs in Spellbook (if you have it) to help you locate these easier.
06) Go west and do "Steelsnap": kill/loot Steelsnap /target.  This hyena patrols counter-clockwise around a somewhat large circle (starting at 18.27) and has two bodyguards.  Original spawn is in Camp E-thok 17.19. (See Video)
07) If you are still working on "A New Ore Sample", then see if you can find the Ore Sample: kill/loot the Gravelsnout Surveyors /target (there are two spawns nearby to check) for the Unrefined Ore Sample (see map for locations).
TBC, 7a) Go west and enter the bottom mountain ramp entrance 12.19, then do "A Dip in the Moonwell": use your quest item Robotron Control Unit at the Concealed Control Panel 9.19 at the bottom of the mesa.  Then control your robot to the Alliance moonwell 90.46 close by and use its collect water ability in it.  NOTE:  Classes that use pets must dismiss them first in order for this to work right.

08) Go to the Highperch (ramp entrance 14.33) and do "Wind Rider": loot 10 Highperch Wyvern Eggs from the ground.
09) In Highperch, go up the mountain ramp 13.40.  Once up there watch out for Heartrazor /target (a patrolling rare elite with a 12 hour respawn) and then accept and do the escort quest there "Homeward Bound" 18.41.  You can have the escort try to help you kill the rare elite.  It's also possible to time it so you can start and complete the escort so the rare is out of the way (just as he passes from the escortee).  If you fail, then this escort can be skipped.
10) Once again, if you are still working on "A New Ore Sample": kill/loot the Gravelsnout Surveyors /target (there are two spawns nearby to check) for the Unrefined Ore Sample (see map for locations, #7 on map).

TBC, 8a) Next to you turn in "A Dip in the Moonwell" 21.33, accept "Testing the Tonic".  You can also get resupplied here from Laer Stepperunner /target.

12) Go to Whitereach Post (21.33, #8 on map) and turn in "Hypercapacitor Gizmo".  Also turn in (if you did it) "Assassination Plot", then accept and do the short escort "Protect Kanati Greycloud", then turn it in.

05) "Rocket Car Parts": pick up 30 Rocket Car Rubble from the ground.
06) "Hardened Shells": kill/loot turtles all over The Shimmering Flats.  ALSO:  If you have not completed the Turtle Meat turn in quest at Tarren Mill in Hillsbrad yet, then make sure you save 10 Turtle Meat for later (do not sell to vender).  You can get the Turtle Meat from any turtle at The Shimmering Flats and there is a large amount up north around 83.55.
07) "Load Lightening": kill/loot vultures (Salt Flats Scavengers & Salt Flats Vultures).  Large amount around 88.66.

10) Once all done, go back to Mirage Raceway (at the gnomes section) and from the dwarf (78.77, #1 on map) turn in "Rocket Car Parts".  Accept "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" and "Hemet Nesingwary Jr.".  Then next to you 78.77, turn in "Salt Flat Venom" and "Hardened Shells".  Accept "Encrusted Tail Fins".
11) East at the goblin section of the Mirage Raceway turn in "Load Lightening" (80.76, #2 on map), accept "Goblin Sponsorship".
12) SE in Mirage Raceway by the starting line turn in "A Bump in the Road" (82.78, #3 on map).
13) Make sure you are level 32 (grind on any mobs until you are).
14) Go south to the path that leads into Tanaris (starting at 74.94)...
15) Follow the path south to Gadgetzan and get the FP there from the Wind Rider Master (52.25 in Tanaris).
16) If you have not accepted "Family Tree" from Freewind Post yet (requires lvl 32), then fly to Freewind Post, Thousand Needles and accept this by the Bonfire.
17) Hearth to Orgrimmar.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

04) Then go across the bridge in Ghost Walker Post 52.53 and turn in "The Burning of Spirits" and "The Corrupter", accept the next part.

06) Go SW to Kormek's Hut and accept "Bone Collector" 62.39.  Requires lvl 33.  If you are not 33, ignore this step and you will be able to accept this later in the guide.  NOTE:  You can get resupplied from Vendor-Tron 1000 /target if he is there.
07) Go to Ghost Walker Post (shortcut at 56.54, #2 on map), then go across the bridge and turn in "Befouled by Satyr" (53.54, #4 on map), accept "Alliance Relations".
08) Next to you, turn in "The Corrupter" 52.53, accept the next part.  Make sure you are resupplied 51.53.
#09

 

01) Go NW up to The Argent Dawn dude and turn in "Sceptre of Light" 39.27, accept "Book of the Ancients".
02) Go to where the boat is under the water around 34.32 and do the first part of "Claim Rackmore's Treasure!": kill/loot the Drysnap mobs for the Silver Key (15% drop rate).  Also start working on "Clam Bait" by kill/looting crabs and looting Giant Softshell Clams.  Use a water breathing potion if you have one.  As you do the following steps, you should find the Golden Key from any Slitherblade mob...
03) Do "Book of the Ancients": grind your way to the NW end of the bigger island and click on the Serpent Statue 28.7, then kill/loot Lord Kragaru when he spawns. (See Video)
04) Do the last part of "Claim Rackmore's Treasure!": Once you find the Golden Key from any Slitherblade mob, click on Rackmore's Chest 30.9 on the bigger island to turn this in.
05) Do "The Corrupter": kill/loot the Slitherblade Oracles /target in the water and on the 2 bigger islands until you get the Oracle Crystal (low drop rate).  Use a water breathing potion if you have one.
06) Finish up "Other Fish to Fry": kill the needed Slitherblade mobs.  The Myrmidon are scattered between the two biggest islands, the Naga are more towards the shore around 36.24 and there are only a few Sorceresses scattered everywhere.
07) Make sure "Clam Bait" is finished: collect Soft-shelled Clam Meat from Enraged Reef Crawlers (crabs) or from the Giant Softshell Clams under the water (around #2 on map around 36.24 and slightly north of it as well).
08) Go up to The Argent Dawn dude and turn in "Book of the Ancients" (39.27, #1 on map).
09) If you have not accepted "Bone Collector" yet, do so now at Kormek's Hut 62.39.
10) Go to Ghost Walker Post (shortcut at 56.54)....
11) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 56.56, then go across the bridge and turn in "The Corrupter" 52.53.  Wait a few seconds, then accept the next part.
12) Next to you, turn in "The Corrupter" 53.54, SKIP the next part (for now).
#10

 

01) At the Kodo Graveyard next to you around 53.57, do "Bone Collector": loot the Kodo Bones on the ground.  NOTE:  Looting some of these bones spawns a high lvl Kodo Apparition.
02) N/A
03) Go to Ghost Walker Post, entrance 57.57...
04) N/A
05) Stop at the hut just north to get repaired/resupplied from Muuran 56.57.
06) Grind your way NE to Kormek's Hut and turn in "Bone Collector" 62.39.
07) Hearth to Shadowprey Village.
08) Just outside of the Inn, and up on the mound, turn in (if you have it) "Hand of Iruxos" 26.68, SKIP "Portals of the Legion" (for now).
09) N/A
10) Go back west to Shadowprey Village and down at the docks turn in "Other Fish to Fry" 23.73 and next to you at the hut "Clam Bait" 23.72.

11) Go to the Wind Rider Master 22.74 at the end of the docks at Shadowprey Village and fly to Camp Taurajo, The Barrens.
#11

 

01) Once at CT, run SE into Dustwallow Marsh ( 51.79 in The Barrens) and collect the 3 quest-objects at the Shady Rest Inn (See Video):  "Suspicious Hoofprints" (on the ground in front of the old Inn 51.79), "Lieutenant Paval Reethe" (a small white dot on a plank 30.48) and "The Black Shield" (on the fireplace 30.49).
02) Go NE to Brackenwall Village 36.32 and turn in "The Black Shield" (accept the next part), "Suspicious Hoofprints" and "Lieutenant Paval Reethe".
03) Next to you from Do'gol, turn in "The Black Shield" 37.31, SKIP the next part (for now).
