06) Go to Gazlowe in the engineering building and turn in "Goblin Sponsorship" (63.36 in The Barrens), accept the next part.
07) Go to the Bank 63.37 and get the following items out:  Kravel's Parts Order, Kravel's Crate and Silk Cloth (to build up First Aid).  Put in the bank:  Belgrom's Sealed Note.
08) Go to the goblin on the docks and turn in "Wharfmaster Dizzywig" 63.38, accept "Parts for Kravel".
09) Get on the boat to go to BB (Booty Bay) 64.39.  While waiting for the boat, build up First Aid.
#12

 
34-36 Stranglethorn Vale (log)
01) As soon as you get off the boat, turn in "Goblin Sponsorship" 26.74, accept the next part.
02) Go in the Inn (The Salty Sailor Tavern) and make BB your home from the Innkeeper 27.77.  Next to you in the Inn, accept "Singing Blue Shards".
03) Go upstairs in the Inn and accept "Bloodscalp Ears", "Hostile Takeover" and "Investigate the Camp".
04) Stay up on the top of the docks and go over past the Alliance FP to Tan-Your-Hide Leatherworks and accept "Supply and Demand" 28.78.
05) Go back over to the top of the Inn and then go up the steps to Baron Revilgaz 27.77 on the boat-deck and turn in "Goblin Sponsorship", accept the next part.
06) Next to you on the roof, go to the Wind Rider Master 27.77 and fly to Grom'gol, Stranglethorn.
07) In Grom'gol accept the following: "The Defense of Grom'gol" 32.29, "Mok'thardin's Enchantment" 32.29, "Bloodscalp Insight" (next to Bonfire 32.29), "Hunt for Yenniku" 32.28,"Bloody Bone Necklaces"and "The Vile Reef".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
08) REMINDER:  The "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" quest requires that you save any Green Hills of Stranglethorn pages you find.  These pages can drop from any humanoid mob in STV.  You will need one of each of the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  I recommend mailing them to your alt for storage.  Once your alt collects all the pages, mail them back to your main.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  You don't need to accept the quest to get the page drops, so accept and turn this in at Nesingwary's Expedition 36.11 if you get all the pages.  You will return to STV numerous times in the future for more chances to find the pages.
#13

 

01) Go to the ledges along the shore around 27.19 and start working on "Singing Blue Shards": kill/loot Crystal Spine Basilisks.  80% drop rate.  There's only 9 mob spawns in the area.  If you run out of mobs, you can start working on the next step(s) then come back to this spot.  You will also have more opportunities later in the guide to complete this.
02) Go south at Bal'lal Ruins, just off the shore around 29.20 and do "Bloodscalp Ears" along with "Hunt for Yenniku", "Bloodscalp Insight" and start working on "Bloody Bone Necklaces": kill/loot the trolls at the ruins.  Kill/loot Bloodscalp Shamans for the totem.   There is another Ruins up NE around 34.16 for more mobs.  NOTE:  "Bloody Bone Necklaces" does not need to be finished now.
03) N/A
04) Go south (just north of Grom'gol, around 32.23) and do "The Defense of Grom'gol": kill Lashtail Raptors.
05) Go to Grom'gol and turn in "Hunt for Yenniku" 32.28, accept "Headhunting".
TBC, 5a) Next to the Bonfire turn in "Bloodscalp Insight" 32.29, accept "An Unusual Patron".

06) Turn in "The Defense of Grom'gol" 32.29, accept the next part.
07) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 32.29.  REMINDER: Once you turn lvl 35, start using the higher tier food/drink.

08) Go NW under the water at The Vile Reef and do "The Vile Reef" along with "Encrusted Tail Fins": loot the Gri'lek the Wanderer tablet 25.23.  Also kill/loot the murlocs.  Use Swim Speed Potions and an Elixer of Water Breathing (unless you are Undead) if you can.

09) Go west to the The Savage Coast island and at the Altar of Naias 20.23 do "An Unusual Patron": use the quest item Gift of Naias then kill/loot Naias (water elemental) when he spawns.
#14

 

01) Go up north to the troll Ruins around 20.14 and do "Headhunting" along with finishing up "Bloody Bone Necklaces": kill/loot the trolls around the Ruins for the necklaces (low drop rate).  And kill/loot the Bloodscalp Headhunters for the Shrunken Heads.  You can kill lower level trolls for the necklaces down south at the two ruins (between step #3 on map).
02) Go to Nesingwary's Expedition 36.11 and turn in "Hemet Nesingwary Jr.", "Hunting in Stranglethorn"  Get repaired/resupplied from Jaquilina Dramet.
03) N/A
05) At the river east around 34.9 of you do "Supply and Demand": kill/loot River Crocolisk.  And continue working on (if you still need to) "Singing Blue Shards" by kill/looting Stone Maw Basilisk around the north end of the river.  Work your way to the next step:
06) Go SE across the river to the Venture Co. Operations Center and do "Hostile Takeover": kill/loot the Venture Co. Geologist /target scattered around the Operations for the 8 crystals.  Also do the first part of "Goblin Sponsorship": work your way to the top of the Operations Center entrance 42.18 and then kill/loot Foreman Cozzle (in the control room, 43.18).  See next step for where to use Cozzle's Key at...
07) Do the last part of "Goblin Sponsorship": loot Cozzle's Footlocker (in the small house 43.20).  NOTE:  Your key will disappear as soon as you click the chest, so make sure you have room in your bags for the Blueprints.
08) Go east and do "Mok'thardin's Enchantment": kill/loot Shadowmaw Panthers /target around 49.23.  Also kill/loot Stranglethorn Tigress around the same area for the fang. 
09) Finish (if you still need to) "Singing Blue Shards": kill/loot Crystal Spine Basilisks just NE around the mountain ledges around 48.8.
10) Go west across the river to Mizjah Ruins around 37.31 and do "The Defense of Grom'gol": kill the required amount of Mosh'Ogg ogres.
12) Hearth to BB.
#15

 

01) In the Inn, turn in "Singing Blue Shards" 27.77, SKIP "Venture Company Mining" (for now).

Go in the Inn (bigger house 62.19).  From Christoph Jeffcoat, accept "Soothing Turtle Bisque" (requires the cooking profession to accept).  From this NPC, buy a Soothing Spices.  Then wWith your Turtle Meat you farmed earlier, simply turn in the quest right there.
15) Go to the Bat Handler 60.19 and fly to Hammerfall, Arathi.

36-37 Arathi Highlands (log)
01) Go in the Inn and make Hammerfall your new home from the Innkeeper 74.32.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) On the balcony next to you, turn in "Trollbane" 74.34, accept "Sigil of Strom".
03) Go down to Tor'gan 75.36 and accept "Foul Magics" and "Guile of the Raptor".
04) Go west and click on the Shards of Myzrael 62.34 to accept "The Princess Trapped".
05) Go east of Hammerfall and do "The Princess Trapped": kill/loot the Drywhisker kobolds, in and outside of the cave (entrance at 83.36).  TIPS:  Look for the tree and rock 81.40, it shows you where the hidden path is to the cave.  If you have mining, then mine as many Lesser Bloodstone Deposits as you can.  These are used for an Alliance quest only at STV and sell good in the neutral AH.
06) Once done, go to the Iridescent Shards (at the end of the cave 84.31) and turn in "The Princess Trapped", accept "Stones of Binding".
07) If you have 225 First Aid now, then go to Hammerfall and in the building accept and do "Triage" 73.37: use the Triage Bandage quest item right there in the room on the wounded soldiers (See Video).  Once done, turn it in.  You can also destroy the Triage Bandage in your inventory as it is no longer needed.  If you were not able to do this quest yet, you will be farming more cloth in Arathi to try again later. NOTE: In WotLK, this doctor no longer advances your First Aid, you now just do that at any First Aid trainer.
08) Go up the steps in Hammerfall and make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  Then take the north exit 73.30 and go west to the Circle of East Binding and do part of "Stones of Binding": loot the Stone of East Binding 67.30 for the Cresting Key.
09) Go west to Dabyrie's Farmstead and do "To Steal From Thieves": kill/loot the 3 named mobs.  Kill/loot Kenata Dabyrie /target (up in the small house 56.36).  See next steps for the other 2...
10) Kill/loot Fardel Dabyrie /target (standing outside next to the barn 57.39, or in front of the stables 54.38)...
11) Kill/loot Marcel Dabyrie /target (in the stables 54.38).
#17

 

01) Go south a bit and do part of "Stones of Binding": loot the Stone of Outer Binding 52.51 for the Thundering Key.
02) Go south and do "Guile of the Raptor": kill/loot Highland Fleshstalkers around 50.64.
03) Go NW to Boulder'gor and do "The Hammer May Fall": kill the Boulderfist Ogres (outside of the mine) and the Boulderfist Enforcers (inside of the mine 35.44).

05) Go west and do the last part of "Stones of Binding": loot the Stone of West Binding 25.30 for the Burning Key.

01) Hearth to Hammerfall.

10) Grind SW to Boulderfist Hall around 54.73 and do "Call to Arms": kill the required amount of Boulderfist ogres.  If you still need to do "Triage", then make sure you get your First Aid skill to 225 before proceeding.

12) On the deck turn in "Call to Arms" 74.34.
13) NOTE: due to the faster leveling changes, we will be skipping the Stromguard quests ("Call to Arms", "The Broken Sigil", "The Real Threat" and "Foul Magics").
14) If you have not done the First Aid quest "Triage" yet, do so now:
(click to show instructions):

15) Go up to the Wind Rider Master 73.33 and fly to Tarren Mill, Hillsbrad.  If "Breaking the Keystone" is still in your quest book, abandon it.

38-38 Dustwallow Marsh (log)
01) West at Brackenwall Village, from Nazeer Bloodpike 35.31, accept "Theramore Spies" and "Check Up on Tabetha".

04) Go to the Innkeeper (blue ogre at small cave entrance 37.32) to make Brackenwall Village your new home.
05) In the small cave accept "The Hermit of Witch Hill" 37.33.
06) Go slightly south of Brackenwall Village and accept "Hungry!" (from the ogre at 35.38).
07) Start working on "Theramore Spies" along with "The Black Shield": kill Theramore Infiltrators /target and kill/loot any type of spiders.  Grind your way to the next step...
Stinky, 7b) See if you can do the escort "Stinky's Escape" 47.18 (#16 on map): escort "Stinky" Ignatz.  NOTE: If he is not there, then skip this for now.  You will have more opportunities to try again later.
08) Go east to North Point Tower (entrance at 47.24) and do "Twilight of the Dawn Runner": at the bottom of the tower talk to Ithania /target to free her, then go to the top of the tower to loot the Warped Crates.
09) Go east to Jarl's cabin 55.26 and turn in "The Hermit of Witch Hill", accept "Marsh Frog Legs".  Next to you at the cabin accept "What's Haunting Witch Hill?" (this NPC is either in the cabin or on the dock).
10) Right next to Jarl's cabin, click on the Loose Dirt 55.26 to accept "The Lost Report".
11) All around Jarl's Cabin do "Marsh Frog Legs" along with "What's Haunting Witch Hill?": kill/loot Giant Marsh Frogs (frog critters /target).  And kill the undead mobs (Risen Spirits and Risen Husks).
12) Back at Jarl's cabin 55.26 (#9 on map) turn in "Marsh Frog Legs", accept "Jarl Needs Eyes".  Next to you at the cabin turn in "What's Haunting Witch Hill?", accept "The Witch's Bane".
13) All around Jarl's cabin start working on "The Witch's Bane" by looting witchbane (plants, mostly around #11 on map), that you see along the way:
14) Go NE to the shore around 57.17 and do "Hungry!": kill/loot the Mirefin (murlocs).
15) Exit at 58.19.
16) If you have not done the escort "Stinky's Escape" 47.18: escort "Stinky" Ignatz and grind mobs during the escort.  NOTE: If he is not there, then skip this for now.  You will have one more opportunity to try this again later.
17) All around Jarl's cabin finish "The Witch's Bane": loot 9 witchbane (plants, mostly around #11 on map).
18) Back at Jarl's cabin turn in "The Witch's Bane" 56.26 (#9 on map), accept "Cleansing Witch Hill".
19) Do "Cleansing Witch Hill": at the end of the dock there at Jarl's cabin 55.27 use the Witchbane Torch quest item then kill Zelfrax (gargoyle) when he spawns.
20) Back at Jarl's cabin turn in "Cleansing Witch Hill" 56.26 (#9 on map).
21) Finish up "Theramore Spies": kill 9 Theramore Infiltrators /target just NE of Brackenwall Villagearound 42.26 (#7 on map).
22) Go west to Darkmist Cavern around 35.22 and do "Jarl Needs Eyes": kill/loot the Darkmist spiders for 20 eyes.  Also make sure "The Black Shield" is finished by kill/looting any spiders.
23) Go to the west side in Brackenwall Village 35.31 (#1 on map) and turn in "Theramore Spies", accept "The Theramore Docks".  And turn in "The Lost Report".
24) From the male blood elf turn in "Twilight of the Dawn Runner" 36.32 .
25) Turn in "The Black Shield" 37.31, accept the next part.
26) At Krog, turn in "The Black Shield" 36.32.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
27) Go just south of Brackenwall Village at the ogre and turn in "Hungry!" 35.38 (#6 on map).
28) Go SE to the ogre (41.37 /target) on the path and accept and do the escort "Questioning Reethe": follow Ogron, then kill the mobs that spawn in next to Paval Reethe.
#27


01) Head SE to Tabetha's Farm and inside the cabin turn in "Check Up on Tabetha" 46.57.  Accept "Help for Mudsprocket".  And next to you accept "The Grimtotem Weapon" and "The Reagent Thief".
02) Just outside at the side of the cabin 46.57 accept "Direhorn Raiders" and "The Zeppelin Crash".
03) Go east to Beezil's Wreck 54.57 and turn in "The Zeppelin Crash".  Accept "Corrosion Prevention" and "Secure the Cargo!".
04) At Beezil's Wreck do "Razzeric's Tweaking": loot the Gizmorium Shipping Crate (box at 54.56).
05) Around the immediate area do "Corrosion Prevention" along with "Secure the Cargo!": stand near a Power Core Fragment to obtain a buff, then use the Ooze Buster quest item on oozes.  NOTE:  You don't actually need to attack the oozes.  And loot 8 Zeppelin Cargo (boxes laying around).
06) Back at Beezil's Wreck 54.57 (#4 on map) turn in "Corrosion Prevention" along with "Secure the Cargo!", accept "Delivery for Drazzit".
07) Go NW to Direhorn Post and in the NE tent kill/loot Apothecary Cylla 47.47, she drops an item which starts "Signs of Treachery", accept it.
08) In the immediate area around Direhorn Post do "Direhorn Raiders": kill 12 Grimtotems (taurens of any type).
09) Around The Quagmire around 43.49 do the next 3 quests together:
10) "The Reagent Thief": kill/loot Noxious Shredders (green serpents) or Darkfang Creepers (orange spiders).  These are around #1 on map.
11) "The Grimtotem Weapon": use the Captured Totem quest item at the Mottled Drywallow Crocolisk or a Drywallow Daggermaw, then kill them.  NOTE: The crocs must be pulsed by the totem on death to get credit.
12) "Deadmire": kill/loot Deadmire /target.  This white crocolisk patrols around, mostly around the eastern part of #11 on map around 48.56.
13) Back at Tabetha's Farm and inside the cabin 46.57 (#2 on map) turn in "The Grimtotem Weapon" and "The Reagent Thief".
14) Just outside at the side of the cabin 46.57 turn in "Direhorn Raiders".

Geologists around 45.21.  These mobs have less armor and die faster.  Instead of the grinding, you could get a 5 man group to do the Scarlet Monastery dungeon.

#36
How to run to the Badlands from Arathi Highlands:

40-41 Badlands (log)
First Pick the Class you are playing top left of screen!!!
Along with the Game Version you are playing on (Era, SoM, TBC or WotLK)
01) As you enter Badlands, grind your way SW to Valley of Fangs and turn in "Martek the Exiled" 42.53, accept "Indurium".  Next to you, accept "Barbecued Buzzard Wings".  And if you have 9 Blue Pearls with you, accept and turn in "Pearl Diving".
02) Go south a bit around 52.66 and do "Indurium": kill/loot the Stonevault troggs.
03) Go back north to Valley of Fangs and turn in "Indurium" (42.53, #1 on map).  Wait a few seconds then accept "News for Fizzle".
04) Go NW to The Dustbowl, and at the gnome 26.45, accept "Study of the Elements: Rock".  If you have a Frost Oil item, accept and complete the quest "Coolant Heads Prevail".  Then if you have a Gyrochronatom item, accept and complete the quest "Gyro... What?".
05) Grind west to Kargath and get the FP there from the Wind Rider Master 4.45.  NOTE:  Keep your Hearthstone set to Grom'gol!
TBC, 5a) At the watchtower entrance accept "Unclaimed Baggage" 6.48.

06) Go up the watchtower and accept "Coyote Thieves" and "Report to Helgrum" 6.47.
07) Go in the Inn 3.46 and accept "Broken Alliances" and (upstairs) "Badlands Reagent Run".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
08) Do "Study of the Elements: Rock" kill/loot Lesser Rock Elementals up north on the cliffs around 17.43.
09) Go to The Dustbowl at the gnome (26.45, #4 on map) and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock", accept the next part.
10) Grind your way south and do "Study of the Elements: Rock": kill/loot Rock Elementals around 38.74.
11) Grind your way north to The Dustbowl at the gnome (26.45, #4 on map) and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock", accept the next part.

TBC, 11a) Go NE to Angor Fortress entrance 43.30, and do "Unclaimed Baggage": make a left at the entrance then in the room before going down the steps loot the orange Empty Barrel 41.29.  Then go back to the main entrance and make a right at it, then at the room before going down the steps loot the Weapon Rack 42.27. 
#01

 

01) Do the following 4 steps below (in any order) around the western part of Badlands:
02) "Broken Alliances": kill/loot Boss Tho'grun /target.  This ogre patrols in a large circle counter-clockwise around the SW part of Badlands.  He has 5 bodyguards.  Pull/kite him away from his guards to take him on 1v1. (See Video).  NOTE:  You can focus on the next steps until he shows up...
03) "Barbecued Buzzard Wings": kill/loot Buzzards.  There is a large amount of Giant Buzzards at 16.61.  And remember to not sell the Buzzard Wings to a vendor.
04) "Coyote Thieves": kill/loot Coyotes.  Focus more on the Elder Crag Coyotes around the western side of Badlands.
05) "Badlands Reagent Run": kill/loot Buzzards, Coyotes and Rock Elementals.  This quest will naturally get done by doing the others.
06) Go south and do "Study of the Elements: Rock": kill/loot Greater Rock Elementals around 14.86, and more around 4.79.
07) Once all that is done, go back to Kargath, and at the bottom of the watchtower turn in "Unclaimed Baggage" 6.48, then go up in the watchtower and turn in "Coyote Thieves".
08) Go in the Inn 50.66 and turn in "Broken Alliances", SKIP the next part (high level group quest).
09) Go upstairs and turn in "Badlands Reagent Run" 50.66, SKIP the next part (dungeon).  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
10) Go east to The Dustbowl at the gnome 51.66 and turn in "Study of the Elements: Rock".  If it is available, accept "This Is Going To Be Hard", and turn it in right next to you and accept the next part.  Turn that in right next to you and accept the next part.  Kill/loot the Fam'retor Guardian that spawns in.  Then turn it in.
11) Also, if you have a Healing Potion and a Lesser Invisibility Potion with you, accept (if it is available) "Liquid Stone", then turn it in.
12) And also if you have a Patterned Bronze Bracers with you, accept (if it is available) "Stone Is Better than Cloth", then turn it in.
13) Go east (in the middle of Badlands) and turn in "Barbecued Buzzard Wings" 42.53.
14) N/A
#02

 

01) Hearth to Grom'gol.
02) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied. 
03) Now could be a good time to see if you have all the pages for "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn".  This quest requires that you have the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  If you have all of them, get them out now and go to Nesingwary's Expedition 36.11 and turn this quest in.  If not, you will return to STV numerous times in the future for more chances to find the pages.
04) Run all the way to Swamp of Sorrows, stopping along the way at Beggar's Haunt to accept "Nothing But the Truth" (in Duskwood at 88.36).
05) Next to you, turn in "Nothing But the Truth" 87.35, skip the next part.
06) Run east through Deadwind Pass, follow the main path and then make a left into Ariden's Camp (52.38 in Deadwind Pass) just to discover it for XP.
07) Continue east into Swamp of Sorrows 61.41...
#03

 
41-42 Swamp of Sorrows (log)
01) As you enter Swamp of Sorrows (SoS) start working on "Dream Dust in the Swamp": kill/loot the dragon whelps around the lake (SW around 13.60 in SoS) for the dream dust.  Just clear the whelps in this area once.  When you run out of whelps to kill, move on to the next steps:
02) N/A
04) Go east and then find and kill/loot Noboru the Cudgel /target.  See patrol path on map (spawns at 47.39).  He drops an item which starts "Noboru the Cudgel", accept it.

10) Do "Lack of Surplus": kill/loot crocolisks.  There are Sawtooth Crocolisks (around the middle of SoS around 57.53) and Sawtooth Snappers (around the NE of SoS around 80.28).

11) Now could be a good time to see if you have all the pages for "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn".  This quest requires that you have the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  If you have all of them, get them out now (we will turn it in a few steps later).  If not, you will return to STV one more time in the future for one last chance to find the pages.
12) West at the shore do "Excelsior": kill/loot an Elder Saltwater Crocolisks /target (lvl 38).  There are 4 possible spawn locations try this one first 33.33, see map.  You should only need to kill/loot one of them.  Also these spawn by killing the regular Saltwater Crocolisks, so you may need to kill some of them first to get these to spawn in.

01) As you do this section start working on the following:  "Army of the Black Dragon" (kill required amount of Firemane dragons), "The Essence of Enmity" (use the Brogg's Totem quest item on Firemane dragon corpses), "Catch a Dragon by the Tail" (loot Wyrmtail plants) and "Identifying the Brood" (kill/loot any dragon whelps):

11) Go west and loot Mok'Morokk's Grog (before the cave entrance 39.66).  Then kill the dragon that spawns.

#20

 

01) Go south to The Writhing Deep and do "Stinglasher" along with "Zukk'ash Infestation": kill/loot the Zukk'ash bugs and also kill/loot Stinglasher /target.  He is at the cave entrance of either 77.61 or 74.64.
02) Go NW and do "Screecher Spirits": kill the Screecher mobs, then use the Yeh'kinya's Bramble quest item on the corpse, then talk to the Screecher Spirit that spawns.  There are some Screechers around 59.51 and see map for more locations.  NOTES:  The Screechers have a spell silence attack.  Do the next step in that same area while doing this:
03) Go north to the entrance of Dire Maul at 43.70 only to get it Discovered.  Simply discovering Dire Maul in this area will allow you to accept a future quest.  You need to actually discover the zone, you will know when you achieved that when your general chat switches over to Dire Maul.
04) Go to the Ruins of Isildien (starting at 61.56 and work your way south) and do "The Ogres of Feralas": kill the required amount of Gordunni ogres.  The Maulers are north and the Shamans are south.  Keep the next step in mind:
05) Do "Dark Ceremony": kill/loot the Gordunni Mage-Lords until you get the Gordunni Orb.  These mobs are south in the Ruins of Isildien 61.95.
WOTLK, 5a) Go just west to The High Wilderness around 55.66 and do "Hippogryph Muisek": use the Hippogryph Muisek Vessel quest item on any Frayfeather hippogryph corpses.  Also do part of "Natural Materials": kill/loot the Frayfeather mobs (hippogryphs) for the 20 Resilient Sinew and 40 Metallic Fragments.  Keep next step in mind:

WOTLK, 5b) South of the Hippogryphs along the cliffs around 58.76, loot a Hippogryph Egg in one of the nests.  This will be turned in at Tanaris for a later quest.  And remember to not sell this!

06) Go NW to the Feral Scar Vale around 55.56 and do "The Mark of Quality": kill/loot the Feral Scar yetis.  NOTE:  While questing in Feralas, you may come across an item called OOX-22/FE Distress Beacon, from it accept "Find OOX-22/FE!" and turn this in through the small cave at Feral Scar 53.56, but do NOT do the follow-up escort quest.  Also the yetis can be quite dangerous inside the cave so try to avoid that if you can.
WOTLK, 6b) Hearth (or go back) to Camp Mojache, then next to the big tent from Witch Doctor Uzer'i 74.43, turn in "Hippogryph Muisek", accept "Faerie Dragon Muisek".  REMINDER:  Do not sell your Hippogryph Egg.

07) In the big tent turn in "The Mark of Quality" 74.43.
08) Between the two tents, turn in "Zukk'ash Infestation" and "Stinglasher" 75.42.  Accept "Zukk'ash Report".
09) From Rok Orhan /target (she patrols back and forth in the town), turn in "Dark Ceremony" and "The Ogres of Feralas", accept "The Gordunni Orb".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
WOTLK, 9a) Go SW around 69.48 and do "A New Cloak's Sheen" (kill/loot the Sprite Darters), and do "Faerie Dragon Muisek" (use the Faerie Dragon Muisek Vessel quest item on Sprite Darter corpses).  Also do part of "Natural Materials": kill/loot the Sprite Darters for the 6 Encrusted Minerals.

WOTLK, 9b) Hearth (or go back) to Camp Mojache, and at Witch Doctor Uzer'i 74.43 turn in "Faerie Dragon Muisek", accept "Treant Muisek".  Also turn in (if it's complete) "Natural Materials".

WOTLK, 9c) On the eastern side of Camp Mojache, turn in "A New Cloak's Sheen" 76.43, accept "A Grim Discovery".

WOTLK, 9d) Do "Treant Muisek" and finish (if you still need to) "Natural Materials": kill/loot  Wandering Forest Walkers /target for the 2 Splintered Logs and use the Treant Muisek Vessel quest item on Wandering Forest Walker /target corpses.  There is several around 72.43.

WOTLK, 9e) Go north and do "A Grim Discovery": kill/loot the Grimtotem taurens for the horns around 67.46.  There's another camp of mobs just north around 69.40.

WOTLK, 9f) Hearth (or go back) to Camp Mojache, and at Witch Doctor Uzer'i 74.43 turn in "Treant Muisek", accept "Mountain Giant Muisek".  And turn in (if you haven't yet) "Natural Materials".

WOTLK, 9g) On the eastern side of Camp Mojache, turn in "A Grim Discovery" 76.43, accept the next part.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

WOTLK, 9h) Head west on the main path all the way to the goblin 45.43 and accept "Zapped Giants".

WOTLK, 9i) Go north (exit at 47.38) to Ruins of Ravenwind around 40.13 and do "Vengeance on the Northspring": kill the needed Northspring harpies.  Also, keep grinding on harpies until the Horn of Hatetalon drops, then use this item for the next quest:

WOTLK, 9j) Up north in Ruins of Ravenwind do "Dark Heart": use the Horn of Hatetalon quest item at the two big stones 41.9 to summon Edana Hatetalon (lvl 50), then kill/loot her..

WOTLK, 9k) Go just SW to The Twin Colossals around 39.24 and do "Mountain Giant Muisek" along with "Zapped Giants": use Zorbin's Ultra-Shrinker quest item on any giant (this removes the elite status), then use the Mountain Giant Muisek Vessel quest item on the corpses.

WOTLK, 9l) Go south to the goblin next to the dock and turn in "Zapped Giants" 45.43 (#9h on map).

WOTLK, 9m) Hearth (or go back) to Camp Mojache.

WOTLK, 9n) From Witch Doctor Uzer'i 74.43, turn in "Mountain Giant Muisek".  Accept "Weapons of Spirit".  Then instead of waiting for the doc, just do the next step:

WOTLK, 9o) At the other side of Camp Mojache under the hut 76.44, turn in "Dark Heart" and "Vengeance on the Northspring".

WOTLK, 9p) Go back to Witch Doctor Uzer'i 74.43 and turn in "Weapons of Spirit".

10) Go to the Wind Rider Master 75.44 and fly to Orgrimmar, Durotar.
#21

 
Orgrimmar (log)
01) Go to the Bank 50.69 and put in the bank:  Hippogryph Egg, Yeh'kinya's Bramble.  And get the following items out of the bank:  Jeweled Pendant.  Also (depending on what cloth donations you already turned in at Orgrimmar) get your Silk Cloth and Mageweave Cloth out of your bank or mailbox 51.70.  You would need 60 or 120 Silk Cloth and 60 or 120 Mageweave Cloth.  This is for Cloth Donation quests you are about to turn in.
02) Go to The Drag and up in the hut, turn in "Zukk'ash Report" 56.47.
03) Drop down to Magar's Cloth Goods and turn in the cloth donations to the Orc's Horde Cloth Quartermaster 64.51.  Also, if you only have 60 Silk Cloth, save that for the other Silk Cloth turn in later instead!
04) Go in Droffers And Sons Salvage and accept "Ripple Recovery" 60.37.  Next to you, turn that in and accept the next part.
05) Go east to the Valley of Honor at Belgrom Rockmaul 75.34 and turn in "Parts of the Swarm" and "A Grim Discovery".  Accept "Betrayed".
GET NEW ABILITIES, 5b) If you need to, get new spells/abilities. 

07) Go to Grommash Hold in The Valley of Wisdom and turn in "Report to Zor" 39.38.  Accept/Complete "Service to the Horde"
08) Go to Darkbriar Lodge in the Valley of Spirits and turn in "The Gordunni Orb" 39.86.
09) Next to you, turn in as much cloth as you can to the Horde Cloth Quartermaster 38.88 (for the troll faction).
11) Get on the zeppelin (51.14 in Durotar) to go to The Undercity.
#22

 
The Undercity
01) Go to the Bank in the middle of The Undercity 66.44 and put the following items into the bank:  Sealed Letter to Ag'tor.  Get out (if you can) 60 Wool Cloth, then 60 Silk Cloth, and then 60 Mageweave Cloth (for cloth donation quests).
02) N/A
03) Next to you, turn in any cloth you have to the Undead's Horde Cloth Quartermaster 72.29.
04) Go west to The War Quarter to the Weapon Master 57.33 and if you have extra gold to spare, feel free to learn all the weapons from him.
05) Go SW to The Apothecarium entrance 52.80 and accept "Seeping Corruption" 49.71.
06) Next to you in the same room, accept "Errand for Apothecary Zinge" 50.68.
07) Out in the other room, turn in "Errand for Apothecary Zinge" 59.55, accept the next part.
08) Go back down in the Apothecarium Quarter entrance 52.80 and turn in "Errand for Apothecary Zinge" (50.68, #5 on map), accept "Into the Field".
08b) Check the AH 71.47 for a Mithril Casing and if you are able to get it, store it in your bank for now for a later quest at Un'goro Crater.  If you can't get it now, you will be reminded twice more later to see if you can get this.
09) Go to the Bank in the middle of The Undercity (66.44, #1 on map) and put the following items into it:  Kravel's Scheme, Stoley's Bottle, Field Testing Kit and Box of Empty Vials.  Get the following items out:  (if you have one) Swim Speed Potion.
12) Leave the UC and get on the zeppelin to go to Grom'gol, STV (62.59 in Tirisfal Glades).
#25

 
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you should already be either well into level 47 or level 48. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into 47-47 Stranglethorn Vale section below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if you are in your 46s you should consider doing this section.  If you are NOT doing this section then abandon "Testing the Vessel", "Lines of Communication".
47-47 Hinterlands
Here are the prerequisites to this section:
-Accept: "Lines of Communication" in the UC (magic quarter)
-Accept: "Grim Message" at Grom'gol (in STV)

01) Go into the Hinterlands (entrance at 87.29 in Hillsbrad Foothills).  NOTE:  Grind on any Trained Razorbeaks or Wild Gryphons you see along the way as they drop Long Elegant Feathers, you need 10 of these for a later quest, and remember to not sell these to a vendor.
02) Go up to Shindigger's Camp and turn in "Ripple Recovery" 27.49, accept "A Sticky Situation".
03) Go all the way to Revantusk Village and from the Blacksmithing Supplies vendor, accept "Vilebranch Hooligans" 78.80.
04) Up in the hut (next to the wagon), from Mystic Yayo'jin, accept "Cannibalistic Cousins" 79.78.
05) At the other hut next to you, from Otho Moji'ko, accept "Message to the Wildhammer" 79.79.
06) Next to you from the Stable Master 79.80, accept "Stalking the Stalkers", "Hunt the Savages" and "Avenging the Fallen".
07) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.  Before you go out and start questing make sure you stock up heavily on food/water/supplies because you won't be back to town for while.
08) Time to do the good ol' Hinterlands grind!  Do the following 12 steps below.  They don't need to be done in that specific order, but start doing the ones on top first.  Have fun!
#26

 

01) At any time you find an item called OOX-09/HL Distress Beacon, from it accept "Find OOX-09/HL!".  If so, go turn this in in the middle of the lake on top of the hill 49.38, but do NOT do the escort yet!
02) "Stalking the Stalkers": kill Silvermane Stalkers (eastern side of Hinterlands ramp entrance 76.62) and Silvermane Howlers (middle of Hinterlands).
03) "Cannibalistic Cousins": kill Vilebranch Scalpers and Vilebranch Soothsayers (trolls).  There are several camps of these mobs (starting at 71.48), another one around 66.45, 53.39, and 48.67.
04) "Testing the Vessel": use the Wildkin Muisek Vessel quest item on the corpse of any Owlbeast scattered around The Hinterlands.  These include the following Owlbeasts:  Marauding, Primitive, Savage, and Vicious.  Focusing on the Savages /targetthe most (#4 on map), because of the next step:
05) "Hunt the Savages": kill Savage Owlbeasts.  There are only 11 mob spawns around the middle-eastern part of Hinterlands.  (this is also #4 on map)
06) "Vilebranch Hooligans": loot Slagtree's Lost Tools.  There are 5 possible spawn locations (73.53, 71.49, 66.45, 57.43, or 53.39).
07) "A Sticky Situation": loot the Horde Supply Crates in the cave at Skull Rock (entrance at 58.40).  Once this is completed, keep the first 2 steps in mind from the next section.
08) "Avenging the Fallen": kill/loot the Razorbeak Skylord /targetfor the Skylord Plume.  There are only two spawn locations.  One patrols around 54.50, and another one patrols around 68.53.
09) "Lines of Communication": at Quel'Danil Lodge, click on the following 3 scrolls on the ground:  The Highvale Records 32.47 in the building.  The Highvale Notes 30.49 outside by the well, barrel and lamppost.  The Highvale Report 29.46 under the gazebo.  While you are here, keep the next step in mind:
10) "Message to the Wildhammer": kill the required amount of Highvale elves around Quel'Danil Lodge.
11) "Rin'ji is Trapped!": in the building and from Rin'Ji in the cage (31.47, #10 on map), accept the escort from him and then escort him out of  Quel'Danil Lodge.  TIP:  Clear out the path in front of the lodge first.
12) "Grim Message": kill/loot the Witherbark trolls to obtain 20 Witherbark Skulls.  There is one village 32.58 and another one 23.58.  Then use Nimboya's Pike quest item at the Hiri'watha village (32.58, #12 on map).  Use it twice:  first to combine the skulls, then to plant the pike in the ground.  Also while doing this quest, accept "Venom Bottles" from one of those little Venom Bottles on the table (at either village).
#27

 

01) Go to the path (starting at 21.48)...
02) Go up the path to Shindigger's Camp and turn in "A Sticky Situation" 27.49.  Accept "Ripple Delivery".
03) Go east to the little island at the sea (use a Swim Speed Potion if you have one) and click on Rin'ji's Secret tablet 86.59 to turn in "Rin'ji is Trapped!", accept "Rin'ji's Secret".
04) Go to Revantusk Village and from the Blacksmithing Supplies vendor, turn in "Vilebranch Hooligans" 78.80.  NOTE:  Save 10 Long Elegant Feathers you have, as it is used for a later quest, and do not sell them to a vendor.
05) Up in the hut (next to the wagon), from Mystic Yayo'jin, turn in "Cannibalistic Cousins" 79.78.
06) At the other hut next to you, from Otho Moji'ko, turn in "Message to the Wildhammer" 79.79.  Only accept and turn in "Another Message to the Wildhammer" if you have 10 Long Elegant Feathers with you then SKIP "The Final Message to the Wildhammer" (for now).  If not, you will have another opportunity to turn this in later.
07) Next to you from the Stable Master 79.80, turn in "Stalking the Stalkers", "Hunt the Savages" and "Avenging the Fallen".
08) Go to the end of the docks and from the Wind Rider Master 82.82, fly to Hammerfall, Arathi.

10)Go to the Wind Rider Master 82.82 and fly to Tarren Mill, Hillsbrad.
11) Go in the small house 61.19 and turn in "Venom Bottles", accept "Undamaged Venom Sac".
#28

 

01) Go back into the Hinterlands (don't fly) (entrance at 87.29 in Hillsbrad)...
02) Go to Shadra'Alor entrance 36.64, go to the top of the temple (grind mobs along the way), then drop down and turn in "The Atal'ai Exile" 34.75 (See Video on where to find this dude at), accept "Return to Fel'Zerul"
03) In the immediate area, do "Undamaged Venom Sac": kill/loot Witherbark Broodguards (spiders) for the Undamaged Venom Sac (low drop rate).
04) REMINDER:  While questing, you may have found an item called OOX-09/HL Distress Beacon, from it accept "Find OOX-09/HL!".  If so, go turn this in in the middle of the lake on top of the hill 49.38, but do NOT do the escort yet!
05) Hearth to Tarren Mill.
06) In the small house next to the Inn, turn in "Undamaged Venom Sac" (61.19 in Hillsbrad).  NOTE:  Only accept "Consult Master Gadrin" if you still need to get a mount as a troll, as you turn it in in the same spot, otherwise skip it.
07) Go to the Bat Handler 60.19 and fly to Undercity, Tirisfal.
08) Go to the Bank 66.44 in the middle of the UC and get the following items out:  Kravel's Scheme and Stoley's Bottle.  And put in the Bank:  Dran's Ripple Delivery, 10 Wildkin Muisek, Wildkin Muisek Vessel and your stack of Long Elegant Feathers (if you still need to turn in the quest for them).
09) If you still need to, go to The Magic Quarter 72.29 and turn in "A Donation of Mageweave" (you need 60 Mageweave Cloth for this).
10) Next to you at the Magic Quarter 73.33, turn in "Rin'ji's Secret" and "Lines of Communication".  Then complete "Oran's Gratitude".
11) Check the AH 71.47 for a Mithril Casing and if you are able to get it, store it in your bank for now for a later quest at Un'goro Crater.  If you can't get it now, you will be reminded once more later to see if you can get this.
12) Leave the UC and get on the zeppelin to go to Grom'gol (62.59 in Tirisfal Glades).
#29

 
47-47 Stranglethorn Vale (log)
01) N/A
02) N/A
03) Go to the Wind Rider Master 33.29 and fly to Booty Bay, Stranglethorn.
04) When you land, next to you from Fleet Master Seahorn accept "The Bloodsail Buccaneers" 27.77.
05) Drop down in the Inn and make BB your new home 27.77.
06) Next to you in the Inn, turn in "Back to Booty Bay" 27.77.
07) Go in the cabin (next to the hanging shark) and turn in "Deliver to MacKinley" 28.77.
08) N/A
09) Go near the end of the docks and accept "The Captain's Chest" 27.74.
10) Go east to the shore and attempt to do "The Captain's Chest" (Elite): kill/loot Gorlash /target.  This lvl 42 elite giant patrols up and down the shore around 37.70.   You may need to group up with someone, if you cannot do it, it can be skipped.
11) Search the shores east of BB for Half-Buried Bottles until a Carefully Folded Note shows up, from it accept "Message in a Bottle".
12) Go to the island east of BB (use Swim Speed if you can), plus watch out for the lvl 41 stealthed panthers there, then turn in "Message in a Bottle" 39.81, accept the next part.  NOTE:  If the Carefully Folded Note item remains in your inventory, you can destroy it.
13) East on the island do "Message in a Bottle": kill/loot King Mukla /target (lvl 42 big gorilla, at 41.84).
14) Back NW on the island turn in "Message in a Bottle" (#12 on map, 39.81).
#30

 

01) Do "The Bloodsail Buccaneers": kill the 3 pirates in each of the 3 ships.  Also find a scroll laying on the ground (can be in any of the 3 ships) called Cortello's Riddle, and from it accept "Cortello's Riddle".  Sometimes this scroll can be in the basement of the ships, so search there too.  The first pirate to kill is Captain Stillwater down the first flight of stairs in the ship 33.88.  See next steps for the other 2:
02) Kill Fleet Master Firallon down the first flight of stairs in the middle ship 31.91.  Watch out for Ironpatch in the room before Firallon, take him on alone first.
03) Kill Captain Keelhaul down the first flight of stairs in the last ship 29.88.  Make sure you find the scroll that starts "Cortello's Riddle" before leaving this area.  NOTE:  After accepting "Cortello's Riddle" you can then destroy the scroll that started the quest as it will no longer be used for anything.
04) Hearth to BB, if Hearthstone is still on a cooldown, die on purpose and res at spirit.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) Turn in (if you did it) "The Captain's Chest" (at the end of the docks 27.74).
06) Go to the Bank 27.77 and get out 15 Silk Cloth from the bank, mailbox or AH, for a later quest.
07) Up in the Inn, from Fleet Master Seahorn turn in "The Bloodsail Buccaneers" 27.77.
08) This is the last time my guide will tell you to turn in "The Green Hills of Stranglethorn" if it is complete.  This quest requires that you have the following pages: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, and 27.  You can also find these pages in the AH or from other players.  If you have all of them, get them out now and go to Nesingwary's Expedition 36.11 and turn this quest in.  If you do go turn it in, afterwards go to Grom'gol's Wind Rider Master 33.29 and fly to Kargath, Badlands.  Otherwise, skip to next step:
09) Next to you on the roof, go to the Wind Rider Master 27.77 and fly to Kargath, Badlands.
10) Go SW into Searing Gorge (entrance at 1.63 in Badlands).
#31

 
47-48 Searing Gorge (log)
01) Go SE in Searing Gorge and from the Wooden Outhouse 66.62, accept "Caught!".
02) At the Grimesilt Dig Site next to you around 63.62, do "Caught!": kill 8 Dark Iron Geologists /target.  Also make sure you have 15 Silk Cloth (it can drop from the dwarves at the Dig Site).  The Geologists are limited, so you will most likely need to grind on mobs until they respawn (or go north for a few more).
03) Go back to the Wooden Outhouse (66.62, #1 on map), and turn in "Caught!", accept "Ledger from Tanaris".  Then pick up the Goodsteel Ledger (it drops on the ground next to the outhouse).
04) Do part of "Ledger from Tanaris": kill/loot Glassweb Spiders /target for the Leg Shafts.  These are scattered on the eastern side of Searing Gorge starting around 61.70.
05) Go up NW and from Kalaran Windblade 39.39 accept "Divine Retribution", then do it by simply talking to him.  Then turn it in.  From him, accept "The Flawless Flame".
06) Go up to Thorium Point (entrance at 33.30)...
07) Accept ALL quests at Thorium Point:  From the WANTED/Missing/Lost & Found sign 38.26 accept "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius", "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass" and "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition".
WOTLK, 7b) Next to you accept "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation" 38.28.

08) Next to you 39.28 accept "Curse These Fat Fingers", "Fiery Menace!" and "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It"
09) Next to you by the Forge accept "What the Flux?" 39.29.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied by this NPC as well.
10) Get the FP from the Wind Rider Master 35.31 at Thorium Point.
#32

 

NOTE:  Do the Searing Gorge grind!  Do this section all at once (in any order):
01) "The Flawless Flame": kill/loot Magma Elementals, Tempered War Golems, and Heavy War Golems for the Golem Oil.  See next step for the Heart of Flame:
02) Kill/loot the fire elementals for the Heart of Flame, these include:  Inferno Elementals, Blazing Elementals and Magma Elementals.
03) "Fiery Menace!": kill Greater Lava Spiders around the western side of Searing Gorge.
04) "Curse These Fat Fingers": kill Heavy War Golems around the middle of Searing Gorge.
05) "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass": kill/loot Dark Iron Steamsmith for the Smithing Tuyere around 40.50.  See next step for where to find the Lookout's Spyglass:
06) Kill/loot Dark Iron Lookouts for the Lookout's Spyglass, these mobs are scattered around the watchtowers at Searing Gorge, there is a large amount around 53.59, see map for other locations.
07) Once "The Flawless Flame" is done, turn it in at Kalaran Windblade 39.39 and accept "Forging the Shaft".
#33

 

01) "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition": enter one of the caves down in The Cauldron (middle of Searing Gorge), I recommend going into the eastern most cave entrance 49.45 and then kill the required amount of Dark Iron dwarves in the cave.
02) "What the Flux?" and "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius": loot the Secret Plans: Fiery Flux 40.36 behind Overseer Maltorius (41.36 in the cave). And kill/loot Overseer Maltorius (lvl 50).
WOTLK, 2b) In the hallways across the room around 45.31 do "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation": loot the pillows along side the walls where the beds are at.  Watch out groups of sleepy dwarves patrol this hall and an elite mob can spawn in the middle of that hall, so be careful!  You can skip this if you don't want to wait for the pillows to respawn or deal with the mobs that are in the way.

03) "Forging the Shaft": kill/loot Dark Iron dwarves for the Thorium Plated Daggers.  NOTES:  This quest requires "The Flawless Flame" to be finished and turned in first.  Save any Dark Iron Scraps you find, you will need 30 of them for a later quest, but you will also have another good chance to find a lot more later on in the lvl 53-54 Burning Steppes section as well.
04) "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It": enter the cave (at 47.46) and kill 20 Incendosaurs.
#34

 

01) Go to Kalaran Windblade 39.39 and turn in "Forging the Shaft", accept "The Flame's Casing".
02) Go up to Thorium Point (entrance at 33.30)...
03) Turn in "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius" 38.27.
WOTLK, 3b) Next to you, turn in (if you did it) "Kill 'Em With Sleep Deprivation" 38.28.

04) Next to you, turn in "Curse These Fat Fingers", "Fiery Menace!" and "Incendosaurs? Whateverosaur is More Like It" 39.28.
05) Next to you, turn in "STOLEN: Smithing Tuyere and Lookout's Spyglass" and "JOB OPPORTUNITY: Culling the Competition" 39.28.
06) At the Forge next to you turn in "What the Flux?" 39.29.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied from this NPC.
07) Go west around 25.35 and do "The Flame's Casing": kill/loot the Twilight mobs until the Symbol of Ragnaros drops (low drop rate).
08) Go east to Kalaran Windblade (39.39, #1 on map) and turn in "The Flame's Casing", accept "The Torch of Retribution".
09) Wait a few seconds, then turn in "The Torch of Retribution", accept the next part.  Then click on the Torch of Retribution in the air next to you to turn in the next part of "The Torch of Retribution".  Accept "Squire Maltrake".  Next to you, turn in "Squire Maltrake", accept "Set Them Ablaze!".
#35

 

01) Do "Set Them Ablaze!": equip the Torch of Retribution quest item and at the top of the 4 watchtowers, click on the Sentry Braziers.  The Northern Tower is at 33.54.  See next steps for the others:
02) The Western Tower is at 36.61.
03) The Southern Tower is at 44.61.
04) The Eastern Tower is at 50.55.  NOTE:  You must cross a bridge to get to it.
05) While doing the quests associated with killing the Dark Iron dwarves, you should have obtained an item called Grimsite Outhouse Key, from it accept "The Key to Freedom".  Then go SE and turn this in at the Wooden Outhouse 66.62.
06) N/A

07) Go NW to Kalaran Windblade 39.39 and turn in "Set Them Ablaze!".
08) Wait a minute (build up First Aid while waiting), then click on the Hoard of the Black Dragonflight 39.39 chest on the ground next to Kalaran Windblade and accept "Trinkets...".  Then click on the chest again to turn this in.  Loot the chest received.
09) NOTES:  Make sure you keep the Black Dragonflight Molt in your inventory as you will need it for a later quest.  Also make sure you have all 20 Solid Crystal Leg Shafts for the quest "Ledger from Tanaris".
10) Head south into Burning Steppes (entrance at 35.87 in Searing Gorge).  NOTE:  You have to go through Black Rock Mountains.
11) Once at Burning Steppes, first head to 25.53 just to discover Draco'dar for XP.
12) N/A
13) Go up the ramp into Flame Crest.  You can get repaired/resupplied in the small cave from the female goblin 66.22.
14) At the Wind Rider Master 66.24 fly to Stonard, Swamp of Sorrows.  NOTE:  If you still have "What the Flux?" or "WANTED: Overseer Maltorius" they can be abandoned.
#36

 
48-49 Swamp of Sorrows (log)
01) Go in the Inn and make Stonard your new home from the Innkeeper 45.57.
02) Go SW on the main path and from Fallen Hero of the Horde 34.66 accept "Fall From Grace".  Then do it by simply talking to the NPC.  Turn it in and accept "The Disgraced One".
03) Go NW and click on A Soggy Scrolll under the bridge 23.48 to turn in "Cortello's Riddle", accept the next part.

#08

 
51-51 Blasted Lands
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you will most likely be well into level 51+. Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into the section right before 51-53 Un'Goro Crator below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are still level 50 you can consider doing this section.  If you are NOT doing this section then abandon (if you have any of them): "The Stones That Bind Us".

01) Go up in the Inn on the roof and from the Wind Rider Master 27.77 fly to Stonard, Swamp of Sorrows.
02) Follow the main path SW and from the Fallen Hero of the Horde 34.66 accept "The Stones That Bind Us".
03) Head south into Blasted Lands...
04) As you enter Blasted Lands go to the small quest hub at 51.14.  Accept all 5 quests there.  These 5 quests requires the following total number of items to complete them.  The items needed can drop without needing to accept the quests.  The color codes below show where the mobs are at on the images and stars represent rare mobs that may not be there. Work on the rest of this section all at once):
Mob - Total Items Needed - After "The Decisive Striker" - And then "Vulture's Vigor"
05) Vulture Gizzards     =14 - 12 - 2
06) Basilisk Brain         =11 - 11 - 11
07) Blasted Boar Lungs =6 - 5 - 5
08) Scorpok Pincers      =6 - 3 - 3
09) Snickerfang Jowls   =5 - 5 - 3
10) 2+ PLAYERS (or could be soloable): While grinding for these items, do "The Stones That Bind Us": drag the different servants close to their Stone of Binding crystal next to each of them, then kill them along with attacking the crystal as well.  This allows you to actually kill the mob and get quest credit.  Each of these different servants are located at different parts of the Blasted Lands.  See map for locations.  If for some reason you cannot solo this quest, it can be skipped.
11) While grinding make sure you get everything discovered for XP (including The Tainted Scar at 44.57).
12) As you get these items, you can turn the quests in for a 60 min stat reward that will help you grind faster in Blasted Lands or even for the next section Un'Goro Crater (although they don't stack).  I recommend only showing the quests on screen that you want the rewards first.  Go to the quest hub (at 51.14, #1 on map) and turn in all 5 of these quests:  "Snickerfang Jowls" (+25 Strength), "A Boar's Vitality" (+25 Stamina), "The Decisive Striker" (+25 Agility), "The Basilisk's Bite" (+25 Intellect) and "Vulture's Vigor" (+25 Spirit).  NOTE:  The follow up quests do NOT yield XP: "Rage of Ages", "Spirit of the Boar", "Salt of the Scorpok", "Infallible Mind", and "Spiritual Domination".
13) While questing in Blasted Lands, you may have came across an item called Imperfect Draenethyst Fragment, turn these in at 52.36 for the quest "Everything Counts In Large Amounts".  NOTE:  I have NEVER found a Flawless Draenethyst Sphere for the quest "To Serve Kum'isha" ..good luck on that.  Also, the follow up quest "One Draenei's Junk" is repeatable but yields no XP.
14) Go to the border of Blasted Lands and SoS, to the Fallen Hero of the Horde (34.66 in SoS) and turn in (if you did it) "The Stones That Bind Us", SKIP "Kirith".  Accept "Heroes of Old".
15) Next to you on the other side of the path, turn in (if you have it) "Heroes of Old" (33.66 in SoS), accept the next part.  Then click on the Spectral Lockbox on the ground next to you to turn it in.
16) Hearth to Ratchet.  NOTE:  If you did not do "The Stones That Bind Us", then abandon the quest.
17) At Ratchet's Bank 63.37, get the following items out:  Violet Tragan and (if you have it) Stone Circle. 
18) Go into Ironzar's Imported Weaponry to get repaired/resupplied from Ironzar 62.37.  You can destroy any remaining unsellable Blasted Lands beast items you have (or bank them if you want).

02) As you enter Gadgetzan (next to the crane 52.27), accept "Super Sticky".

05) Next to the AH, from Marin Noggenfogger 52.29, turn in "Delivery for Marin".  NOTE:  "Noggenfogger Elixir" can take up to a minute for the NPC to make the item for you, it does not yeild any XP, so you can do this when you are not in a hurry (such as after level 60).  The Noggenfogger Elixir item is used for fun and can be stored in the bank when you want to use it.
06) At the Bank 52.29, get out: (if you have it) Mithril Casing (this item may be in your mailbox 52.28), this is used for a quest in Un'Goro Crater, and remember to not sell this to a vendor.  You can also check the neutral AH in Gadgetzan for it as well.
07) Head south to the Broken Pillar and turn in "The Stone Circle" 53.46.
08) Go SW into Un'Goro Crater (entrance at 27.54 in Tanaris)...
#10

 
(log)
01) At the bottom of the path into Un'Goro Crater, go up the hill next to the waterfall 72.76 and accept "The Apes of Un'Goro" and "The Fare of Lar'korwi".
WOTLK, 1b) Just down the hill a bit, accept "Toxic Tolerance" 71.74.

02) Go north a bit and do "The Fare of Lar'korwi": loot the big Fresh Threshadon Carcass lying on the ground 69.57.
03) Grind north to the entrance of Marshal's Refuge and accept "Chasing A-Me 01" 46.13.  While on your way, also start collecting crystals (the red, blue, green and yellow) scattered everywhere.  Kill/loot oozes too!
04) Go up into Marshal's Refuge and accept ALL quests there:  From the goblin on top of the hill, accept "Shizzle's Flyer" 44.12.
05) Move into Marshal's Refuge 44.8 and accept "Lost!", (from the Wanted Sign Beware of Pterrordax) "Beware of Pterrordax".
06) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.
07) Next to you accept "Roll the Bones" 43.7.
08) Next to you 44.7 accept "Alien Ecology" and "Expedition Salvation".  NOTE:  If you have already found A Mangled Journal, accept and turn in "Williden's Journal".
09) On the east side of Marshal's Refuge behind the hill, accept "Larion and Muigin" 46.9.
10) Time to do the Un'Goro Grind! NOTES For the Grind:
  - Don't worry about looting the Un'Goro Dirt Piles, you will find enough Un'Goro Soil from mob drops.  Also do not sell the Un'Goro Soil to vendors, you will need these for quest turn-ins!
  - Don't worry about looting the Bloodpetal Sprouts, as they are not turned in for any XP.
  - The Dinosaur Bones you need for the quest "Roll the Bones" will drop from many different mobs as you do the other quests.
  - As your questing, kill/loot Oozes (slimes) you see, and collect15 Ooze samples (15 is the max you can hold for WOTLK), this is for "... and a Batch of Ooze".  These Oozes are scattered all around Un'Goro Crater.
  - Also while questing, loot 7 crystals of each color (red, blue, green, and yellow) for a later quest.  These crystals are scattered all around Un'Goro Crater.
  - The Bloodpetel mobs (plants) drop Savage Fronds, you could collect 30 of these for a later quest at lvl 59.  As you are doing the Un'Goro quests associated with these mobs you should get about half, so grind on these that you see along the way and see what you can get before leaving Un'Goro Crater, and you could always check the AH for more of these, or just skip this entirely.
 - Watch out for the Elite Devilsaurs patrolling around this zone.  I highlighted them through out the images so you can be alerted if you are in a danger zone for them.
#11

 

LET THE GRIND BEGIN:
01) Just south of Marshal's Refuge around 46.16 do "Super Sticky": kill/loot the Tar mobs around the tar pits.
02) Go NE to Fungal Rock and start working on "The Apes of Un'Goro": kill/loot the apes (in and outside of the cave).  Also go in the cave entrance 64.16 and then make a right and turn in "Chasing A-Me 01" 68.17, then if you have a Mithril Casing with you, accept and turn in "Chasing A-Me 01".  Then accept and do the escort "Chasing A-Me 01": escort A-Me 01 to Marshal's Refuge, and then turn it in once you get there.
03) Start working on "Larion and Muigin": kill Bloodpetal Flayers around the north-middle of Un'goro Crater.  NOTE:  These mobs are also found south-middle as well, so you don't need to finish all of this now, work your way to the next step..
04) Go up on the mountain ledge (ramp entrance at 55.12) and start working on "Shizzle's Flyer": kill/loot the Pterrordax /target.  There are only 6 mob spawns here, so clear the area once and move on to the next step..
05) Make sure "The Apes of Un'Goro" is finished: kill/loot the apes (in and outside of the cave around 64.16, #2 on map).
06) Do the next part of "Larion and Muigin": kill Bloodpetal Lashers and Threshers around the NE side of Un'goro Crater.  Also, kill/loot any Dinosaurs you see for part of "Shizzle's Flyer" (don't need to finish it now though).  Keep the next step in mind:
07) Go SE and do part of "Expedition Salvation": under the tent, loot the Crate of Foodstuffs 69.37.
08) Finish up killing the Bloodpetal Flayers for "Larion and Muigin" by starting below the river around 61.48 and work you way south.  Kill/loot any Diemetradon you see as well for "Shizzle's Flyer".  NOTE:  Try to avoid the Raptors as you will be grinding them soon with more quests associated with them.
09) Grind your way SE to Torwa Pathfinder on top of the hill by the waterfall and turn in "The Apes of Un'Goro" 72.76.  Accept "The Mighty U'cha".  And turn in "The Fare of Lar'korwi", accept "The Scent of Lar'korwi".
#12

 

01) Just to the west of you at The Marshlands do "Bone-Bladed Weapons" along with "The Scent of Lar'korwi": kill/loot raptors and to get the Ravasaur Pheromone Glands you need, run across the egg nests (there is 5 total: 67.73 63.77 67.67 61.72 62.66) and then kill/loot the Lar'korwi Mates that spawn in.  Keep the next step in mind:
WOTLK, 1b) Also more north in the raptor area around 69.53 do "Toxic Tolerance" by letting the Venomhide Ravasaurs /target (darker colored ones) spit poison on you 20 times.

02) At The Marshlands, at the western most pond, north on it's shore, click on A Wrecked Raft 63.69 to accept "It's a Secret to Everybody".  Then right next to the wrecked raft, under the water, click on A Small Pack to turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", accept the next part.  NOTE:  To free up bag space, do NOT loot A Small Pack yet!
WOTLK, 2b) Just down the hill a bit, turn in "Toxic Tolerance" 71.74, accept "Venomhide Eggs".

03) Go east to Torwa Pathfinder on top of the hill by the waterfall and turn in "The Scent of Lar'korwi" 72.76, accept "The Bait for Lar'korwi".  NOTE:  To free up bag space, do NOT loot A Small Pack yet!
WOTLK, 3b) Around the raptor area around 67.73 do "Venomhide Eggs": loot 4 Venomhide Eggs (around the little nests as with previous quest), see Questie locations on map.

WOTLK, 3c) Turn in "Venomhide Eggs".  NOTE: "They Grow Up So Fast" can be done if you like, but requires items you would need to search around for, also costs 80 gold.  But this can reward you with a new epic Whistle of the Venomhide Ravasaur mount.

04) Go west to The Slithering Scar and do "Alien Ecology" along with "Bungle in the Jungle": enter the cave entrance 50.81.  Make a left in the cave and then use your Unused Scraping Vial quest item on the mound in the center of the room.  And keep kill/looting the Gorishi bugs until you get the Gorishi Scent Gland (can drop from any of them, but has a low drop rate).  These bugs can be found in and outside of the cave.
05) Go south up at the ledges around 56.90 to continue obtaining the Webbed Pterrordax Scales (you will be able to get more of these later) for "Shizzle's Flyer".  There is another spot of these mobs around 45.90.
06) Grind NW a bit to the old camp site 38.66 and do the last part of "Expedition Salvation": loot the Research Equipment (looks like a stack of brown boxes next to a tent).
07) All around the western side of Un'Goro Crater, finish up "Larion and Muigin" (kill Bloodpetal Trappers) along with finishing up "Beware of Pterrordax", "Shizzle's Flyer" and "Roll the Bones" (kill/loot Frenzied Pterrordax).  The Dinosaur Bones can also drop from Elder Diemetradon and regular Diemetradon mobs (found south-middle).  Keep the next step in mind:
08) At the Golakka Hot Springs (below the hotspring mountain), accept "Finding the Source" 31.50.
#13

 

01) Do "Volcanic Activity" along with "Finding the Source".  First go to the north entrance at Fire Plume Ridge (spiky bridge entrance at 53.43).  Follow the path upwards to the top and use Krakle's Thermometer quest item at the Fire Plume Ridge Hot Spot (the one at 50.46).  Watch out for the lvl 56 Elite mob in the cave at the top of the Ridge.  Also kill/loot the fire elementals around Fire Plume Ridge for the 9 Un'Goro Ash and to start collecting Core of Elements for a future quest (I recommend at least getting about 10 for now).
02) Make sure all the quests above are done, including obtaining 7 crystals of each color (red, blue, green, and yellow), then go to the small cave at the eastern side of Fire Plume Ridge and turn in "Lost!" 52.50.
03) Accept and do the escort "A Little Help From My Friends": escort Ringo /target back to Marshal's Refuge 44.9.  You will need to use the quest item Spraggle's Canteen on Ringo to wake him up if he faints.
04) Turn in ALL quests at Marshal's Refuge and accept new ones:  Turn in "Shizzle's Flyer" (on top of hill 44.12).
05) turn in "A Little Help From My Friends" and "Beware of Pterrordax" 44.9.
06) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.
07) Next to you turn in "Roll the Bones"43.7.
08) Next to you 44.7 turn in "Alien Ecology", "Expedition Salvation" and (if you haven't yet) accept and turn in "Williden's Journal" from the item A Mangled Journal (this item/quest is obtained while killing any mob in Un'Goro Crater that should be in your inventory by now).
09) Enter the cave at Marshal's Refuge entrance 43.6 and at the very end of the cave accept and turn in "Crystals of Power" 42.3.  Then accept the 3 Pylon quests:  "The Northern Pylon", "The Eastern Pylon" and "The Western Pylon".  You can destroy any remaining crystals in your inventory (to free bag space).
10) Open and loot A Small Pack in your inventory and then go to Linken (in the middle of Marshal's Refuge behind some trees 45.8) and turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", accept the next part.  NOTE:  The Faded Photograph is not needed and will remain in your inventory forever unless you destroy or bank it.
11) At the eastern side of Marshal's Refuge behind the hill, turn in "Larion and Muigin" 46.9, accept "Marvon's Workshop".
#14

 

01) Go east up on the ledges and do "The Northern Pylon": click on the Northern Crystal Pylon 57.13.
02) Go east to Fungal Rock and do "The Mighty U'cha": enter the cave entrance 65.16, then keep left in the cave until you get to U'cha (lvl 55 red colored ape) and kill/loot him.
03) On the eastern side of Un'Goro Crater, go up on the ledges and do "The Eastern Pylon": click on the Eastern Crystal Pylon 77.50.
04) Just to the east of you on the mountain ledges there, do "The Bait for Lar'korwi": at the old dinosaur bones, there is a gray flat rock on the ground at 80.50.  At this spot open your Torwa's Pouch quest item and first use the Preserved Threshadon Meat quest item, then use the Preserved Pheromone Mixture at this spot, then kill/loot Lar'korwi that spawns in (See Video).
05) Go south to Torwa Pathfinder on top of the hill by the waterfall 72.76 and turn in "The Bait for Lar'korwi" and "The Mighty U'cha".
06) Go west (kill/looting oozes along the way if you still need to get the 15 Slime samples) and up on the mountain ledges do "The Western Pylon": click on the Western Crystal Pylon 24.59.
07) Go NE to Golakka Hot Springs (below the hotspring mountain) and turn in "Finding the Source" 31.50, accept "The New Springs".
08) Grind your way back north to Marshal's Refuge entrance 46.13.  Make sure you get all 15 Un'goro Slime Samples along the way by kill/looting oozes (slimes).  Also at this time see if you can obtain 30 Savage Fronds from the Bloodpetel mobs (plants all around Un'Goro), 30 of these can be used for a later quest at lvl 59.  If you are still far behind on that then don't worry about it, you may find more in the AH (or just skip it).
09) Enter the cave at Marshal's Refuge entrance 43.6 and work your way to the end of the cave and turn in "The Northern Pylon", "The Eastern Pylon" and "The Western Pylon".  Then accept and turn in "Making Sense of It".  NOTE:  The Crystal Pylon User's Manual can be destroyed or saved in your bank for later reference and you can always get a new one from this NPC in the cave.
10) Leave the cave, make sure you are repaired/resupplied 43.8.
11) Go up on the eastern side of Marshal's Refuge, to the Flight Master 45.6 and fly to Gadgetzan, Tanaris.
#15

 

01) As you enter Gadgetzan (next to the crane) turn in "Super Sticky" 52.27.
02) In the NW hut, turn in "Bungle in the Jungle" 51.27.
03) Go to the Wind Rider Master 52.25 (north of Gadgetzan) and fly to Thunder Bluff, Mulgore.
04) Go to Elder Rise and from Arch Druid Hamuul Runetotem, accept "Un'goro Soil" 79.29.
05) On the north side of Elder Rise, turn in "Un'goro Soil" 77.22.
06) Go back to Arch Druid Hamuul Runetotem and accept "Morrowgrain Research" (79.29, #4 on map).
07) Go to Bashana Runetotem at Elder Rise in the hut by the bridge and turn in "Morrowgrain Research" 71.34, SKIP the next part.
WOTLK, 8b) If you need to, you can upgrade First Aid from the First Aid Trainer 30.21 on The Spirit Rise.  NOTE: Heavy Mageweave Bandage requires skill 240, Runecloth Bandage skill 260 and Heavy Runecloth Bandage skill 290.

09) Hearth to Ratchet.
10) In Liv Rizzlefix's hut 62.39, turn in "Marvon's Workshop", SKIP "Zapper Fuel" (Dungeon quest).  Also turn in "Volcanic Activity".
11) NOTE:  IF the boat is already at the docks then do this step at BB's Bank instead:  At the Ratchet Bank 63.37 get the following items out:  stack of Pupellyverbos Port, Black Dragonflight Molt and your stack of Dark Iron Scraps.  And put the following items in the bank:  stack of Un'Goro soil, Un'goro Slime Samples, stack of Savage Fronds, Linken's Training Sword, 8 White Ravasaur Claws and any Core of Elements in there (to free up bag space).
12) Go to the end of the docks at Ratchet 64.39 to get on the boat to go to BB.
13) Once at BB, go in the Inn and turn in (if you haven't yet) "Whiskey Slim's Lost Grog" 27.77.

13a) Go up in the Inn to the roof at the Wind Rider Master 27.77 and fly to Kargath, Badlands.
14) At Kargath, just outside of the Inn by the Bonfire, turn in "Vivian Lagrave" 3.48, SKIP "Lost Thunderbrew Recipe" (Dungeon).
15) Next to you, accept "The Rise of the Machines".
16) Find Thal'trak Proudtusk /target, he patrols south and east of Kargath (or could be chillin by the Bonfire).  From him accept "Dreadmaul Rock".  NOTE: Sometimes you must wait until he stops patrolling and then stands by the Bonfire 3.48 in order to accept this.
17) Go to the Wind Rider Master 4.45 and fly to Flame Crest, Burning Steppes.
#16

 
53-53 Burning Steppes (log)
01) Once at Flame Crest, under the red tent 65.24, accept: "Broodling Essence" and "Tablet of the Seven".
02) Go in the small cave and turn in "Yuka Screwspigot" 66.22, SKIP "Ribbly Screwspigot" (Dungeon).
03) Go east around 80.29 and do "Broodling Essence": use the Draco-Incarcinatrix 900 quest item on Broodlings (dragon whelps), then kill them and loot the Broodling Essence crystal that drops.  Also throughout questing in Burning Steppes, kill any dragon whelps you see, they are squishy mobs that die real fast.  Keep the next step in mind:
04) With the Black Dragonflight Molt with you, work your way east to the small cave 95.32 then accept and turn in "A Taste of Flame" (you will need to talk to him first after getting the quest), SKIP the next part (Dungeon quest).
05) Go up to the top of Dreadmaul Rock (bridge entrance at 76.38).  Once at the top do "Dreadmaul Rock": click on Sha'ni Proudtusk's Remains 80.46 (laying on the table between four pillars).  Then from the ghost of Sha'ni Proudtusk that spawns, turn in "Dreadmaul Rock", accept "Krom'Grul".
06) Do "Krom'Grul": find Krom'Grul /target inside one of the three caves at Dreadmaul Rock and kill/loot him.  First check the small cave next to you at the top of Dreadmaul Rock 82.44.  If he is not there, then check the other 2 caves SW entrance 77.47.  For the cave on the eastern side NE entrance 82.39, keep making right turns until you get to him.  ALSO:  Make sure you collect a total of 30 Dark Iron Scraps, this will be used for a later quest.  All the Ogres in Burning Steppes can drop them.
07) Go west to Ruins of Thaurissan around 67.38 and do "The Rise of the Machines": kill/loot War Reavers.  Keep the next step in mind:
08) Do "Tablet of the Seven": loot the Tablet of the Seven 54.41 next to the dwarf statue.
09) Go to Flame Crest and under the red tent 65.24, turn in "Broodling Essence", accept "Felnok Steelspring".  Also turn in "Tablet of the Seven".
10) Go to the Wind Rider Master at Flame Crest 66.24 and fly to Kargath, Badlands.
#17

 

01) At Kargath, just outside of the Inn, turn in "The Rise of the Machines" 3.48, accept the next part.  Find Thal'trak Proudtusk /target, he patrols south and east of Kargath (or could be chillin by the Bonfire).  From him, turn in "Krom'Grul".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
02) Go east to The Dustbowl at the gnome and turn in "The Rise of the Machines" 26.45, SKIP the next part (Dungeon quest).
03) Hearth to Ratchet.
04) Go to the Ratchet Bank 63.37 and put the following items into it:  30+ Dark Iron Scraps, and any Core of Elements.  And get the following items out:  8 White Ravasaur Claws, Tinkee's Letter and Linken's Training Sword.  Also get out (if you have it) 60 Runecloth for cloth donation to the Orc faction coming up at Orgrimmar.
05) Go to the Flight Master 63.37 at Ratchet and fly to Orgrimmar, Durotar.
06) Go in the Inn at Orgrimmar and make it your new home 54.68.
#18

 
PLEASE READ: With all the faster leveling changes, by the time you get to this section you already be level 54 (or really close to it). Because of this when following my guide, you should skip this entire section and go right into the section right before 54-54 Felwood below.  I will leave this section for reference purposes only.  However if for some reason you are early into level 53 you can consider doing this section.  If you are NOT doing this section then abandon (if you have it) "Betrayed".
53-54 Azshara
00) Go up to the Wind Rider Master 45.64 and fly to Valormok, Azshara.
01) Once at Valormok, accept "Betrayed" 22.51.
02) Go NE to the hill entrance 53.20...
03) Go up to the top of the hill and accept "Kim'jael Indeed!" 53.22.
04) Go south to Thalassian Base Camp and do "Kim'jael Indeed!" along with "Betrayed": kill the needed Blood Elves, loot Kim'jael's Equipment boxes on the ground and to Find Magus Rimtori's camp (go to 60.31).
05) At the same coordinates as previous step (60.31, #4 on map), from the Kaldorei Tome of Summoning (book on pedestal), turn in "Betrayed", accept the next part.
06) At that same spot, do the next part of "Betrayed": click on an Arcane Focusing Crystal to summon Magus Rimtori and kill/loot her.  You may need to face some guards first so be prepared.
07) Go north to the hill entrance (53.20, #2 on map)...
08) Go up to the top of the hill and turn in "Kim'jael Indeed!" 53.22.  Accept "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment".
#19

 

01) Drop down to The Shattered Strand 51.36 (be careful, its a long drop), then do "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment": kill/loot Spitelash naga until Some Rune drops (low drop rate).
02) Leave The Shattered Strand, (an exit is at 46.38, see map for more exits)..
03) Go to Valormok and turn in "Betrayed" 22.51, accept the next part.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
04) Head east to the hill entrance 53.20...
05) Go up to the top of the hill and turn in "Kim'jael's "Missing" Equipment" 53.22.
06) At this point you should almost be lvl 54.  Grind the rest of the way to level 54.5 (10 bars into 54) on Blood Elves around Thalassian Base Camp around 58.28.  NOTES:  Blood Elves are squishy mobs (mobs that have 30% fewer health/armor, than the average mob that level), so they die relatively fast and yield good XP/hour.  They also are a good farm spot for Runecloth, which you will need for cloth donations and First Aid.  OPTIONAL:  Instead of the grinding, you could go do BRD instance instead.
07) Hearth to Orgrimmar.
#20

 
54-54 Felwood

05) N/A

07) Go SW and up to the Wind Rider Master 45.64 and fly to Splintertree Post, Ashenvale.

#21

 
(log)
00) Go north a bit into Felwood to Emerald Sanctuary and get the FP from the Flight Master 52.82 (#1 on map).

01) Accept "Forces of Jaedenar" 51.82.  And next to you in the tent, accept "Verifying the Corruption" 51.82.
02) To the west just off of the main path at Maybess Riverbreeze 47.83, accept "Cleansing Felwood".
03) Start working on "A Sample of Slime...": clear the Cursed Ooze mobs (slimes) at the two green pools (one at around 41.72, and another one at around 42.68).  Eventually get 15 Felwood Slime Samples (once again, 15 is the max you can hold for WOTLK).  You don't need to get all of them now.
04) Go north a bit to Jaedenar entrance 40.58 and do "Forces of Jaedenar": kill the required amount of Jaedenar mobs in the area.

#22

 

01) Go up north a bit on the east side of the path to Shatter Scar Vale around 41.42 and do "Verifying the Corruption": walk across one of the craters in the Vale to get it explored, and kill the required amount of Entropic fire elementals.

#23

 
54-55 Winterspring (log)
01) Right outside of the cave, accept "Winterfall Activity" (28.35 in Winterspring).  Also turn in "Speak to Salfa".
02) Follow the main path south to Donova Snowden 31.45 and accept "Threat of the Winterfall".  Turn in "The New Springs", accept "Strange Sources".  Also turn in "It's a Secret to Everybody", SKIP "The Videre Elixir".  NOTE:  You do not have to wait for the NPC, just leave..
03) Follow the main path east and just to your left as you enter Everlook accept "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38.
04) From Felnok Steelspring under the blue tent 62.39, turn in "Felnok Steelspring", SKIP "Chillwind Horns" (for now).
TBC, 4b) Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, accept "Ursius of the Shardtooth".

05) South in Everlook, go in the Inn and make Everlook your new home from the Innkeeper 61.39.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
06) Also in the Inn accept "The Everlook Report", "Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff" and "Sister Pamela".
07) Go to Everlook's Bank 61.37 and put the following items in there:  15 Blood Amber, Felwood Slime Samples, Everlook Report, Studies in Spirit Speaking any Core of Elements and (if you have it) Deadwood Ritual Totem.
