TBC, 9b) As you go questing, keep an eye out for a bear named Ursius /target (he looks just like all other other ones), if so, kill him for "Ursius of the Shardtooth", but don't intentionally try to find him now:

10) Exit Everlook to the right, then go east to Winterfall Village around 67.35 and do"Winterfall Activity": kill the required amount of Winterfall furbolgs.  NOTE:  This place is usually busy with lvl 60+ players farming Winterfall Firewater here.  If that's the case then this quest can be skipped (there is no follow up).  You can also skip this if you are only level 53 right now as it is kind of a higher level quest.
11) Just north of Everlook (around the mountains 61.33) do part of "Wild Guardians": kill 15 Raging Owlbeasts /target.  Also start working on "Are We There, Yeti?" by kill/looting Ice Thistle Yeti.  Also try to finish "Ursius of the Shardtooth" by finding and killing Ursius /target (white bear), he patrols around on the mountains.
#24

 

01) Go west starting around 54.37 and finish "Wild Guardians" (kill 15 Ragged Owlbeasts /target) and continue working on "Are We There, Yeti?" (kill/loot Rogue Ice Thistle yeti).  Also work on the next step as well:
02) Do "Threat of the Winterfall": kill the required amount of Winterfall furbolgs.  There are two camps of these mobs (one at around 42.43 and one at around 32.37).  While doing this quest you should find an item, which starts "Winterfall Firewater" (accept it).
03) Go SW to Donova Snowden 31.45 and turn in "Strange Sources", "Threat of the Winterfall" and "Winterfall Firewater", accept "Falling to Corruption".
04) Hearth (or go back) to Everlook.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
TBC, 4b) IF YOU DID IT:  Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, turn in "Ursius of the Shardtooth".

05) Go SE a bit to Ice Thistle Hills around 66.42 (#7 on map) and finish "Are We There, Yeti?": kill/loot Yeti for the Thick Yeti Fur.
06) Hearth (or go back) to Everlook and near the entrance next to the hut, turn in "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38, accept the next part.
07) Go SE a bit back to Ice Thistle Hills around 66.42 and do "Are We There, Yeti?": kill/loot Ice Thistle Matriarchs and Ice Thistle Patriarchs for the 2 horns (low drop rate).  There is a higher concentration of these mobs in the cave entrance up at 68.42.
08) Hearth (or go back) to Everlook and turn in  "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38, accept the next part.  NOTE:  If you are deep in the cave you can just die on purpose and res at spirit to get back to Everlook.
09) Do part of "Are We There, Yeti?": at Legacki 62.39 /target (next to the Inn), use Umi's Mechanical Yeti quest item to scare her.
10) Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
11) Go to Everlook's Bank 61.37 and put the following items into it:  Umi's Mechanical Yeti and (if you have it) Winterfall Ritual Totem.  Get out of the Bank: 15 Blood Amber.
12) Exit Everlook to your right and go to the Wind Rider Master 60.36 to fly to Bloodvenom Post, Felwood.
#25

 
55-56 Felwood (log)
01) Once at Bloodvenom Post, turn in "Wild Guardians" 35.53, SKIP the next part (for now).
TBC, 1a) At the Wind Rider Master 34.54 fly to Emerald Sanctuary.

02) At Emerald Sanctuary turn in "Forces of Jaedenar" 51.82, accept "Collection of the Corrupt Water".  And next to you in the tent, turn in "Verifying the Corruption" 51.82.

03) Go west to Maybess Riverbreeze and turn in "Cleansing Felwood" 47.83.  Then click the NPC again and talk to her to get a Cenarion Beacon.

04) Just south a bit next to the main path, from Grazle (furblog 51.85), accept "Timbermaw Ally" (no exclamation mark).
05) Go south to Deadwood Village and do "Timbermaw Ally" along with "A Husband's Last Battle": kill the required amount of Deadwood furbolgs and also kill/loot Overlord Ror (gray colored furbolg 48.94).
06) Go north a bit along the main path to Grazle /target (51.85, #4 on map) and turn in "Timbermaw Ally".  Accept "Speak to Nafien".
07) Go up NW in Ruins of Constellas and do "Well of Corruption": use your Hardened Flasket quest item in the green moonwell there 32.67.
08) Follow the main path north to enter Jaedenar entrance 41.58..
09) Go west in Jaedenar and do "Collection of the Corrupt Water": use your Empty Canteen quest item in the green moonwell 35.60.
10) Go north to Bloodvenom Post and turn in "Well of Corruption" (34.52, #1 on map).  Wait a few seconds (do next step while waiting), then accept "Corrupted Sabers".
11) Also at Bloodvenom Post, turn in "A Husband's Last Battle" 35.53.  NOTE:  Do not sell your Corrupted Soul Shards to a vendor.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

TBC, 12) At Bloodvenom Post, go to the Wind Rider Master 34.54 and fly to Emerald Sanctuary.
#26

 

01) At Emerald Sanctuary turn in "Collection of the Corrupt Water" 51.82, SKIP "Seeking Spiritual Aid".

02) Go west to Maybess Riverbreeze and turn in "Salve Via Hunting" 47.83.  NOTES:  If for some reason you do not have the 6 Corrupted Soul Shards, grind on any mob in Felwood until you get them.  The quest is repeatable, but doesn't yield additional XP.  The reward item Cenarion Plant Salve is used to cleanse the various corrupted plants around Felwood and the reward you get from them is decent (but no XP).

03) Go NW in Ruins of Constellas and do "Corrupted Sabers": use your Winna's Kitten Carrier quest item at the green moonwell there 32.67.
04) Go north to Bloodvenom Post and once you get there talk to the Corrupted Saber that is following you, then turn in "Corrupted Sabers" 34.52.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) Follow the main path NE to Felpaw Village and clear the mobs around the Deadwood Cauldron at 60.6, or distract the mobs some how (See Video), then click on the Cauldron to turn in "Falling to Corruption", accept "Mystery Goo".
06) Go NE, right before the Timbermaw Hold entrance and turn in "Speak to Nafien" 65.8.  Also turn in any feathers you may have (repeatable quest to increase rep).
07) Go through the cave to get to Winterspring and right outside of the cave, turn in "Winterfall Activity" (28.34 in Winterspring).
08) Follow the main path south to Donova Snowden 31.45 and turn in "Mystery Goo", accept "Toxic Horrors".  Then if you are not level 56, grind on any mob until you are.
09) Hearth to Everlook.
10) Exit Everlook to your right and go to the Wind Rider Master 60.36 to fly to Orgrimmar, Durotar.
11) Once at Orgrimmar, get your level 56 spells/abilities. 
#27

 

01) Leave Orgrimmar south and get on the zeppelin (51.14 in Durotar) to go to The Undercity.
02) At the UC go to the Innkeeper and make it your new home 68.38.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) Go to the Bank in the middle of the UC 66.44 and get the following items out:  Felwood Slime Samples, Un'goro Slime Samples, Everlook Report and Studies in Spirit Speaking.  And if you have 60 extra Runecloth (for cloth donation) get that out as well.  Put the following items in your bank:  Cenarion Beacon, any Corrupted Soul Shards and any Cenarion Plant Salves.
04) Find Harbinger Balthazad /target, he patrols around the bank (wearing a white shirt with a lantern), once you find him, accept "A Call to Arms: The Plaguelands!".
05) If you have the 60 extra Runecloth, go to the Magic Quarter and turn in "A Donation of Runecloth" 72.29, for undead faction.
WOTLK, 5b) If you need to, you can upgrade First Aid from the First Aid Trainer 73.55 at Rogues' Quarter.  NOTE: Runecloth Bandage requires skill 260 and Heavy Runecloth Bandage skill 290.

06) Go SW to The Apothecarium and next to Chemist Fuely 48.73 use the two Testing Equipments (both red and green) to turn your Slime Samples into the quest items you need.  Then turn in "A Sample of Slime..." and "... and a Batch of Ooze".  Accept "Melding of Influences".  NOTE:  You can destroy any remaining Slime Samples to free bag space.
07) Go to the Bank in the middle of the UC (66.44, #3 on map) and put the following items into it:  Encased Corrupt Ooze.   Get out of the bank:  Swim Speed Potions, (if you have all of them) 30+ Dark Iron Scraps and 30+ Savage Fronds.  And there is a quest in Eastern Plaguelands that wants these items:  2 Thorium Bar, Golden Rod, 8 Hi-Explosive Bomb, 8 Unstable Trigger (get these out, either from the AH or bank if you can get all of them).  You can then mail these items (including the 30+ Dark Iron Scraps and 30+ Savage Fronds) to yourself and retrieve them from the mailbox at Light's Hope in EPL later.
08) Leave The Undercity and go east in Tirisfal Glades to the The Bulwark (83.69 in Tirisfal Glades)..
#28

 
56-56 Western Plaguelands (log)
01) Make a left on the main path as you enter The Bulwark and turn in "A Call To Arms: The Plaguelands!" (83.69 in Tirisfal Glades), accept "Scarlet Diversions".  Then next to you loot the Box of Incendiaries to obtain a Flame in a Bottle.
02) Next to you, turn in "The Everlook Report" 83.68.  From this same NPC, complete "Argent Dawn Commission".  NOTE:  The trinket received called Argent Dawn Commission will allow you to collect Scourgestones from undead mobs in the Plaguelands (both eastern and western).  Right-click it in your inventory to wear it.  As long as you have it on, this will allow you to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn, which will allow you to buy items such as Enriched Manna Biscuits later.
WOTLK, 2a) Get the Flight Path from the Bat Handler 84.70.

03) Go to the other side of The Bulwark, and behind the bigger tent accept "A Plague Upon Thee" 83.72.
04) Go east into Western Plaguelands (WPL) to Felstone Field and up in the house, accept "Better Late Than Never" (38.54 in WPL).
05) Go in the barn next to the house and click on Janice's Parcel 39.55 to turn in "Better Late Than Never".  Then click Janice's Parcel again to accept the next part of "Better Late Than Never".
06) Go just NE a bit to the small Scarlet camp 41.52 and do "Scarlet Diversions": clear the mobs in the area (you need to work quickly to avoid respawns) then click on the Command Tent to burn it and then use the Scourge Banner quest item at the camp. (See Video)
07) Go back to The Bulwark (make a right on the path) and turn in "Scarlet Diversions" (83.69 in Tirisfal Glades, #1 on map).  Accept "The Scourge Cauldrons".
08) Make sure you are resupplied 83.70.  NOTE:  Make sure you save all Bone Fragments you find, you will need 30 of them for a later quest.
09) Go to the other side of The Bulwark and turn in "The Scourge Cauldrons" 83.72, accept "Target: Felstone Field".
#29

 

Cauldron quest chain Info:  You need to kill the Cauldron Lord at each targeted farm field (listed below in the next section), grab their key and use it at the big cauldron in the middle of the farm fields.  You will be going back and forth from The Bulwark to the next field.  As you enter each field, the Cauldron Lord spawns near the Scourge Cauldron in the middle of each field, if the Cauldron Lord is not there, leave the field and come back.  The last few Cauldrons can be tough to solo so you may want to start finding a group for them, but you should be able to solo them with good skill.
#30

 

01) At Felstone Field, do "Target: Felstone Field": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Bilemaw /target 37.57.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Felstone Field", accept "Return to the Bulwark".
02) Go back to The Bulwark and turn in "Return to the Bulwark" (83.72 in Tirisfal Glades), accept "Target: Dalson's Tears".
03) At Dalson's Tears, do "Target: Dalson's Tears": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Malvinious /target 46.52.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Dalson's Tears", accept "Return to the Bulwark".
04) There is a small quest chain here at this farm that yields good XP:  Go in the Barn at Dalson's Tears and click on Mrs. Dalson's Diary 48.51 laying on the ground to complete "Mrs. Dalson's Diary".
05) Find and kill/loot the Wandering Skeleton /target.  This one skeleton wanders around the NE side of Dalson's Tears.  He circles around the barn as well.
06) Click on the Outhouse 48.50 behind the barn to complete "Locked Away".  Then Farmer Dalson will spawn next to the Outhouse, kill/loot this zombie.
07) Go up in the house (next to the barn) and click on the Locked Cabinet 47.50 to turn in "Locked Away".  Quest chain complete.
08) Go back to The Bulwark and turn in "Return to the Bulwark" (83.72 in Tirisfal Glades, #2 on map), accept "Target: Writhing Haunt".
09) At The Writhing Haunt, do "Target: Writhing Haunt": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Razarch /target 53.66.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Writhing Haunt", accept "Return to the Bulwark".
10) Go in the small house at The Writhing Haunt and accept "The Wildlife Suffers Too" 54.65.
11) Go back to The Bulwark and turn in "Return to the Bulwark" (83.72 in Tirisfal Glades, #2 on map), accept "Target: Gahrron's Withering".  Make sure you are resupplied.
12) At Gahrron's Withering, do "Target: Gahrron's Withering": kill/loot Cauldron Lord Soulwrath /target 63.59.  Then click on the Scourge Cauldron to turn in "Target: Gahrron's Withering", accept "Return to the Bulwark".   NOTE:  We will turn this in later.
13) Go east into Eastern Plaguelands (follow path on map) 5.38 in EPL..
#31

 
56-57 Eastern Plaguelands (log)
01) Go north along Thondoril River to Tiron Fordring 5.38 and accept "Demon Dogs", "Blood Tinged Skies" and "Carrion Grubbage".
02) Grind SE to The Marris Stead 23.68 and accept "To Kill With Purpose", "Un-Life's Little Annoyances" and "The Ranger Lord's Behest".
03) Go south to The Undercroft and down in the crypt, from the Torn Scroll 24.78 laying on the ground, accept "Hameya's Plea".
04) Grind east to Darrowshire to Pamela Redpath and turn in "Sister Pamela" 32.84, accept "Pamela's Doll".
05) At Darrowshire do "Pamela's Doll": find the 3 parts to Pamela's Doll:  the head, the left side and right side.  These parts have multiple spawn locations around Darrowshire in each of the 3 buildings in the area (excluding the building where Pamela is at).  They look like little yellow things.  Search the buildings for the 3 parts: big house 34.85, torn down house 35.85, small house 35.83, then click one of them to combine them to make Pamela's Doll.  (See Video)
06) Go back to Pamela Redpath at Darrowshire and turn in "Pamela's Doll" ( 32.84, #4 on map).  Accept "Auntie Marlene" and "Uncle Carlin".
07) Around southern EPL around 36.76, finish "Blood Tinged Skies": kill 30 Plaguebats.  Also do part of "Demon Dogs": kill 20 Plaguehound Runts.  And start working on "Carrion Grubbage": kill/loot Carrion mobs for the worm meat.  All of these mobs are found in southern Eastern Plaguelands and the Carrion worm mobs are all over Eastern Plaguelands.  Once you are nearing completion, work your way east to the next step:
08) Grind to Light's Hope Chapel 74.58 and accept "The Restless Souls" and "Zaeldarr the Outcast".
#32

 

01) Go north a bit and turn in "Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff" 76.54.  Also from this NPC, if you have at least 20 Minion's Scourgestones, complete a "Minion's Scourgestones" (and dont consume the item received).
02) Next to you, turn in "Uncle Carlin" 76.54.  Accept "Defenders of Darrowshire".
03) Get resupplied/repaired from Craftsman Wilhelm 75.54 next to the Forge.  NOTE:  If for some reason you are already Friendly with The Argent Dawn, then stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits from the female dwarf Miranda Breechlock 76.54 by the tent.
WOTLK, 3a) You should be friendly now with The Argent Dawn, if so:  Right in front of the Inn's Entrance, go to Rayne 75.53 and if you have the 30 Savage Fronds (in the mailbox?) accept and turn in "Savage Fronds".

WOTLK, 3b) Also (if you are friendly with The Argent Dawn):  Go in the Inn and in the back from the human with black hair 76.52 , accept and turn in "Binding the Dreadnaught" (requires the 30 Dark Iron Scraps, that could be in your mailbox).

04) At the other side of the Inn, from the human on the horse and with your one Argent Dawn Valor Token you might have, accept and turn in "They Call Me "The Rooster" 75.52.  If you dont have it yet, do this when you can.

05) If you were able to obtain the items needed for "That's Asking A Lot", then turn it in (at the dwarf Smokey LaRue by the Campfire, 75.52 ), accept "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent".
06) North in Light's Hope, get the FP there from the Bat Handler 74.51.

07) OPTIONAL: The quest "Bolstering Our Defenses" 74.52 is a PvP tower capture quest that is optional and up to you if you would like to do it or not, my guide will not cover it further.  I don't recommend doing this if you are trying to level quickly.
#33

 

01) Go SW to Corin's Crossing around 55.62 and do "To Kill With Purpose": kill/loot the undead mobs there to obtain Living Rot (work fast they only last 10 min), then use your Mortar and Pestle quest item to combine 7 of them to make the item you need.  Also start working on "Defenders of Darrowshire": kill Gibbering Ghouls and then talk to the spirit that spawns (don't need to finish this now though).
02) Just north around 56.50, do part of "Demon Dogs" and "Un-Life's Little Annoyances": kill 5 Plaguehounds /target and 20 Noxious Plaguebats.  These mobs are found in the middle-eastern side of Eastern Plaguelands.
03) Grind north to Zul'Mashar (ramp entrance at 59.19 )..
04) Go up into Zul'Mashar and do "Hameya's Plea": kill/loot Infiltrator Hameya /target.  This troll patrols around in front of the temple 65.13.  Stay away from the dirt mounds in front of the temple as undeads will spawn near them, it's better to wait until Infiltrator Hameya patrols up front for you to take on alone.  Also he hits hard so be prepared.
05) Finish up "Carrion Grubbage", "Demon Dogs" and "Un-Life's Little Annoyances": kill/loot Carrion mobs for the worm meat, kill 5 Frenzied Plaguehounds and kill 10 Monstrous Plaguebats.  These mobs are found NE around the open fields of Eastern Plaguelands around 56.50.  NOTE:  You can find some more spirits for "Defenders of Darrowshire" around 61.35 from the Diseased Flayers, and you can find more on the later steps as well.
06) GROUP 2+ Players (or could be soloable): Go north to Quel'Lithien Lodge entrance 47.17 and try to do "The Ranger Lord's Behest": kill the needed blood elf mobs and go into the lodge, make a right, then loot the Quel'Thalas Registry (book laying on a bench 48.14 ).  This quest may be hard to solo.  If you are almost lvl 57, then grind to it before doing this quest.  You may need to distract the mobs in the lodge, grab the book and run out some how.  If you cannot solo this, or find help then it can be skipped.
07) Go west to Plaguewood (starting at 44.26 ) and do the following 3 quests together:
08) "A Plague Upon Thee": loot the Large Termite Mounds, these are spaced all around Plaguewood (see map).
09) "Defenders of Darrowshire": continue working on this by killing Cannibal Ghouls and talk to the spirits that spawn.  You will have more opportunities later to finish this.
10) If you have it do "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent": go in the various ziggurats around Plaguewood and use Smokey's Special Compound quest item inside the buildings.
#34

 

01) Go west to Terrordale and in the hut turn in "The Restless Souls" 11.29, SKIP the next part (dungeon).  And next to you, accept "Augustus' Receipt Book".
02) Do "Augustus' Receipt Book": go NE a bit to the Terrordale Inn (the largest building in the area entrance 14.28 ) and go up in the Inn, make a right and between two split open barrels loot Augustus' Receipt Book 14.26 on the ground.  NOTE:  Kill/loot all Crypt Fiends you see to start obtaining Crypt Fiend Parts (used for a later quest).
03) Go back to the hut in Terrordale and turn in "Augustus' Receipt Book" ( 11.29 , #1 on map).  NOTE:  You can now get resupplied from this NPC.
04) OPTIONAL:  Grind on Crypt Fiend mobs all around Terrordale to collect 30 Crypt Fiend Parts.  This can be used for a later quest.  There is more in the next step as well (but watch out for the Elite):

05) Go through the long cave entrance 12.25.  NOTES:  This cave journey is not easy.  There is a lvl 60 elite spider /target (with a 1 hour respawn) that patrols back and forth to the 2 entrances of this cave.  Try to sneak past it if you can.  If this cave is too hard for you, then another possible quick alternative is to take the Tower Gryphon ride from the Plaguewood Tower 19.27 to the Crown Guard Tower, as long as the horde has control over the towers that is.  Try to get all 30 Crypt Fiend Parts before leaving this area.  If you are still far off from that, then you can skip getting the remaining ones.
06) Once you get out to the other side of the cave (or take the tower ride), north along Thondoril River at Tiron Fordring 5.38 , turn in "Demon Dogs" "Blood Tinged Skies" and "Carrion Grubbage".  Accept "Redemption".  Simply talk to Tiron Fordring to complete "Redemption", accept "Of Forgotten Memories".
07) Grind SE to The Marris Stead 23.68 and turn in "To Kill With Purpose", "Un-Life's Little Annoyances" and (if you did it) "The Ranger Lord's Behest".  Accept "Duskwing, Oh How I Hate Thee..." and "The Corpulent One".
TBC, 7b) Do "Duskwing, Oh How I Hate Thee...": kill/loot Duskwing /target.  This large lvl 60 bat patrols just north and NE of The Marris Stead around 28.63.

TBC, 7c) Go back to The Marris Stead 23.68 and turn in "Duskwing, Oh How I Hate Thee...".

08) Go south to The Undercroft, go down in the crypt and then do "Zaeldarr the Outcast": kill/loot Zaeldarr the Outcast 24.78.  (See Video)
09) Behind the crypt, next to the wooden wagon, click on the Mound of Dirt 24.79 to turn in "Hameya's Plea".
10) Next to you, do "Of Forgotten Memories": click on the Loose Dirt Mound 25.80 that is next to Tirion Fordring's Grave, then kill/loot Mercutio Filthgorger (he has 3 bodyguards).  IMPORTANT TIP:  These mobs spawn between the two dirt mounds (from the previous step).  Mercutio Filthgorger spawns in first, then his bodyguards soon after.  So act fast and immediately pull Mercutio Filthgorger away from his spawn point to avoid fighting his guards too, as this fight can be really tough to do solo without this tactic.
#35

 

01) Go NW to Tiron Fordring 5.38 and turn in "Of Forgotten Memories", accept "Of Lost Honor".
1b) Go NE to Corin's Crossing around 54.64 and continue working on (if you still need to) "Defenders of Darrowshire": kill any Gibbering Ghouls or Diseased Flayers and then talk to the spirit that spawns!  You will have another opportunity later to finish this.
02) Go to the south side of Light's Hope Chapel, then turn in "Zaeldarr the Outcast" 74.58.
03) Go north in Light's Hope Chapel and turn in (if it's finished) "Defenders of Darrowshire" 76.54.
04) Next to you at Duke Nicholas Zverenhoff /target 76.54, turn in any Scourgestones you have.  If you haven't yet, save at least one Argent Dawn Valor Token for a later quest.  You can consume the rest to increase rep with The Argent Dawn.
05) Get repaired/resupplied from Craftsman Wilhelm 75.54 next to the Forge.
07) In the Inn, if you have the 30 Crypt Fiend Parts, accept and turn in "Cryptstalker Armor Doesn't Make Itself..." (from Huntsman Leopold).

08) Go north a bit more in Light's Hope Chapel and turn in (if you did it) "When Smokey Sings, I Get Violent" 75.52.  If you turned this in, you can then destroy Smokey's Special Compound.
09) If you still need to:  Next to you at the human on the horse and with your Argent Dawn Valor Token, accept and turn in "They Call Me "The Rooster" 75.52.

10) Go NE a bit around 70.46 and finish (if you still need to) "Defenders of Darrowshire": kill any Gibbering Ghouls or Diseased Flayers and then talk to the spirit that spawns.
11) Grind north to Northdale and do "Of Lost Honor": loot the Symbol of Lost Honor 66.29 (under the lake).
TBC, 11b) Do "The Corpulent One": kill/loot Borelgore /target (lvl 60 big yellow worm, around 50.27)

12) IF YOU STILL NEED TO: Go SE to Light's Hope Chapel and turn in "Defenders of Darrowshire" (#3 on map) 76.54.
TBC, 12b) Go back to The Marris Stead 23.68 and turn in "The Corpulent One".

13) Grind all the way back west to Tiron Fordring ( 5.38 , #1 on map) and turn in "Of Lost Honor", accept "Of Love and Family".
#36

 

01) Go south at the island of Caer Darrow in Western Plaguelands (use a Swim Speed Potion) and turn in "Of Love and Family" 66.75 in WPL.  SKIP the next part (Dungeon).
02) Hearth to UC.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) At the Bank in the middle of the UC, put the following items into it: any remaining Crypt Fiend Parts, Savage Fronds, any Core of Elements and any Insignias.
04) Drop down east to Guild Creation and turn in "Better Late Than Never" 70.43, accept "The Jeremiah Blues".
05) Underneath the bank from the Cockroach Vendor 68.44, turn in "The Jeremiah Blues", accept "Good Luck Charm".
06) Find Harbinger Balthazad /target, he patrols around the bank (wearing a white shirt with a lantern), once you find him, accept "The New Frontier".
07) Also if you still need to turn in 60 extra Runecloth (for cloth donation), go to the Magic Quarter and turn in "A Donation of Runecloth" 72.29, for undead faction.

#37

 
57-58 Western Plaguelands (log)
01) Make a right at The Bulwark and turn in "Return to the Bulwark" 83.72.
02) Next to you behind the tent, turn in "A Plague Upon Thee" 83.72, accept the next part.  NOTE:  If you have any remaining Plagueland Termites in your inventory, you can destroy them.
TBC, 2a) From the male blood elf 83.71 accept "The So-Called Mark of the Lightbringer".

03) At the other side of The Bulwark, complete "Mission Accomplished!" 83.69.  Also accept "All Along the Watchtowers".
04) If you have turned Friendly with The Argent Dawn, then you can stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits next to you from Argent Quartermaster Hasana 83.68.  These biscuits will help you level faster by receiving mana faster from consuming these biscuits.

05) Go into Western Plaguelands to Felstone Field and up in the house, turn in "Good Luck Charm" 38.54, accept "Two Halves Become One".
06) Go south of Felstone Field around 37.58 and do "Two Halves Become One": kill/loot the Jabbering Ghoul /target (only one mob spawn that patrols around the farm).  This mob drops Good Luck Other-Half-Charm.  Right-click this item in your inventory.
07) Go back up in the house at Felstone Field and turn in "Two Halves Become One" (38.54, #5 on map).
08) Go north to Northridge Lumber Camp and do "A Plague Upon Thee": clear the mobs at the lumber camp, click on the Northridge Lumber Mill Crate 48.32 to place the termite barrel.  Then click on the spawned Termite Barrel on top of the crate to turn in "A Plague Upon Thee", accept the next part.  (See Video)
09) Go NE down a long slope in Northridge Lumber Camp and accept "Unfinished Business" 52.28.
10) Go south a bit and do "Unfinished Business": kill the required amount of Scarlet humans in the area.  The mages and knights are around 51.41.  NOTE: They share spawns with each other.  See next step for the medics and hunters:
11) Go south a bit to the small Scarlet camp and kill the needed medics and hunters around 52.44.
#38

 

01) Go back up NE in Northridge Lumber Camp, down the long slope and turn in "Unfinished Business" 52.28, accept the next part.
02) Go east a bit and do "Unfinished Business": kill Huntsman Radley 58.36 /target.  See next step for the other named mob to kill:
03) Kill Cavalier Durgen /target.  He patrols around in the tower 55.24.  Also go up this tower and do "The So-Called Mark of the Lightbringer" by looting the Holy Coffer.
04) Go back down in Northridge Lumber Camp and turn in "Unfinished Business" (52.28, #1 on map), accept the next part.
05) Go north to Hearthglen and do the last part of "Unfinished Business": go to the top of the watchtower 46.18 to complete the quest.  Just mount up and avoid the mobs.  Having a healing potion on hand helps.  (Watch This Video to see a shortcut and how to do this quest).
06) Go back down in Northridge Lumber Camp and turn in "Unfinished Business" (52.28, #1 on map).
07) Make sure "The Wildlife Suffers Too" is finished: kill 8 Diseased Wolves /target.  These wolves are found mostly above Dalson's Tears farm.  They share spawns with the spiders in the area, so kill them if not enough wolves.  Work your way to the next step:
08) Go south to the small house at The Writhing Haunt and turn in "The Wildlife Suffers Too" 54.65, accept the next part.
09) Do "The Wildlife Suffers Too" (part 2): kill 8 Diseased Grizzly /target.  These bears are found north and east of The Writhing Haunt.  They share spawns with the spiders, so kill spiders if not enough bears.
10) Go back to the small house at The Writhing Haunt and turn in "The Wildlife Suffers Too" (54.65, #8 on map).  Accept "Glyphed Oaken Branch".
11) Go SW to Sorrow Hill and in the house (up or down in it) turn in "Auntie Marlene" 49.79, accept "A Strange Historian".
12) Do "A Strange Historian": next to the house your at, loot Joseph Redpath's Monument in the graveyard 50.77.  (See Video)
13) Enter Ruins of Andorhal (bridge entrance at 49.72)..
#39

 

01) Make a left at the tower and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Four 47.71.
02) Go west and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower One 40.72.  NOTE: You must use the torch item right in the doorway of these towers in order for it to work, don't waste your charges!
03) Go north and enter the Andorhal Inn entrance 40.68, go upstairs and turn in "A Strange Historian" 39.67, accept "The Annals of Darrowshire".  Also accept "A Matter of Time".
04) Enter the City Hall building in the middle of Andorhal entrance 44.69 and do "The Annals of Darrowshire": loot the Annals of Darrowshire (blue book).  The actual Annals of Darrowshire book looks different from the others, it has a lighter tint on the top portion of the pages in the book, unlike the rest which has a darker tint on the top portion of the pages. (See Video)
05) Go north and do part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Two 42.66.
06) Go NE and do the last part of "All Along the Watchtowers": use the Beacon Torch quest item at Tower Three 44.63.
07) Go east a bit and do "A Matter of Time": use the Temporal Displacer quest item near the silos around 47.63 then kill the Temporal Parasites that spawn.  NOTE: The quest item only works on the silos emitting blue beams of light.
08) Go west and enter the Andorhal Inn (entrance 40.68, #3 on map), go upstairs and turn in "A Matter of Time" and "The Annals of Darrowshire".  Accept "Counting Out Time" and "Brother Carlin".
09) All around Ruins of Andorhal do "Counting Out Time": loot the Small Lockboxes all around the broken down houses.  There is usually one at each broken down house.
10) Enter the Andorhal Inn (entrance 40.68, #3 on map), go upstairs and turn in "Counting Out Time".  NOTES:  This quest is repeatable, but you will not get additional XP from it.  The quest reward Attuned Dampener can only be used on Araj the Summoner in the middle of Andorhal, and there will be quests later associated with killing him.
#40

 

TBC, 00) Go west and make a left at The Bulwark and turn in "The So-Called Mark of the Lightbringer" (83.71 in Tirisfal Glades, #1 on map).  Wait a few seconds then accept "Defiling Uther's Tomb".

01) Turn in "A Plague Upon Thee" 83.72 in Tirisfal.
02) At the other side of The Bulwark turn in "All Along the Watchtowers" 83.69.  Accept "Scholomance".  You can then destroy the Beacon Torch item.
03) Next to you at Argent Officer Garush 83.68, turn in any Scourgestones you may have.  Then use the Argent Dawn Valor Token's received to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn.
04) Make sure you are resupplied.  You can stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits next to you from Argent Quartermaster Hasana 83.68.  These biscuits will help you level faster by receiving mana faster from eating/drinking.
05) At The Bulwark turn in "Scholomance" 83.69, accept "Skeletal Fragments".
06) Go back to Ruins of Andorhal around 40.69 in WPL and do "Skeletal Fragments": kill/loot skeleton mobs for the 15 Skeletal Fragments.  Also keep grinding until you have collected 30 Bone Fragments for a later quest.
TBC, 6a) Go SE to Uther's Tomb 52.84 and do "Defiling Uther's Tomb": equip the quest item Corrupted Mark of the Lightbringer, then use it at the tomb.

07) Go west and make a right at The Bulwark and turn in "Skeletal Fragments" (83.69 in Tirisfal Glades, #2 on map), SKIP "Mold Rhymes With...".
08) Next to you at Argent Officer Garush 83.68, turn in any Scourgestones you may have.
09) If you are a class that uses mana, then stock up on Enriched Manna Biscuits 83.68.  NOTE:  I personally buy about 600 biscuits.

TBC, 9a) At the other side of The Bulwark turn in "Defiling Uther's Tomb" 83.71.

10) Go west in Tirisfal Glades and get on the zeppelin 61.59 to go to Orgrimmar.  While waiting, build up First Aid.
#41

 

01) In Orgrimmar, go to the Inn and make Orgrimmar your new home from the Innkeeper 54.68.
02) Go to the Bank 50.69 and put your Enriched Manna Biscuits in there so that you only have about 160 left in your inventory.  Also put into the bank:  your stack of 30+ Bone Fragments, any extra Scourgestones, Extended Annals of Darrowshire and Attuned Dampener.  And get the following items out of the bank:  Umi's Mechanical Yeti, Encased Corrupt Ooze, and your current stack of Core of Elements.
03) Cloth Donations are turned in NE at Magar's Cloth Goods to the Orc's Horde Cloth Quartermaster 64.51.  You may have already turned in the 60 Runecloth to them in the past.

05) Go up the spiral ramp next to you, then go get new spells/abilities. 
06) Cloth Donations are also turned in at Darkbriar Lodge (Valley of Spirits) from the Troll's Horde Cloth Quartermaster 38.88.  Following the guide you would still need to turn in 60 Mageweave Cloth and 60 Runecloth to them.  NOTE:  The "Additional Runecloth" quests do NOT yield XP (only rep).
07) Go up to the Wind Rider Master 45.64 and fly to Thunder Bluff, Mulgore.

#44

 
58-59 Silithus (log)
01) As you enter Silithus go to the small camp on your left (Valor's Rest) and turn in "Wasteland" 82.19, accept "The Spirits of Southwind".
02) Follow the main path west to Cenarion Hold and turn in "Taking Back Silithus" 51.38, accept "Securing the Supply Lines".

03) Next to you at the Wanted Poster: Deathclasp 51.38, accept "Wanted - Deathclasp, Terror of the Sands" (Elite).

04) Go up in the Inn next to you and accept "Deadly Desert Venom" 52.39.

05) Go up in Cenarion Hold and accept "The Twilight Mystery" 50.37.

06) Behind the moonwell, accept "Secret Communication" 49.38.  And IF YOU ARE LVL 60 accept: "Abyssal Contacts", and (next to you) "Twilight Geolords".

07) Next to you, accept "Report to General Kirika" 49.37.

08) At the Wind Rider Master 49.37 in Cenarion Hold, get the FP.
09) Go in the building next to you and accept "Dearest Natalia" 49.34.

10) Go south to Southwind Village around 62.52 and do "The Spirits of Southwind" along with "A Reliquary of Purity": kill 8 Tortured Druids and 8 Tortured Sentinels around the village and work your way up in the village into the building and loot the Dusty Reliquary 63.55.
11) Grind/loot all mobs as you go NE to Valor's Rest and turn in "The Spirits of Southwind" (82.19, #1 on map), accept "Hive in the Tower".
12) Go back down SW to Southwind Village and do "Hive in the Tower": go to the top of the swarming bug tower 60.53 and click on the Hive'Ashi Pod, then kill/loot the Hive'Ashi Ambushers until you get the Encrusted Silithid Object.  Also while doing Southwind Village quests you should find an item (which drops from the bugs), which starts "Brann Bronzebeard's Lost Letter", accept it.  And keep grinding until you get it.
13) Grind NE to Valor's Rest and turn in "Hive in the Tower" (82.19, #1 on map), accept "Umber, Archivist".
#45

 

01) Go to the small camp above Staghelm Point around 66.21and do "Secret Communication": kill/loot the Twilight mobs.  NOTE:  IF YOU ARE LVL 60 the quest "Abyssal Contacts" can be done here and multiple times later in this zone but my guide will not cover it or the follow ups, but its worth doing.  And keep the next step in mind:

02) All around NE Silithus do "Deadly Desert Venom" along with "Securing the Supply Lines": kill/loot Stonelash Scorpids (scorpids), kill/loot Sand Skitterers (spiders), and kill 15 Dredge Strikers (snakes).

03) Go to Cenarion Hold and turn in "Securing the Supply Lines" 51.38, accept "Stepping Up Security".

04) Next to you, go up in the Inn and turn in "Deadly Desert Venom" 52.39.  Accept "Noggle's Last Hope".  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.

05) Go up in Cenarion Hold and behind the moonwell, turn in "Secret Communication" 49.38.  NOTE:  The repeatable quest "Encrypted Twilight Texts" does not yield XP, only rep with the Cenarion Circle.  But save your additional Encryped Twilight Texts for a future quest.

06) Go to the NW end of Silithus around 27.15and do "The Twilight Mystery": loot the tablets on the ground.  And kill/loot Dust Stormers & Whirling Invaders (wind elementals) until you get a total of 30 Core of Elements (used for a later quest), however if you are far off from the 30, you can optionally skip this.

07) All around the middle of Silithus do "Stepping Up Security": kill 20 Dredge Crushers.  Also do part of "Noggle's Last Hope": kill/loot Stonelash Pincers.  Work your way to the next step:

08) SE at the small horde camp, turn in "Report to General Kirika" 51.70, accept "Scouring the Desert".  NOTE:  For "Scouring the Desert", keep an eye out for a Silithyst Geyser which looks like a glowing red thing all around Silithuis, click on it and you will get a buff, maintain the buff while bringing it back to the PVP Horde base and stand in the teleporter looking thing, then turn the quest in for good XP!

09) Grind south to Bronzebeard Encampment and turn in "Brann Bronzebeard's Lost Letter" 41.88.  Also at this same NPC, talk to him to do part of "Dearest Natalia".

10) Next to you at Frankal Stonebridge 41.89(sitting down at a tent), finish "Dearest Natalia" by talking to this NPC.
#46


01) All around southern Silithus finish "Noggle's Last Hope": kill/loot Stonelash Flayers (scorpids) and Rock Stalker (spiders).
02) Grind north to Cenarion Hold and just outside of the Inn, turn in "Stepping Up Security" 51.38.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
03) Go up in the Inn next to you and turn in "Noggle's Last Hope" 52.39.  Then next to you, accept "Noggle's Lost Satchel".
04) Go up in Cenarion Hold and turn in "The Twilight Mystery" 50.37, accept "The Deserter".
05) Go in the building next to you and turn in "Dearest Natalia" 49.34, SKIP "Into The Maw of Madness" (group quest).
06) Go SE to Ortell's Hideout (small cave) and turn in "The Deserter" 67.70, accept "The Twilight Lexicon".
07) Grind NW to Twilight Base Camp and do "The Twilight Lexicon": kill/loot the 3 named mobs.  First one is Twilight Keeper Havunth /target.  This orc patrols around the Twilight Base Camp 41.43.  Also start working on (if you have the lvl 60 quest) "Twilight Geolords" by killing the Twilight Geolords (and you will see more of these mobs in the next 2 camps coming up).  See next steps for the other two:
08) Grind west to Twilight Post and kill/loot Twilight Keeper Mayna /target (female human 26.37).  NOTE:  Feel free to grind on the Twilight mobs here to obtain Encryped Twilight Texts.  You will need 10 for a future quest and you will have one more opportunity in the next step to get some more:
09) Go south to Twilight Outpost and kill/loot Twilight Keeper Exeter /target (red armored human with fiery sword 16.86).  NOTES:  You can avoid Hive'Zora & the Alliance camp by hugging the west side of Silithus mountains.  Also keep grinding on the Twilight mobs until you get 10 Encryped Twilight Texts, this will be used for an easy turn-in quest later.  And make sure "Twilight Geolords" is finished now.
10) Go right behind Bronzebeard Encampment to the mountain entrance 43.90 and try to do "Wanted - Deathclasp, Terror of the Sands" (Elite): kill/loot Deathclasp /target.  This level 59 Elite scorpid is soloable but could give you some troubles.  If you cannot solo it, or find a group to help you, then it can be skipped.  (See Video)
11) Also at the same area of Deathclasp, do "Noggle's Lost Satchel": loot Noggle's Satchel (yellow sack on ground 45.91).  (See Video)

#49

59-60 Winterspring (log)
01) After you have Hearthed to Orgrimmar, go to the Bank 50.69 and put the following items into it:  any remaining Core of Elements, Bone Fragments, Savage Fronds, Dark Iron Scraps and any Insignias.

05) Go through Timbermaw Hold entrance 65.7 and make a right in the middle after the bridge to enter Moonglade up north.

12) Try to do "Winterfall Runners": try to find the pack of Winterfall Runners (three lvl 57s) that patrols the main path from the Timbermaw Hold cave 28.34 to Winterfall Village 68.38 and kill/loot them for the Winterfall Crate.  See map for exact patrol path.  If after making one pass and you cannot find these mobs, then this can be skipped for now.
13) If (or when ever) you have completed "Winterfall Runners", go west to Donova Snowden (31.45, #11 on map) and turn it in.  Accept "High Chief Winterfall" .  NOTE:  My guide will not cover "High Chief Winterfall".

#50


01) Just to your left as you enter Everlook (next to the hut) turn in "Are We There, Yeti?" 61.38.  Then complete (if you have the engineering profession) "A Yeti of Your Own".
02) From Felnok Steelspring under the blue tent 62.39, accept "Chillwind Horns".
03) Back in Everlook by the cage 62.38, accept "Luck Be With You" (Elite) and (if you don't have it yet) "Ursius of the Shardtooth".
04) Go in the Inn and make Everlook your new home from the Innkeeper 61.39.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
05) Go south to Frostwhisper Gorge entrance 59.66 and start working on "Luck Be With You" (Elite): loot Frostmaul Shards.   Just try to distract the giants to loot the shards.  Try to sneak in and take as much as you can, you can return here later to get more.
06) Hearth to Everlook.
07) Go just north of Everlook on the mountain slopes (starting at 61.32) and do (if you still need to) "Ursius of the Shardtooth": keep grinding on mobs while working north along the mountain slopes and then kill Ursius /target (lvl 56 bear) when he shows up.  See map for his patrol path.
08) Hearth (or go back to Everlook), by the cage 62.38, and turn in (if its done) "Luck Be With You", then accept and turn in "Cache of Mau'ari".  Also turn in (if you still need to) "Ursius of the Shardtooth", accept "Brumeran of the Chillwind".  NOTE:  The Cache of Mau'ari allows you to turn in E'kos for a temporary stat boost as long as the item remains in your bags.
09) Go south (starting around 55.47) and do "Brumeran of the Chillwind": kill Brumeran /target (lvl 58 Chimera).  See map for his patrol path.
10) If you need to, finish "Luck Be With You": go south to Frostwhisper Gorge (entrance 59.66, #5 on map) and loot Frostmaul Shards.  Distract the elite giants and sneak in to loot the shards.
#52

 

01) Go east to the Owl Wing Thicket entrance 64.59 and do "Wild Guardians": kill Moontouched Owlbeasts.
02) Once your Hearthstone cooldown is less than 5 minutes (keep grinding on owls until then), go back to Everlook (Don't Hearth) and back by the cage, turn in "Brumeran of the Chillwind", SKIP "Shy-Rotam".  Also, turn in (if you still need to) "Luck Be With You".
03) Exit Everlook to your right and go to the Wind Rider Master 60.36 and fly to Bloodvenom Post, Felwood.
04) At Bloodvenom Post, turn in "Wild Guardians" 35.53, accept the next part.
05) Hearth to Everlook.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
06) Go north of Everlook around 65.21 and do "Wild Guardians": kill 10 Berserk Owlbeasts.  While questing here you might find an called Blue-feathered Necklace, which starts "Guarding Secrets", accept it.
07) Just west of you do "Chillwind Horns": kill/loot Chillwind Ravagers.  Once finished, keep grinding until your Hearthstone cooldown is less than 11 minutes.
08) Die on purpose to get back to Everlook.
09) Go to the Wind Rider Master 60.36 and fly to Bloodvenom Post, Felwood.
10) At Bloodvenom Post, turn in "Wild Guardians" 35.53.  Also turn in (if you found the item) "Guarding Secrets", accept the next part.
11) At Bloodvenom Post, go to the Wind Rider Master 34.54 and fly to Thunder Bluff, Mulgore.
12) Once at TB, go to The Elder Rise to Nara Wildmane 76.32 and turn in "Guarding Secrets".
13) Hearth to Everlook.  Make sure you are repaired/resupplied.
14) In Everlook, from Felnok Steelspring under the blue tent 62.39, turn in "Chillwind Horns", accept "Return to Tinkee".  NOTE:  You can turn this in next time you head to Burning Steppes.
15) At this point you should be very close to level 60.  I usually grind the rest of the way to level 60 at the Owl Wing Thicket (entrance 64.59, #1 on map).  But here is a list of other things you could do instead of the owl grinding:
#53

 
Here are the things you can do to level from 59-60:
-->  You can go back into Eastern Plaguelands and do the many group quests that you couldn't solo.
-->  You can grind away at mobs in Andorhal to increase your rep with The Argent Dawn.
-->  You can grind away at Deadwood furbolgs up north in Felwood to gain rep with the Timbermaw.
-->  You can do Black Rock Depths Instance and all the quests associated with it.
-->  You can also do Stratholme Instance and all the quests associated with that.
-->  You can simply grind on any mob you like until you hit 60.
